Preliminary policy analysis suggests that peak-aware off-hours boosting can reduce full-cycle energy load by about 9% with roughly 7% runtime overhead, while naive throttling can increase total energy through overhead effects.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [MASt3R-Nav: WayPixel Navigation in Relative 3D Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24111)
*Vansh Garg,Rohit Jayanti,Krish Pandya,Sarthak Chittawar,Siddharth Tourani,Muhammad Haris Khan,Sourav Garg,Madhava Krishna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为“WayPixel Costmap”的像素级导航地图表示方法，利用局部相对3D几何信息取代全局几何依赖，提升了机器人视觉导航的精确度和执行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉导航存在两极分化：经典的3D地图依赖全局几何一致性但构建复杂，而基于图像或物体的拓扑图虽然简单但不具备高精度的几何导航能力。研究旨在寻找一种既能提供几何准确性又无需全局一致性依赖的地图表示方法。

Method: 1. 基于3D接地图像匹配技术，通过计算图像序列中图像对之间的像素对应关系构建连接图；2. 对像素级图进行稀疏化和近似处理，生成“WayPixel Costmap”（像素级代价地图）；3. 训练一个以该代价地图为条件的控制器，用于预测轨迹规划。

Result: 在四个模拟器导航任务及真实世界演示中，实验证明基于像素级代价地图的导航系统在控制预测精度上优于现有的图像级和物体级表示，展现了更强的导航能力。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种基于像素级相对几何的地图表示方法“WayPixel Costmap”。通过利用局部图像对之间的3D对应关系进行连接，该方法在无需全局几何一致性的前提下，实现了比传统图像级或物体级导航更精确的控制与规划，有效提升了视觉导航的性能。

Abstract: Visual navigation ability is strongly tied to its underlying representation of the world. Unlike classical 3D maps that require globally-consistent geometry, image- or object-relative topological graphs almost entirely do away with geometric understanding. But, this comes at the cost of navigation capability, often limiting it to merely teach-and-repeat. In this work, we propose a novel map representation in the form of pixel-relative connectivity, which is geometrically accurate but does not require global geometric consistency. Inspired by recent progress in 3D grounded image matching, we construct a map from an image sequence through inter-image connectivity based on pixel correspondences in the relative 3D coordinate systems of individual image pairs. We then use this pixel-level graph to perform global path planning by approximating and sparsifying intra-image pixel connectivity. Through this, we derive a ''WayPixel Costmap'' representation and train a controller conditioned on it to predict a trajectory rollout. We show that this dense pixel-level costmap based on relative geometry is a more accurate conditioning variable for control prediction than its image- and object-level counterparts. This enables a highly capable navigation system, as validated on four types of navigation tasks in the simulator and through real world demonstrations.

</details>


### [113] [Anisotropic Diffusion-Driven Ergodic Coverage in Multi-Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24125)
*Thales C. Silva,Anoop Kiran,Nora Ayanian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于各向异性Perona-Malik扩散的遍历搜索方法，解决了传统热方程模型在空间误差传播上过于均匀的问题，实现了对目标分布更精准的轨迹引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的遍历搜索算法（基于各向同性热方程）在处理目标分布变化时，会不加区分地向所有方向均匀传播误差，导致搜索效率在复杂环境下不足。

Method: 引入了各向异性扩散公式来求解遍历性问题，并利用Perona-Malik扩散的梯度来引导智能体运动，将传统的各向同性热方程方法作为该广义框架的一个特例。

Result: 通过一系列仿真证明，该方法能有效 generalize 现有的各类方法，且在多机器人系统下的遍历搜索表现优于传统扩散方法。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于各向异性扩散（特别是Perona-Malik扩散）的遍历搜索框架，能够根据目标分布的变化自适应地调整搜索行为，相比传统各向同性热方程方法具有更好的通用性和灵活性。

Abstract: We consider the problem of combining potential field and ergodic search on multi-robot systems. Traditional ergodic search algorithms use metrics for ergodicity that account for the desired distribution at different scales. Recently, a heat equation-driven ergodic approach was proposed, which adds flexibility to the smoothing of the ergodic metric. However, such an approach, as it is an isotropic diffusion, propagates the error uniformly in all directions, regardless of changes in the desired distribution. We introduce a general class of anisotropic diffusion formulation of the ergodicity problem, which generates a potential field for the ergodic search. We demonstrate that this approach generalizes previous results, which consider radial basis functions and the solution of the heat equation to represent the difference between the goal density distribution and the covered trajectories. In our solution, the agent movement is directed using the gradient of the solution of the Perona-Malik diffusion, and our formulation includes the heat equation as a special case. We demonstrate the methodology with a series of simulations in different scenarios.

</details>


### [114] [Investigating the Effect of a Series Elastic Actuation Retrofit to Black-Box Actuators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24127)
*Ivan Tregear,Ayhan Aktas,Ferdinando Rodriguez y Baena*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过集成定制化、低成本串联弹性（SE）元件来改进现有执行器性能的方法，成功将力控带宽提升了约3倍，并实现了优于昂贵商用传感器的性能，为提升机器人交互与力控性能提供了一种经济高效的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统刚性执行器存在顺应性不足、对环境交互不够友好且力控性能受限的问题；而现有的串联弹性执行器（SEA）虽提升了顺应性，但常受限于系统带宽。本研究旨在通过定制SE元件来缓解这一矛盾，同时解决黑盒执行器中的非线性干扰，实现低成本的高性能力反馈。

Method: 1. 设计并制造了一个扭转式串联弹性（SE）元件（刚度为2155.4 Nm/rad），通过有限元分析（FE）进行验证；2. 将该SE元件集成至现有的“黑盒”执行器中；3. 进行了开环和闭环力控实验，对比了改造前后的带宽，并与商用力传感器进行了性能与成本对比。

Result: 1. SEA集成使得系统开环力控带宽从10.32 Hz提升至30.32 Hz（提升2.93倍）；2. 该方案在力控表现上超越了商用传感器7.63%；3. 模块成本仅为25英镑，极具成本效益优势。

Conclusion: 通过引入低成本的定制化串联弹性执行器（SEA）模块，在不牺牲力控精度的前提下，显著提升了执行系统的力控带宽及整体性能，验证了该方案在机器人应用中实现高性价比力反馈的有效性。

Abstract: In robotic applications, actuators are typically designed to be stiff with minimal backlash to ensure precision and repeatability. However, this limits compliance, leading to potential damage and poor force control in uncertain environments. Series Elastic Actuation (SEA) introduces compliance to enhance disturbance rejection and enable force measurement via Hooke's Law but reduces system bandwidth.
  A custom Series Elastic (SE) element was retrofitted to a black-box actuator to mitigate non-linearities like backlash and static friction. Integrating the SE element enabled high-fidelity force measurements, improving force control bandwidth and performance.
  A torsional SE element was designed through Finite Element (FE) analysis, yielding a stiffness of 2155.4 Nm/rad. Open-loop force control bandwidth was measured for the original motor and the SEA-integrated configuration, while closed-loop bandwidth was assessed using feedback from the SEA and a commercial force sensor. The SEA module increased bandwidth from 10.32 Hz to 30.32 Hz, a 2.93X improvement. Additionally, it outperformed the commercial sensor by 7.63% despite costing 25 GBP, a fraction of the price.

</details>


### [115] [Afford-VLA: Action-Aligned Visual Planning via Internalized Affordance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24203)
*Runze Wang,Yuqian Fu,Yu Li,Tao Lin,Tianwen Qian,Mohamed Elhoseiny,Bo Zhao,Yanwei Fu,Yu-Gang Jiang,Xiangyang Xue*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Afford-VLA 是一种将任务条件下的可供性显式集成进 VLA 模型的视觉规划框架，通过内部生成的对齐交互区域优化动作预测，显著提升了机器人操作的精度与适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型在复杂场景中的空间推理能力有限，且现有的视觉规划方法存在耦合度低、依赖外部信号或缺乏任务相关性等问题。

Method: 提出 Afford-VLA 框架：引入可学习的 <AFF> 标记（Token）查询交互区域，通过多模态特征解码可供性掩码（Affordance masks），并将其转化为紧凑嵌入（Embedding）以调节动作生成，实现感知与动作的紧密耦合训练。

Result: 在 LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus 和 SimplerEnv 等多个仿真基准测试中均达到最先进水平（SOTA），并在真实世界环境实验中表现优异。

Conclusion: 将可供性（Affordance）内部化为一种与动作对齐的视觉规划，是提升视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型性能的有效范式，能够显著改善机器人在复杂场景下的空间推理与操作能力。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have shown strong potential for generalist robot manipulation, yet they remain limited by insufficient spatial reasoning, particularly in determining where to interact in complex visual scenes. While recent efforts introduce various forms of visual planning to address this issue, existing approaches either rely on global geometric cues, symbolic intermediate representations, or externally generated visual signals, which are often weakly coupled with downstream action prediction. In this work, we revisit visual planning in VLA systems and argue that effective planning should be local, visually grounded, internally generated, and directly aligned with action. Based on this insight, we propose Afford-VLA, a unified framework that internalizes task-conditioned affordance as an explicit visual planning interface within VLA models. Concretely, we introduce learnable <AFF> tokens to query task-relevant interaction regions, decode affordance masks from multimodal features, and convert them into compact embeddings that directly condition action generation. This design enables affordance to be both generated and utilized within the VLA, forming a tightly coupled perception-action pathway. To further support this integration, we adopt a training strategy that allows the affordance pathway to be jointly optimized with action prediction, improving its effectiveness for downstream control. We evaluate our method on multiple simulation benchmarks, including LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, and SimplerEnv, achieving consistent state-of-the-art performance, along with strong real-world results. These findings demonstrate that internalizing affordance as action-aligned visual planning provides a powerful paradigm for improving VLA systems.

</details>


### [116] [AcroRL: Learning Aggressive Quadrotor Inversion using Bidirectional Thrust](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24301)
*Gabriel Rodriguez,Henri Sayag,Abhishek Rathod,John Stecklein,Siddharth Saha,Christopher Barngrover,Wennie Tabib*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习的框架，通过调制参考轨迹，有效解决了双向推力四旋翼在翻转过程中的执行器饱和与控制难题，显著提升了翻转任务的位置精度与动态响应速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的几何控制方法在处理双向推力翻转时，受限于执行器饱和与电机反转延迟，依赖启发式调度与人工调参，难以在动态反转中保持高精度与稳定性。

Method: 提出了一种基于学习的框架，通过强化学习训练针对“常规-反转”及“反转-常规”两种转换模式的独立策略，对恒定的参考轨迹进行实时调制，以实现紧凑且受位置约束的翻转。

Result: 在仿真中，该方法较最优优化基准对比，位置均方根误差（RMSE）降低了32%，稳定时间缩短了57%；硬件实验验证了系统在多种偏航配置下的翻转成功性，且具备向下兼容轨迹跟踪的能力。

Conclusion: 该研究证实了基于强化学习的方法在处理双向推力四旋翼的高动态反转任务中，优于传统的几何控制方法，能够通过简单的策略调制实现精确的姿态转换，并兼顾通用导航控制。

Abstract: Bidirectional thrust grants quadrotors a second equilibrium condition and increased control authority, expanding the envelope of possible aggressive maneuvers and enabling inverted flight, perching, and sensing. Prior geometric control approaches extend differential flatness through Hopf fibration-based attitude representations to support bidirectional thrust, but struggle with actuator saturation and motor reversal delay during inversions, requiring heuristic thrust posture scheduling and waypoint tuning. We propose a learning-based framework that modulates a constant reference trajectory to perform compact, position-constrained quadrotor inversions while remaining compatible with traditional trajectory generation and tracking across flight regimes. Separate policies are trained via reinforcement learning for nominal-to-inverted and inverted-to-nominal transitions. In JAX-based simulation, the proposed method achieves the lowest position deviation and settling time across all evaluated baselines, reducing position root mean square error (RMSE) by 32% and settling time by 57% relative to the strongest optimization-based baseline. Hardware experiments demonstrate successful inversion across multiple yaw configurations with position RMSE below 0.35m, and compatibility with downstream trajectory generation and control through circular flight in both regimes. Additionally, we provide an open-source implementation of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [117] [Terrain-Adaptive Grouser Wheel for Optimal Planetary Exploration: Design and Experimental Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24311)
*Vincent Griffo,Yashwanth Kumar Nakka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种可实时调节轮齿高度的行星探测器轮毂设计，实验证明该设计在处理颗粒状和复杂地形时，相比传统固定轮毂能显著提升机动效率、降低滑移并节省能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有行星探测器在面对多变地形（如坡度和颗粒状地形）时面临巨大的移动挑战。目前关于多模式轮的研究多集中在刚度、顺应性和直径的调整，但对可调节轮齿的设计研究尚处于空白，而轮齿高度对改善颗粒地形上的性能至关重要。

Method: 设计并开发了一种名为 [Anonymized Robot Name] 的多模式轮，支持轮齿高度的连续调节。研究团队在四种典型地形（乙烯基地板、粗糙岩石、豌豆砾石和两种压实状态的沙子）上进行了 750 次实验，并开发了一种用于分析地形颗粒度与最优轮齿高度关系的简化缩放分析模型。

Result: 实验数据表明，使用自适应轮齿配置后，在颗粒地形中的滑移率降低了 30.0%–58.0%，运行时间和能耗最高改善了 77.4%。验证了并不存在单一的轮齿高度能适应所有地形。

Conclusion: 固定轮毂设计在多样化地形中存在局限性，通过调整轮齿（grouser）高度实现的可变形态轮设计是提升行星探测器在复杂地形下机动性、降低滑移率和能耗的有效解决方案。

Abstract: Planetary rovers operating in extraterrestrial environments often encounter significant mobility challenges due to varying terrain features such as gradients and granularity. While recent works in multimodal wheel design have explored adjustments in stiffness, compliance, and diameter as a means to improve terrain adaptability, full wheel grouser-adjustable designs remain largely unexplored. Grousers are a compelling feature to actuate, as granular terrains tend to require increased grouser height for improved wheel performance. As a result, we introduce [Anonymized Robot Name], a multimodal wheel capable of continuously adjusting its grouser height for terrain adaptation. The platform was evaluated across four representative surfaces, including vinyl flooring, coarse rock, pea gravel, and sand under two packing states, spanning a range of granular conditions. Results from 750 experimental trials demonstrate that adaptive deployment reduces slip by 30.0--58.0\% and improves travel time and energy consumption by up to 77.4\% in granular regimes relative to fixed configurations. Using the terrain trial data, a simplified scaling analysis was developed and validated, suggesting a relationship between terrain granularity and optimal grouser height for the tested configuration. No single grouser height minimized slip across all terrains, underscoring the limitations of fixed-wheel systems commonly used for planetary exploration. This observation reinforces the potential of grouser-adaptive morphology, such as [Anonymized Robot Name], as an effective solution for enhancing rover mobility across diverse and mobility-challenging extraterrestrial environments.

</details>


### [118] [IsaacIPC: Coupling High-Fidelity Simulation and Realistic Rendering for Contact-Rich Robotic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24339)
*Qixin Liang,Zhongqing Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了 IsaacIPC，这是一个集成 GPU 加速 IPC 和 GMCP 技术的新型机器人仿真框架，可实现实时的柔体仿真、高保真渲染及高精度触觉压力解析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人仿真框架在处理复杂刚体-柔体交互、实时高保真渲染以及高精度触觉感知方面存在局限，难以满足大规模数据采集和策略评估的需求。

Method: 1. 将 GPU 加速的增量势接触（IPC）算法集成到 IsaacSim/Lab 框架中；
2. 实现了模拟网格与视觉网格之间的变形映射；
3. 开发了几何砂浆接触势（GMCP）方法，在触觉表面通过势垒函数定量描述接触压力分布。

Result: 在多个典型机器人任务（四足机器人、灵巧手、UMI 夹爪）中验证了框架的有效性，并在接触基准测试中证明了 GMCP 对接触压力分布解析的优越性。

Conclusion: IsaacIPC 成功将 GPU 加速的 IPC 技术集成到 IsaacSim/Lab，并通过 GMCP 有效提升了触觉感知的准确性，为刚体-柔体机器人仿真提供了一种高效、高保真的解决方案。

Abstract: We present IsaacIPC, a robotic simulation framework that couples GPU accelerated incremental potential contact (IPC) with IsaacSim/Lab. IsaacIPC maps simulated deformation between simulation and visual meshes, enabling real-time realistic rendering with applications to data collection and policy evaluation. For tactile sensing, we introduce the geometric mortar contact potential (GMCP), which defines a barrier potential over contact samples on tactile surfaces to better resolve contact-pressure distributions. We evaluate GMCP on contact benchmarks and demonstrate IsaacIPC on rigid-deformable robotic simulations including a quadruped robot, a dexterous hand, and a universal manipulation interface (UMI) gripper.

</details>


### [119] [PACT: Proactive Asking for Continual Task Assistance in Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24350)
*Chengbo He,Sheng Li,Chenyang Ma,Bochao Zou,Li Sun,Jiansheng Chen,Junliang Xing,Yuanchun Shi,Huimin Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 为了解决长期人机协作中因缺乏用户先验知识导致的辅助效率低问题，本文提出了 PACT 框架，通过引入“主动询问”策略和澄清效用评估，使机器人能够动态决定在辅助前是否需要向用户确认，从而实现更精准、更具适应性的长期协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长期人机协作中，机器人往往面临部分观测问题，且对用户习惯缺乏初始了解。传统的被动推断方法在面对未知习惯时效率低下，为了实现持续的个性化辅助，机器人需要具备主动寻求澄清的能力。

Method: 提出了一个名为 PACT 的“询问或行动”（ask-or-act）框架。该方法结合当前观察和历史交互数据评估“上下文充分性”，通过强化学习实现其策略，并引入了“澄清效用指标（clarification utility metric）”来量化辅助准确性与询问频率之间的权衡。

Result: 实验表明，PACT 在多日实体协作场景中显著优于被动推断基线，不仅提高了辅助准确率，还通过引入的澄清效用指标展现出更好的交互平衡性。

Conclusion: 在长期的多日人机协作中，主动询问（Proactive Asking）是提高辅助准确性和协作效率的关键机制。PACT框架证明了通过评估上下文充分性来决定是否询问，优于被动的“先推理后行动”模式。

Abstract: Robotic assistants in long-term human-robot collaboration need to assist users under partial observations while leveraging cross-day interaction history. However, human traits and routines are often unknown at the beginning of collaboration, making passive infer-then-act assistance ineffective and inefficient. To address this challenge, we study a cross-day proactive asking setting for continual task assistance and propose PACT (Proactive Asking for Continual Task Assistance), an ask-or-act framework that determines whether clarification should be sought before taking action. PACT leverages current observations together with accumulated interaction history to evaluate contextual sufficiency, enabling the robot to provide more reliable assistance and progressively adapt to the user over time. We implement its primary learned instantiation using reinforcement learning and evaluate alternative instantiations under the same framework. To assess such behavior, we further introduce a clarification utility metric that quantifies the trade-off between assistance accuracy and the frequency of clarification requests. Experiments in multi-day embodied collaboration scenarios demonstrate that, compared with passive inference baselines, PACT consistently improves both assistance accuracy and clarification utility, highlighting the importance of proactive asking in continual human-robot collaboration.

</details>


### [120] [RoboHitch: Learning Visual Affordance from Disordered Keypoints for Hitch Knots Tying](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24394)
*Jiahui Zuo,Boyang Zhang,Fumin Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboHitch 是一种通过从人类演示中学习，利用多模态融合技术（无序关键点与视觉信息）实现复杂柔性物体结系绑的框架，无需显式拓扑追踪，有效解决了遮挡带来的操纵难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人打结方法过度依赖精确的拓扑状态追踪和有序关键点，在柔性物体（DLO）存在频繁弯曲、交叉和自遮挡时容易出现追踪漂移和拓扑匹配失败。

Method: 提出 RoboHitch 框架，结合动态图自编码器（处理无序 3D 关键点）和卷积自编码器（提取视觉特征），并通过双向交叉注意力机制融合多模态信息，联合预测抓取与放置倾向，实现隐式状态推理。

Result: 实物实验表明，RoboHitch 能够在存在自遮挡的复杂场景中成功执行系扣（hitch knot）任务，展现了良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: RoboHitch 成功摆脱了对显式拓扑结构和有序关键点的依赖，证明了利用无序关键点和视觉上下文信息在存在遮挡情况下执行复杂柔性物体（DLO）打结任务的可行性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation of deformable linear objects (DLOs) presents significant challenges due to complex dynamics and frequent self-occlusions. Existing robotic knot tying methods typically rely on precise topological state tracking with ordered keypoints and explicit edge connectivity. This reliance makes them prone to failures due to tracking drift and topology mismatch caused by repeated bending and crossings during knot formation.To address these limitations, we introduce RoboHitch, a novel framework that learns to perform hitch knot tying from human demonstrations using only disordered 3D keypoints and RGB images. This eliminates the need for explicit topological order, allowing for more flexible manipulation. Our method employs a dynamic Graph Autoencoder to extract geometric features from untracked keypoints, complemented by a Convolutional Autoencoder that captures essential visual context. A bidirectional cross-attention mechanism then fuses these modalities to jointly predict pick and place affordances, facilitating implicit reasoning about the rope's state and enabling knot tying under occlusion.Real-world experiments demonstrate the effectiveness and generalizability of our approach, successfully completing hitch knots in scenarios with self-occlusions.

</details>


### [121] [Smoother Action Chunking Flow Policy via Prior-Corrected Orthogonal Trust-Region Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24433)
*Kai Fang,Hailong Pei,Xuemin Chi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: POTR 是一种用于流动匹配机器人策略的先验修正正交置信域引导方法，通过改进中间权重和引入正交约束，解决了动作分块推理中的不连续及抖动问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决流程匹配机器人策略中动作分块（action-chunking）导致的边缘不连续问题，现有方法（如 RTC）存在中间时间步权重不足及产生无约束横向扰动导致轨迹不平滑的缺陷。

Method: 1. 引入了数据先验尺度 (σ_d) 以改进 RTC 指导权重，增强了中间时间步的修正能力；2. 采用了正交置信域约束，通过将修正矢量分解为平行和垂直于去噪速度的分量，并将垂直分量限制在信任区间内，从而消除不必要的横向扰动。

Result: 在 LIBERO 任务中，相比 RTC 方法，POTR 提高了成功率，并显著降低了动作分块边界的不连续性、加速度和抖动。消融实验证实了先验修正权重是提升性能的主要手段，而正交置信域有效增强了稳定性。

Conclusion: POTR 提出了一种有效的轨迹引导策略，通过引入数据先验权重和正交置信域约束，显著提升了流动匹配机器人策略在动作分块推理中的连续性和稳定性，在 LIBERO 基准测试中优于现有的 RTC 方法。

Abstract: Flow-matching robot policies commonly use action-chunking inference for efficient closed-loop control, but chunk boundaries can introduce discontinuous action transitions. Existing RTC guidance improves continuity by injecting correction signals during denoising, yet its weight schedule is weak at intermediate timesteps and its unconstrained correction direction may introduce transverse perturbations. We propose POTR, a **p**rior-corrected **o**rthogonal **t**rust-**r**egion guidance method. First, we incorporate a data-prior scale $σ_d$ into the RTC guidance weight, yielding stronger intermediate-time correction. Second, we decompose the guidance vector into components parallel and perpendicular to the denoising velocity, and constrain the perpendicular component within a trust region. On LIBERO with $π_{0.5}$, POTR improves success rate and consistently reduces chunk-boundary discontinuity, acceleration, and jerk compared with RTC. Ablations show that the prior-corrected weight provides the main correction gain, while the orthogonal trust region further improves stability.

</details>


### [122] [Polymander II: an amphibious salamander-inspired robot with contact and flow sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24465)
*Qiyuan Fu,Sudong Lee,Andrea Grillo,Jonathan Arreguit,Louis Gevers,Josie Hughes,Auke J. Ijspeert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于霍尔效应传感器的两栖机器人力感知方案，通过紧凑、易防水的传感器设计实现了陆地接触力与水下流体动力的高频同步采集，提升了机器人在两栖环境下的环境适应与反馈控制能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有两栖机器人普遍缺乏对陆地与水下复杂环境交互力的感知能力，限制了其在不同地形间的适应性与鲁棒性。

Method: 1. 设计并集成霍尔效应传感器以测量足端接触力与侧向流体动力。2. 构建双总线通信架构，实现500Hz的环境感知数据（外感受）与100Hz的关节状态数据（本体感受）同步采集。3. 利用霍尔传感器非接触式测量特性，结合密封机制克服水下防水难题。

Result: 该传感器系统能够稳定运行，提供高灵敏度的微小力测量，并成功支持机器人在两栖环境中的穿越任务。

Conclusion: 证明了基于霍尔效应传感器的外骨架/足端力感知方案在两栖环境下的有效性，通过高频数据采集实现了对机器人环境交互的感知，为两栖复杂地形的闭环运动控制提供了技术路径。

Abstract: Robots benefit from sensory information to coordinate body movement, gain robustness against perturbations, and transit between different modes to adapt to various terrains. However, few amphibious robots can sense interactions with both terrestrial and aquatic environments. In this paper, we present a solution that uses Hall-effect sensors to sense foot contact forces and lateral hydrodynamic forces on a salamander-inspired amphibious robot. With two bus lines, the robot can simultaneously acquire this exteroceptive information at more than 500 Hz and proprioceptive information, such as joint positions and loads, at 100 Hz. The Hall-effect sensors used are compact, making them suitable for embedding in multiple positions within a robot, and exhibit high sensitivity to small forces. Moreover, because the sensor can be positioned separately from the measured object, waterproofing can be implemented with relative ease. Our tests demonstrate the robot's capabilities in traversing amphibious environments and its potential in using feedback control for more complex locomotion tasks.

</details>


### [123] [Elevator-LIO: Robust LiDAR-Inertial Odometry for Multi-Floor Navigation under Elevator-Induced Non-Inertial Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24495)
*Yifan Zhang,Yudong Huang,Yuchong Zhang,Changze Li,Haoran Liu,Ming Yang,Tong Qin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Elevator-LIO 是一种新型 LiDAR-惯性里程计，通过解耦非惯性电梯运动模型和事件触发的约束更新，有效解决了机器人跨楼层定位过程中的发散和不连续问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LiDAR-惯性里程计（LIO）系统在电梯环境中由于非惯性参考系问题、垂直运动导致的位姿发散以及环境剧烈变化，往往无法实现连续且精确的跨楼层定位。

Method: 提出了一种解耦的状态估计模型，将机器人相对于电梯的运动与电梯本身的运动分开建模，并嵌入到模式依赖的迭代误差状态卡尔曼滤波器（IESKF）中。引入了基于 LiDAR 测距统计的状态检测器来管理电梯模式，通过事件触发的零速和零加速度更新抑制垂直漂移，并采用自适应体素降采样以应对环境尺度变化。

Result: 在包含 79 次电梯行程的 20 个真实场景序列中，Elevator-LIO 均保持了连续定位。在 17 个序列中，该系统的终端高度误差低于 1 cm，显著优于现有主流定位系统。在 Hilti 数据集等标准场景中也表现出极强的竞争力。

Conclusion: Elevator-LIO 成功解决了机器人进出电梯时的非惯性系定位难题，实现了跨楼层的鲁棒、连续定位，在包含复杂挑战的真实场景中表现出优于现有主流系统的性能，同时在常规场景下保持了竞争力。

Abstract: This paper presents Elevator-LIO, a LiDAR-inertial odometry framework designed to achieve continuous robot localization during elevator travel, thereby supporting cross-floor robotic tasks. To address the state-estimation problem in non-inertial frames, Elevator-LIO establishes a decoupled state-estimation model that separately models the robot motion relative to the elevator and the elevator motion itself, and embeds it into a mode-dependent iterated error-state Kalman filter framework. This framework degenerates to conventional LIO estimation in ordinary indoor environments, while enabling the propagation and constrained update of elevator-related states in elevator non-inertial environments, thereby achieving continuous and stable localization. An elevator mode manager detects elevator entry and exit events using LiDAR ranging statistics and estimated states, and introduces event-triggered zero-velocity and zero-acceleration updates when the elevator stops to suppress accumulated vertical drift. In addition, this paper adopts an adaptive voxel downsampling strategy to maintain a stable number of effective points under significant environmental scale changes. We conduct extensive experiments on 20 real-world sequences containing 79 elevator rides, including practical challenges such as large-scale spaces, long vertical travel, dynamic pedestrian interference, and mirror reflections. The results show that Elevator-LIO maintains continuous localization accuracy in all sequences, with terminal height error below 1 cm in 17 sequences. In contrast, existing representative localization systems perform poorly on these elevator sequences. Tests on the Hilti 2022/2023 datasets further show that the proposed method remains competitive in standard indoor scenarios. The project page is available at https://xiaofan4122.github.io/Elevator_LIO_Page/.

</details>


### [124] [MuGen: Multi-Skill Generative Locomotion Controller for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24592)
*Yusen Feng,Xiang Wang,Heyuan Yao,Zixi Kang,Xinyu Huo,Boyang Yu,Pengyun Qiu,Ruijie Zhao,Baoquan Chen,Libin Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MuGen 是一个针对人形机器人设计的生成式模仿学习框架，通过 VQ-VAE 和策略蒸馏技术，使机器人能够学习并高效执行多样化的人类运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人形机器人运动控制难以实现人类般的表达性动作和多技能切换；旨在解决如何利用大量异构人类动作数据，使机器人学习并泛化执行各种复杂运动的问题。

Method: 1. 使用向量量化自编码器（VQ-VAE）从异构人类表现数据集中学习运动的生成式表示；2. 结合基于模型的强化学习（model-based RL）；3. 采用教师-学生（teacher-student）学习框架及新型策略蒸馏策略（policy distillation），将复杂的生成模型转化为可部署的学生策略。

Result: 实验表明，该框架能使机器人在模拟和真实环境中精确追踪并模仿未见过的复杂人体动作，并能成功利用学得的潜空间完成其他任务。

Conclusion: MuGen 框架成功实现了人形机器人在复杂、多元人类动作下的高保真模仿学习，并证明该生成的潜空间（latent space）具有极强的任务迁移和复用能力。

Abstract: This paper presents MuGen, a data-driven framework for learning and deploying multi-skill locomotion on humanoid robots. MuGen enables a robot to perform expressive motions like humans under the guidance of example motion sequences. To achieve this, we employ vector-quantized autoencoders (VQ-VAEs) trained with model-based reinforcement learning, resulting in a generative representation of locomotion that captures key patterns of human motion from hours of heterogeneous human performance data. We employ a teacher-student learning framework and develop a new policy distillation strategy to enable a deployable student policy learning this efficient latent representation. This policy allows the robot to track and mimic unseen human motions and further enables the robot to reuse the learned latent space for other tasks. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework through a diverse set of motions and accurate execution.

</details>


### [125] [PoseRefer: Pathway-Local Parameters for Semantically Grounded Reference Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24622)
*Anna Deichler*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对现有3D语义基础基准的不足，本文提出了MM-Conv数据集，并通过一种完全解耦的后期融合架构证明了，在不解耦路径的情况下，语义基础系统的表现可能被类别表示伪影所误导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D基础数据集存在局限性（如描述是事后生成的、手势是模板化的等），无法充分模拟真实的机器人人机交互场景，且现有的融合评估方法难以区分语义基础效果与类别表示带来的偏差。

Method: 提出了MM-Conv数据集（包含自然协同语音手势、全身动捕及3D场景图），并采用了一种解耦的后期融合（decoupled late-fusion）架构，其中姿态（pose）和文本（text）路径之间不共享任何学习参数。

Result: 融合了冻结的MiniLM类别嵌入后，模型在所有参考类型上均超越了单独使用姿态或文本的基线，达到31.9%的top-1准确率；研究还证明了该架构能有效诊断模型在不同输入条件下切换策略的能力。

Conclusion: 研究强调，在评估语义基础（semantic grounding）系统时，必须使用架构解耦（architecturally decoupled）的方法，否则所谓的融合准确度可能仅仅是类别表示伪影（category-representation artifacts）的结果。

Abstract: A robot resolving ``put the cup on that one'' must fuse gesture, language, and scene geometry, yet 3D grounding benchmarks only partially capture this regime: descriptions are written post-hoc, gestures are templated, or pointing is staged for the camera. MM-Conv captures natural co-speech gesture from dyadic VR interaction alongside full-body motion capture and 3D scene graphs. We use it to evaluate pose-language fusion with a decoupled late-fusion architecture in which pose and text pathways share no learned parameters. The two choices together make category, pose, and text contributions easier to isolate through controlled ablations. Fusion with frozen MiniLM category embeddings exceeds pose alone and the best text-only pathway on every reference type, reaching 31.9% top-1. The learned scalar gate flips between opposing policies depending on whether the text pathway has category access. This is a reliability diagnostic: fusion-accuracy claims for semantic grounding systems are indistinguishable from category-representation artifacts unless pathways are architecturally decoupled.

</details>


### [126] [Sum of Costs Diffusion with Dynamic Guidance for Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24690)
*Aysu Aylin Kaplan,Özgür Erkent*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 为了提升机器人运动规划的泛化能力，本文提出了一种基于扩散模型的方法，通过动态的碰撞成本梯度引导，实现了在多样化任务中更鲁棒、更高效的轨迹生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人运动规划方法在多样化场景下缺乏泛化能力，难以有效解决复杂环境中的轨迹规划问题。

Method: 提出了一种利用扩散模型生成无碰撞轨迹的方法，核心在于通过总碰撞成本的梯度来引导去噪过程，并引入了一种动态选择梯度引导起始步骤的策略。

Result: 在Mπnets数据集的多样化测试场景中，该方法相比其他竞争方法取得了最优性能。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的动态梯度引导扩散模型在应对机器人操纵任务的运动规划时，展现出了优于现有方法的高泛化能力和鲁棒性。

Abstract: The motion planning problem for robotic manipulation can be addressed through classical or deep learning approaches. Existing methods face significant challenges in generalizing to diverse settings. In this study, we present a method with high generalization capability that generates collision-free trajectories using diffusion models where the denoising process is guided by the gradient of the total collision cost. We are also presenting a dynamic approach for choosing start step of the gradient guidance. Experimental results demonstrate that guiding the diffusion model dynamically with the sum of collision costs offers more robust performance by overcoming the generalization issues faced by competing methods. The proposed model demonstrates its effectiveness by achieving the highest performance on diverse test settings in M$π$nets\ dataset among the compared methods.

</details>


### [127] [Geometric Workspace Analysis and Transmission-Aware Dynamics of a Serial Spherical Tool for Microsurgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24760)
*Anestis Mablekos-Alexiou,Lyndon da Cruz,Christos Bergeles*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于显微手术的串联球形机器人设计框架，通过解析式工作空间法和动力学模型优化传动设计，并开源了相关分析工具，经实验验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对显微手术中对手术机器人运动学紧凑性及传动精度的高要求，解决现有设计流程中缺乏解析式几何指导和复杂传动系统力矩预测困难的问题。

Method: 1. 几何解析工作空间建模；2. 基于动力学的自锁传动机构力矩评估模型；3. 开发了涵盖摩擦识别与逆动力学分析的开源软件。

Result: 成功应用于玻璃体视网膜手术机器人原型，实验验证了模型在预测执行器性能方面的准确性，并提供了实用的工程设计指导。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一套针对显微手术机器人设计的系统性框架，通过理论分析与实验验证，证明了该方法在提升机器人设计效率和性能预测方面的有效性。

Abstract: We present a kinematic and transmission-aware design framework for a serial spherical mechanism with an additional translational degree of freedom for microsurgery. The first contribution is an analytical workspace formulation that provides geometric insight into reachable motion and enables rapid selection of rotation axis orientations without numerical optimization. The second contribution is a dynamics-informed methodology for mechanisms driven by self-locking transmissions, supporting evaluation of torque requirements for a prescribed workspace geometry. The framework is accompanied by an open-source software package for friction identification and inverse dynamics analysis. Experiments on a purpose-built robotic tool for vitreoretinal surgery validate the predictive capability of the models and demonstrate their practical utility for engineering design.

</details>


### [128] [Enhanced INS/GNSS State Estimation using GNSS-Based Acceleration Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24767)
*Gal Versano,Itzik Klein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过历史GNSS数据提取加速度观测来辅助车辆INS/GNSS导航的方案，有效解决了低动态场景下惯性传感器误差观测不足的问题，提升了定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的INS/GNSS融合算法在遭遇低动态运动时，GNSS位置更新对姿态和惯性传感器误差状态的观测能力较弱，导致定位精度下降。

Method: 提出了一种在传统INS/GNSS融合框架中引入加速度观测的新方法；利用过往的GNSS测量数据结合车辆运动模型，提取车辆加速度信息，并将其作为额外的观测项融入卡尔曼滤波中。

Result: 在两个不同的真实无人车数据集上进行测试，相比传统的仅基于GNSS位置辅助的滤波器，平均定位均方根误差（RMSE）分别降低了11.40%和20.74%。

Conclusion: 通过引入基于历史GNSS数据推导的加速度观测，能够显著增强低动态环境下INS/GNSS融合系统的状态可观测性，从而提升自主地面车辆的定位精度和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Accurate and reliable navigation is essential for autonomous ground vehicle operations. Standard INS/GNSS fusion relies on GNSS position updates, which provide limited observability of orientation and inertial sensor error states, particularly during low-dynamic motion. In this work, we propose utilizing past GNSS measurements alongside a motion model to extract meaningful vehicle acceleration information. This acceleration measurement is then integrated into the INS/GNSS filter to improve its robustness and accuracy. The proposed approach is evaluated on two real-world unmanned ground vehicle datasets collected from different mobile platforms and inertial sensor grades. Results demonstrate consistent positioning accuracy improvements relative to the standard position-aided filter, with mean position root mean square error improvements of 11.40 % and 20.74 % on the two datasets, respectively.

</details>


### [129] [MR-LiDAR: A Multi-Resolution Roadside LiDAR Benchmark for Perception Diagnostics and Deployment Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24777)
*Shunlai Cui,Peng Cao,Yuan Zhu,Yongjiang He,Jiacheng Yin,Xiao Huo,Gang Cao,Xiaobo Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文推出了MR-LiDAR基准测试集，通过实证研究揭示了激光雷达配置与路侧感知性能的量化关系，反驳了“高线数等于高性能”的认知，为低成本、高效能的传感器选型提供了决策依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路侧感知系统中，激光雷达选型直接影响性能与成本，但目前缺乏实证基准来指导科学的传感器选择与部署，导致设计盲目。

Method: 构建了MR-LiDAR多分辨率基准测试集，在相同路侧场景下同步使用16、32、80和128线激光雷达，通过对比分析研究了多项影响因素（束数、分布、距离、遮挡等），并基于两种主流检测算法进行了性能评估。

Result: 发现各参数对感知性能均有实质性影响；验证了优化束分布的80线激光雷达在感知效果上可达到128线均匀分布雷达的同等水平；提供了基于实证的选型参考指南。

Conclusion: 明确了激光雷达束数、分布、距离和遮挡对路侧感知的具体影响。提出了“束数并非越高越好”的观点，并验证了优化束分布的80线激光雷达性能可媲美或超越均匀分布的128线产品，为路侧感知系统的经济高效配置提供了实践指南。

Abstract: LiDAR model selection is a critical issue in roadside sensing systems, as it directly determines both perception capability and deployment cost. However, the lack of empirical benchmarks for comparing perception performance across different LiDAR configurations has greatly constrained scientific sensor selection and deployment planning. To address this gap, we present MR-LiDAR, a controlled multi-resolution LiDAR benchmark for roadside perception diagnostics. Using 16-, 32-, 80-, and 128-beam LiDARs in identical roadside scenarios, we collect point clouds and ground-truth annotations for diverse traffic participants, including vehicles and vulnerable road users (VRUs), across varying distances. This controlled design isolates intrinsic LiDAR specifications, particularly beam count and beam distribution, as the key variables for precise performance diagnostics. Based on MR-LiDAR, we conduct systematic empirical analyses to examine how beam count, beam distribution, target distance, object category, and vehicle occlusion affect LiDAR perception performance. The results reveal that all of these factors have substantial impacts. In particular, contrary to the common assumption that higher beam counts always yield better perception, we show that an 80-beam LiDAR with optimized beam distribution can match or even outperform a 128-beam LiDAR with uniform beam distribution. In addition, we provide a practical reference guide for LiDAR selection, including target point-count statistics and detection performance comparisons based on two widely used detection algorithms. This work offers a diagnostic benchmark and practical guidance for determining cost-effective LiDAR configurations in roadside perception applications.

</details>


### [130] [Manifold-Constrained MPPI: Real-Time Sampling-Based Control Under Hard Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24813)
*Seulchan Lee,Sanghyun Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了 MC-MPPI，一种通过 VAE 潜在空间规划与 QP 执行修正相结合的采样控制框架，实现了复杂机器人系统在满足实时要求的前提下，对硬流形约束的精确处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的 MPPI 控制方法依赖成本函数的软惩罚项，无法保证硬约束的满足，限制了其在闭链操作等受限机器人任务中的应用。

Method: 提出了一种名为 MC-MPPI 的框架，主要包含两个核心部分：1. 规划阶段：利用变分自编码器（VAE）学习约束流形的低维潜在空间表示，使 MPPI 能够生成接近可行性的候选轨迹；2. 执行阶段：结合基于线性化约束的二次规划（QP）控制器，通过单次求解修正残差，实现对硬相等约束的实时满足。

Result: 在 14 自由度闭链双臂系统上的实验表明，MC-MPPI 能够以 100 Hz 的频率稳定运行，在动态环境中可靠地保持硬约束，且跟踪精度显著优于基准方法。

Conclusion: MC-MPPI 通过将流形约束整合进采样控制框架，解决了传统 MPPI 无法处理硬约束的问题，在高自由度闭链操作任务中表现出了优异的实时性和跟踪精度，证明了该方法在复杂约束机器人控制中的有效性。

Abstract: Sampling-based model predictive control methods, such as Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI), offer derivative-free optimization and robustness in complex robotic systems. However, standard MPPI relies on cost-based soft penalties that cannot guarantee hard-constraint satisfaction, severely limiting its applicability to highly constrained tasks such as closed-chain manipulation. To address this, we propose Manifold-Constrained MPPI (MC-MPPI), a real-time sampling-based control framework that enforces manifold-based equality constraints while preserving the computational advantages of MPPI. The key idea is to decouple the constrained optimal control problem into latent-space planning and execution-level correction. At the planning stage, a Variational Autoencoder (VAE) learns a low-dimensional latent representation of the constraint manifold, enabling MPPI to efficiently generate near-feasible candidate trajectories without per-sample modification. Since this reference enables accurate linearization of the equality constraints, an execution-level Quadratic Programming (QP) controller resolves the residual manifold mismatch in a single solve rather than through iterative projection. Experiments on a 14-DoF closed-chain dual-arm system in both simulation and real-world settings demonstrate that MC-MPPI operates stably at 100 Hz, reliably navigates dynamic environments while effectively maintaining hard equality constraints, and significantly outperforms baseline methods in tracking accuracy. Supplementary videos and implementation details are available at https://rcilab.github.io/mcmppi.

</details>


### [131] [MuJoCoUni:Persistent Batched Runtime Primitives for MuJoCo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24922)
*Yufei Jia,Junzhe Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MuJoCoUni 是一个基于 C++/pybind11 的高性能 MuJoCo 分支，通过引入 BatchEnvPool 实现了高吞吐的并行物理仿真，同时完全兼容原始 MuJoCo 的核心物理语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在在线机器人学习和大规模物理评估场景中，标准 MuJoCo 在高吞吐并行执行任务时存在的性能限制，同时确保与原始 MuJoCo 物理仿真语义的完全兼容性。

Method: 开发了 MuJoCoUni 库，核心是基于 C++/pybind11 的 BatchEnvPool 执行器。它通过管理每个环境的 mjModel 副本和每个线程的 mjData 工作线程，结合内部线程池，支持批处理轨迹生成、状态化环境执行、稀疏重置、在线领域随机化以及批处理传感器/雅可比矩阵查询等功能。

Result: 构建了一个开源的 Python 包（mujoco-uni），提供了一套完整的 API 和工具集，能够高效支持高并发的物理仿真工作负载，并通过提供的基准测试验证了其性能。

Conclusion: MuJoCoUni 通过 C++/pybind11 实现的 BatchEnvPool，在保持上游 MuJoCo 物理语义、模型和约束不变的前提下，为在线机器人学习和大规模批处理评估提供了高性能、高吞吐的物理仿真支持。

Abstract: We present MuJoCoUni, a downstream MuJoCo distribution for online robot learning and batched physics evaluation. Alongside the open-loop batched trajectory generation already provided by upstream mujoco.rollout, MuJoCoUni supplies runtime primitives for stateful environment execution. The target workloads need high-throughput parallel execution while retaining upstream CPU MuJoCo semantics for models, sensors, contact, and constraints. Its core object, BatchEnvPool, is a C++/pybind11 executor that owns per-environment mjModel copies, per-thread mjData workers, and an internal thread pool. It provides final-state-only short stepping, sparse reset, reset-lifecycle domain randomization, batched sensor forward evaluation without advancing dynamics, and batched Jacobian and height-field queries. The implementation is confined to the Python binding layer; MuJoCo's solver, contact model, integrator, and core source tree retain upstream semantics. This report describes the BatchEnvPool API, implementation boundary, relationship to rollout, and the validation and benchmark scripts shipped with the open-source mujoco-uni package, which is installed with \texttt{pip install mujoco-uni}.

</details>


### [132] [Dynamic Neural Koopman Distillation for Real-Time Robot Control Using Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24924)
*Lei Zheng,Peiqi Yu,Zengqi Peng,Changliu Liu,Armin Lederer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了DNKD框架，通过因子化Koopman蒸馏技术将复杂的扩散模型去噪过程简化为单步前向推理，在保留多模态特性的同时，显著提升了机器人路径规划的推理速度，使其适配高频闭环控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型虽然在机器人路径规划中表现出色，但其迭代去噪的特性导致推理延迟较高，无法满足机器人高频闭环控制的实时性要求。

Method: 提出“动态神经Koopman蒸馏”（Dynamic Neural Koopman Distillation, DNKD）框架。核心在于引入“因子化动态Koopman层”（Factorized Dynamic Koopman layer），通过状态依赖的模态增益（state-dependent modal gains）对去噪过程进行因子化的潜在转换建模，从而将多步扩散去噪过程蒸馏为单步前向传播。

Result: 在D4RL MuJoCo运动学基准测试中，相比现有单步蒸馏方法具有显著性能优势；将推理延迟降低至毫秒级；在Kinova机械臂硬件实验中，实现了平滑且快速的闭环轨迹控制，且维持了任务成功率和精度。

Conclusion: DNKD通过引入因子化Koopman层，成功实现了扩散模型的高效单步蒸馏，在保持多模态生成能力的同时，大幅降低了推理延迟，使其能够满足机器人高频闭环实时控制的需求。

Abstract: Diffusion models excel at generating diverse and multimodal trajectories for robotic planning, yet their iterative denoising process introduces latency that is incompatible with high-frequency closed-loop control. To address this problem, we propose Dynamic Neural Koopman Distillation, a framework that distills multistep diffusion inference into a single forward pass while retaining the multimodal expressivity of the teacher model. Specifically, we introduce a Factorized Dynamic Koopman layer that models the denoising process through a factorized latent transition with state-dependent modal gains. We evaluate the proposed method on standard D4RL MuJoCo locomotion benchmarks and a physical Kinova manipulator, comparing against one-step baselines. The results show that our method significantly outperforms existing one-step distillation approaches on the reported locomotion tasks, and reduces the inference latency to the millisecond regime compared with the teacher policy. Hardware experiments further demonstrate that our method enables smooth and fast closed-loop execution while maintaining task success and comparable accuracy. A project page is available at https://fdkoopman.github.io/.

</details>


### [133] [Learning High-Frequency Continuous Action Chunks in Latent Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24931)
*Kunyun Wang,Yuhang Zheng,Yupeng Zheng,Jieru Zhao,Wenchao Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对高频机器人控制中动作不连贯的问题，提出了一种基于VAE潜在空间学习和异步块级细化处理（Reuse-then-Refine）的方法，有效提升了复杂控制任务的时空一致性与平滑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的动作分块（Action Chunking）技术在中等频率下表现良好，但在高频（如60Hz）控制任务中面临时间不连贯和空间不一致的问题，导致机器人动作生硬且难以执行复杂的接触任务。

Method: 提出在变分自编码器（VAE）的潜在空间中进行高频动作学习，并引入“Reuse-then-Refine”机制，在异步推理下通过块级细化策略增强相邻动作块的连续性。

Result: 在三个真实的接触密集型机器人任务中，该方法实现了高频且动作平滑的执行效果，大幅减少了机器人运动中的停顿和抖动。

Conclusion: 通过将高频动作学习从动作空间转移到VAE潜在空间，并结合“Reuse-then-Refine”细化策略，能够有效解决高频控制下的时空一致性问题，显著提升机器人执行复杂接触任务的平滑度。

Abstract: Modern robotic policies increasingly rely on action chunking to execute complex tasks in the physical world. While action chunking improves temporal consistency at moderate action frequencies, it becomes insufficient when the action frequency is further increased (e.g., to 60~Hz). At such high frequencies, policies often fail to generate actions that are both temporally smooth and spatially consistent. We address this challenge by shifting high-frequency action learning from the action space to a latent space with variational autoencoder (VAE). This formulation significantly improves both temporal and spatial consistency of high-frequency control. To enable smooth real-time execution, we further introduce Reuse-then-Refine, a chunk-level refine strategy that improves continuity between adjacent action chunks under asynchronous inference. As a result, robots controlled by our policy can execute complex contact-rich tasks continuously, with less pauses and jerky motions. Experiments on three real-world contact-rich robotic tasks show that our approach consistently completes tasks with smooth motions. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/tars-robotics/RTR.

</details>


### [134] [HumanEgo: Zero-Shot Robot Learning from Minutes of Human Egocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24934)
*Zhi,Wang,Botao He,Kelin Yu,Seungjae Lee,Ruohan Gao,Furong Huang,Yiannis Aloimonos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HumanEgo 是一个无需机器人数据的框架，通过将人类第一视角演示转化为通用实体交互表示并进行流匹配训练，实现了高效、高泛化能力的机器人操作技能学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决人类第一视角（Egocentric）视频中包含的丰富操作技能向机器人迁移时，因视觉外观和运动学差异（体现鸿沟）而导致的困难。

Method: 提出了一种将人体演示提升为“实体级手-物体交互表示”的方法，并通过带有密集辅助目标的流匹配（Flow Matching）策略进行训练。该方法无需额外的机器人数据，且与特定机器人硬件解耦。

Result: 仅需30分钟视频即可达到92.5%的平均任务成功率（15分钟视频可达75%），在任务表现上比同等时长的人工遥控演示高出41%，并表现出强大的跨机器人、跨环境的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: HumanEgo 框架能够通过人体视频演示实现高效、硬件无关且具备零样本迁移能力的复杂操作技能学习，在数据效率和成功率上均优于手动遥控演示。

Abstract: Human egocentric video captures rich manipulation demonstrations without any robot hardware, yet transferring these skills to robots remains challenging due to the embodiment gap between human and robot in both visual appearance and kinematics. We present HumanEgo, a framework that bridges the embodiment gap by lifting each human demonstration to an entity-level representation of hand-object interaction, and training a flow matching policy with dense auxiliary objectives that amplify supervision from every trajectory. HumanEgo is robot-data-free, hardware-agnostic, data-efficient, and zero-shot human-to-robot transferable. With only 30 minutes of human videos per task, HumanEgo achieves 92.5% average success across four real-world tasks (75% with just 15 minutes), outperforms matched-time robot teleoperation by 41%, and robustly transfers zero-shot across novel robots, cameras, and environments.

</details>


### [135] [Bridging the Gap: Enabling Soft Actor Critic for High Performance Legged Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24975)
*Gianluca Sabatini,Chenhao Li,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过改进SAC算法的核心机制（初始化、价值目标、回报估计），解决了其在大规模并行计算中难以稳定训练的问题，使其在足式机器人学习中能与PPO媲美，且具备更好的样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PPO虽是足式机器人训练的标准，但因同策略（on-policy）特性导致样本效率低，难以直接应用于真实硬件的在线微调；而离策略（off-policy）的SAC虽理论上更高效，却难以在大规模并行环境中稳定训练并达到PPO的性能。

Method: 针对SAC在大规模并行训练中的局限性，提出了三项核心优化：1. 改进策略初始化；2. 采用感知超时（timeout-aware）的价值目标；3. 引入多步回报估计（multi-step return estimation）。

Result: 通过上述改进，SAC在大规模并行仿真设置下实现了稳定训练，在多种足式机器人平台和运动任务中，其实际表现完全匹配（闭合）了PPO的性能基准。

Conclusion: 该研究成功弥合了SAC在足式机器人大规模并行训练中与PPO的性能差距，证明了经过改进的离策略算法（SAC）在机器人高效、在线学习及Sim-to-Real适应任务中的潜力和实用性。

Abstract: Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) has become the de facto standard for training legged robots, thanks to its robustness and scalability in massively parallel simulation environments like IsaacLab. However, its on-policy nature makes it inherently sample-inefficient, preventing its use for continuous adaptation and fine-tuning on real hardware. Soft Actor-Critic (SAC), by contrast, is an off-policy algorithm that can reuse past experience, making it a natural candidate for sim-to-real transfer workflows where the same algorithm can be used both in simulation and for online learning on the real robot. Despite these advantages, SAC has consistently failed to match PPO's empirical performance in massively parallel training settings. This work identifies the root causes of this gap and introduces targeted modifications, covering policy initialization, timeout-aware critic targets, and multi-step return estimation, that enable SAC to train stably at scale. Evaluated across multiple legged robot platforms and diverse locomotion tasks, our approach closes the performance gap with PPO entirely.

</details>


### [136] [Loosely Coupled Factor Graph Optimization for Pseudolite-Augmented Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24980)
*Chih-Chun Chen,Lipeng Tan,Shiyu Bai,Heike Vallery*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将伪卫星、GNSS与IMU通过因子图优化进行深度融合的定位框架，有效改善了GNSS退化环境下的定位精度与几何约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在GNSS卫星可见性受限（如遮挡）的环境中，现有利用伪卫星（PL）增强定位性能的策略在基于优化的框架中应用依然有限。

Method: 提出了一种松耦合因子图优化（FGO）框架，将GNSS/PL的最小二乘（LS）解与惯性测量单元（IMU）数据进行融合。

Result: 在实验中，相比标准LS方法，FGO实现了22.8%至41.3%的平均三维定位误差降低；同时验证了引入伪卫星能进一步提升GNSS-IMU基准系统的定位精度，但效果受几何构型影响。

Conclusion: 在GNSS退化环境下，基于因子图优化（FGO）的融合框架能有效利用伪卫星（PL）补充信号，从而显著提升定位精度；相比传统最小二乘法（LS），FGO在处理多源传感器融合时表现出更好的稳定性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: In Global Navigation Satellite System (GNSS)-degraded environments, pseudolites (PLs) provide additional signal sources to enhance positioning performance, but their integration in optimization-based frameworks remains limited. This paper presents a loosely coupled factor graph optimization (FGO) framework that fuses the GNSS/PL least-squares (LS) solutions with inertial measurement unit (IMU) data. The evaluation considers low GNSS visibility scenarios with four high-elevation GNSS satellites and up to two PL transmitters over an 80~s window. FGO achieves a 22.8\% to 41.3\% reduction in mean 3D error compared to standard LS methods. Compared to a GNSS-IMU baseline, incorporating PL transmitters further improves positioning accuracy, with performance depending on geometry.

</details>


### [137] [Learning, locomotion, and navigation of soft synthetic snakes in three-dimensional, heterogeneous environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24985)
*Xiaotian Zhang,Ali Albazroun,Tixian Wang,Songyuan Cui,Prashant G. Mehta,Mattia Gazzola*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一个基于强化学习与仿生建模的计算框架，使柔性蛇形机器人在复杂的三维非结构化地形中实现高效、稳定的导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的工程化人工柔性蛇形机器人尚无法达到生物体在复杂非结构化三维地形中表现出的卓越运动灵活性与控制能力。

Method: 采用基于生物启发的驱动与传感模型以降低控制复杂度；利用强化学习架构训练导航策略；采取“先简单环境学习运动基元，后复杂环境合成适应策略”的渐进式训练流程。

Result: 该框架成功使柔性仿生蛇在由真实世界图像重构的高保真三维环境中实现了可靠的导航。

Conclusion: 提出了一种可用于连续体机器人控制的物理真实仿真平台，为在复杂自然地形下实现鲁棒的类蛇机器人导航提供了有价值的参考和策略。

Abstract: Limbless terrestrial animals exhibit exceptional locomotor versatility and control, currently unmatched by engineered counterparts. Here, we introduce a computational framework that enables soft synthetic snakes to navigate unstructured, heterogeneous 3D terrains. Our approach is grounded in bio-inspired actuation and sensing models that reduce the control complexity inherent to high-degree-of-freedom, continuum bodies. These models are integrated into a reinforcement learning architecture to derive environment-traversing policies. Training first occurs in simplified, homogeneous terrains to learn locomotion primitives. These are then composed into adaptive strategies for complex landscapes. We demonstrate robustness by deploying a snake in high-fidelity 3D environments reconstructed from real-world imaging, achieving reliable navigation. Overall, this work provides a physically-realistic simulation platform and practical insights for the control of continuum systems in natural terrains.

</details>


### [138] [Stiffness Optimization for Concentrated Bending in Magnetically Actuated Catheters: Maintaining Steerability under Gradient Stiffness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25005)
*Jiewen Tan,Junnan Xue,Shing Shin Cheng,Shuang Song,Erli Lyu,Jiaole Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种SO-MAC磁驱动导管，通过刚度梯度设计与解耦机制，解决了导管在推进力传输与灵活转向之间的权衡难题，实现了高精度、抗屈曲的体内导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁驱动软体导管在设计上存在“推进力传输（刚度需高）”与“近端弯曲操控性（刚度需低）”之间的矛盾，低刚度虽然易于弯曲但容易在推进时发生屈曲（kinking/buckling）。

Method: 提出了基于刚度梯度架构（gradient-stiffness architecture）的SO-MAC导管，集成了解耦的转向-推进机制（decoupled steering-advancement mechanism），利用弹簧主干的弹性恢复力来抵抗摩擦引起的弯折与屈曲。

Result: 该1.5mm直径的导管实现了180°转向，弯曲半径为3mm，平均形状误差为1.39±0.56mm，转向轴心误差为0.35±0.10mm，并在支气管仿体中验证了其在复杂弯曲和分叉路径中的导航能力。

Conclusion: 该研究成功通过刚度梯度架构和解耦的转向推进机制，解决了磁驱动软体导管在推进性能与弯曲操控性之间的矛盾，实现了高精度的软体导管导航。

Abstract: Achieving both efficient pushability (propulsion transmission) and proximally concentrated bending for steerability is challenging for magnetically actuated soft catheters: higher axial/bending stiffness improves force transmission but reduces steerability, whereas lower stiffness enables large, proximally concentrated bending yet increases kinking/buckling risk under compressive push loads. To address this trade-off, we propose a stiffness-optimized multi-segment magnetically actuated catheter (SO-MAC) that integrates a decoupled steering-advancement mechanism with a gradient-stiffness architecture. The SO-MAC concentrates bending about a stable proximal pivot during advancement while the distal section passively self-straightens to transmit propulsion, aided by the optimized stiffness distribution and elastic recovery of the spring backbone against friction-induced kinking/buckling. Over $0{-}180^{\circ}$ combined steering and advancement, the pivot remained stable and the distal tip advanced near-straight toward the target direction. A 1.5 mm-diameter SO-MAC achieved up to $180^{\circ}$ steering with a 3 mm bending radius at its 10 mm tip, with an average shape error of $1.39 \pm 0.56$ mm and a steering-pivot error of $0.35 \pm 0.10$ mm. Visual feedback control in a bronchial phantom further confirmed robust navigation through highly curved, bifurcating paths.

</details>


### [139] [Performance Comparison of Classical and Neural Sampling Algorithms for Robotic Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25010)
*Hichem Cheriet,Badra Khellat Kihel,Samira Chouraqui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究表明，引入 AI 支持的采样策略可显著优化机器人导航的路径质量和平滑度，Neural Informed RRT* 被证明是效果最佳的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨将人工智能（AI）集成到基于采样的运动规划中，以提升自主导航的效率和路径质量。

Method: 在包含凸形和凹形障碍物、且具有不同障碍物密度的环境中，对 RRT*、Neural RRT* 和 Neural Informed RRT* 三种算法进行了实现与对比评估。

Result: 神经导向算法优于传统 RRT*，路径长度缩短最多 14%，轨迹平滑度提升 55-75%；其中 Neural Informed RRT* 在路径长度和平滑度方面表现最优。

Conclusion: AI 引导的采样策略能显著提高机器人和无人机导航的轨迹质量与平滑度，尽管计算时间略有增加，但其在提升复杂环境下的路径规划效率和可靠性方面具有重要价值。

Abstract: Integrating artificial intelligence (AI) into sampling-based motion planning provides new possibilities for improving autonomous navigation efficiency. In this paper, three algorithms, namely RRT*, Neural RRT*, and Neural Informed RRT*, are implemented and evaluated on environments containing convex and concave obstacles with different obstacle densities. The obtained results indicate that neural-guided planners improve path quality, producing up to 14\% shorter paths and 55--75\% smoother trajectories compared with the conventional RRT* algorithm. Among the evaluated methods, Neural Informed RRT* achieves the best overall performance in terms of path length and trajectory smoothness. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of AI-guided sampling strategies for improving reliability and trajectory efficiency in robotic and UAV navigation, despite a slight increase in computation time. Overall, the study highlights the growing importance of artificial intelligence in real-time robotic path planning applications.

</details>


### [140] [Micro-Swarm Locomotion Optimization in Dynamic Flow using Multi-Objective Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25025)
*Josef Berman,Oren Gal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种 CFD 与多目标强化学习耦合的新框架，利用梯度冲突解决技术成功实现了微型机器人集群在复杂动态流体环境中的高效导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微型机器人集群在生理相关、时变流体环境中的控制是生物医学与环境应用的一大挑战。现有的控制策略往往难以平衡进度、能效和平滑性，且未能有效处理流体环境的复杂动态。

Method: 提出了一种混合计算流体力学 (CFD) 与多目标多智能体强化学习 (MOMARL) 的框架，将高保真不可压缩纳维-斯托克斯求解器与去中心化近端策略优化 (PPO) 耦合，并引入 PCGrad 梯度手术技术以解决多目标优化中的梯度冲突。

Result: 使用 PCGrad 的策略在奖励值、能效（达到 0.63-0.65）及平滑度（达到 0.97-0.99）上均显著优于基准模型。训练中涌现了“双层流体节流”、“循环同步棘轮”和“个体化进场”三种高效行为模式。

Conclusion: 该研究证实了将物理流体动力学与多目标强化学习直接耦合的可行性。通过梯度冲突解决（PCGrad）策略，微型机器人集群能在复杂的脉动流环境中实现能量高效、平滑且精准的导航，并涌现出多种高效的流体交互行为。

Abstract: Coordinating micro-robotic swarms in physiologically realistic, time-dependent fluid environments remains an unsolved challenge for biomedical and environmental applications. We present a hybrid Computational Fluid Dynamics - Multi-Objective Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning framework that directly couples a high-fidelity incompressible Navier-Stokes solver with decentralized proximal policy optimization to learn physically consistent swarm control strategies in oscillatory flow. Sixteen magnetically actuated micro-robots navigate a pulsatile arterial waveform, simultaneously optimizing upstream progression, energy conservation, and motion smoothness, reconciled using PCGrad surgery. Without PCGrad, energy efficiency and smoothness rewards collapse to near zero within 10,000 training steps while progress exhibits persistent large-amplitude oscillations, confirming that gradient conflict resolution is a structural requirement rather than an optional refinement in this domain. The converged policy achieves a progress reward of 6.5-7.0, a sustained energy efficiency of 0.63-0.65, and near-maximum smoothness (0.97-0.99), representing improvements over brute-force baselines on the primary objective while both baselines yield negative energy efficiency throughout. Training reveals three emergent behavioral phases: a collective two-layer hydrodynamic throttling formation that suppresses peak channel velocities during forward flow, a cycle-synchronized ratchet mechanism that exploits flow reversals for upstream repositioning, and an individualized final approach as agents near the success boundary. These results establish that time-dependent fluid-agent interactions can be captured directly within multi-objective reinforcement learning loops, offering a physically grounded paradigm for micro-swarm control in biomedical navigation, environmental monitoring, and industrial microfluidics.

</details>


### [141] [ParkingWorld: End-to-End Autonomous Parking Reinforcement Learning from Corrective Experience in 3DGS Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25029)
*Zhengcheng Yu,Changze Li,Haoran Liu,Tong Qin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对传统自动泊车训练效率低和环境泛化难的问题，本文提出了CIL-SERL框架，结合3DGS仿真环境与多级分层重放缓冲区，实现了在复杂泊车场景下的高效、安全学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有自动泊车方案的局限性：模仿学习依赖大规模专家数据且泛化能力弱；传统强化学习存在训练成本极高、探索效率低及在复杂环境下难以收敛的问题。

Method: 提出修正反馈闭环（Correction-in-the-loop）的样本高效强化学习（CIL-SERL）框架：1. 使用3D高斯溅射（3DGS）构建高保真仿真环境；2. 设计多级经验回放缓冲区，分层存储标准RL轨迹、人类纠正干预、失败轨迹及撤回纠正段，实现针对性训练与高效采样。

Result: 在3DGS仿真及实车测试中，该方法显著提升了泊车成功率、操作效率和安全性，证明了其优于传统强化学习的性能。

Conclusion: CIL-SERL框架通过整合3DGS仿真环境与多级经验回放机制，显著提升了端到端自动泊车在狭窄、复杂场景下的成功率、训练效率与安全性，验证了该方案在物理平台上的实用性。

Abstract: Autonomous parking demands precise low-speed maneuvering within narrow, cluttered, and highly constrained environments, where vehicles must navigate tight spaces while avoiding static obstacles and complex geometric boundaries. Unlike imitation learning, which typically requires massive volumes of high-quality expert demonstrations to converge to a stable policy and often suffers from limited generalization to unseen scenarios, traditional reinforcement learning (RL) methods face persistent challenges including excessive training overhead, inefficient exploration, and even failure to learn viable parking strategies in challenging settings. To address these limitations, this paper presents a correction-in-the-loop sample-efficient reinforcement learning (CIL-SERL) framework for end-to-end autonomous parking, which is entirely trained in a photorealistic 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) parking simulator that enables high-fidelity digital reconstruction of real-world scenes. Inspired by error-correction notebooks used in learning practice, we design a novel multi-level replay buffer mechanism. These buffers hierarchically organize and store standard RL rollouts, human corrective interventions, failed exploration trajectories, and rollback-based correction segments in separate yet interconnected memory regions, facilitating structured sampling and targeted learning during training. The proposed framework is systematically evaluated in both the 3DGS simulation environment and a physical vehicle platform. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that our method achieves substantial improvements in parking success rate, operational efficiency, and safety performance across diverse scenarios, validating the effectiveness and practical applicability of the proposed CIL-SERL-based end-to-end autonomous parking solution.

</details>


### [142] [RAMBA: 4D Radar Mapping by Bundle Adjustment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25041)
*Jianzhu Huai,Yiwen Chen,Binliang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了 RAMBA，一种用于 4D 雷达的离线全局束调整框架，通过联合优化几何残差、IMU 因子和速度约束，显著提升了机器人雷达建图的全局一致性与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现有的 4D 雷达感知多集中于在线状态估计，缺乏针对全局建图的离线精细化方案，且需要一种能利用 4D 雷达多维特征（距离、方位、俯仰、多普勒）在复杂环境下实现鲁棒全局映射的方法。

Method: 提出了 RAMBA（Radar Bundle-Adjustment）框架，利用雷达惯性里程计的前端输出作为初始位姿，通过融合协方差加权的几何残差（扩展的 GICP）、IMU 预积分因子和雷达自运动速度约束进行联合优化；引入体素化对应关系并支持闭环约束以增强全局一致性。

Result: 在 ColoRadar 和 SNAIL Radar 数据集上的实验表明，RAMBA 显著提升了地图的一致性，并在多数情况下提高了轨迹的估计精度。

Conclusion: RAMBA 框架通过有效的多帧优化和位姿细化，证明了在 4D 雷达全局建图中实现更高一致性和精度的可行性，优于现有的雷达惯性里程计及位姿图优化方法。

Abstract: 4D radar is increasingly attractive for robotic mapping because it provides range, azimuth, elevation, and Doppler measurements while remaining robust in adverse visibility conditions. Although recent radar and radar--inertial odometry methods have achieved promising online state estimation performance, offline global map refinement for 4D radar remains underexplored. This paper presents RAMBA, a radar bundle-adjustment framework for globally consistent 4D radar mapping. Given initial poses and radar frames from a radar--inertial odometry front-end, RAMBA jointly refines radar frame states using covariance-weighted geometric residuals, IMU preintegration factors, and radar ego-velocity constraints. The geometric residuals extend pairwise GICP to a multi-frame optimization by forming voxel-based correspondences across selected frames and weighting each residual with point covariances. To improve robustness against drift and revisits, RAMBA enforces temporal consistency during correspondence formation while explicitly supporting loop-closure constraints. Experiments on the ColoRadar and SNAIL Radar datasets show that RAMBA improves map consistency and usually enhances trajectory accuracy over radar--inertial odometry and pose-graph optimization baselines.

</details>


### [143] [X-DiffVLA: X-Embodied Diffusion Action Heads for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25044)
*Boyu Li,Chaoyi Xu,Haoqi Yuan,Xinrun Xu,Börje F. Karlsson,Dongbin Zhao,Haoran Li,Zongqing Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: X-DiffVLA是一种基于扩散模型的通用跨机身机器人策略学习框架，通过Embodiement Forcing和形态树扩散技术，实现了在不同机身与末端执行器间的有效知识迁移与策略生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型过于依赖特定机身的微调，导致泛化能力受限，阻碍了不同机器人平台间相似任务的知识迁移。

Method: 1. 采用基于扩散模型的VLA架构，设计了统一的跨机身动作生成头；2. 提出“Embodiment Forcing”：通过无分类器引导（CFG）技术隐式引导动作生成，无需显式监督即可捕获机身特定的结构细节；3. 提出“Morphological Tree Diffusion”：建模不同末端执行器之间的行为相关性，提升异构演示数据的迁移利用率。

Result: 在RoboCasa和Isaac Gym基准测试中，分别提升了15.3%和12.5%的性能，达到SOTA水平，并在真实世界环境中验证了其鲁棒性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: X-DiffVLA 通过统一的跨机身动作头和创新引导机制，有效解决了机器人领域中跨机身策略学习的泛化性难题，证明了其在异构机器人平台间实现高效策略迁移和行为泛化的能力。

Abstract: Learning universal policies from cross-embodied data remains a fundamental challenge in robotics. Although Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are pre-trained on large and diverse datasets, they typically rely on embodiment-specific fine-tuning to achieve strong performance in downstream tasks. This requirement severely limits their generalization capability and restricts knowledge transfer across embodiments performing similar tasks. To overcome these limitations, we focus on cross-embodied settings with shared robotic bases and heterogeneous end-effectors, and propose X-DiffVLA, a diffusion-based VLA model featuring a unified cross-embodied action head. X-DiffVLA can leverage the generative strengths of diffusion models to capture both the diversity and latent correlations in cross-embodied datasets. Specifically, we introduce Embodiment Forcing, a classifier-free guidance technique to implicitly steer action generation toward embodiment-specific functional components, capturing fine-grained structural nuances without explicit supervision. In addition, a Morphological Tree Diffusion approach is designed to strengthen behavioral correlations across diverse end-effectors, maximizing the transferability of heterogeneous demonstrations. Experimental results across RoboCasa and Isaac Gym, covering different embodiments from grippers to dexterous hands, show that X-DiffVLA achieves state-of-the-art performance, with improvements of 15.3% and 12.5%, respectively. Real-world evaluations further validate the robustness of the proposed framework and its effectiveness in scalable cross-embodied policy learning.

</details>


### [144] [A Decentralized LiDAR-SLAM System with Certifiably Optimal Pose Graph Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25051)
*Baoshan Song,Feng Huang,Li-Ta Hsu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种集成了可认证最优PGO后端（基于RBCD算法）的去中心化LiDAR-SLAM系统，解决了传统方法在全局一致性上的不足，在鲁棒性及精度上均大幅超越现有技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的去中心化多机器人LiDAR-SLAM框架多依赖局部搜索优化或单次坐标对齐，在大规模或退化环境中易陷入局部最优且难以维持长期一致性。

Method: 提出了一种基于黎曼块坐标下降（RBCD）算法的去中心化Pose Graph Optimization (PGO) 后端，用于解决去中心化条件下的全局一致性问题。

Result: 实验结果表明，该框架显著提升了鲁棒性，轨迹均方根误差（RMSE）相较于当前最先进的DiSCo-SLAM算法最高降低了48.9%。

Conclusion: 本文提出了首个集成可认证最优PGO后端的去中心化LiDAR-SLAM系统。利用RBCD算法，该系统在无需精确初始值的情况下实现了全局一致的轨迹估计，并在鲁棒性方面显著优于现有的DiSCo-SLAM框架。

Abstract: Decentralized multi-robot LiDAR-SLAM is essential for collaborative missions but faces significant challenges in maintaining global consistency. Existing frameworks predominantly rely on local-search optimization or one-time coordinate alignment, which are prone to suboptimal convergence and long-term inconsistency, especially in large-scale or degenerate environments. To address these limitations, this paper presents the first decentralized LiDAR-SLAM system that integrates a state-of-the-art certifiably optimal Pose Graph Optimization (PGO) backend. By leveraging the Riemannian Block Coordinate Descent (RBCD) algorithm, our system ensures globally consistent trajectory estimation without requiring accurate initial guesses. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves superior robustness, improving trajectory RMSE by up to 48.9% compared to the state-of-the-art DiSCo-SLAM.

</details>


### [145] [Soft Pneumatic Actuators for Soft Robotics: A Motion-Based Review of Actuation Mechanisms and Performance Trade-offs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25109)
*Mohammed Abboodi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统性地综述了柔性气动执行器的结构设计策略，通过构建基于运动模式的分类框架，分析了设计要素对系统性能的影响，为构建高效、可靠的柔性机器人系统提供了设计指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柔性气动执行器虽应用广泛，但其性能受多种设计要素（几何、材料、约束等）综合影响。现有文献缺乏统一的分类与比较视角，导致研究者在为特定应用选型及对比跨研究成果时面临困难。

Method: 系统综述（Review）：根据四类运动模式（线性、弯曲、扭转、全向）对柔性气动执行器进行分类，并深入剖析了影响变形路径的结构特征（如编织角、折叠几何、纤维取向等）及其实际性能表现。

Result: 揭示了设计决策与执行器性能（运动输出、力生成、气动需求、耐久性等）之间的定量与质性关系，明确了比较执行器时需考虑的关键外部条件（如压力、负载、迟滞等）。

Conclusion: 通过归纳四种基础运动类型的结构设计策略及其性能权衡，本文建立了柔性气动执行器的分类框架，旨在指导研究者针对特定应用选择最优设计方案，并推动该领域向更紧凑、高效、可复现的方向发展。

Abstract: Soft pneumatic actuators are widely used in soft robotics because they can produce large motions while remaining compliant enough to interact safely with objects, environments, and the human body. However, their performance is not solely determined by pressure. Instead, the response depends on the way the actuator is built, including the shape of its chambers, the placement of reinforcements, the use of folds, material stiffness, and the constraints that guide its deformation. As the literature has expanded, it has become more difficult to determine which mechanism is most suitable for a given application and which reported results can be compared across studies. This review examines soft pneumatic actuators according to the design strategies used to generate four motion classes: linear, bending, twisting, and omnidirectional actuation. For each class, it analyzes the structural features that define the deformation path, including braid angle, fold geometry, fiber orientation, chamber arrangement, structural asymmetry, and internal constraint layers. It then discusses how the design choice affect motion output, force generation, air demand, repeatability, durability, fabrication difficulty, and robotic integration. The review further identifies key conditions that must be considered when selecting or comparing actuators, including pressure, loading condition, actuator size, pneumatic supply, and hysteresis This approach helps explain why actuators with similar motion outputs may differ substantially in design requirements, pneumatic demand, and practical suitability. It also highlights the design priorities needed for compact, efficient, repeatable, and deployable soft pneumatic systems in wearable, biomedical, and mobile robotic applications.

</details>


### [146] [InvariantCloud: A Globally Invariant, Uniquely Indexed Point Cloud Framework for Robust 6-DoF Tactile Pose Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25216)
*Pengfei Ye,Yuxiang Ma,Yi Zhou,Wei Chen,Wenzhen Dong,Molong Duan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: InvariantCloud 是一种新型 6-DoF 触觉姿态估计框架，通过利用传感器标记的全局不变性，消除了累积漂移并提升了偏航角估计精度，实现了更稳健的机器人操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习和视觉语言模型对高保真触觉感知的需求日益增长，旨在解决 6-DoF 触觉物体姿态估计中长序列累积漂移以及偏航角（Yaw）估计精度不足的问题。

Method: 提出了一种基于视觉触觉传感器表面标记点群全局不变性（Global Invariance）的 6-DoF 点云配准框架，实现了一次性（One-shot）全局姿态估计。

Result: 实验验证表明，与现有基准相比，InvariantCloud 在偏航角跟踪精度和重定位重复性方面表现更优，在长序列机器人操作任务中展现出极高的精度和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: InvariantCloud 通过利用触觉传感器表面标记的全局不变性，有效解决了长序列操作中的累积漂移和偏航角（Z轴）估计难题，在精度和鲁棒性上显著优于现有基准。

Abstract: Recent advances in imitation learning and vision-language models highlight the need for high-fidelity tactile perception, with 6-DoF tactile object pose estimation providing a crucial foundation for precise robotic manipulation. We introduce InvariantCloud, a 6-DoF pose estimation framework that leverages the global invariance of surface marker constellations on vision-based tactile sensors. In contrast to recent approaches, our one-shot globally invariant point cloud registration suppresses cumulative drift and overcomes long-standing limitations in accurately estimating yaw (Z-axis) rotation. Experimental verifications show that InvariantCloud achieves superior yaw tracking accuracy and re-localization repeatability compared to existing benchmarks, demonstrating its precision and robustness in long-sequence manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [147] [FusionCore: A 23-State Unscented Kalman Filter for IMU, Wheel Encoder, GPS, and Visual SLAM Fusion in ROS 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25239)
*Manan Kharwar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FusionCore 是一个基于 UKF 的高性能 ROS 2 传感器融合框架，通过在线偏置估计、自适应噪声处理和原生 ECEF GPS 支持，显著提升了机器人在长时导航中的稳定性和定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主流传感器融合解决方案（如 robot_localization）在长时运行和复杂动态环境下存在数值不稳定性及偏置校准不足的问题，需要更鲁棒的开源工具。

Method: 构建了一个基于 23 状态无迹卡尔曼滤波（UKF）的 ROS 2 软件包，具备在线系统性偏置估计、ECEF 原生 GPS 处理、基于马氏距离的异常值剔除以及自适应噪声协方差调整功能。

Result: 在 NCLT 数据集测试中，FusionCore 在 12 个序列中的 10 个序列内显示出更低的绝对轨迹误差（ATE），性能提升显著；相比之下，对比算法在相同数据集上出现数值发散。

Conclusion: FusionCore 在处理多传感器融合和复杂导航环境方面优于传统的 robot_localization，展现出更高的稳定性和定位精度，特别是在 GPS 拒绝或长时间运行的场景下表现优异。

Abstract: We present FusionCore, an open-source ROS 2 sensor fusion package that fuses IMU, wheel encoder odometry, GPS, and Visual SLAM pose into a single 100 Hz odometry stream using a 23-state Unscented Kalman Filter (UKF). The 23rd state is an online estimate of the wheel encoder's systematic yaw rate bias, identified through GPS heading cross-covariance and subtracted during GPS blackouts to reduce heading drift in coast mode. FusionCore also estimates gyroscope and accelerometer biases as explicit filter states, handles GPS natively in ECEF without a separate coordinate projection node, applies per-sensor Mahalanobis chi-squared outlier gating calibrated to measurement degrees of freedom, and adapts sensor noise covariance automatically from the innovation sequence. VSLAM pose fusion enables GPS-denied operation with any visual odometry or SLAM system, including automatic recovery from map reinitialization. We evaluate against robot_localization on twelve full-length sequences (55-92 min each) from the NCLT public dataset. FusionCore achieves lower Absolute Trajectory Error (ATE) on ten of twelve sequences, with improvements ranging from 1.2x to 22.2x on winning sequences. The robot_localization UKF diverges numerically on all twelve sequences. FusionCore is available at https://github.com/manankharwar/fusioncore under the Apache 2.0 license.

</details>


### [148] [GreenSeg: Ground Segmentation Algorithm for Agricultural Robots in Mediterranean Greenhouses using RGB-D Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25279)
*Fernando Cañadas-Aránega,José C. Moreno,José L. Blanco-Claraco*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Greenhouse agriculture in the Mediterranean region faces significant automation challenges due to its unique structural and environmental constraints. These environments are characterized by extremely narrow aisles, heterogeneous terrains ranging from concrete to tilled soil and severe optical interference caused by polyethylene covers, which induce specular reflections and "ghost points" in depth sensors. While autonomous navigation is essential for digitizing agricultural tasks, traditional solutions often rely on expensive 3D LiDAR systems that are economically unscalable for most facilities. To address this, this paper presents GreenSeg, a robust perception framework for autonomous navigation using RGB-D sensing. The proposed method introduces a dual-layer validation strategy: a robust global plane fitting combined with a surface curvature filter for terrain adaptability, and a seed-point-based Region Growing constraint to ensure the spatial continuity of the navigable plane. Experimental validation was conducted using the AGRICOBIOT I platform across four diurnal scenarios with varying solar elevations. The results show that GreenSeg consistently outperforms benchmark segmentation methods, achieving peak improvements of 11.58% in mean Recall and 19.24% in mIoU during critical rotational maneuvers at the end of corridors. These findings confirm that the proposed algorithm enables stable and safe autonomous navigation in unstructured, dynamic agricultural environments that are subject to budget constraints and sensitive to lighting conditions.

</details>


### [149] [Prior Policy Guided Dual-Agent Coordinated Manipulation Planning of Spacecraft-Manipulator System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25362)
*Yuhui Hu,Dong Zhou,Kaihong Ouyang,Zhongliang Yu,Jianfeng Lv,Xiangyu Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DACMP 框架利用含专家引导机制的深度强化学习，实现了空间机械臂的高精度作业与航天器底座的姿态协同控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间机器人机械臂与基座之间存在强动力学耦合，严重威胁航天器姿态稳定性及任务安全性，亟需一种平衡高精度作业与姿态稳定的控制规划方案。

Method: 提出了一种基于双智能体协同作业规划（DACMP）的框架；引入了具备时间步级专家切换引导（TESG）机制的先验策略引导深度强化学习算法。

Result: 在实验中显著优于基准 DRL 算法，并在系统约束、环境干扰及感知不确定性等多重复杂场景下展现了优异的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DACMP 框架通过协调机械臂与基座，有效解决了空间机器人作业时的动力学耦合问题，提升了系统的控制精度、任务成功率及鲁棒性。

Abstract: The strong dynamic coupling between the manipulator and the base poses a significant challenge to maintaining spacecraft attitude stability, potentially compromising mission safety. In this paper, we propose a Dual-Agent Coordinated Manipulation Planning (DACMP) framework that simultaneously achieves high-precision end-effector pose reaching for a 6-DoF space manipulator and attitude stabilization of the base spacecraft. To enhance learning efficiency, we present a prior policy-guided Deep Reinforcement Learning algorithm incorporating the Timestep-level Expert Switching Guidance (TESG) mechanism, thereby promoting global convergence and improving task success rates. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DACMP significantly outperforms baseline DRL algorithms in terms of task success rate and control precision. Furthermore, the robustness of DACMP is validated under various challenging scenarios, including system constraints, environmental disturbances, and perception uncertainties. The code and simulation configurations are available on GitHub: https://github.com/HIT-YuhuiHu/DACMP.

</details>


### [150] [FOUND-IT: Foundation-model-first Task-driven 3D Scene Graphs with Granularity on Demand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25371)
*Dominic Maggio,Nicolas Gorlo,Luca Carlone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FOUND-IT 是一种实时、任务驱动的 3D 场景图构建方法，通过动态调整场景粒度以适应演变的复杂任务，并利用几何基础模型实现高效的空间感知，在基准测试和真实部署中均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 场景图构建方法在面对非预定义、动态演变的复杂任务时灵活性不足，难以在实时性与不同粒度表示需求之间取得平衡。

Method: 提出 FOUND-IT 框架，利用现有几何基础模型（如 VGGT）并增加额外头部来重建可通行性信息；采用任务驱动机制，根据当前任务需求动态调整场景图中物体和区域的粒度，并配备智能体查询机制。

Result: 在 ASHiTA SG3D 基准测试中准确率提升了 79%；在 Jetson Thor 设备上实现实时运行；成功应用于 YouTube 的房地产室内视频，展现了环境鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FOUND-IT 实现了实时、任务驱动的 3D 场景图构建，能够根据复杂任务动态调整粒度，在环境泛化性和处理动态任务需求方面表现出色。

Abstract: We present the first approach to build hierarchical task-driven 3D scene graphs of arbitrary indoor or outdoor environments using an uncalibrated monocular camera in real-time. We leverage geometric foundation models to estimate geometric attributes of the scene graph (e.g., object bounding boxes), but we also observe that traversability information (the "places" layer of a scene graph) can be directly reconstructed by adding an extra head to existing geometric foundation models, like VGGT. Our approach is task-driven in the sense that we adjust the granularity of the objects and regions in the map depending on the task; for instance, during a manipulation task, our approach is able to resolve small knobs on a stove, while during a navigation task it can focus on large objects (e.g., the entire stove). However, in a major departure from related work, we consider the realistic case where the list of tasks is not predefined and fixed, but evolves as the robot operates. This naturally allows dealing with complex loco-manipulation tasks, where the robot can dynamically adjust its representation as the task unfolds. We dub the resulting approach FOUND-IT. FOUND-IT also includes an agentic approach to query information in the scene graph. In addition to achieving 79% higher accuracy on the ASHiTA SG3D task grounding benchmark, we demonstrate FOUND-IT runs in real-time on a ground robot using a Jetson Thor. Furthermore, to highlight the robustness of our method, we demonstrate constructing 3D scene graphs on casually captured realtor apartment tours from YouTube. Code will be made available upon publication.

</details>


### [151] [Path Following Control System of Line-of-Sight Guidance for Robotic Dolphin with Multi-Link Mechanism in Underwater Simulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25401)
*Takumi Asada,Takao Oki,Hideo Furuhashi,Kenta Tabata,Renato Miyagusuku,Koichi Ozaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对多关节仿生水下机器人（BAUV）路径跟踪系统设计难度大、缺乏统一评估标准的问题，本文提出了一套针对性的路径跟踪系统及仿真评估方法，实现了参数优化与控制策略验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
