Motivation: 多关节仿生水下机器人（BAUV）在环境探测中具有优势，但现有的路径跟踪系统在应对多关节复杂动力学模型时存在局限性，且缺乏针对该类机型的评估机制。

Method: 提出了一种专门针对多关节机制BAUV的路径跟踪系统架构，并构建了水下仿真环境进行参数优化与控制策略的系统性评估。

Result: 实现了BAUV路径跟踪系统的定制化设计，能够通过仿真环境准确获取系统参数，并完成对不同控制算法的性能验证。

Conclusion: 本研究成功为多关节仿生水下机器人（BAUV）设计了一套有效的路径跟踪系统，验证了在仿真中确定控制参数的可行性，并评估了不同控制方法在处理多关节机构时的表现。

Abstract: Biomimetic autonomous underwater vehicle (BAUV) with multi-link mechanism is widely used in aquatic life observation and environmental surveys due to its low power consumption and high maneuverability. An environmental survey requires a path following system that automatically follows specific points. However, the path following system of BAUV is limited, and its evaluation with multi-link mechanism robots has not yet been clarified. The path following system in BAUV requires prior simulation because the model differs depending on the type of biomimetics. In this study, we propose a path following system for BAUVs with a multi-link mechanism and evaluation in underwater simulation. In this result, it was possible to design a path following system suitable for BAUV, determine parameters using a simulator, and evaluate control methods.

</details>


### [152] [How to Mitigate the Distribution Shift Problem in Robotics Control: A Robust and Adaptive Approach Based on Offline to Online Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25414)
*Hyung-Suk Yoon,Seung-Woo Seo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种鲁棒的“离线到在线”自适应模仿学习框架，通过在离线阶段扩充专家数据覆盖范围，并在在线阶段动态检测偏移并进行自监督学习，有效解决了模仿学习中的分布偏移难题，并显著提升了模型的泛化与适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习中存在的分布偏移问题（Distribution Shift），即当智能体进入训练阶段未覆盖的状态时无法做出正确的决策，这是由于专家演示数据的状态-动作空间覆盖范围有限造成的。

Method: 提出了一种鲁棒的离线到在线自适应模仿学习框架：1. 离线阶段：利用判别器结合辅助演示数据，扩展策略的状态-动作覆盖范围；2. 在线阶段：引入检测分布偏移的机制，并利用自我监督学习从在线经验中进行实时微调。

Result: 在MuJoCo环境下的广泛评估表明，该方法在应对分布偏移时表现出比基准算法更强的鲁棒性和更好的在线环境适应性能。

Conclusion: 本研究提出的基于离线到在线自适应的学习框架证明了其在解决模仿学习中分布偏移问题上的有效性，通过结合离线数据增强与在线自适应学习，显著提升了策略的鲁棒性与环境适应能力。

Abstract: Distribution shift in imitation learning refers to the problem that the agent cannot plan proper actions for a state that has not been visited during the training. This problem can be largely attributed to the inherently narrow state-action coverage provided by expert demonstrations over the full environment. In this paper, we propose a robust offline to adaptive online imitation learning framework that handles the distribution shift problem in a lifelong, multi-phase scheme. In the offline learning phase, we leverage supplementary demonstrations to broaden the state-action coverage of the policy by utilizing a discriminator to effectively train the policy with supplementary demonstrations, thereby enhancing the robustness of the policy to distribution shift. In the subsequent online inference phase, our framework detects the occurrence of distribution shift and conducts self-supervised imitation learning from online experiences to adapt the policy to the online environments. Through extensive evaluations in MuJoCo environments, we demonstrate that our method exhibits better robustness to distribution shift and better adaptation performance to online environments than the baseline algorithms, which indicates superior performance of our framework against the distribution shift.

</details>


### [153] [OPAL: Omnidirectional Path-efficient Aerial 3D expLoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25423)
*Yoga Satwik Chappidi,Avideh Zakhor*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OPAL 是一种新型的无人机三维勘探框架，通过原地 360 度旋转替代复杂的全局路径规划，在保证计算高效的同时，显著减小了机器人的总行驶距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自主勘探算法往往依赖复杂的全局路径规划，计算开销大。为了提升计算效率并优化机器人在未知环境下的行驶距离，研究人员希望设计一种计算更简单、路径更高效的勘探方案。

Method: 提出了一种名为 OPAL 的自主勘探框架。其核心机制是在环境的“歧义分支点”进行 360 度原地偏航旋转（Yaw rotation）以获取信息；在旋转完成后，采用三种不同的策略（免模型、基于大型语言模型 LLM 或视觉语言模型 VLM）来选择下一个探索前沿；同时支持通过调整“周边搜索半径”来权衡行驶距离与勘探时长。

Result: 仿真和实机实验证明，虽然 OPAL 因原地旋转增加了探索的总时间，但其计算更简便，且在“覆盖率/距离”指标上表现更优。实机测试显示，OPAL 的行驶距离较对比算法 FALCON 最多可减少 25%。

Conclusion: OPAL 通过在关键点进行 360 度旋转替代复杂的全局路径规划，证明了在牺牲一定任务总耗时的前提下，能够显著降低机器人的行驶距离，且具有更好的计算效率和灵活性。此外，调整前沿搜索半径可平衡行驶距离与勘探时间。

Abstract: Autonomous exploration is critical for robot mapping unknown environments. Desirable characteristics of exploration algorithms include compute efficiency and small traversed distance during the exploration process. Motivated by these, we present Omnidirectional Path-efficient Aerial 3D expLoration (OPAL), an exploration framework centered on deliberate 360-degree yaw rotation at ambiguous branch points rather than compute-heavy global tour planning. We devise multiple variants of OPAL to determine the frontier-selection strategy once the yaw pan is completed. One variant is model-free, while others use large language models (LLMs) or vision-language models (VLMs). We characterize the performance of these variants while varying the vicinity search radius to include frontiers in the selection process. Through simulations we find that although the time-consuming in-place yaw rotation increases total exploration time relative to more computationally complex baselines such as EDEN and FALCON, OPAL is computationally simpler and achieves shorter travel distances and higher coverage-versus-distance area under the curve. We also show that adjusting the frontier-selection search radius enables a tradeoff between travel distance and total exploration time. We verify our results on a Modal AI drone in two indoor environments by comparing OPAL against FALCON, and find that the traveled distance for a variant of OPAL to be as much as 25% lower than FALCON.

</details>


### [154] [EXPO-FT: Sample-Efficient Reinforcement Learning Finetuning for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25477)
*Perry Dong,Kuo-Han Hung,Tian Gao,Dorsa Sadigh,Chelsea Finn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EXPO-FT 是一个通过强化学习微调预训练 VLA 模型的高效系统，能在极短的在线训练时间内实现复杂机器人操作任务的完美成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的预训练 VLA 模型在机器人操作中虽然具有泛化能力，但可靠性不足，无法满足真实世界部署的要求。现有的微调方法要么难以利用预训练先验，要么样本效率低下，导致无法在复杂任务中达到实用的成功率。

Method: 提出了一种名为 EXPO-FT 的系统，旨在对预训练的视觉-语言-动作 (VLA) 策略进行稳定且样本高效的强化学习 (RL) 微调。该方法充分利用了预训练先验知识，并针对实际部署需求进行了优化，提升了学习效率和策略成功率。

Result: EXPO-FT 在多项高难度操作任务（如绕灯串、击打台球、插花等）中表现优异，在平均 19.1 分钟的在线机器人数据下实现了 100% 的成功率 (30/30)，显著优于从零开始的 RL 和现有的 VLA 微调方法。

Conclusion: EXPO-FT 证明了通过强化学习对预训练 VLA 模型进行微调，可以实现极高的任务成功率和样本效率，为解决机器人领域真实世界部署的可靠性问题提供了一种有效方案。

Abstract: The ability to efficiently and reliably learn new tasks has been a foundational challenge in robotics. Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong generalization across diverse manipulation tasks, yet pretrained policies consistently fall short of the reliability required for real-world deployment. Reinforcement learning (RL) fine-tuning offers a promising path to bridge this gap, but existing approaches either train from scratch without fully leveraging pretrained priors, or fine-tune VLAs without achieving the sample efficiency and success rates that practical deployment demands. We present EXPO-FT, a system for stable, sample-efficient RL finetuning of pretrained VLA policies that closes this gap. Our system solves a suite of challenging manipulation tasks, including routing string lights and inserting the plug to light it up, striking a pool ball into a pocket, and inserting a flower into a wine bottle, each requiring combinations of high precision, dynamic actions, and robustness to varied initial states. Our system achieves perfect task performance (30/30 successes) across all evaluated tasks within an average of 19.1 minutes of online robot data, outperforming both prior RL-from-scratch and VLA finetuning approaches. We release an open-source codebase with the aim of facilitating broader adoption of RL finetuning of VLA models in robotics.

</details>


### [155] [RepSAM: Bridging Foundation Models to Robotic Vision via Representation-Guided Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25495)
*Wenhui Chu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对大模型在机器人场景下性能下降的问题，RepSAM 通过分析 Transformer 层级表征偏移，引入 CKA 引导的自适应秩分配和多模态融合技术，实现了轻量级、高性能的下游任务适配，大幅降低了计算开销并提升了环境交互的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基础模型（如 SAM）在非结构化机器人环境下的零样本感知能力不足，研究发现这是由 Transformer 各层间的非均匀表征偏移所导致的。

Method: 提出了一种名为 RepSAM 的 PEFT 框架，核心包含：1. 基于 CKA（中心核对齐）的层级化表示引导秩分配策略，针对不同层级的领域差异进行自适应调整；2. 集成多模态融合模块，提升对透明和复杂场景的感知能力。

Result: 在六个基准测试和操作任务中，RepSAM 仅用 4.0M 可训练参数（相比全微调减少 158 倍）达到了全微调 97.9% 的效果（89.0% mIoU）；相比 LoRA 的 RGB 基线，机器人操作成功率提升了 12.0%；训练时间仅为 4 小时（减少 96 倍）。

Conclusion: RepSAM 通过 CKA 引导的参数高效微调策略，有效解决了基础模型在非结构化机器人场景中的领域偏移问题，在保持高性能的同时，极大降低了训练的计算成本和参数规模，是机器人视觉任务的一种高效实用方案。

Abstract: Robotic perception in unstructured environments remains challenging despite the zero-shot capabilities of foundation models such as SAM. This work attributes performance degradation to non-uniform representation shifts across transformer layers: shallow layers exhibit substantial domain gaps (CKA < 0.5), whereas deep layers transfer effectively (CKA > 0.7). Based on this observation, we propose RepSAM, a representation-guided parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) framework for adapting foundation models to robotic vision. RepSAM employs a theoretically grounded CKA-guided rank allocation strategy combined with a multi-modal fusion module for robust handling of challenging robotic scenarios, including transparent objects and cluttered scenes. Experimental evaluation across six benchmarks and robotic manipulation tasks demonstrates that RepSAM achieves 97.9% of full fine-tuning performance (89.0% vs. 90.9% mIoU) while reducing trainable parameters by 158x (from 632M to 4.0M). RepSAM outperforms DoRA by 7.9% mIoU with just 4 hours of training on a single A100 GPU (a 96x reduction from full fine-tuning, which takes 384 GPU-hours). These improvements are statistically significant (p < 0.01) and translate to a 12.0% absolute improvement in robotic manipulation success rates over the LoRA (RGB) baseline.

</details>


### [156] [Action-Prior Denoising for Smooth Real-Time Chunking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25537)
*Dongyang Liu,Zhaowen Zheng,Yu Sun,Longxu Zhang,Yixuan Liu,Hao Wan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Soft RTC 是一种改进的训练时实时分块（RTC）策略，通过软约束而非二值掩码来模拟动作执行延迟，在几乎不增加推理成本的情况下，显著提升了控制策略的平滑性和执行准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的训练时 RTC（Real-time chunking）方法使用二值前缀掩码，无法精细化模拟异步执行中“早期重叠动作固定、后期重叠动作需平滑过渡”的特性，导致策略在应对推理延迟时不够平稳。

Method: 提出 Soft RTC，将二值前缀掩码扩展为基于动作先验去噪的软约束。训练阶段通过部分去噪状态构建重叠 token，推理阶段利用轻量级逐 token 混合规则（blending rule）将先前的动作 chunk 注入当前决策，实现对异步执行的更好模拟。

Result: 在 Kinetix 基准测试中，Soft RTC 在总体解决率上与硬 RTC 相当，但在动作抖动（jerk）和差异（delta）指标上分别降低了 9.6% 和 9.1%；在机器人真实实验中表现出最低的动作差分度量。

Conclusion: Soft RTC 通过引入基于动作先验去噪的机制，有效解决了传统 RTC 在异步执行建模上的不足，在保持极低推理开销的同时，显著提升了动作的平滑度及对早期动作规划的依从性。

Abstract: Real-time chunking (RTC) lets chunked action policies operate under inference delay by conditioning a newly generated action chunk on actions already committed by the previous chunk. Training-time RTC simulates this delay during learning and avoids expensive guidance at deployment, but its binary prefix mask treats all non-prefix tokens as fully unconstrained. This under-models asynchronous execution: early overlap actions are fixed, while later overlap actions remain editable but should still stay close to the previous plan. We propose Soft RTC, a training-time RTC generalization based on action-prior denoising. Soft RTC constructs corrupted overlap tokens from partially denoised states instead of pure noise and injects the aligned previous chunk as the same prior during inference through a lightweight token-wise blending rule. On the 12 released large Kinetix levels, a short soft window nearly matches hard training-time RTC in overall solve rate (0.809 vs. 0.815), while a medium window reduces high-delay action delta and jerk by 9.1% and 9.6% relative to hard RTC. Both variants keep near-naive runtime, unlike inference-time RTC baselines. A small preliminary real-robot sorting study provides additional evidence that training-time RTC can improve completion and that Soft RTC gives the lowest commanded-action finite-difference metrics among the tested policies.

</details>


### [157] [Safety-Critical Whole-Body Control for Humanoid Robots via Input-to-State Safe Control Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25546)
*Kwanwoo Lee,Sanghyuk Park,Gyeongjae Park,Myeong-Ju Kim,Jaeheung Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于输入状态安全控制障碍函数（ISSf-CBF）的分层类人机器人全身体控框架，通过层级过滤机制，在存在未知干扰的情况下实现了稳健的安全约束满足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的类人机器人安全控制方法在面对模型不确定性、轨迹跟踪误差和外部干扰时，运动学安全保障能力会下降，导致在复杂环境下操作不够稳健。

Method: 提出了一种分层全身体控（WBC）架构：1. KinWBC生成优先任务的运动参考；2. ISSf-CBF安全滤波器对参考指令进行修正以满足干扰下的运动学约束；3. DynWBC实现对修正后指令的跟踪，并确保动力学可行性和接触稳定性。

Result: 在仿真及真实机器人实验中验证了该框架。结果表明，该方法在位姿控制、远程操作和单腿平衡等任务中，即使存在模型偏差，也能有效提升安全阈值并实时执行多项安全约束。

Conclusion: 通过将ISSf-CBF集成到分层全身体控架构中，该方法能够在存在模型不确定性和外部干扰的情况下，为类人机器人提供稳健且实时可执行的运动学与动力学安全保障。

Abstract: Safety-critical control is essential for humanoid robots operating in complex human-centered environments, where physical safety constraints such as joint limits, self-collision avoidance, obstacle avoidance, and workspace boundaries must be satisfied during real-robot operation. However, existing approaches remain limited because kinematic safety guarantees can be degraded in the presence of unknown disturbances, such as model uncertainties, trajectory-tracking errors, and external perturbations. This paper presents a hierarchical safety-critical whole-body control framework for humanoid robots based on input-to-state safe control barrier functions (ISSf-CBFs). The proposed architecture integrates a kinematic-level whole-body controller (KinWBC), an ISSf-CBF safety filter, and a dynamic-level whole-body controller (DynWBC). KinWBC generates nominal joint-motion references from prioritized tasks; the ISSf-CBF filter minimally modifies these references to satisfy kinematic safety constraints under bounded disturbances; and DynWBC tracks the filtered references while enforcing full-body dynamic feasibility and contact stability. Safety constraints are imposed on a whole-body kinematic model, and the ISSf-CBF parameters are conservatively tuned so that the resulting kinematic safety guarantees can be transferred to full-order humanoid dynamics under unknown disturbances. Simulation and real-robot experiments demonstrate that the proposed framework improves safety margins under model mismatch and reliably enforces multiple safety constraints in real time during locomotion, teleoperation, and single-leg balancing with hand control. Project website: https://kwlee365.github.io/SafeWBC-Website/

</details>


### [158] [TapSampling: Inference-Time Sampling with a Task-Progress-Understanding Verifier for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25547)
*Sizhe Zhao,Shengping Zhang,Shuo Yang,Weiyu Zhao,Shuigen Wang,Xiangyang Ji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 TapSampling，一个策略无关的推理时插拔框架，通过结合潜在空间采样与语义动作验证，有效提升了现有具身控制模型的任务执行性能，突破了单次推理的瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注缩放模型规模和训练数据，忽视了推理阶段的优化潜力；现有的单次推理（single-shot）模式限制了非确定性生成模型（如扩散/自回归模型）在具身控制中的性能上限。

Method: 1. Action-VAE：将策略生成的动作映射至低维潜在空间，通过多次采样与解码生成多个候选动作；2. 动作验证器：利用机器人数据序列结构训练语义验证器，对候选动作进行任务进度预测以筛选最优动作。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人环境中，该方法在不需要对策略进行进一步微调的情况下，显著提升了多种通用策略（generalist policies）的表现。

Conclusion: TapSampling 通过在推理阶段引入采样与验证机制，无需微调策略即可显著提升具身智能模型的性能，为现有推理范式提供了一种高效的替代性扩展路径。

Abstract: Existing embodied control research demonstrates remarkable performance improvements by scaling training data and model size. We instead explore inference-time strategy as an alternative axis. Non-deterministic generative models, such as diffusion and autoregressive models, have been widely adopted in the field of embodied control. However, the single-shot inference paradigm limits their performance. In this paper, we propose \textbf{TapSampling}, a plug-and-play framework for inference-time sampling. First, we introduce an Action-VAE that represents actions in a low-dimensional latent space by mapping policy-generated initial actions into a compressed posterior distribution, from which any number of latent samples can be drawn and decoded into candidate actions that approximate the true action distribution. Second, we formulate action verification as task-progress outcome prediction, using the intrinsic sequential structure of robotic datasets to train a semantically grounded verifier for interpretable action selection. Furthermore, TapSampling is a policy-agnostic framework. Extensive experiments in both simulated and real-world environments demonstrate that our method substantially improves multiple generalist policies without further policy finetuning. Code and models are available at the project page.

</details>


### [159] [Acting on the Unseen: Communication-Free Collaborative Filtering for Decentralized Multi-Robot Task Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25584)
*Alexander Apartsin,Yigal Meshulam,Yehudit Aperstein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对多机器人任务分配的零知识（ZK-MRTA）极端场景，提出了一种基于在线协同过滤（SwarmCF）的去中心化方法，无需通信即可通过观测队友表现自适应学习任务分配策略，实现了样本效率和任务性能的大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人任务分配（MRTA）传统上高度依赖通信、先验模型或协调者，而现实场景中往往缺乏此类基础设施。研究旨在探索在“零知识（ZK-MRTA）”极端条件下——即无先验知识、无通信、仅能通过噪声观测队友部分表现——如何实现高效的多机器人协作。

Method: 提出了一种名为SwarmCF（基于广播的在线低秩协同过滤）的算法。该算法利用观测到的队友任务结果这一公共流，通过在线学习任务-机器人的隐藏低秩结构，实现对未知任务的高效分配与适应，无需显式通信或协调。

Result: 理论证明了SwarmCF在样本复杂度和累积奖励上优于结构无关学习者（达到Θ(d)而非Θ(n)的复杂度）。实验结果显示，该方法在感知任务中表现出极强的掩码鲁棒性，能够恢复约80%的理想上限性能，并体现出良好的正向协同规模效应。

Conclusion: 研究证明了在零知识（无先验知识、无通信）环境下，通过SwarmCF算法实现多机器人任务分配是可行的。相比无结构的独立学习者，SwarmCF在样本复杂度和累积奖励方面具有阶跃式的显著优势，且能够在大规模任务场景下恢复接近集中式通信系统的性能。

Abstract: Multi-robot task allocation usually assumes some combination of communication, known task models, or a coordinator. We study the opposite extreme, a regime common in practice but overlooked in theory, which we name Zero-Knowledge MRTA (ZK-MRTA): a robot team with no prior knowledge (no task models, not even the latent rank), no communication (no messages, no parameter sharing, no coordinator), and only a partial and privately-noisy view of a public stream of teammates' outcomes. A hidden low-rank structure governs which robot suits which task, and there are far more tasks than rounds, so most (robot, task) pairs are never attempted. Yet each robot can act well on tasks it never attempted, and onboard new tasks, by running online low-rank collaborative filtering over the broadcast (SwarmCF). The advantage over any structure-free learner is categorical, not a constant factor: a structure-free learner is provably at the prior-mean error floor on unseen pairs. We prove a matching per-robot sample complexity (Θ(d) versus Θ(n), in the rank d and the task count n), an anytime (cumulative-reward) separation under task scarcity, and a deterministic condition under which decentralized recovery from the masked broadcast is exact (validated empirically). Experiments quantify the value of the broadcast, a positive scaling law (per-robot unseen-pair skill rises with team size), and the strongest masking-robustness and anytime profile among low-rank methods, recovering most (about 80% on earned skill) of a centralized full-communication ceiling, and holding under capacity-1 contention and in a robotics-grounded sensing instance.

</details>


### [160] [Compliant Non-Prehensile Pushing Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25672)
*Francesco Cufino,Mario Selvaggio,Fabio Amadio,Fabio Ruggiero*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模型预测控制（MPC）和能量池滤波器的控制策略，实现了机械臂在非抓取式推动任务中的安全被动顺应性与精确轨迹追踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人类共享环境中，机器人进行非抓取式推动操作时，既需要保证操作的精确性，又必须具备被动顺应性（passive compliance）以确保人机交互的安全性。现有模型难以在实现目标运动的同时有效抑制因追踪误差导致的推动力异常增大。

Method: 1. 扩展了现有的推动模型，使其适配阻抗控制机器人；2. 基于该模型开发了模型预测控制（MPC）框架，用于联合优化机械臂的位置/速度设定点，以实现期望的推动力和接触点自适应；3. 引入“能量池（Energy Tank）”被动性滤波器，通过调制机械臂速度设定点来防止能量失控积累。

Result: 通过仿真和两套不同机器人系统的实物实验验证，证明了该方法在人机交互中的被动顺应性、轨迹追踪性能以及对物体物理参数变化的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过引入能量池（Energy Tank）被动性滤波器，该方法成功解决了阻抗控制机械臂在非抓取式推动任务中可能出现的力过大问题，实现了安全性、合规性（compliance）与执行性能的平衡。

Abstract: In this paper, we address the challenge of performing non-prehensile pushing operations with a compliant robotic manipulation system. To ensure safe operations in human-populated environments, robots must comply with external physical interactions and exhibit passive behavior. To achieve this, we extend a state-of-the-art pushing model to integrate it with impedance-controlled robots. We develop a model predictive control framework built upon this model that enables compliant pushing through optimal modulation of the robot's position/velocity set-point, jointly realizing the required pushing force and contact point adaptation to obtain desired object motion. However, external interactions may induce tracking errors, causing a consequent potentially indefinite increase of the pushing force. To prevent this, we integrate an energy tank passivity filter that further modulates the robot velocity set-point to guarantee passivity and avoid uncontrolled energy buildup. The proposed method has been rigorously tested in simulation and validated through experiments on two different robotic systems, demonstrating passive compliance during human-robot interactions and assessing trajectory tracking performance and robustness to variations in the object's physical parameters.

</details>


### [161] [HumanFlow -- Diffusion-Driven MAV Navigation Among Humans via Tightly-Coupled Motion Tracking, Forecasting, and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25685)
*Simon Schaefer,Joshua Näf,Stefan Leutenegger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HumanFlow 是一个统一的人体运动追踪与预测模型，利用潜在扩散模型结合 3D 场景上下文，解决了重度遮挡下的运动估计难题，并成功将其集成至机器人 MPC 导航策略中，提升了社交导航的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理重度遮挡或部分可见环境时，难以预测出真实且符合 3D 场景约束的人体运动，从而限制了机器人在现实场景中的安全性与效率。

Method: 1. 提出 HumanFlow：一个基于 3D 场景上下文条件的潜在扩散模型（Latent Diffusion Model），用于统一人体运动追踪与预测。
2. 引入流匹配（Flow-matching）机制：将人体运动的潜在表征与模型预测控制（MPC）策略紧密耦合。
3. 验证场景：通过模拟真实人类轨迹，在 MAV（微型无人机）社交导航任务中验证了算法的鲁棒性。

Result: 1. 在人体运动追踪精度上超越了当前最先进（SOTA）的方法。
2. 模型具有更高的计算效率。
3. 在部分可观测环境下，基于该架构的导航策略能够实现更优的避障效果，保持导航的连贯性与安全性。

Conclusion: HumanFlow 有效提升了在复杂遮挡环境下的 3D 人体运动估计准确性。通过将潜在空间与 MPC 策略耦合，该模型能够实现更具社交意识的机器人自主导航，并展现出优于现有方法的避障与轨迹预测能力。

Abstract: Robust and accurate perception of humans in their 3D scene context is essential for integrating robots into everyday environments. Existing approaches, however, often fail to predict plausible and accurate human motion estimates that are consistent with the surrounding scene, especially in the presence of heavy occlusions or partial visibility. This can limit both safety and efficiency for robotic operations. We introduce HumanFlow, a latent diffusion model that unifies human motion tracking and forecasting, conditioned on the 3D scene context. We show that our human motion model produces smooth and accurate predictions under challenging conditions, including heavy occlusions, and outperforms state-of-the-art methods in tracking accuracy while being significantly more efficient. Furthermore, we show how HumanFlow's latent space can be tightly coupled with control by conditioning a flow-matching-based, approximate MPC policy on these representations. We validate our policy in simulation with real human trajectories for MAV social navigation, demonstrating superior navigation performance and remaining collision-free, even under partial observability of the human.

</details>


### [162] [Implicit Null-space Manifold Generation for Redundant Robotic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25770)
*Taiki Ishigaki,Teresa Vidal-Calleja,Ko Ayusawa,Eiichi Yoshida*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种利用隐式标量场表征冗余机器人解流形的新方法，克服了传统方法无法保留解空间全局几何结构的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于雅可比矩阵的局部方法虽然能计算出解或轨迹，但无法保留解空间本身的全局几何结构信息。

Method: 提出了一种表示中心论（representation-centric）的方法：通过基于雅可比矩阵引导的采样策略，在构型空间中构建一个隐式标量场；该标量场的零水平集即为解流形，并能自然诱导出表示解空间距离场的几何模型。

Result: 在三自由度平面机器人和七自由度Franka机械臂上的实验验证了该方法能有效捕捉解空间的局部与全局结构，并能实现任务族解空间的一致性建模。

Conclusion: 通过隐式标量场表征冗余机器人任务空间的几何结构是有效的，不仅能够精确建模单一任务的解流形，还能推广到具有连续变化的任务族。

Abstract: Robotic systems with redundant degrees of freedom can achieve the same task outcome using multiple configurations, resulting in solution sets that form manifolds in the configuration space. Existing approaches typically exploit such redundancy locally through Jacobian-based techniques to compute individual solutions or trajectories. While effective for solution computation, these methods do not retain a representation of the geometry of the solution set itself. In this work, we adopt a representation-centric approach to estimate the geometric structure of the solution space. We consider solution manifolds induced by general task-defining maps and construct an implicit scalar field over the configuration space, whose zero-level set corresponds to the solution manifold. To this end, we generate samples in the neighborhood of the solution manifold using a Jacobian-guided exploration strategy, which efficiently captures its local and global structure. The resulting implicit representation is defined over the configuration space and naturally induces a continuous, distance field that encodes proximity to the solution manifold. Experiments on a planar three-link robot and a seven-degree-of-freedom Franka manipulator demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed representation. Furthermore, the framework enables consistent modeling of solution spaces across families of tasks with continuous variation.

</details>


### [163] [ParkourFormer: Integrating Predictive Supervision and Sequence Modeling into Parkour Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25782)
*Yanheng Mai,Wenhao Xu,Zirui Huang,Yifei Fu,Shengwei Dong,Xinjue Wang,Kailun Huang,Yanzhe Xie,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ParkourFormer 引入基于 Transformer 的未来状态预测机制，通过联合推理历史运动与预测未来动态，大幅提升了人形机器人在复杂环境下的跑酷和穿越能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的全身运动策略多为响应式（Reactive），缺乏对未来身体动态的显式建模，导致在处理楼梯、缝隙及障碍物等需要前瞻性规划的复杂地形时表现不足。

Method: 提出 ParkourFormer 框架，将人形运动视为“未来条件化的决策问题”。该模型基于 Transformer 架构，通过交叉注意力（Cross-attention）机制聚合历史传感器轨迹，并引入轻量级预测头生成短期未来本体感受状态；将预测的未来态与时间特征融合，实现对历史感知与未来动态的联合推理。

Result: 在多地形基准测试中，ParkourFormer 实现了 93.85% 的平均成功率，在模拟环境和真实人形机器人上均表现出色，相比 MLP、MoE-MLP 和普通 Transformer 基准模型，性能提升最高达 42.73%。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模未来状态，ParkourFormer 显著提升了人形机器人在复杂崎岖地形下进行敏捷全身运动的鲁棒性和泛化能力，证明了未来状态预测对动态决策的重要性。

Abstract: Humanoid parkour requires locomotion policies to coordinate whole-body dynamics across rapidly changing terrains such as stairs, gaps, slopes, and obstacles. Existing reinforcement learning policies are largely reactive, mapping observations directly to actions without explicitly modeling future body states. Such modeling becomes critical in agile locomotion tasks where successful motion execution depends strongly on anticipating upcoming contact transitions and body dynamics.We present ParkourFormer, a Transformer-based sequence modeling framework that reformulates humanoid locomotion as a future-conditioned decision-making problem. The current robot state queries historical sensorimotor trajectories through cross-attention, while a lightweight prediction head forecasts short-horizon future proprioceptive states. The predicted future states, trained with supervised signals, are fused with temporal features to generate actions, enabling the policy to jointly reason over motion history and anticipated future dynamics. We evaluate ParkourFormer on a diverse multi-terrain humanoid parkour benchmark including stairs, gaps, slopes, rough terrain, and obstacle traversal. Experiments in simulation and on a real humanoid robot show that ParkourFormer achieves a 93.85% average traversal success rate on highly challenging terrains, with improvements of up to 42.73% over strong MLP, MoE-based MLP, and vanilla Transformer baselines, while maintaining a single unified policy across all terrain types. These results demonstrate that explicit future-state modeling significantly improves robustness and generalization for agile whole-body locomotion.

</details>


### [164] [HoLoArm: Deformable Arms for Collision-Tolerant Quadrotor Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25790)
*Quang Ngoc Pham,Jonas Eschmann,Yang Zhou,Alejandro Ojeda Olarte,Giuseppe Loianno,Van Anh Ho*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HoLoArm 是一款仿生柔性四旋翼无人机，通过受蜻蜓翅膀启发的柔性结构与强化学习控制，实现了卓越的抗碰撞性能、快速姿态恢复及高负载下的飞行稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着无人机在人类中心环境（human-centric applications）的应用增加，亟需研发能够承受碰撞并快速恢复的柔性设计，以降低对环境及人类的安全风险。

Method: 1. 结构设计：受到蜻蜓翅膀节点结构（nodus）的启发，设计了具有全方位柔性形变能力的四旋翼无人机手臂。2. 控制策略：集成强化学习（RL）算法，以优化碰撞后的姿态恢复速度及悬停稳定性。

Result: 1. 物理性能：支持全方位（含轴向）被动形变，碰撞恢复时间仅需 0.3-0.6 秒。2. 鲁棒性：最高可在 7.6 m/s 的撞击速度下存活，并能挂载 540 g 有效载荷维持稳定飞行。

Conclusion: HoLoArm 成功结合了仿生柔性结构与强化学习控制，实现了高抗碰撞能力与飞行稳定性的平衡，为未来开发具备高敏捷性与安全性的全柔性无人机奠定了基础。

Abstract: The increasing use of drones in human-centric applications highlights the need for designs that can survive collisions and recover rapidly, minimizing risks to both humans and the environment. We present HoLoArm, a quadrotor with compliant arms inspired by the nodus structure of dragonfly wings. This design provides natural flexibility and resilience while preserving flight stability, which is further reinforced by the integration of a Reinforcement Learning (RL) control policy that enhances both recovery and hovering performance. Experimental results demonstrate that HoLoArm can passively deform in any direction, including axial one, and recover within 0.3-0.6 s depending on the direction and level of the impact. The drone can survive collisions at speeds up to 7.6 m/s and carry a 540 g payload while maintaining stable flight. This work contributes to the morphological design of soft aerial robots with high agility and reliable safety, enabling operation in cluttered and human shared environments, and lays the groundwork for future fully soft drones that integrate compliant structures with intelligent control.

</details>


### [165] [Extending Embodied Question Answering from Perception to Decision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25813)
*Xicheng Gong,Qiwei Li,Peiran Xu,Yadong Mu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为 EQA-Decision 的大规模具身问答数据集及 RoboDecision 基准，通过整合感知、推理与决策四个维度，弥补了现有具身智能评估体系的碎片化缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的具身问答（EQA）数据集碎片化严重，缺乏统一的大规模框架来综合评估复杂的具身推理技能（如空间理解与程序性推理）。

Method: 构建了包含超过四百万个问答对的大规模具身问答数据集（EQA-Decision），涵盖静态场景构建、空间理解、任务动态推理和即时决策四个维度；并提出了相应的基准模型 RoboDecision，用于统一评估感知、推理与决策。

Result: EQA-Decision 有效地对现有的 VLM 模型进行了基准测试，实验表明该框架能够显著促进模型在空间和交互推理方面的能力提升。

Conclusion: EQA-Decision 为具身智能研究提供了一个涵盖四个核心维度的大规模基准，有助于提升视觉语言模型（VLM）在空间感知与交互推理方面的能力。

Abstract: Embodied Question Answering (EQA) connects perception, reasoning, and interaction within embodied environments. However, existing datasets and benchmarks remain fragmented, each focusing on a limited subset of reasoning skills such as spatial understanding or procedural reasoning, without offering a unified large-scale framework for comprehensive evaluation. We present EQA-Decision, a large-scale embodied QA dataset that systematically covers four complementary dimensions of embodied reasoning: static scene construction, spatial understanding, task dynamics reasoning, and instant decision. The dataset contains over four million question-answer pairs with hierarchical annotations across diverse embodied scenarios. In addition, we develop RoboDecision, a strong baseline model aligned with the EQA-Decision Benchmark, providing a unified framework that jointly evaluates perception, reasoning, and action-level decision-making in embodied environments. Results demonstrate that EQA-Decision effectively benchmarks and enhances VLM capabilities in spatial and interaction reasoning, providing a solid foundation for advancing embodied intelligence research.

</details>


### [166] [OASIS: Observation-Action Space Alignment via SE(3) Trajectory Prediction for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25829)
*Xinzhe Chen,Sihua Ren,Liqi Huang,Haowen Sun,Mingyang Li,Xingyu Chen,Zeyang Liu,Xuguang Lan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OASIS 是一种新型机器人策略，它通过引入 SE(3) 端到端轨迹预测器，将视觉中间表征与动作空间的刚体几何属性对齐，从而显著提升了机器人操作的表现与泛化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 和 WAM 模型虽利用了辅助空间特征或视觉预测，但其中间表征仍局限于观测空间，缺乏与动作空间（刚体几何）的内在联系，导致动作解码器难以有效学习几何映射，限制了操控精度和泛化能力。

Method: 提出 OASIS 模型：1. 采用 3D 感知特征编码器（融合视觉-语言与度量深度特征）；2. 引入 SE(3) 端到端轨迹预测器，将视觉表征引导至刚体几何空间；3. 动作解码器基于带位姿监督的隐状态生成符合刚体运动规律的动作块。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界实验中，OASIS 在任务成功率和分布外（OOD）泛化能力方面均优于现有的 VLA 和 WAM 基准模型。

Conclusion: OASIS 框架通过将视觉表征与动作空间的刚体几何特性显式对齐（SE(3) 轨迹预测），有效提升了机器人操控任务的性能和泛化能力。

Abstract: Recent vision-language-action (VLA) models and world action models (WAMs) advance robotic manipulation by enriching intermediate representations with auxiliary spatial features or future visual-state prediction. However, these representations largely remain within the observation space and do not share the rigid-body geometry of the action space, forcing the action decoder to implicitly recover this geometry. We propose OASIS, a visuomotor policy that aligns the intermediate representation with the action space via $SE(3)$ end-effector trajectory prediction. OASIS couples a 3D-aware feature encoder that fuses vision-language and metric-depth features with an $SE(3)$ trajectory predictor that produces a camera-frame end-effector trajectory. Conditioned on the predictor's pose-supervised hidden states, the action decoder generates action chunks consistent with rigid-body motion. Across simulation and real-world experiments, OASIS outperforms VLA and WAM baselines in success rate and out-of-distribution generalization. Our project page is available at https://npuhandsome.github.io/OASIS_web.

</details>


### [167] [When Search Becomes Memory: Turning Robot Design Trials into Transferable Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25832)
*Yunfei Wang,Xiaohao Xu,Yang Li,Xiaonan Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Auto-Robotist 是一种具备自进化能力的 LLM 智能体，它将机器人演化过程中的设计经验结构化为可查询的“技能库”，显著提升了设计搜索效率及跨任务的知识迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型（LLMs）驱动的演化机器人设计循环通常是“无记忆”的，仿真实验的结果仅用于塑造下一代种群，而未能作为可复用的设计知识被有效沉淀和积累。

Method: 提出了 Auto-Robotist 系统，包含两个核心组件：1. 技能库（Skill Library）：存储结构原型、基于证据的规则（正/负向）及对应的设计案例。2. 闭环优化流程：在进化搜索中通过检索技能来指导LLM编辑（引导更新），并结合遗传算法（GA）进行探索，最后通过“添加、诊断、合并”机制更新技能库。

Result: 在涵盖运动、遍历和物体交互的七项 EvoGym 任务中，Auto-Robotist 在 5x5 的冷启动搜索中表现优异，并成功将学习到的技能迁移至更复杂的 10x10 设计空间，在所有任务中均优于传统的遗传算法（GA）。

Conclusion: 本文提出的自进化LLM智能体（Auto-Robotist）证明了将昂贵的物理仿真评估转化为可复用、可审计的设计原则的可行性，有效解决了进化机器人设计中“无记忆”迭代的问题。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as proposal generators for evolutionary robot design, yet most loops remain memoryless: simulator results shape the next population but are not preserved as reusable design knowledge. We present Auto-Robotist, a self-evolving LLM agent that distills morphology-search traces into an explicit natural-language skill library. Each skill stores a structural archetype, evidence-grounded positive and negative rules, and the evaluated designs that support them, making design memory inspectable rather than implicit in a population. During search, the agent retrieves skills to condition LLM edits of elite bodies while retaining a Genetic Algorithm (GA) mutation path for exploration; after evaluation, it updates the library through Add, Diagnose, and Merge. Across seven EvoGym tasks spanning locomotion, traversal, and object interaction, Auto-Robotist improves cold-start 5x5 search and transfers learned skills to 10x10 design spaces, where reference-conditioned transfer outperforms GA on every task. These results suggest that LLM agents can convert expensive physical evaluations into reusable, auditable design principles. Our code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [168] [AnyScene: Towards Highly Controllable Driving Scene Generation at Anywhere and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26113)
*Haiming Zhang,Junfei Zhou,Feng Jiang,Jingzhong Li,Zhenglong Guo,Penglin Dai,Jifeng Dai,Yan Xie,Benjin Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AnyScene 是一个统一的以占用网格为中心的驾驶场景生成框架，通过空间-时间扩散 Transformer 实现对 BEV 布局的精准控制，并利用几何引导模型合成具备高度时序一致性与视角灵活性的大规模驾驶视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于占用网格的驾驶场景生成方法存在条件控制浅显、依赖特定参考帧以及无法兼容灵活多相机配置等局限，导致难以满足自动驾驶长尾安全场景的高质量仿真需求。

Method: 1. 提出空间-时间占用扩散 Transformer (Spatial-Temporal Occupancy Diffusion Transformer)：以自回归方式联合编码鸟瞰图(BEV)与占用特征，实现对任意BEV布局的精准控制；2. 设计几何引导视角扩展模块 (Geometry-Grounded View Expansion)：以占用网格作为标准空间表示，实现无参考帧的、支持灵活相机配置的时序一致多视角视频合成。

Result: 实验表明，AnyScene 在占用网格生成和多视角视频合成任务中均达到行业领先水平 (SOTA)，具有极强的泛化能力（支持未见过的自定义布局），并可有效赋能稀疏视角3D重建等下游任务。

Conclusion: AnyScene 通过以占用网格为中心的统一生成框架，有效解决了自动驾驶仿真中长尾场景生成、细粒度可控性以及跨视角一致性等核心难题，确立了该领域的新基准。

Abstract: Generating high-fidelity and controllable synthetic data is critical for advancing end-to-end autonomous driving, particularly for addressing the long tail of rare safety-critical scenarios. Existing occupancy-guided methods typically rely on shallow conditioning mechanisms and reference-frame-dependent video synthesis, which limits fine-grained controllability from arbitrary BEV layouts and restricts their applicability for scalable simulation. In this paper, we propose AnyScene, a unified occupancy-centric framework for driving scene generation. AnyScene generates semantic occupancy sequences from BEV layouts through a Spatial-Temporal Occupancy Diffusion Transformer that jointly tokenizes BEV and occupancy features in an autoregressive manner. This design enables precise controllability from cross-dataset and user-defined BEV inputs while naturally supporting long-horizon generation. Building upon the generated occupancy, a Geometry-Grounded View Expansion module treats occupancy as the canonical spatial representation and synthesizes temporally consistent multi-view driving videos in a reference-free and autoregressive fashion, supporting flexible camera configurations at inference time. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AnyScene achieves state-of-the-art performance in both occupancy and video generation. It exhibits strong generalization to unseen and customized layouts, and provides measurable benefits for downstream tasks such as sparse-view 3D reconstruction.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [169] [Decompose, Optimize, and Reconstruct: Very Large Constant Multiplication at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23998)
*Théo Cantaloube,Nicolai Fiege,Anastasia Volkova,Christine Solnon*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 针对大规模常量乘法（VLCM）优化问题，本文提出了一种结合约束求解（CP/SAT）的新型框架。通过允许模式重叠和实现全局优化的重构过程，该方法在处理高精度（数千位）常量时，显著超越了传统启发式算法的性能水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多常量乘法（MCM）优化方法仅适用于中等精度（约12位），而现实中的超大常量乘法（VLCM）往往依靠性能次优的启发式算法，缺乏处理高位长（数十到数千位）任务的高效精确方法。

Method: 提出了一种改进的分解与重构流程：1. 利用约束规划（CP）和SAT求解器改进模式选择（允许重叠以减少MCM子问题的规模）；2. 对重构步骤执行全局优化而非启发式搜索；3. 集成最新的最优MCM数学模型；4. 提出了一种针对VLCM的全局最优解法并探讨其规模限制。

Result: 在合成测试及实际信号处理和密码学基准测试中，该方法展现了卓越的扩展性，能够有效处理高达数千位的常量，且在资源开销上显著优于现有主流方法。

Conclusion: 该研究通过引入允许模式重叠的机制以及对重构步骤进行全局优化，成功克服了现有VLCM启发式方法的局限性，提出了一种能够扩展到极高精度并优于现有基准的VLCM解决框架。

Abstract: Efficient arithmetic circuit design for resourceconstrained hardware involves challenging combinatorial optimization problems, among which Multiple Constant Multiplication (MCM) is a prominent example. MCM aims at implementing multiplications by fixed integer constants using bit-shifts and additions/subtractions but optimal methods are typically limited to moderately-sized constants, e.g. 12 bits. For practical applications targeting larger precision, Very large Constant Multiplication (VLCM) is solved instead. Existing approaches typically address VLCM through a heuristic flow that decomposes large constants into patterns, applies MCM optimization techniques on moderately-sized targets, and reconstructs the final result. This paper proposes multiple improvements to this flow: new declarative optimization models for the pattern selection and for the reconstruction, as well as applying recent optimal MCM models. The cornerstones of the obtained improvements are (i) allowing the patterns to overlap, minimising the number of unique target constants for the MCM step and (ii) performing the reconstruction step optimally, instead of heuristically. In addition, we propose a globally-optimal VLCM approach and characterize its limits. We employ a mix of constraint programming and SAT to solve each step. Experimental results on synthetic and real-life signal processing and cryptographic benchmarks, with coefficient word lengths ranging from tens to thousands of bits, demonstrate that the proposed approach scales to very large precisions and consistently outperforms existing baselines.

</details>


### [170] [CMAX-CAMEL: A Coarse-to-Fine Adaptive, Memory-Efficient, and Low-Power Edge Processor for Contrast Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24017)
*Kyeongpil Min,Jongin Choi,Kyeongwon Lee,Woojoo Lee*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: CMAX-CAMEL 是一种针对 CMAX 运动估计的软硬件协同设计，通过自适应执行策略和内存高效架构，实现了边缘设备上的实时、高性能与节能计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的对比度最大化（CMAX）方法在边缘端面临计算需求高、内存访问频繁的问题，限制了其在实时、低功耗场景下的部署。

Method: 提出了一种粗细粒度自适应执行策略（根据事件分布动态调整计算层级），并设计了一种以存储为中心的架构（包括分库并行存储器组织和子采样耦合累加结构），以优化数据流并减少内存访问。

Result: 在 Virtex FPGA 上测试显示，该设计将估计精度提升了 19%，处理延迟降低了 53.3%，存储访问次数减少了 42%，整体系统能耗降低了 52.2%。

Conclusion: CMAX-CAMEL通过软硬件协同设计，有效降低了基于事件的运动估计的功耗与延迟，特别适用于边缘计算设备的实时应用。

Abstract: Contrast maximization (CMAX) is a direct geometric framework for event-based motion estimation, but its iterative warp-and-accumulate pipeline incurs input-dependent computation and frequent memory accesses, challenging real-time, low-power edge deployment. We present CMAX-CAMEL, a coarse-to-fine adaptive, memory-efficient, low-power edge processor for CMAX. CMAX-CAMEL combines a runtime-adaptive execution strategy with a memory-centric processor architecture. It adjusts coarse-to-fine execution according to the observed event distribution, prioritizing stages likely to improve estimation accuracy while suppressing low-value iterations and unnecessary stage transitions. Architecturally, a banked parallel memory organization sustains real-time throughput while reducing latency, and a subsampling-coupled accumulation structure lowers memory-access activity along the warp-and-accumulate dataflow. On a Virtex FPGA prototype operating at 200 MHz, CMAX-CAMEL improves estimation accuracy by up to 19% over fixed coarse-to-fine schedules, reduces processing latency by 53.3%, lowers effective memory accesses by 42%, and cuts total system energy by 52.2%, including adaptation overheads. These results show that CMAX-CAMEL is an HW-SW co-design that co-optimizes execution policy and data movement for real-time, low-power event-based motion estimation at the edge.

</details>


### [171] [Adaptive KV Cache Reuse for Fast Long-Context LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24022)
*Fei li,Song Liu,Yan Liu,Jinhua Cui,Shiqiang Nie,Jinyu Wang,Weiguo Wu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: CacheTune 是一种针对长上下文 LLM 推理的 KV Cache 复用系统，通过频域分析识别关键语义并结合硬件感知 I/O 优化，在打破传统前缀匹配限制的同时，实现了低延迟、高吞吐的推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文 LLM 推理中预填充阶段的 TTFT 延迟是主要瓶颈；传统 KV Cache 复用仅限于前缀匹配，非前缀场景下直接复用会破坏注意力关系导致质量下降，且异构存储下的 I/O 开销进一步加剧了延迟。

Method: 1. 语义恢复：利用频域分析离线识别关键 KV 对，在线仅重计算关键 tokens 以保持语义一致性；2. 系统优化：采用稀疏 KV 传输、多流异步重叠、延迟位置编码恢复及硬件自适应重计算比例调整，协同优化计算与 I/O。

Result: 在主流 LLM 和任务上实现了 3.72x-4.86x 的 TTFT 加速和 3.93x-6.21x 的吞吐量提升，即使在 I/O 受限的存储环境下仍能保持超过 2.3x 的加速。

Conclusion: CacheTune 通过频域分析与硬件感知的协同优化，有效解决了长上下文 LLM 推理中的非前缀 KV Cache 复用难题，在保证生成质量的前提下显著降低了 TTFT 延迟并提升了吞吐量。

Abstract: In long-context Large Language Model (LLM) inference, the Time-To-First-Token (TTFT) latency incurred by the prefill stage has become the foremost bottleneck limiting interactive performance and deployment cost. KV Cache reuse offers a direct path to reduce redundant prefill, yet traditional prefix caching applies only to strict-prefix scenarios; directly reusing KV Cache in non-prefix settings breaks the cross-chunk global attention relationships and causes significant degradation in generation quality. When reusable KV Cache is offloaded to GPU-external cache pools, I/O overheads across heterogeneous hardware tiers further emerge as a new TTFT bottleneck. Efficient non-prefix KV Cache reuse therefore requires both semantic-consistency recovery and compute-I/O co-optimization.
  This paper presents CacheTune, a frequency-guided and hardware-aware KV Cache reuse system for long-context LLM serving. CacheTune first identifies, offline, the KV pairs most critical to cross-attention recovery through frequency-domain analysis, and then selectively recomputes only these semantic-critical tokens online while reusing the remaining KVs. To turn this semantic selection into end-to-end latency reduction, CacheTune further combines sparse KV transfer, multi-stream asynchronous overlap, deferred positional-encoding recovery, and hardware-aware adaptive recomputation-ratio tuning to balance computation and data movement across heterogeneous cache pools. Evaluations on mainstream LLMs and long-context tasks show that CacheTune achieves 3.72x-4.86x TTFT speedup and 3.93x-6.21x higher throughput while maintaining generation quality close to full recompute. Even when caches are offloaded to I/O-bound SSD/HDD storage, CacheTune sustains 2.34x-2.36x TTFT speedup through adaptive recomputation.

</details>


### [172] [A Per-Access Upper Bound for Shared-Resource Interference in Direct-Mapped Multicore Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24026)
*Felipe T. Pedroni*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在高度受限的确定性多核架构下，通过解析分析直接计算任务间最大执行时间干扰（WCET分析）的数学方法，为高安全性领域的强制认证提供了理论边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多核处理器由于复杂的资源共享机制导致的最坏执行时间（WCET）预测困难问题，以满足高安全性、高确定性系统的认证需求。

Method: 针对多核处理器设定了严格的体系结构不变量（直接映射L2缓存、禁用MSHR、单体内存、确定性任务映射、悲观裁决策略），通过解析法（analytical derivation）计算并证明了任务间干扰的上限。

Result: 证明了针对目标任务T的单次临界访问所遭受的干扰上限为(N-1)Lmem，并指出该极限可通过特定同步的竞争负载达到。

Conclusion: 在严格的体系结构约束下，证明了多核处理器的任务间最大干扰是可以精确量化的，为支持航空电子软件的DO-178C/CAST-32A认证提供了可追踪的方法论。

Abstract: We present a formal bounding analysis for maximum credible interference in multicore processors under strict architectural invariants: direct-mapped L2 cache (1-way associativity), disabled Miss Status Handling Registers (MSHRs), single-bank main memory, deterministic pinned tasks with fixed physical memory mapping, and a pessimistic L2/memory arbitration policy. We prove that, under these invariants, the per-critical-access stall imposed on a target task T is bounded above by (N-1)Lmem, and that this bound is attained by a synchronized adversarial workload of N-1 congruent-different-tag memory requests issued in phase with T's critical accesses. The argument is per-access and direct, requiring no informal multiplicative interference function. The derivation is purely analytical and discussed in the context of DO-178C/CAST-32A certification objectives for airborne software. Limitations and conditions for applicability are explicitly stated. This work provides a traceable method for separating multicore interference from Worst-Case Execution Time (WCET) budgets under fixed architectural constraints.

</details>


### [173] [EVA: Accelerating LLM Decoding via an Efficient Vector Quantization Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24144)
*Bowen Duan,Cong Guo,Chiyue Wei,Haoxuan Shan,Yuzhe Fu,Xinhua Chen,Yifan Xu,Ziyue Zhang,Changchun Zhou,Hai Li,Yiran Chen*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: EVA是一种新型高效矢量量化架构，通过将LLM解码中的GEMV计算转换为GEMM计算并消除存储冲突，显著提升了LLM推理的性能和能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型（LLM）解码阶段受到内存带宽限制，传统的权重向量量化（VQ）虽然减小了模型大小，但存在GEMV计算单元利用率低、码本查找时内存冲突频繁等导致性能低下的问题。

Method: 1. 计算范式转换：跳过权重的显式重构，直接执行输入向量与权重码本（codebook）的点积，将原本稀疏/低效的GEMV计算转变为高效的GEMM计算；2. 存储优化：设计结构化的中间输出缓存查找机制，彻底消除了硬件内存库冲突；3. 软硬件协同设计：开发了专用于LLM解码但兼容预填充阶段的专用架构。

Result: 在LLM解码任务中，相比当前最先进的VQ架构，最高可实现11.17倍的加速和7.17倍的能效提升，且能够保持原有的算术精度。

Conclusion: EVA通过重构计算范式（将GEMV转换为GEMM）和优化存储访问机制，在保持量化精度的前提下，实现了对现有最先进查找表式VQ架构在性能和能效上的显著超越。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved impressive performance across diverse domains but remain inefficient during the autoregressive decoding phase. Unlike the prefill stage, which employs compute-bound GEMM operations, decoding executes a sequence of small GEMV-like computations that are memory-bound and underutilize modern accelerators. Weight-only vector quantization (VQ) has emerged as an effective compression technique that clusters model weights into a shared codebook and replaces the original weight matrix with low-precision indices, enabling 2-bit-level weight compression. While this approach substantially reduces model size and memory bandwidth, it still suffers from two critical inefficiencies: the low utilization of GEMV computation and frequent memory conflicts during codebook lookups.
  This paper presents EVA, an efficient vector-quantization-based architecture that addresses both computational and memory bottlenecks in LLM decoding. EVA builds on a simple yet effective insight that combines input-codebook computation with conflict-free memory access. Instead of reconstructing quantized weights from indices, EVA directly performs dot products between input vectors and the weight codebook, transforming LLM decoding from GEMV to GEMM computation. It then performs structured lookups from an intermediate output buffer, eliminating memory bank conflicts. We further design a hardware-software co-optimized architecture specialized for LLM decoding while remaining compatible with conventional prefill execution. Evaluations show that EVA achieves up to 11.17$\times$ speedup and 7.17$\times$ higher energy efficiency compared with the SOTA lookup-based architecture, while preserving arithmetic precision after vector quantization. Our code is available at https://github.com/dbw6/Eva.git.

</details>


### [174] [MX-SAFE: Versatile Inference- and Training-Proof Microscaling Format with On-the-Fly Exponent and Mantissa Bit Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24391)
*Dahoon Park,Jahyun Koo,Sangwoo Hwang,Jaeha Kung*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了 MX-SAFE (MXSF) 量化格式及配套的训练-推理加速器，通过自适应模式切换和分块硬件设计，在提高模型训练与推理精度的同时，显著降低了深度学习任务的能源消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着深度学习需求增加，MX 格式（微缩放格式）作为一种硬件友好的动态量化方案成为降低成本的关键。针对现有 MXFP 格式在训练与推理精度及效率上的局限性，需要一种改进方案以优化性能和功耗。

Method: 1. 提出 MX-SAFE (MXSF) 格式：通过自适应切换宽尾数模式 (FP8 E2M5) 和亚正规浮点模式 (FP5 E3M2) 以支持训练与推理。
2. 设计基于分块（Tile-based）的硬件架构：通过优化分块设计减少训练过程中的重新量化负担，提升硬件效率。

Result: 相比 MXFP8 E2M5 和 MXFP8 E4M3，MXSF 在推理/全训练任务中平均准确率分别提升了 0.05%/11.1% 和 3.55%/3.57%；此外，在训练-推理加速器上实现了与 BF16 基准相当的精度，同时总能耗降低了 24.9%。

Conclusion: MX-SAFE (MXSF) 格式通过自适应模式和分块设计，在保持与 BF16 基准相当的准确率的同时，显著提升了硬件效率并降低了能源消耗，为深度学习的训练与推理提供了一种高效的量化方案。

Abstract: As the demand for deep learning grows, cost reduction through quantization has become essential for both training and inference. In 2022, the Open Compute Project (OCP) consortium standardized narrow precision formats for deep learning, called the microscaling (MX) format. The MX format is a hardware-friendly dynamic quantization scheme that effectively reduces the data size by sharing an 8-bit exponent across multiple operands. The MX format can be categorized into two types with their own strengths: (i) MXINT which focuses on a high precision consisting only of mantissa bits and (ii) MXFP which focuses on a wider dynamic range by allowing local exponent bits. In this work, we present a versatile MXFP format, called MX-SAFE (MXSF in short), that adaptively uses two modes, i.e., a wider mantissa mode (FP8 E2M5) and a subnormal FP mode (FP5 E3M2), to support both training and direct-cast inference. Furthermore, we propose a tile-based block design to increase hardware efficiency by reducing the burden of re-quantization process during the training with the MXSF format. Owing to the use of the proposed MXSF format, 0.05%/11.1% and 3.55%/3.57% improvements in accuracy, on average, for inference/full-training compared to MXFP8 E2M5 and MXFP8 E4M3 are observed, respectively. Moreover, we present a training-inference accelerator that supports the MXSF format and it achieves similar accuracy to the BF16 baseline while using 24.9% less total energy consumption.

</details>


### [175] [Provisioning to Runtime Optimization of a +100 MW AI Cluster](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24461)
*Ehsan K. Ardestani,Leonardo Piga,Jovan Stojkovic,Pavan Balaji,Mustafa Ozdal,Mikel Jimenez Fernandez,Mihaela Dimovska,Luka Tadic,Hao Shen,Devika Vishwanath,Richa Mishra,Melaku Mihret,Valentin Andrei,Mauricio Cespedes,Julien Prigent,James Monahan,Tyler Graf,Bin Li,Charles Marquez,Shobhit Kanaujia,Kaushik Veeraraghavan,Chunqiang Tang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文是首篇阐述超大规模AI数据中心端到端电力管理的论文，通过150MW集群的实测数据，详细分析了从初期规划到运行时动态调优的全流程，旨在缓解AI发展过程中日益严峻的电力瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI数据中心的电力供应已成为比加速器供应更严重的制约因素，但目前缺乏关于超大规模数据中心端到端电力管理的公开资料与实践洞察。

Method: 分析并描述了超大规模AI数据中心的端到端电力管理流程，包括：前瞻性电力规划（在硬件发布前6-12个月）、部署后功率调优，以及针对动态负载的运行时功率管理；并利用拥有83K颗GB200 GPU的150MW数据中心进行了实证测量。

Result: 提供了基于150MW数据中心（83K GB200 GPUs）的海量实测电力数据，归纳了从规划到运行的各阶段管理经验，为行业提供了参考基准。

Conclusion: 电力供应已成为制约通用人工智能（AGI）发展的核心瓶颈，通过全生命周期的精细化电力管理，能够有效提升超大规模数据中心的运行效率与可靠性。

Abstract: The electric power supply for AI data centers is now the most significant bottleneck in the race toward Artificial General Intelligence, surpassing even the constraint of AI accelerator availability. To our knowledge, this paper is the first to describe the end-to-end power management process for a hyper-scale AI datacenter; from early power planning to accommodate next-generation accelerators 6--12 months before their general availability, to tuning power settings after large scale deployment, and finally to dynamic, runtime power management for evolving workloads. We present detailed power measurements for a 150 MW datacenter hosting a cluster of 83K GB200 GPUs. We share insights from building this state-of-the-art AI cluster. We hope this work encourages practitioners across the industry to share their own experiences as well.

</details>


### [176] [XL-HD: Extended Learning in Hyperdimensional Computing via Deterministic Projections for In-Memory Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24788)
*Sabrina Hassan Moon,Abu Kaisar Mohammad Masum,Sercan Aygun,Dayane Reis*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: XL-HD 是一种创新的可学习 HDC 框架，通过确定性投影和实数域原型优化，实现了适用于高性能、低功耗存内计算的边缘 AI 部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 HDC 依赖伪随机向量和启发式更新，需要高维度且难以在资源受限的边缘硬件上高效部署，因此亟需一种更适合存内计算（IMC）且具备更高学习能力的确定性 HDC 方案。

Method: 提出了一种名为 XL-HD 的确定性、投影式、可学习 HDC 框架，该方法利用 Sobol 序列进行输入投影，并在实数空间优化类原型，最终进行二值化以匹配 ReRAM 等存内计算硬件架构。

Result: 在 MNIST、UCIHAR 和 ISOLET 等数据集上达到竞争性准确率，且在硬件实现上展现出优异性能：面积仅为 0.395 mm²，单次推理功耗仅为 0.40 μJ。

Conclusion: XL-HD 通过将确定性投影与可学习的原型优化相结合，成功构建了一种高效、紧凑且适合存内计算（IMC）部署的 HDC 架构，证明了在保持高精度的同时实现极低功耗与面积的可行性。

Abstract: Hyperdimensional computing (HDC) is a promising approach for energy-efficient edge machine learning (ML), where low latency, low power, and tight memory budgets are essential. However, traditional HDC relies on symbolic binding and pseudo-random high-dimensional vectors, which require large dimensionality and heuristic updates to reach competitive accuracy, limiting deployment on edge hardware. We introduce XL-HD, a deterministic, projection-based, fully learnable HDC framework tailored for in-memory acceleration within edge computing systems. The method uses a fixed Sobol sequence to project binary inputs, extending learning beyond conventional HDC. During training, class prototypes are optimized in real-valued space and later binarized, enabling an entirely binary dot-product inference pipeline ideal for IMC hardware such as ReRAM crossbars. XL-HD achieves competitive accuracy on MNIST, UCIHAR, and ISOLET while maintaining a compact IMC-based inference engine with $0.395 \ \text{mm}^2$ area and only $0.40 \ μ\text{J}$ per single-cycle inference.

</details>


### [177] [Architectural Limits of Cloud TPUs in Finite-Field Cryptography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25367)
*Hung Dang,Xuan Phu Dang,Tue Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文实证研究表明，TPU作为为AI优化的脉动阵列，因缺乏宽整数算术逻辑单元且空间调度效率低下，在有限域密码学运算中相比GPU存在数千倍的性能劣势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在量化云端TPU与GPU在执行有限域密码学任务时的成本效率差距，并探究导致TPU在这些非AI原生工作负载上性能表现极差的根本架构原因。

Method: 开发了评估工具“codename”，将低度多项式映射为矩阵形式的数论变换（NTT）；通过调度器将异构多项式堆叠为致密2D矩阵以优化K维度占用；利用结构评估与HLO验证器屏蔽编译器优化，对比TPU（v4/v5p）与NVIDIA A100 GPU（cuZK）在有限域密码学计算中的性能表现。

Result: TPU（v5p/v4）相比A100 GPU存在4,693倍至6,908倍的巨大效率差距；即使通过矩阵堆叠实现了极高的K维度占用率，但在M维度上存在严重的利用率不足，并受限于Montgomery归约带来的VPU停顿，无法发挥出计算潜能。

Conclusion: AI优化设计的脉动阵列（TPU）在处理有限域算术（如密码学中的模乘计算）时，由于缺乏宽整数ALU和在执行Montgomery归约时的空间利用率低下，导致其在密码学性能上远劣于GPU。结论明确指出，TPU架构在处理此类精确、高吞吐的域算术方面存在结构性缺陷。

Abstract: We empirically characterise the cost-efficiency deficit between cloud Tensor Processing Units and GPUs for finite-field cryptography. Against A100 GPU baselines (cuZK), we measure a $[5{,}558\times, 6{,}908\times]$ deficit across v5p and v4 architectures under an FP32-mantissa staging discipline, and a $\sim$$4{,}693\times$ deficit using v5p's native \texttt{int32} accumulator. We analytically project this deficit into a fundamental arithmetic penalty (lacking wide-integer ALUs) and a spatial penalty. We demonstrate that evaluating concurrent multi-tenant deployments, where strict separation forces eager Montgomery reduction, yields a projected $5.19\times$ spatial collapse; relaxing this constraint theoretically recovers these spatial cycles, yet the underlying arithmetic penalty remains. To facilitate this characterisation, we deploy \codename as a measurement vehicle. By mapping low-degree polynomials onto matrix-form Number Theoretic Transforms, the scheduler stacks heterogeneous polynomials into dense 2D matrices, achieving $\sim$$100\%$ K-dimension column occupancy on uniform workloads ($>$$92\%$ on mixed-degree traces). However, despite optimal K-dimension packing, severe M-dimension under-utilisation (e.g., $6.25\%$ on v4) combined with overwhelming VPU-bound Montgomery reduction stalls mathematically starve the systolic arrays. A post-hoc HLO validator ensures these measurements remain structurally isolated against the XLA fusion engine. Our findings empirically demonstrate the structural inadequacy of AI-optimised systolic arrays for exact, high-throughput field arithmetic.

</details>


### [178] [ZK-Tracer: A High-Performance Heterogeneous Accelerator for Zero-Knowledge VM Trace Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25493)
*Jieran Cui,Zhengkai Wen,Haowen Fang,Yinan Zhu,Jia Xiong,Cheng Ni,Mingchi Zhang,Nan Guan,Xi Wang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 针对 zkVM 前端轨迹生成效率低下的瓶颈，本文提出 ZK-Tracer 硬件加速器，在实现轨迹生成 1829 倍加速的同时，大幅提升了零知识证明系统的端到端性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的硬件加速研究仅关注后端证明阶段，导致 zkVM 的前端执行和轨迹生成逐渐成为新的性能瓶颈，限制了零知识证明系统的整体效率和应用场景。

Method: 提出了一种专门针对 zkVM 前端的异构硬件架构 ZK-Tracer，包含主轨迹单元（Main Trace Unit）和并行排列轨迹单元（Permutation Trace Units），并设计了轻量级指令集扩展以实现高效的任务卸载。

Result: 在 ASIC 实现中，ZK-Tracer 在轨迹生成速度上比高性能多核 CPU 快 1829 倍，集成后使零知识证明系统的端到端性能提升了 963 倍。

Conclusion: ZK-Tracer 重新定义了 zkVM 的性能瓶颈，通过专门针对前端执行和轨迹生成阶段的硬件加速，显著提升了端到端的零知识证明系统性能。

Abstract: Zero-knowledge virtual machines (zkVMs) are a key technology for driving the large-scale adoption of zero-knowledge proofs (ZKP), but their performance bottlenecks severely limit their practicality. While current hardware acceleration research has exclusively focused on backend proving, we identify that the frontend execution and trace generation phase is rapidly emerging as the new system bottleneck. To address this challenge, we propose ZK-Tracer, the first hardware accelerator architecture specifically designed for the zkVM frontend. ZK-Tracer features a novel heterogeneous design comprising a Main Trace Unit and parallel Permutation Trace Units. It exposes a fine-grained interface to the host software through a lightweight instruction set extension, enabling efficient task offloading. Our ASIC implementation results demonstrate that ZK-Tracer achieves up to 1829x speedup in trace generation over a high-performance multi-core CPU. When integrated with existing backend proving accelerators, it delivers a remarkable 963x end-to-end performance improvement for the entire ZKP system.

</details>


### [179] [Co-Designing Graph-based Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search at Billion Scale for Processing-in-Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25522)
*Sitian Chen,Yusen Li,Yao Chen,Minwen Deng,Jintao Meng,Amelie Chi Zhou*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文针对图检索（ANNS）中内存吞吐瓶颈，提出了一种软硬件协同设计的PIM加速方案，通过压缩索引、流水线调度及优化计算内核，实现了性能与规模的双重突破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图检索（ANNS）方法受限于内存墙问题（内存带宽限制CPU性能），而GPU在大规模索引数据存储上存在容量瓶颈；PIM虽然理论上适合该负载，但在实际落地中面临处理单元内存小、通信开销大、计算能力弱等架构不匹配问题。

Method: 提出了一种软硬件协同设计方案，包含三个关键组件：1. 紧凑型索引布局（将内存占用减少14.5倍）；2. 异步流水线调度机制（确保持续利用主控到PIM的互连带宽）；3. 消除了乘法运算的距离计算内核（保持召回率损失低于0.08%）。

Result: 在三个十亿级基准测试中，该设计吞吐量相比CPU和GPU基线分别提升最高20倍和17.1倍，在高性能召回率场景下较现有PIM加速器性能提升129倍，并支持跨多节点扩展。

Conclusion: 提出的软硬件协同设计通过紧凑索引布局、异步流水线调度和无乘法距离计算内核，成功解决了PIM架构在处理大规模图检索时的内存、计算和通信瓶颈，在保持高召回率的同时，显著提升了吞吐量并实现了良好的扩展性。

Abstract: Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search (ANNS) is a core primitive in modern AI systems, and graph-based methods currently offer the best accuracy-efficiency trade-off at scale. The workload is fundamentally memory-bound: graph traversal produces frequent, irregular memory accesses that cap CPU throughput at main-memory bandwidth, while GPUs lack the high-bandwidth memory capacity to host billion-scale indexes. Processing-in-Memory (PIM) is a natural candidate, as placing computation next to data unlocks the abundant internal bandwidth that such bandwidth-starved workloads demand. Porting graph-based ANNS to PIM, however, exposes several architectural mismatches: each processing unit has only a small local memory, inter-unit communication is costly, host coordination adds overhead, and in-memory compute units are relatively weak -- limitations that have forced prior PIM-based ANNS designs to fall back on cluster-based indexing, whose recall ceiling is far below that of graph methods. This paper presents an algorithm-architecture co-design that overcomes these obstacles through three components: a compacted index layout that shrinks the PIM-resident memory footprint by 14.5x; an asynchronous pipelined scheduler that keeps the host-to-PIM interconnect saturated; and a multiplication-free distance kernel that loses under 0.08% recall. Across three billion-scale benchmarks, the proposed design achieves up to 20x and 17.1x higher throughput than CPU and GPU baselines, respectively, outperforms prior PIM accelerators by 129x in the high-recall regime, and scales gracefully across multi-node deployments and emerging PIM architecture.

</details>


### [180] [Code size reduction by advanced near addressing modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25602)
*Kajetan Nuernberger,Thomas Roecker,Gergely Fueto,Gabor Spaits,Horst Lehser*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在RISC-V架构中增加近地址寻址模式的方法，旨在解决实时系统中全局变量过多导致的溢出问题，从而优化代码规模与运行性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决实时系统中因全局变量过多导致超过全局指针寻址范围，从而被迫使用多指令寻址带来的代码臃肿与性能下降问题。

Method: 研究并设计了多种针对RISC-V指令集架构（ISA）的近地址寻址扩展方案，并通过代表性工作负载对其代码规模压缩效果进行了实验评估。

Result: 通过引入近地址寻址特性，实现了代码规模的显著缩减，并提升了系统的执行效率。

Conclusion: 向RISC-V架构引入近地址寻址（near addressing）模式是解决大规模全局变量寻址带来的代码膨胀与性能损耗的有效方案，显著优化了实时系统的资源利用率。

Abstract: To enable debugging and calibration of real time systems, which are in interaction with the real plant, the software used on those systems often has a huge number of global variables. The huge number of global variables exceed the range addressable relative to the global pointer. Therefore, addressing these variables normally needs two instructions. Other CPU architectures commonly used in the real time control systems domain address these by various near addressing modes. This results in significant code size reductions and performance boost. This paper discusses different variants to add such near addressing features to the RISC-V ISA. The impact on the code size is evaluated with different representative workloads.

</details>


### [181] [DiSC: Resolution-Scalable Acceleration of Diffusion Models by Exploiting Sparsity and Cached Token Reuse with Hash-based Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25798)
*Jieon Yoon,Hangyeol Lee,Jaehoon Heo,Joo-Young Kim*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: DiSC 是一个针对 Transformer 扩散模型的软硬件协同加速器，通过在算法层利用 Token 重用和注意力稀疏化技术，配合精简的硬件映射架构，大幅降低了高分辨率扩散模型的计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer 扩散模型在高分辨率下因自注意力机制的二次复杂度以及迭代推理过程，带来巨大的计算开销，迫切需要能效比更高的加速方案。

Method: 1. 软件优化：提出了 Cached Token Reuse (CTR) 以重用时间步间的冗余 Token；提出了 Softmax Thresholding (ST) 以迫使注意力计算产生稀疏性并重用稀疏掩码。
2. 硬件架构：设计了无需专用稀疏处理单元的架构，利用哈希分布方案在通用计算引擎上高效处理混合稠密-稀疏工作负载。

Result: 在 DiT 和 PixArt-Sigma 模型上，DiSC 相较于 NVIDIA A100/H100 GPU 实现了 2.48-4.74 倍的加速，能效提升了 46.4%-68.1%。

Conclusion: DiSC 通过软硬件协同设计（算法层的 CTR 和 ST，架构层的哈希分布内存管理），成功解决了 Transformer 扩散模型在大分辨率下的计算冗余问题，在实现显著性能提升和能效降低的同时，保持了硬件开销的最小化。

Abstract: Transformer-based diffusion models offer superior scalability and performance but suffer from high computational overhead due to the iterative nature and quadratic complexity of self-attention at high resolutions. In this paper, we propose DiSC, a resolution-scalable, sparsity-aware hardware accelerator. At the software level, DiSC introduces two algorithms: Cached Token Reuse (CTR), and Softmax Thresholding with Sparsity Mask Reuse (ST). CTR introduces a mechanism that translates spatial variations in the input latent difference across steps into a token-level reuse decision, effectively eliminating redundant token computation. ST induces sparsity in attention operations by reusing a generated sparsity pattern, leveraging temporal similarity to bypass costly prediction overhead. Together, these algorithms provide resolution-scalable computational benefits and yield a moderate sparsity and hybrid dense-sparse workload.
  To exploit this efficiently, we design a specialized hardware architecture and unified dataflow. This architecture avoids dedicated sparsity-handling components; instead, a hash-based distribution over on-chip memory banks allows DiSC to reuse its existing compute engines for sparse operations, efficiently exploiting the induced sparsity with minimal hardware overhead. Evaluated on DiT and PixArt-Sigma, DiSC achieves 3.47-4.74x and 2.48-3.50x speedups over NVIDIA A100 and H100 GPUs, respectively, with energy savings ranging from 46.4% to 68.1%.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [182] [Algometrics: Forecasting Under Algorithmic Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23978)
*Marc Schmitt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文引入了“Algometrics”框架来分析算法决策如何通过反馈回路改变市场数据，指出传统的历史预测指标无法准确衡量算法实际部署风险，并证明了模型表现受市场拥挤程度影响，建议在评估中引入反馈敏感性指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在算法市场中，预测模型本身会通过交易和决策改变其所预测的数据，形成“算法反馈回路”。现有的评估标准通常基于被动观测数据，无法衡量模型在实际部署中与环境交互产生的真实表现（部署风险）。

Method: 提出“Algometrics”框架以形式化算法反馈环境；通过数学推导证明反馈闭环中的风险演变及不可识别性；探讨了利用随机化或工具变量操作来识别反馈并推导有限样本下的部署风险估计界限。

Result: 1. 部署风险无法仅从被动历史数据中识别；2. 预测模型在拥挤（crowding）场景下会出现排名倒置现象；3. 通过引入随机干预可以识别短期线性反馈，并确定了部署风险估计的有限样本界限。

Conclusion: 在算法市场中，传统的被动预测评估存在局限。研究强调，时间序列基准测试必须同步报告“反馈敏感性”（feedback sensitivity）与预测准确性，以全面评估算法在实际部署中的表现。

Abstract: In algorithmic markets, predictive models become part of the data-generating process they aim to forecast. Once their outputs are converted into trades, allocations, execution schedules, or risk controls, they change the future data on which they are evaluated. I introduce algometrics, a framework for time series whose evolution depends on the predictive algorithms forecasting them. The framework distinguishes historical risk, measured under passive forecasting, from deployment risk, measured when forecasts drive actions. I prove three results. First, deployment risk is not identifiable from passive historical data alone: even in a one-step linear feedback model, infinitely many algorithm-mediated environments induce the same historical law while implying different deployment risks for the same forecaster. Second, historical model rankings can invert under crowding, so a predictor with lower passive error can have higher deployment error once similar algorithms are adopted. Third, randomized or instrumented actions identify short-horizon linear feedback, and I derive a finite-sample bound for deployment-risk estimation. These results suggest that time-series benchmarks in algorithmic markets should report feedback sensitivity alongside predictive accuracy.

</details>


### [183] [CAFD: Concept-Aware DNN Fault Detection using VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24008)
*Amin Abbasishahkoo,Mahboubeh Dadkhah,Lionel Briand*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种概念感知故障检测（CAFD）方法，通过由视觉语言模型辅助生成的“概念故障率（CFR）”特征，在低计算开销下实现了显著优于现有技术的 DNN 故障检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的混合式 DNN 故障检测方法虽性能较好，但计算开销巨大，难以在实际场景中扩展和应用。作者旨在解决现有方法在性能与效率之间的平衡问题。

Method: 提出了一种名为概念感知故障检测（CAFD）的基于学习的方法。该方法整合了三种关键特征：模型输出信号（model-based）、距离度量特征（distance-based）以及一种利用视觉语言模型（VLM）提取图像文本概念并计算其与模型故障关联性的创新特征——概念故障率（CFR）。

Result: 在三个数据集（包括 ImageNet）和三种 DNN 模型上的实证研究表明，CAFD 在不同预算下均优于五种最先进的基准方法，平均故障检测率（FDR）提升了 18.3%。

Conclusion: CAFD 是一种高效且高性能的 DNN 故障检测方法，通过引入概念感知特征（CFR）有效利用了多源信息，在保持计算效率的同时显著优于现有基准。

Abstract: Fault detection for Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) has received increasing attention in recent years. While more advanced hybrid approaches have been proposed to combine multiple sources of information and outperform earlier techniques, they often incur substantial computational overhead, limiting scalability and practicality in real-world settings. In this paper, we introduce Concept-Aware Fault Detection (CAFD), a learning-based approach that achieves superior fault detection performance by effectively integrating multiple information sources while maintaining practical efficiency. Specifically, CAFD is trained using a carefully selected set of informative features, including model-based signals derived from the DNN's outputs, distance-based features, and a novel concept-based feature, called Concept Failure Ratio (CFR). CFR leverages Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to extract textual concepts from images and quantify the likelihood that their presence is associated with DNN failures. By incorporating this feature, CAFD benefits from complementary semantic information, enabling more effective fault detection. Our results demonstrate that CFR serves as an effective indicator for DNN fault detection. We conduct an extensive empirical evaluation of CAFD, comparing it against five state-of-the-art baselines across three subject DNN models and datasets, including ImageNet. Across a wide range of constrained selection budgets, CAFD consistently outperforms all baselines in Fault Detection Rate (FDR), achieving average FDR improvements of 18.3% across all investigated subjects and budget sizes.

</details>


### [184] [A computational phase transition for learning-to-sample from Ising models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24752)
*Andrej Risteski,Thuy-Duong Vuong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Ising模型中的“学习采样”问题，证明了该任务在谱阈值处存在计算复杂性相变，且比传统的参数学习更困难；同时指出高效学习者在处理硬实例时必然表现出“记忆-幻觉二分法”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: “学习采样”（learning-to-sample）是生成模型的核心算法任务。本文旨在研究Ising模型下，该任务在谱阈值附近的计算复杂性边界及其实际表现。

Method: 构造了一类处于谱阈值之外的常数宽度Ising模型，并利用标准密码学假设证明了该类模型的学习采样在计算上是困难的。

Result: 1. 建立了谱阈值处的计算复杂性相变；2. 证明了学习采样任务可能比参数学习任务更难；3. 揭示了高效学习者在面对硬实例时必须经历“记忆-幻觉二分法”（即要么复制训练集，要么产生大量低概率样本）。

Conclusion: 作者证明了Ising模型在谱阈值处存在“学习采样”任务的计算相变，并揭示了该任务与参数学习之间的难度差异。此外，指出了高效学习者不可避免地会陷入“记忆-幻觉二分法”。

Abstract: We study \emph{learning-to-sample} -- a basic algorithmic task underlying generative modeling -- for Ising models, a standard testbed for algorithmic ideas in both theoretical computer science and machine learning. Given i.i.d. samples of an unknown target distribution, the goal of learning-to-sample is to learn a computationally efficient generation procedure that produces new samples following approximately the same distribution. We construct a family of Ising models of constantly bounded-width which lie just beyond the spectral threshold $λ_{\max}(J)-λ_{\min}(J)=1$, and show that learning-to-sample for this family is computationally hard under standard cryptographic assumptions, even when the learner is given both polynomially many i.i.d. samples from the model and explicit access to its parameters. Combined with results of [AJKPV24,KLV25] showing tractability of learning-to-sample below the spectral threshold, this establishes a sharp computational phase transition at the spectral threshold. Moreover, combined with prior results on parameter learning for bounded-width Ising models [KM17,WSD19,VML20], this shows that learning-to-sample can be more difficult than parameter learning. Finally, we show that any efficient learner for these hard instances exhibits a natural memorization-hallucination dichotomy: the learner must either output configurations that, after a simple transformation, match the (transformed) training data or place substantial mass on configurations of negligible probability under the target distribution.

</details>


### [185] [Hidden-State Privacy Has an Empty Middle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24042)
*Alexander Okezue Bell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了单一高斯机制在保护大模型隐藏层隐私时的局限性，证明了完全隐私保护的不可能性，并通过提出Split-memory Transformer架构，证明了协同架构设计比单纯优化噪声机制更能有效防御自适应检索攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对大语言模型隐藏层（hidden-state）隐私泄露问题，现有高斯噪声添加策略在实用性（Utility）与隐私保护（Privacy）之间存在难以调和的矛盾（“空心”现象），缺乏一种能同时满足二者要求的标准化发布机制。

Method: 1. 理论推导：证明了Fisher-ball下界，即任何具有适度效用（utility）的高斯发布都会导致马氏信号（Mahalanobis signal）随隐藏层宽度线性增长。
2. 实验验证：测试了1,536个高斯发布模型及多种机制，包括基于对角线逆Fisher信息（diagonal inverse-Fisher）的机制。
3. 攻击评估：使用自适应检索攻击者（adaptive retrieval attacker）及全轨迹序列逆转器进行隐私压力测试。
4. 模型设计：提出并训练了Split-memory Transformer，评估其在隐私保护效能上的优势。

Result: 1. 经验性发现：所有测试的高斯发布机制均无法在自适应攻击下实现平衡。
2. 理论结果：证明了高斯机制存在隐私与效用的二律背反。
3. 机制优化：对角线逆Fisher机制虽是极大极小最优，但处于隐私/效用边缘。
4. 新架构表现：Split-memory Transformer在相同语言模型损失惩罚下，展现出比基础模型高出6–24倍的隐蔽性优势。

Conclusion: 对于隐藏层隐私保护，单纯依靠高斯机制进行协方差设计无法同时满足实用性和隐私需求。研究指出应转向“架构与发布机制协同设计”（architecture or release co-design）的范式，而非仅在单一高斯机制框架内优化。

Abstract: Of $1{,}536$ Gaussian release covariances we tested for single-layer hidden-state privacy, zero achieve both moderate utility and moderate privacy against an adaptive retrieval attacker. We prove a complementary Fisher-ball lower bound: every full-rank Gaussian release at $O(1)$ Fisher utility admits a direction whose Mahalanobis signal grows linearly in hidden width, ruling out uniform Gaussian safety in the class and matching the empirical empty middle. The diagonal inverse-Fisher release $Σ^\star_{\mathrm{diag}}(\mathcal{K}) = (2\mathcal{K}/d)\,\mathrm{diag}(1/F_{ii})$ is the unique minimax-optimal diagonal mechanism at first-order KL budget $\mathcal{K}$ and the only release with worst-attacker top-1 $\le 0.001$ at every point of a 32 model-layer grid, but it sits on a privacy/utility edge rather than filling the middle. A generalized-eigen mechanism reaching $13\times$ Pareto reduction under Euclidean retrieval collapses to $100\%$ top-1 under the adaptive Mahalanobis attacker, and a full-trajectory sequence inverter recovers $94\%$ of clean GPT-2 prefixes but $0\%$ under $Σ_{\mathrm{diag}}$. A split-memory transformer trained from scratch reaches $G_{\mathrm{Mah}} \in [20, 33]$ at 90M and maintains a $6$--$24\times$ advantage over same-budget GPT baselines from 30M to 1B at a fixed-token language-modeling loss penalty; pretrained models top out at 9.3. These results reframe hidden-state release from mechanism-design within the Gaussian class to architecture or release co-design.

</details>


### [186] [A Large-Scale Dataset and Benchmark: Do Protein-Ligand Models Learn Binding Sites or Just Binding Likelihood?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24045)
*Zhaohan Meng,Zhen Bai,Ke Yuan,Iadh Ounis,Zaiqiao Meng,Hao Xu,Joseph Loscalzo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: InteractBind 是一个包含约10万对蛋白质-配体的大规模数据集及基准，旨在通过精细的非共价相互作用映射，评估药物发现模型在结合位点定位方面的能力，以弥补当前评估标准在可解释性和物理机制理解上的缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准侧重于二元结合预测和亲和力回归，缺乏对模型能否准确识别结合位点及理解分子识别底层物理机制的评估，难以衡量模型的可解释性和物理合理性。

Method: 构建了包含约10万对蛋白质-配体结构的大规模数据集 InteractBind；引入细粒度评估任务，即基于六种主要非共价相互作用（如氢键、疏水作用等）的结合位点定位任务；并设计了结合亲和力和蛋白质相似度可控的划分方案。

Result: 对八种现有模型进行评测发现，尽管模型在二元结合预测任务上表现良好，但在结合位点的细粒度定位上能力有限，且在不同类型的非共价相互作用上表现出明显的性能差异。

Conclusion: InteractBind 为蛋白质-配体模型提出了新的评估范式，能够有效揭示现有模型在非共价相互作用定位方面的局限性，旨在推动更具可解释性和符合物理规律的药物设计模型的发展。

Abstract: Protein-ligand modeling underpins computational drug discovery and molecular design. Existing protein-ligand benchmarks typically evaluate whether a protein and ligand interact and how strongly they bind, through tasks such as binary binding prediction and affinity regression. However, these evaluations provide limited evidence of whether models can localize binding sites or identify the non-covalent interactions underlying molecular recognition. To address this gap, we introduce InteractBind, a large-scale protein-ligand dataset comprising approximately 100k protein-ligand pairs, together with a benchmark for fine-grained evaluation. The core fine-grained task is that of binding-site localization, which uses protein-residue and ligand-atom interaction maps spanning six major types of non-covalent interactions to assess whether model-derived interaction maps localize binding sites. InteractBind further includes binding affinity and protein similarity-controlled splits to support realistic generalization assessment. Using InteractBind, we evaluate eight existing sequence-based and interaction-aware models, assessing binary binding prediction and binding-site localization. Results reveal limited binding-site localization despite strong binary binding prediction, with marked variation across non-covalent interaction types. Overall, InteractBind establishes a benchmark paradigm that encourages the development of more interpretable and physically grounded protein-ligand models.

</details>


### [187] [Mixture of Complementary Agents for Robust LLM Ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24048)
*Yichi Zhang,Kevin Lu,Yuang Zhang,Jie Gao,Lirong Xia,Fang-Yi Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于“互补性”的 LLM Proposer 选择框架，通过高效的贪心算法自动选择最具协同效应的模型组合，提升了多模型协作系统的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多 AI 协同方法多关注模型准确性或独立多样性，忽略了 Proposer 之间以及 Proposer 与 Summarizer 之间的复杂交互，且标准特征选择算法在 LLM 应用中成本过高。

Method: 将 Proposer 选择建模为组合优化问题（类似于特征选择），并提出了一系列具备计算可行性的贪心选择算法，通过一小部分标注数据集来评估模型间的互补性。

Result: 实验表明，考虑互补性的选择策略显著优于仅考虑准确性或多样性的方法，验证了该方案的高效性与实用性。

Conclusion:  complementarity（互补性）是多模型协同中选择 Proposer 的核心准则，通过贪心策略进行选择可以在性能与计算成本之间取得最优平衡。

Abstract: Multi-AI collaboration, such as ensembling or debating large language models (LLMs), is a promising paradigm for aggregating information and boosting performance. A foundational step in these pipelines is to feed the responses of several proposer LLMs into a summarizer LLM, which synthesizes a better answer. However, choosing which proposers to include is non-trivial. Existing approaches primarily focus either on accuracy (picking the strongest models) or diversity (ensuring variety), and often overlook the interactions among proposers and with the summarizer. We reframe proposer selection as a combinatorial selection problem akin to feature selection, where the value of an LLM lies in its complementarity with others. However, directly applying standard feature-selection algorithms is impractical in the LLM setting due to prohibitive time complexity. Motivated by this limitation, we explore an extensive range of computationally feasible, greedy-style selection algorithms that assess complementarity using a small labeled set. Our experiments validate complementarity as a guiding principle for proposer selection and identify methods that achieve the best performance-cost trade-offs in practice.

</details>


### [188] [Truthful Online Preference Aggregation for LLM Fine-Tuning in Mobile Crowdsourcing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24052)
