*Shugang Hao,Lingjie Duan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对LLM微调中众包反馈的策略性谎报问题，本文通过动态贝叶斯博弈模型和在线加权聚合策略，实现了对真实反馈的激励兼容，并将遗憾界从线性降至亚线性 $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{T})$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动众包平台利用人类反馈微调大语言模型（LLM）时，工作者可能为了获利而策略性地谎报偏好。现有的EM权重估计等方法无法在在线设置下识别出最准确的工作者，导致遗憾界达到线性 $\mathcal{O}(T)$。

Method: 1. 建立动态贝叶斯博弈模型（Dynamic Bayesian Game）来描述平台与博弈工作者之间的在线学习交互过程。 2. 设计了一种在线加权聚合机制，根据工作者反馈的实时准确度动态调整权重。 3. 在每时间片反馈受限的挑战性环境下对机制进行了扩展。

Result: 该机制在理论上确保了工作者的真实性（truthfulness），证明了遗憾达到亚线性 $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{T})$；在真实数据集上的实验表明，该方法比基准方案具有显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 本文通过构建动态贝叶斯博弈模型，提出了一种在线加权聚合机制。该机制能够有效促使移动众包工作者提交真实反馈，在理论上实现了亚线性遗憾界 $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{T})$，并克服了现有方法在动态在线场景下的局限性。

Abstract: To better serve users' demands in mobile applications (e.g., navigation), mobile crowdsourcing platforms can iteratively align large language model (LLM)-generated content (e.g., AI-generated traffic condition predictions) with human feedback collected from crowdsourcing workers (e.g., mobile users). However, workers may strategically misreport their online preference feedback to maximize their influence or payment. Existing pipelines in mobile crowdsourcing (e.g., EM-based weight estimation) fail to identify the most accurate worker in this online setting, resulting in a linear regret $\mathcal{O}(T)$ over $T$ time slots. In this paper, we study truthful online preference aggregation for LLM fine-tuning in mobile crowdsourcing. We formulate a new dynamic Bayesian game to model the multi-agent online learning process between the platform and strategic mobile workers. We propose a novel online weighted aggregation mechanism that dynamically adjusts each worker's weight in the preference aggregation according to their feedback accuracy. We prove that our mechanism ensures truthful feedback from strategic workers and achieves a sublinear regret $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{T})$ over $T$ time slots. We further extend our mechanism to a challenging scenario with limited worker feedback per time slot, still guaranteeing a sublinear regret $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{T})$. Experiments on LLM fine-tuning with real-world datasets further demonstrate significant performance gains of our mechanisms over benchmark schemes.

</details>


### [189] [Cascade-KDE: Robust Time-Series Restoration under Out-of-Distribution Impulse Corruptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24055)
*Yuefeng Liu,Ning Yang,Ziyu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对含有脉冲噪声的时间序列，Cascade-KDE 提出了一种无需训练、基于密度估计与截断鲁棒处理的恢复框架，在去噪的同时能出色地保留关键局部形态与导数特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实时间序列数据常掺杂高斯噪声及大数值脉冲异常，现有方法在降低重构误差的同时，难以有效地保护局部形态特征（如ECG波形、电池退化斜率），影响下游任务性能。

Method: 提出了一种名为“Cascade-KDE”的训练免费恢复框架：1. 估计二维时幅密度；2. 应用密度截断鲁棒期望（Density-Truncated Robust Expectation）限制异常点影响；3. 通过具有自适应停止功能的指数级联（exponential cascade）进行序列细化。

Result: 在多项基准数据集上，Cascade-KDE 在曲线保真度、导数保留、下游分类准确性及运行效率方面均优于传统滤波器和主流的基于学习的基线方法。

Conclusion: Cascade-KDE 是一种有效的、无需训练的时间序列恢复方案，特别适用于需要保留局部形状、导数特征及下游任务关键信息的工业、医疗等场景。

Abstract: Real-world time-series data in industrial sensing, healthcare, and energy systems is often corrupted by a mixture of Gaussian noise and occasional large-magnitude impulse outliers. For tasks that depend on local shape, such as ECG morphology analysis and battery degradation monitoring, the main requirement is not only low reconstruction error but also preservation of derivative peaks and task-critical features. We propose Cascade-KDE, a training-free restoration framework for corrupted time series. The method first estimates a two-dimensional temporal-amplitude density, then applies a Density-Truncated Robust Expectation to limit the influence of distant abnormal points, and finally refines the sequence through an exponential cascade with adaptive stopping. This design aims to improve robustness under out-of-distribution impulse corruptions while keeping the restored trajectory close to the original local structure. Across several benchmark datasets, the proposed method shows consistent gains over classical filters and representative learning-based baselines on curve fidelity, derivative preservation, downstream classification, and runtime efficiency. These results suggest that bounded density-based restoration is a practical option for feature-preserving preprocessing in noisy time-series pipelines.

</details>


### [190] [Signs Beat Floats: Low-Rank Double-Binary Adaptation for On-Device Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24058)
*Yoshihiko Fujisawa,Yuma Ichikawa,Yudai Fujimoto,Akira Sakai,Katsuki Fujisawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoRDBA是一种用于大语言模型边缘适配的新型LoRA适配器，它通过二进制化和通道缩放技术，将适配器压缩至原来的1/10以上，在保持高质量生成的同时，有效降低了边缘设备的存储和部署延迟压力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备上进行大语言模型适配（Adapter-mode）时，现有的浮点LoRA适配器在部署过程中因引入额外的稠密参数分支而带来存储、训练状态维护和通信开销，限制了资源受限设备的部署效率。

Method: 提出LoRDBA方法：将LoRA的两个低秩矩阵转换为二进制符号矩阵，并使用轻量级通道缩放因子表示幅值。通过将稠密分支转化为两次符号累加矩阵乘法并结合通道缩放，实现了适配器结构的极简化。运用有限样本分析理论指导重构质量评估。

Result: 实验显示，LoRDBA在同等模型大小下优于现有的低位宽基准方法，在特定设定下达到了FP16 LoRA的质量水平。在rank=16时，适配器体积减小超过10倍，预填充（prefill）延迟开销增加不超过8%，训练显存开销约为FP16 LoRA的1.6倍。

Conclusion: LoRDBA通过二进制化LoRA的低秩权重，在显著减小适配器存储占用的同时保持了与FP16 LoRA相当的性能，并在部署时具备极低的延迟开销，是边缘设备上高效适配大语言模型的有效方案。

Abstract: On-device adaptation of large language models commonly keeps a quantized base model frozen while training and deploying a small, task-specific LoRA adapter. In the unmerged adapter-mode setting, however, the adapter is more than a compact storage module; it introduces an additional dense floating-point branch, maintains a trainable state for local updates, and acts as a unit of communication and hot-swapping.We introduce LoRDBA, a LoRA-compatible adapter that replaces both low-rank factors with binary sign carriers while representing magnitudes through lightweight, channel-wise scales, converting the dense adapter branch into two sign-accumulation matrix multiplications interleaved with channel-wise scaling. A finite-sample analysis shows that reconstruction quality is governed by the residual-to-magnitude ratio of the original LoRA factors. In adapter-mode experiments, LoRDBA outperforms low-bit baselines at matched model sizes while matching fp16 LoRA quality in selected regimes. The unmerged adapter incurs at most 8% prefill latency overhead at matched rank r=16 despite an over 10x reduction in adapter footprint, with moderate training memory overhead of approximately 1.6x that of fp16 LoRA.

</details>


### [191] [Spectral Probe-Circuits: A Three-Step Recipe for Identifying Attention-Head Circuits in Pretrained Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24059)
*Yongzhong Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需监督的通用三步法，用于识别Transformer中的关键注意力头电路，验证了该方法在不同架构和规模模型下的有效性，并发现模型中专门化计算头的比例和感应电路的规模具有跨模型的规律性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决预训练Transformer模型中注意力头功能识别困难的问题，提供一套无需监督信号的标准化方法，以深入理解模型内部的计算机制。

Method: 提出了一个三步识别配方：1. 通过“时间积分参与率”进行谱分析，评估注意力头的计算贡献；2. 使用“任务模式筛选”聚焦特定任务候选电路；3. 通过“组消融实验”对比匹配随机对照组以验证因果性。

Result: 在不同规模（51M-7B）、架构（稠密/MoE）和训练流程中通过了验证。实验表明，感应电路在所有模型中均为必不可少，消融后合成感应任务表现下降94-100%；Pythia系列中专门化计算头比例稳定在17-19%，且感应电路规模与总头数相比呈亚线性增长。

Conclusion: 提出了一种无需标签或归因梯度的三步法，可有效识别预训练Transformer中的注意力头电路，并证明了该方法的通用性、预测性及跨尺度的一致性。该研究揭示了注意力头专门化计算比例趋于稳定以及感应电路规模呈亚线性的规律。

Abstract: We present a three-step recipe for identifying attention-head circuits in pretrained transformers. A per-head spectral signal -- the time-integrated participation ratio of each head's attention output -- ranks heads doing sustained content-dependent computation without labels or attribution gradients. A task-pattern screen filters this general indicator into a task-specific candidate circuit, and group ablation against a matched-random control completes the causal claim. We validate across an 8x parameter range (51M to 1B-active / 7B-total), two architecture families (dense, mixture-of-experts), and four pretraining pipelines. The recipe ports: a 2-6 head induction circuit is causally necessary in every model tested, with a 94-100% drop in synthetic-induction top-1 after ablation. The spectral signal is predictive without supervision: on six independent seeds of a 51M-parameter probe model, the same computation identifies the seed-specific circuit on each seed. The fraction of heads doing identifiable specialized computation is conserved at 17-19% across the Pythia family (124M to 410M), while specific induction circuits stay 3-11 heads -- sublinear in total head count. This paper is the methodology anchor of a three-paper program; companion papers extend the recipe to developmental trajectories during pretraining and to composed-task circuits where pattern selectivity decouples from task-causal structure.

</details>


### [192] [Federated Learning over Human-Body Communication for On-Body Edge Intelligence: A Survey, Taxonomy, and BODYFED-HBC Scheduling Vignette](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24062)
*Koffka Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了将人体通信（HBC）与联邦学习（FL）相结合用于可穿戴设备的可行性，提出了 BODYFED-HBC 框架和优化调度算法，旨在解决隐私保护下的边缘智能部署难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可穿戴设备学习研究往往抽象掉了通信层，而 HBC 研究则忽略了学习和模型更新流量，两者缺乏深度集成；为了解决此问题，需探索一种考虑人体信道特性的联邦学习（body-channel-aware FL）。

Method: 1. 将生理/行为传感的联邦学习（FL）与人体通信（HBC）技术相结合。2. 提出一种分类法，区分了体内、体枢纽、跨用户和临床云等 FL 部署场景。3. 引入 BODYFED-HBC 参考架构及相关的优化公式和调度算法。4. 提供了一个结合公共可穿戴设备数据集与经验性体耦合通信信号损失模型的模拟场景。

Result: 文章系统性地综述了 HBC、无线体域网、可穿戴 FL、身体隐私及边缘智能优化的交叉领域，明确了以用户姿态、剩余能量、感应器内存和隐私风险为约束的联邦学习协议，并为相关研究提供了评价指标、开放数据集和研究方向。

Conclusion: 文章通过提出 BODYFED-HBC 架构和优化调度算法，为 HBC 与 FL 的结合奠定了理论和实验基础，并指出了该领域面临的隐私、功耗及信道动态性等研究挑战。

Abstract: Human-body communication (HBC) is a promising physical substrate for wearable body-area networks because it can localize communication around the body and reduce the burden of conventional radio links. Federated learning (FL) is a promising learning substrate because it can reduce raw-data centralization for physiological and behavioral sensing. Yet these two literatures remain weakly connected: FL for wearables usually abstracts the communication layer, whereas HBC research usually abstracts learning and model-update traffic. This article surveys the intersection of HBC, wireless body-area networks, wearable FL, Internet-of-Bodies privacy, and edge-intelligence optimization. We propose a taxonomy that distinguishes intra-body, body-hub, cross-user, and clinical-cloud FL deployments, and we identify the open problem of body-channel-aware FL: learning protocols whose client selection, update compression, and aggregation are controlled by posture-dependent HBC links, residual energy, sensor memory, and privacy risk. To make the research agenda concrete, we introduce BODYFED-HBC as a reference architecture and provide an optimization formulation and scheduling algorithm. We further specify a reproducible simulation vignette that combines public wearable datasets with empirical body-coupled-communication signal-loss models. The article concludes with open datasets, evaluation metrics, limitations, and research directions for computer scientists working above the hardware layer.

</details>


### [193] [Characterizing the Representational Capacity of Neural Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24210)
*Robin Young*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了主流神经过程（NP）架构的表达能力，揭示了从 CNP 到 TNP 的严格层级关系，厘清了不同机制（如注意力、平移等变性、潜变量）对模型逼近函数空间的影响，为 NP 的架构选择提供了理论指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在明确不同神经过程（NP）架构在函数逼近能力上的差异及边界，解决目前模型选择缺乏严谨数学指导的问题，从而帮助研究者根据任务的特性（如局部性、全局交互等）选择最合适的架构。

Method: 采用理论数学分析方法，通过分析 CNP、ANP、TNP 及其潜变量变体在表达函数空间上的差异，界定各类架构的表示边界；利用函数依赖性、归纳偏置（如平移等变性与平稳性）以及交互跳数（hop interactions）作为比较基准。

Result: 1. 证明了 NP 架构存在严格的层次结构：CNP 仅能表示依赖有限预期特征的函数；ANP 由于查询依赖重加权机制严格泛化了 CNP；ConvCNP 与 ANP 互不包含（分别受平稳性限制和平移等变性限制）；TNP 的深度决定了其捕获上下文交互的跳数。2. 潜变量 NP 虽能产生连贯采样，但无法避免编码器的固有局限，若要匹配 GP 后验分布，潜变量维度需随上下文大小缩放。

Conclusion: 本文通过理论分析确立了神经过程（NP）架构的表达能力分层结构，为根据特定任务结构选择模型提供了理论依据。发现简单的架构受到特征提取能力的限制，而随着架构复杂性的增加（如结合注意力机制或全连接自注意力），模型能够捕捉更高阶的交互和分布属性。

Abstract: What functions can Neural Processes represent? We analyze the representational capacity of popular NP architectures: Conditional Neural Processes (CNPs), Attentive Neural Processes (ANPs), Transformer Neural Processes (TNPs), and their latent variants. We prove these architectures form a strict hierarchy. CNP-representable functions are exactly those depending on finitely many expected features of the context distribution. ANPs strictly generalize CNPs via query-dependent reweighting, enabling kernel smoothers. ConvCNPs and ANPs are incomparable; each contains functions outside the other, separated by stationarity versus translation equivariance. TNPs with $L$ self-attention layers capture $L$-hop context interactions. For latent NPs, we show finite-dimensional latents provide coherent sampling but do not circumvent encoder limitations; matching GP posterior distributions requires latent dimension scaling with context size. These results provide a theoretical foundation for architecture selection based on task structure.

</details>


### [194] [Generative Representation Learning on Hyper-relational Knowledge Graphs via Masked Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24064)
*Jaejun Lee,Seheon Kim,Joyce Jiyoung Whang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对传统HKG推理局限于单点填空的限制，本文提出了首个生成式HKG学习框架KREPE，通过掩码离散扩散模型统一了链接预测与事实生成任务，能够灵活处理复杂的多组件缺失推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的HKG推理方法多局限于简单的“单点链接预测”，无法应对现实场景中事实的多个或全部组成部分同时缺失的问题。

Method: 提出KREPE模型，结合了基于上下文的消息传递（捕捉事实内依赖）和随机抽样的上下文聚合（捕捉事实间相关性）。利用“掩码离散扩散（masked discrete diffusion）”建模缺失组件的概率分布，从而实现灵活的事实补全与生成。

Result: KREPE在标准的HKG链接预测基准测试中达到了最先进的性能，并在生成新颖且准确的事实方面优于现有的基于LLM的基线方法。

Conclusion: 提出了一种名为KREPE的超关系知识图谱（HKG）生成式学习框架，该框架通过掩码离散扩散模型统一了链接预测与事实生成任务，并证明了其在处理多成分缺失的事实建模方面优于现有模型及大型语言模型（LLM）。

Abstract: Hyper-relational knowledge graphs (HKGs) effectively represent complex facts. While inferring new knowledge in HKGs is a critical problem, current methods cast it as a simple link prediction, assuming that nearly all entities and relations within a fact are known, leaving only a single blank to be filled. However, this restricted assumption may not hold in real-world scenarios in which multiple, or even all, constituent components of a fact may be missing simultaneously. To bridge this gap, we introduce a task called fact generation: generating a valid hyper-relational fact from an arbitrarily masked query, i.e., completing a partially observed fact or generating a fact from scratch. We propose KREPE, the first generative representation learning method for HKGs that learns to model the probability distributions of missing components conditioned on the local fact components and global structure of HKGs via a masked discrete diffusion. KREPE models both the intra-fact dependencies by contextual message passing and inter-fact correlations by aggregating stochastically sampled contexts. KREPE seamlessly unifies link prediction and fact generation within a single training framework, achieving state-of-the-art performance on standard HKG link prediction benchmarks and outperforming LLM-based baselines in generating novel and correct facts.

</details>


### [195] [Not All Transitions Matter: Evidence from PPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24071)
*Ajhesh Basnet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种通过随机丢弃25%的序列数据来打破经验轨迹中的冗余性，从而提升PPO强化学习算法训练稳定性的简单且有效的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ऑन-पॉलिसी（On-policy）学习中，序列数据存在强因果关联和冗余性，导致梯度信号重复，使得策略更新不稳定，而训练曲线往往难以直观反映这种内部的不稳定性。

Method: 提出一种简单的辅助采样方法：在PPO的Rollout阶段随机丢弃固定比例（建议为25%）的过渡（transitions），同时确保奖励信号的完整性。该方法作为无需额外架构改动、即插即用的组件实现。

Result: 在五个不同难度的环境中（CartPole, Acrobot, LunarLander, HalfCheetah, Hopper），该方法在保持奖励水平与标准PPO相当的同时，显著改善了KL散度、策略熵和价值估计的训练稳定性。

Conclusion: 通过在策略更新前随机丢弃一部分（约25%）过渡数据，可以有效打破强化学习中序列数据的冗余性并稳定训练过程，且无需修改算法核心架构。

Abstract: Training a reinforcement learning agent on-policy means collecting fresh experience at every update, and that experience comes with a hidden problem. Each state in a rollout is the direct output of the previous one, causally chained together by the agent's own actions. Because of this, consecutive transitions are never truly independent. They carry overlapping information, and the gradient signal the network receives ends up far more repetitive than the batch size suggests. The same directions get reinforced over and over, the value network struggles to keep up as the policy shifts, and training becomes quietly unstable in ways that reward curves alone rarely reveal.
  This paper asks whether that redundancy can simply be removed. We show that randomly dropping a fixed fraction of transitions from the rollout, at the right stage so the reward signal stays intact, is enough to break the repetitive gradient structure and stabilize training. The change is minimal: one sampling step, no new components, no modification to the core algorithm, and it works with any PPO implementation. Across five environments of increasing difficulty, CartPole-v1, Acrobot-v1, LunarLander-v2, HalfCheetah-v5, and Hopper-v5, the method matches vanilla PPO on reward while producing more consistent training dynamics across KL divergence, policy entropy, and value estimates. Dropping 25% of transitions turns out to be the sweet spot: enough to disrupt the redundancy, not enough to thin the batch.

</details>


### [196] [Private Adaptive Covariance Estimation via Gaussian Graphical Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24295)
*Cecilia Ferrando,Miguel Fuentes,Brett Mullins,Cameron Musco,Daniel Sheldon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PACE-GGM是一种针对协方差估计的自适应差分隐私方法，通过重点保护关键矩阵条目并利用最大熵重构，在保持隐私的同时有效优化了高维数据的估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统对协方差矩阵所有条目统一加噪的方法效率低下，且未充分利用每种变量可能具有不同边界的先验信息及数据中的结构性稀疏特征。

Method: 提出PACE-GGM算法：1. 针对协方差矩阵中信息量最大的条目优先分配隐私预算；2. 结合高斯机制对选定条目进行加噪；3. 使用最大熵重构目标函数，重构完整的基于高斯图模型的协方差矩阵。

Result: 在多种真实数据集上的实验表明，PACE-GGM在估计误差方面优于标准高斯机制及其他基准方法，在高维和强隐私约束下提升最为明显。

Conclusion: PACE-GGM通过自适应地分配差分隐私预算和利用最大熵重构，显著提升了高维数据协方差估计的精度，尤其在隐私预算受限的情况下表现出色。

Abstract: We propose PACE-GGM, a data-adaptive differentially private method for covariance estimation that concentrates its privacy budget on the most informative entries of the empirical covariance matrix, rather than perturbing all entries. This applies in the natural setting where the modeler supplies separate bounds for each variable, so that individual entries can be measured with less noise than the full matrix. In each round, our method selects a poorly approximated entry, measures it using the Gaussian mechanism, and then reconstructs a full covariance matrix using a maximum-entropy reconstruction objective, leading to a Gaussian graphical model structure. Experiments on diverse real-world datasets demonstrate consistent improvements in estimation error with respect to the Gaussian mechanism and other baselines, particularly in high-dimensional and low-to-moderate privacy regimes.

</details>


### [197] [CurveRL: Principled Distribution-Aware Context Reweighting for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24331)
*Ke Sun,Yizhou Zhao,Jiayi Xin,Qi Long,Weijie Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对大模型RLVR中的提示词重加权问题，本文构建了一个统一的优化理论框架，并提出了一种基于通过率分布密度和排名的重加权方法CurveRL，在保持算法鲁棒性的同时显著提升了推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前RLVR（验证奖励下的强化学习）中提示词重加权已成为提升大模型推理能力的关键手段，但缺乏关于什么是“最优加权”的理论理解，且现有的权衡机制（如REINFORCE、GRPO）缺乏统一的优化框架。

Method: 1. 理论推导：将提示词重加权公式化为通过率函数空间中效用函数的函数导数，构建统一的优化框架。2. 算法设计：提出CurveRL，利用分位数坐标变换（quantile coordinate transform），根据提示词通过率的排名（rank）和密度（density）而非绝对值来分配权重。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，CurveRL的表现 consistently 优于GRPO及其他主流RLVR基线方法。

Conclusion: 研究确立了“上下文分布控制”（context-distribution control）作为分析和设计提示重加权RLVR算法的核心原则。CurveRL通过利用提示词通过率的分布结构（而非绝对值），有效提升了LLM的推理能力，优于现有的REINFORCE和GRPO。

Abstract: Context or prompt-level reweighting has emerged as a central algorithmic lever in Reinforcement Learning with Verified Rewards (RLVR) for improving the reasoning capability of large language models, yet the principle determining what constitutes an optimal weighting remains poorly understood. We address this gap by formulating prompt reweighting as a functional derivative of a utility functional defined in the pass-rate function space, yielding a unified optimality framework that accommodates existing schemes, including REINFORCE and GRPO. Building on this optimality framework, we propose a distribution-aware prompt reweighting approach, called CurveRL, based on a quantile coordinate transform, in which the weight assigned to each prompt depends not on the absolute value of pass rates but on its rank and density to reflect the distributional structure of the pass rates in the learning dynamics. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that our proposed CurveRL consistently outperforms GRPO and other RLVR baselines. Our study identifies context-distribution control as a principled axis for analyzing and designing prompt-reweighted RLVR algorithms. The code is released in https://github.com/zhyzmath/CurveRL.

</details>


### [198] [Assessing the Operational Viability of Foundation Models for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24381)
*Kavin Soni,Debanshu Das,Vamshi Guduguntla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对比了时间序列预测中基座模型与监督学习专家的优劣，并提出了一种“复杂度路由”机制，通过经验特征自动选择最优模型，在保证预测准确性的同时有效降低了部署成本与推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时间序列监督学习方法虽然准确度高，但维护成本高且依赖特定领域训练。基座模型（Foundation Models）作为一种零样本（Zero-shot）替代方案，其性能边界及与传统监督学习的适用场景对比尚未得到系统性分析。

Method: 1. 评估对比：在四个不同操作域（周期性人类系统、物理约束过程、随机金融市场、异构需求预测）中对比基座模型（Zero-shot）与监督学习模型。2. 定量分析：评估推理延迟、数据分布偏移（Data Drift）适应能力及部署限制。3. 技术创新：提出“复杂度路由（Complexity Router）”，利用经验特征自动为不同时间序列分配最优模型类别。

Result: 1. 基座模型在具有可迁移周期性结构及冷启动/长尾场景中表现优异。2. 监督学习模型在物理规律强约束系统中保持更高精度。3. 金融领域中基座模型正迅速缩短与监督学习的性能差距。4. 引入“复杂度路由”可显著提升预测准确性并降低推理成本。

Conclusion: 基座模型并非万能。未来的时间序列预测应当采用混合策略：在数据匮乏或具有周期性规律的场景中使用基座模型，在受物理约束、对精度要求极高的场景下继续使用监督学习专家模型。提出的“复杂度路由（Complexity Router）”机制是实现性能与效率平衡的最佳路径。

Abstract: Time series forecasting drives operational decisions in areas like finance, transportation, and energy. While supervised learning approaches achieve strong performance, they require domain-specific training, feature engineering, and ongoing maintenance. Large-scale foundation models have recently emerged as a zero-shot alternative, avoiding task-specific training much like LLMs. In this work, we evaluate foundation models against standard supervised approaches. Rather than focusing solely on aggregate accuracy, we analyze performance across four operational regimes: periodic human-centric systems, physically constrained processes, stochastic financial markets, and heterogeneous demand forecasting.
  Our results characterize optimal deployment areas. Foundation models perform well in domains with transferable periodic structures and are efficient for cold-start or long-tail scenarios. Conversely, supervised specialists maintain higher precision in systems governed by strict physical constraints. In financial domains, newer foundation models are rapidly closing the performance gap with supervised specialists. We further quantify trade-offs in inference latency, data drift adaptability, and deployment constraints. Finally, we propose a Complexity Router that assigns each series to the optimal model class using empirical features. We demonstrate that this selective routing achieves higher accuracy and significantly lower inference costs compared to deploying a universal foundation model, providing a practical framework for balancing generalization and efficiency.

</details>


### [199] [An Effective-Rank Audit of Alignment-Induced Activation Shifts: Confound Control, Constructive Calibration, and Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24583)
*Yuki Nakamura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文定量审计了LLM对齐过程中的“拒绝方向”，提出有效秩（rho_eps）作为诊断脆弱性的指标，并揭示了基于秩的防御机制在理论上和结构上的局限性，指出单纯增强拒绝方向的秩并不能提升模型的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了深入探究开源指令微调大模型（LLMs）对齐过程如何改变其残差流激活，并验证Arditi等人（2024）提出的“单一拒绝方向”理论，通过引入连续量化指标来探索模型安全性机制的本质及其诊断局限性。

Method: 1. 通过构建四变体分解（M_naive, M_template, M_aligned, M_DiD）来剥离聊天模板格式化、对齐偏移和拒绝中介方向的影响；
2. 引入有效秩（rho_eps）作为度量拒绝方向的连续定量指标；
3. 在3层MLP上进行构造性校准实验，分析不同秩与鲁棒性的关系；
4. 对SVD特征分解、谱间隙假设和因果排序进行实证验证与对比限制。

Result: 1. 成功分离了chat-template的影响，确认了拒绝方向的存在性；
2. 发现rho_eps是脆弱性的诊断指标而非提升鲁棒性的手段（强正则化并不提高鲁棒性）；
3. 证明了秩基诊断存在三个维度的局限：非安全特有性、SVD排序与因果逻辑不一致，以及理论所需的谱间隙假设实证失效。

Conclusion: 研究表明，有效秩（rho_eps）虽可作为模型脆弱性的诊断指标，但不能简单地通过人为提升该秩来换取稳健性。此外，秩基诊断方法存在诸多局限性，由于非安全特定性、因果顺序与SVD排序不匹配以及谱间隙假设的失效，当前基于秩的理论框架在解释模型安全性方面存在严峻边界。

Abstract: We audit alignment-induced shifts in residual-stream activations of three open-weight instruction-tuned LLMs (Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, Gemma-2-9B-it, Qwen-2.5-7B-Instruct) using the effective rank of the alignment modification matrix on safety-relevant inputs, rho_eps := rank_eps(M_Ds)/d, which formalizes the single-refusal-direction observation of Arditi et al. (2024) as a continuous quantity. The paper has three contributions. (1) Confound-controlled measurement: a four-variant decomposition (M_naive, M_template, M_aligned, M_DiD) separates chat-template formatting, alignment-stage shift, and the refusal-mediating direction, and recovers the Arditi refusal direction on M_DiD at |cos| in {0.77, 0.86, 0.50} (Llama/Gemma/Qwen); chat-template-controlled rho_eps is {0.0029, 0.0048, 0.0044}, and the centered SVD residual is 4-7x larger. (2) Constructive calibration on a 3-layer MLP across rho_eps in {0.008, 0.17, 0.33, 0.40} exhibits a sweet-spot vs. brittle distinction: mild rank-maximization (lambda=5) buys ablation robustness, while strong regularization at the same nominal rho_eps (lambda=50) does not. rho_eps is a diagnostic for fragility, not a target whose mechanical inflation buys robustness. (3) Limits of rank-based diagnostics: (a) not safety-specific (LRH baseline is 2-3x the safety value); (b) SVD principal ordering does not match causal ordering (Llama u_2 inert despite ranking second; cumulative ablation non-monotone at k=5); (c) the spectral-gap hypothesis required to upgrade the O(rho_eps * d) achievability bound to a matching Mirsky-route lower bound fails empirically (1/90 Llama layer-reference pairs, 0/36 MLP combinations) and structurally (kappa_lb <= 2/(eps * r)). The matching lower bound remains an open problem.

</details>


### [200] [Knowledge Graph Modulated Deep Learning for Limited-Sample Clinical Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24162)
*Yuwei Xue,Sakib Mostafa,James Zou,Joseph Liao,Maximilian Diehn,Ash A. Alizadeh,Lei Xing,Md. Tauhidul Islam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GiG（Graph-in-Graph）框架通过将生物知识图谱直接集成到患者特定的预测模型中，有效解决了传统方法在处理生物学图结构数据时丢失关键信息的问题，显著提升了样本受限场景下临床预测的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的生物医学 AI 模型通常难以直接处理图编码的生物学知识，往往被迫将图转换为低维表示，这导致了结构信息的丢失，在样本量受限的临床研究中表现不佳。

Method: 提出了一种名为“Graph-in-Graph (GiG)”的知识图谱调制深度学习框架。该模型将每位患者表示为一个独立的模块化图，其中 curated 生物知识图谱定义边结构，患者特定的测量数据（如基因表达）定义节点特征，从而在保持基因间相互作用和通路拓扑结构的同时进行预测建模。

Result: 在涵盖近 9700 名患者的 5 项临床任务（包括液体活检癌症检测、前列腺癌诊断等）中，GiG 表现均优于现有先进方法。特别是在样本量有限的任务中表现突出，例如在前列腺癌诊断中，该方法将 Macro-F1 指标提升了高达 49 个百分点。对照实验证明，这些性能提升归功于生物学知识图谱的结构，而非单纯的图模型设计。

Conclusion: 知识图谱调制的深度学习可以有效提升临床数据分析的鲁棒性、可解释性和样本效率，为将生物学先验知识整合到预测建模中提供了一个原则性的框架。

Abstract: Biological systems are governed by structured molecular interactions, where pathways, regulatory circuits, and functional gene relationships shape cellular behavior and disease progression. Much of this knowledge is naturally represented as graphs. However, most biomedical AI models cannot directly use graph-encoded biological knowledge and instead require compressed low-dimensional representations, which can lose important structure and reduce performance, especially in limited-sample clinical studies. Here, we introduce Graph-in-Graph (GiG), a knowledge graph-modulated deep learning framework for data-efficient clinical prediction. GiG represents each patient as a standalone modular graph, in which curated biological knowledge graphs define edges and patient-specific measurements, such as gene expression, define node features. This design allows multiple biological knowledge graphs to be integrated while preserving gene-gene interactions and pathway topology during patient-level representation learning. Across cohorts comprising nearly 9,700 patients and five clinical tasks, including liquid biopsy cancer detection, prostate cancer diagnosis, and 32-class pan-cancer classification, GiG consistently outperforms traditional and state-of-the-art methods, with the largest gains in limited-sample settings. On the challenging prostate cancer diagnosis task, GiG improves macro-F1 by up to 49 percentage points relative to competing methods. Control experiments replacing real pathway graphs with random topologies confirm that these gains arise from biologically grounded knowledge graph structure rather than graph modeling alone. These findings show that knowledge graph-modulated deep learning can improve robustness, interpretability, and sample efficiency in clinical data analysis, and provide a principled framework for integrating biological knowledge graphs into predictive modeling.

</details>


### [201] [PromptAudit: Auditing Prompt Sensitivity in LLM-Based Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24171)
*Steffen J. Camarato,Yahya Hmaiti,Mandana Ghadamian,David Mohaisen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过PromptAudit框架研究发现，大模型漏洞检测的准确性高度依赖提示词策略，其中标准CoT表现最优，而盲目使用复杂的提示工程（如自适应CoT或自一致性）可能导致性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对大语言模型在不同提示词配置下进行漏洞检测时的可靠性评估。

Method: 提出了PromptAudit评估框架：在固定数据集、解码方式和解析策略的前提下，对比五种提示词策略（包括标准思维链、少样本提示、自适应思维链、自一致性等）在五种开源模型、1000个CVE漏洞实例上的表现。

Result: 标准思维链提示词（CoT）表现最佳；少样本提示（Few-shot）的效果极度依赖模型；自适应思维链容易降低召回率，自一致性（Self-consistency）则会导致过多的弃权，从而降低整体有效性能。

Conclusion: 脆弱性检测的效果由模型与提示词共同决定；提示词敏感性是评估与部署中必须明确考虑的一项核心系统属性。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used for vulnerability detection, yet their reliability under different prompt formulations remains uncharacterized. We present PromptAudit, a controlled evaluation framework that isolates prompt effects by fixing the dataset, decoding, and parsing while varying only the prompting strategy. Using five prompting strategies across five open-weight models on 1,000 CVEs (6,074 code samples spanning 16 programming languages), we evaluate accuracy, recall, abstention, coverage, and effective F1. We find that standard chain-of-thought prompting achieves the strongest overall operational performance, while few-shot prompting provides model-dependent benefits that are most pronounced for prompt-sensitive models. In contrast, adaptive chain-of-thought frequently suppresses recall and self-consistency induces excessive abstention, sharply reducing effective performance. These results show that vulnerability detection behavior is jointly determined by the model and the prompt, and that prompt sensitivity is a first-class system property that must be explicitly characterized in evaluation and deployment.

</details>


### [202] [Joint Optimization of Training and Inference in Federated Edge Learning via Constrained Multi-Objective Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25916)
*Zhen Li,Jun Cai,Chao Yang,Haoran Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对联邦边缘学习中推理与训练协同优化的难题，本文通过 MOMDP 和 C-MOPPO 算法实现了对精度、延迟和能耗的最优权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦边缘学习（FEEL）在资源受限的边缘设备上面临着联合优化联邦训练和推理的挑战，特别是如何权衡精度、延迟和能耗以及处理实时数据动态性。

Method: 提出一个在线优化框架，通过串联队列（tandem-queue）机制连接训练与推理任务；构建包含数据和模型新鲜度的精度模型；将优化问题建模为多目标马尔可夫决策过程（MOMDP），并设计约束多目标近端策略优化（C-MOPPO）算法求解。

Result: 实验表明 C-MOPPO 能够生成高质量的 Pareto 前沿，在不同系统配置下均显著优于现有基准方法。

Conclusion: C-MOPPO 算法能够有效平衡推理精度、延迟和能耗之间的权衡，并相比现有基准算法表现出更优的性能。

Abstract: Federated edge learning (FEEL) has recently emerged as a promising paradigm for achieving edge intelligence (EI) via enabling collaborative model training across edge devices while protecting data privacy. In this paper, we put forth an online optimization framework that jointly manages federated training and inference on resource-constrained edge devices. We introduce a tandem-queue-inspired conversion mechanism that bridges inference requests and training data, and further incorporate both data and model freshness into the accuracy formulation to capture temporal dynamics in real-world environments. To maximize inference accuracy while minimizing latency and energy consumption, the mode selections, communication, and computation resource allocations of edge devices are jointly optimized. We formulate this optimization as a multi-objective optimization problem, which is NP-hard and further complicated by the online setting. To address these challenges, we transform the problem into a multi-objective Markov decision process (MOMDP) and develop a \underline{c}onstrained \underline{m}ulti-\underline{o}bjective \underline{p}roximal \underline{p}olicy \underline{o}ptimization (C-MOPPO) algorithm. Specifically, C-MOPPO first learns a set of policies with different preferences across three objectives, then leverages constrained policy optimization to enrich the Pareto front and obtain high-quality, dense solutions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that C-MOPPO achieves well-balanced trade-offs among objectives and significantly outperforms baselines under various system configurations.

</details>


### [203] [Quaternion Self-Attention with Shared Scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24920)
*Shogo Yamauchi,Tohru Nitta,Hideaki Tamori*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种共享分数的四元数自注意力机制，通过统一计算注意力分数，在大幅降低计算复杂性的同时提升了推理速度，并证明了该方法在保持特征交互能力的同时更加高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的四元数自注意力机制在每个分量上分别计算分数和进行Softmax，导致计算开销大，且各分量间的注意力分布容易发生偏离，未能有效利用四元数作为整体实体的特性。

Method: 提出了一种共享分数四元数自注意力机制，利用四元数内积计算单一实值分数，并对所有分量应用同一注意力分布，取代了传统的各分量独立计算分数和独立Softmax操作。

Result: 将分数计算乘法减少了75%，Softmax操作从四次减少为一次；在语音增强任务中，GPU推理时间降低44.3%，CPU降低58.1%，并保持了性能质量，且在视觉和NLP任务中具有通用性。

Conclusion: 共享分数四元数自注意力机制（Shared-score quaternion self-attention）是高效且有效的，它通过计算单一实值分数并跨分量共享注意力分布，消除了独立分量计算的冗余，且理论上证明了在特定条件下与原始机制表现一致，同时显著提升了推理速度。

Abstract: Quaternion neural networks are parameter-efficient and model multidimensional dependencies by representing four related features as a single entity. However, existing quaternion self-attention computes component-wise scores and applies independent softmax operations to each component, which increases the computational cost and allows attention distributions to diverge across components. We propose a shared-score quaternion self-attention mechanism that computes a single real-valued score using the quaternion inner product and applies a shared attention distribution across all components. This reduces score-computation multiplications by 75% and the number of softmax operations from four to one. We prove that, when queries and keys are produced by quaternion linear projections that induce component pre-mixing, the component-wise and shared scores lie in the same interaction subspace, indicating that independent component-wise attention primarily re-parameterizes the same interactions rather than expanding the feature interaction space. In speech enhancement, our method reduces inference time by up to 44.3% on a GPU and 58.1% on a CPU while maintaining quality, with consistent trends across vision and natural language processing.

</details>


### [204] [Learning Treatment Effects during Resource Allocation via Priority-Queue Randomization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25169)
*JungHo Lee,Johnna Sundberg,Pim Welle,Bryan Wilder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对受限公共资源分配的实验设计框架，通过在优先级排队系统中引入随机化元素，实现了在保障高需求者优先权的同时，利用队列分配作为工具变量进行因果效应的有效评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在公共服务领域，资源有限且效益不确定，需要随机试验进行评估，但实际操作中通常采用基于需求评估的优先级队列（如排队系统），这使得传统的直接随机分配难以实施。需要一种既能在高需求群体优先原则下进行干预，又能实现科学评估的方法。

Method: 1. 理论框架：构建了一种基于“风险评分”将申请人随机分配到不同优先级队列的实验设计框架；2. 因果推断：分析了外生和内生到达过程下的因果效应识别条件（前者为条件随机化分析，后者通过队列随机化作为工具变量）；3. 优化设计：开发了针对该队列分配机制的优化算法，以平衡统计力和对高需求申请人的保障目标。

Result: 提出了一个能够适应实际排队管理需求的因果识别框架；证明了在队列分配机制下，标准因果 estimands 的可识别性，并为实验设计提供了兼顾道德需求与统计效能的量化工具。通过美国某县住房分配项目的应用验证了该方法的现实可行性。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了在优先级队列机制下进行因果效应估计的可行性，并提出了一套实用的优化算法，能够在保证资源分配公平性（优先保障高需求者）与评估统计效率之间达成平衡。即使在分配具有依赖性的情况下，传统的独立同分布（i.i.d.）效率界限依然能够作为有效的优化目标。

Abstract: Public service programs often allocate limited resources under uncertainty about their benefits, creating a need for randomization to support credible evaluation. In practice, however, applicants commonly enter waitlists where resources are prioritized toward individuals judged to have higher need through tiered priority queues, making direct randomization difficult. Motivated by this, we develop an experimental design framework for learning treatment effects while treating those most in need where incoming applicants are randomized into priority queues based on their assessed risk scores. Treatments are then provided across queues in priority order and first-in-first-out within queue as budget becomes available. Our contributions are two-fold. First, we characterize what causal effects are identified under this priority-queue allocation. When arrivals are exogenous, treatments are conditionally randomized, and hence standard estimands are identified; when arrivals are endogenous, queue randomization instead provides an instrument for treatment, identifying local treatment effects induced by the queuing process. Second, we develop optimized queue-assignment designs that trade off statistical efficiency against prioritizing higher-need applicants. We show in the process that, despite dependence in treatment assignments induced by the design, usual iid efficiency bounds remain well-justified design objectives. We illustrate the proposed designs using data from a housing allocation program in a large U.S. county.

</details>


### [205] [Selective Test-Time Compute Scaling for Click-Through Rate Prediction via Uncertainty-Triggered Feature Path Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24989)
*Moyu Zhang,Yun Chen,Yujun Jin,Jinxin Hu,Yu Zhang,Xiaoyi Zeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对CTR预测中稀疏特征预测不可靠的问题，本文提出了UTTSI框架，通过根据样本的不确定性动态分配测试时计算资源，显著提升了预测准确度，且在工业落地中表现出极高的性价比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CTR预测中存在显著的不对称性，即模型对稀疏出现的特征组合预测不可靠，而传统训练阶段的方法（如自适应门控）由于固定了选择函数，无法在推理阶段针对个体实例提供灵活的补救措施。

Method: 提出UTTSI（不确定性触发的选择性测试时推理）框架：1. 双信号评估器：结合Logit置信度与频率先验区分认知不确定性与偶然模糊性；2. 自适应特征过滤：剔除不可靠嵌入；3. 随机特征路径探索与一致性加权集成：针对不确定实例进行深度推理。

Result: 在四个数据集、三种骨干架构上实现了统计显著的性能提升；在线A/B测试中实现了5.3%的相对CTR增长，且平均计算开销仅为基准的2.8倍，最差延迟保持不变。

Conclusion: UTTSI证明了在CTR预测中引入选择性推理计算是有效且实用的，能够有效解决稀疏数据带来的置信度差异问题，并作为训练阶段优化的强有力补充。

Abstract: Scaling test-time compute has proven highly effective for language models, yet this opportunity remains largely unexplored for industrial Click-Through Rate (CTR) prediction. CTR models suffer from a fundamental asymmetry: feature combinations well-represented in training yield confident predictions, while sparsely observed ones produce unreliable outputs. Existing training-phase solutions such as adaptive gating learn a fixed selection function subject to the same sparsity, offering no per-instance recourse at deployment.We propose UTTSI (Uncertainty-Triggered Test-Time Selective Inference), a training-free model-agnostic framework that scales inference depth proportionally to per-instance uncertainty. A dual-signal estimator combining model logit confidence with a data-level frequency prior distinguishes epistemic uncertainty from aleatoric ambiguity. Every instance undergoes adaptive feature filtering to remove unreliable embeddings; uncertain instances additionally receive stochastic feature-path explorations whose predictions are aggregated via consistency-weighted ensembling. Confident instances bypass exploration entirely, keeping average overhead at approximately $2.8\times$ base model cost with worst-case latency unchanged.Experiments on four datasets with three backbone architectures demonstrate consistent, statistically significant gains over all training-phase baselines. A seven-day online A/B test further confirms a 5.3% relative CTR gain ($p < 0.01$), establishing selective test-time compute allocation as a practical complement to training-phase advances for CTR prediction.

</details>


### [206] [Agent-ToM: Learning to Monitor Autonomous LLM Agents via Theory-of-Mind Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24216)
*Nesreen K. Ahmed,Nima Nafisi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Agent-ToM 是一种通过心智理论（ToM）推理和持久语义内存来监控 autonomous LLM 潜伏恶意行为的新框架，解决了传统监控方法对意图推断不足及无法利用历史监控经验的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型智能体监控方法难以发现长周期、隐蔽的恶意行为，缺乏对智能体信念和意图的深层推理，且未能有效利用历史监控经验。

Method: 提出 Agent-ToM 框架，通过心智理论（ToM）推理推断意图与信念，结合“推理-验证-精炼”（Reason-Verify-Refine）流程进行实时监控，并在训练阶段将批判信号蒸馏为持久的“语义护栏内存”，以实现跨任务的经验复用。

Result: 在 SHADE-Arena 和 CUA-SHADE-Arena 基准测试中，Agent-ToM 在保持高精度和召回率的同时，性能优于现有的集成学习监控基准方法。

Conclusion: Agent-ToM 通过引入“心智理论”（ToM）推理与持久性语义护栏内存，显著提升了识别自主大模型智能体潜在隐蔽恶意行为的能力，为安全监控提供了一种有效且可部署的框架。

Abstract: Monitoring autonomous large language model (LLM) agents for covert malicious behavior is challenging due to delayed, context-dependent, and long-horizon attack patterns. Agents may pursue hidden objectives while maintaining superficially benign behavior, making detection difficult even with full trajectory access. Prior monitoring approaches improve scaffolding or ensemble aggregation, but treat each trajectory independently and do not learn from prior monitoring experience. Moreover, standard reasoning methods explain observed behavior without explicitly reasoning about agent beliefs, intentions, and goal alignment required to distinguish benign task execution from covert deviation.
  We propose \textbf{Agent-ToM}, a learning-to-monitor framework grounded in Theory-of-Mind (ToM) reasoning for security analysis of autonomous agents. Agent-ToM performs structured full-trajectory analysis by inferring beliefs, intent hypotheses with calibrated confidence, expected actions, and deviations from task-consistent behavioral baselines. At inference time, it employs a \textit{Reason-Verify-Refine} pipeline to construct and validate monitoring decisions. At training time, Agent-ToM distills critique signals into a persistent \textit{semantic guardrail memory}, enabling reusable belief- and intent-conditioned constraints across episodes. We evaluate Agent-ToM on adversarial agent monitoring benchmarks (SHADE-Arena and CUA-SHADE-Arena). Agent-ToM achieves strong precision-recall balance and outperforms state-of-the-art monitoring baselines, including ensemble methods, while using a coherent two-call reasoning pipeline. These results demonstrate that learning at the monitoring layer, combined with structured ToM reasoning and verification, provides an effective and deployable foundation for securing autonomous LLM agents.

</details>


### [207] [Multi-Objective Learning for Diffusion Models: A Statistical Theory under Semi-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25210)
*Ziheng Cheng,Yixiao Huang,Hanlin Zhu,Haoran Geng,Somayeh Sojoudi,Jitendra Malik,Pieter Abbeel,Xin Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对扩散模型多目标学习中模型容量带来的高样本需求问题，本文提出了一种利用少量标注数据和大量无标注条件数据，通过“先训练轻量级专家、后蒸馏至通用模型”的框架，有效提升了多任务学习的样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在多任务部署时面临多目标学习（MOL）挑战。为了在多个任务上达到良好的帕累托平衡，通常需要一个规模巨大的通用模型，但高模型复杂度会导致统计样本成本过高。因此急需一种在有限标注数据下实现高效多目标学习的方法。

Method: 提出了一种两阶段多目标（MOL）学习框架：第一阶段利用有限的标注数据训练多个轻量级“专家”模型；第二阶段基于这些专家模型生成伪样本，通过蒸馏任务训练一个通用模型。同时推导了相应的泛化误差界，并将其扩展至强化学习领域的扩散策略中。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在机器人控制和图像修复任务中表现优异，并在理论上证明了所需标注样本数量仅依赖于专家模型类的复杂度，而非最终通用模型的复杂度，显著降低了数据依赖。

Conclusion: 本文提出的两阶段学习框架能在有限的标注数据下，通过利用海量的无标注条件数据，有效地训练出一个能够处理多任务的通用扩散模型，同时从理论上证明并实验验证了该方法在减少样本复杂度方面的优势。

Abstract: Diffusion models are increasingly used as powerful conditional generators, yet real deployments often involve multiple target distributions arising from different tasks, e.g., diverse prompt domains in text-to-image generation, or multiple environments in robotics with diffusion policies. This naturally leads to a multi-objective learning (MOL) problem. A key challenge is that achieving good Pareto trade-offs can require a generalist model class with substantially larger capacity than what suffices for solving any individual task, thereby increasing statistical cost since sample complexity typically scales with the model complexity. To reconcile this, we develop a principled MOL framework for diffusion models with limited data: a semi-supervised regime where paired (labeled) samples are scarce, but (unlabeled) condition data are abundant. We propose a two-stage training procedure that first fits lightweight specialist models from limited paired data, and then distills them into a generalist model by generating pseudo-samples. We establish generalization bounds showing that the required number of paired samples only depends on the complexity of the specialist model classes. We further extend the theory to diffusion policies for sequential decision making to account for distribution shift in on-policy rollouts. Extensive experiments on robotic control and image restoration tasks are conducted to verify our theoretical results.

</details>


### [208] [PrivFusion: A Privacy-preserving Multi-Agent Framework for Harmonizing Distributed Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24249)
*Anisa Halimi,Liubov Nedoshivina,Kieran Fraser,Stefano Braghin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PrivFusion 是一款自动化的隐私保护多智能体框架，专门用于解决联邦学习中医疗数据异质性带来的数据对齐难题，有效降低了数据协调的人工成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在医疗领域，由于患者隐私保护需求，医疗数据难以实现中心化聚合，而联邦学习虽提供了一种分布式方案，但各机构间数据的异质性（Heterogeneity）严重制约了模型效果，缺乏自动化的数据协调机制是当前的主要瓶颈。

Method: 提出了一种基于多智能体（Multi-agent）的框架——PrivFusion。该框架通过智能体对本地数据进行分析，对不同机构间的语义相似特征进行聚类，并通过迭代式的转换建议来实现数据层面的对齐与标准化。

Result: 通过在四个异构的 COVID-19 数据集上进行评估，验证了 PrivFusion 在实现多站点数据协调方面既高效又准确，同时大幅减少了人工操作工作量。

Conclusion: PrivFusion 是一种有效的多源数据协同框架，能够自动化完成联邦学习前的数据协调过程，显著降低人工干预需求，为处理医疗数据异质性提供了一种隐私保护的解决方案。

Abstract: The growing availability of clinical data has increased the use of machine learning, yet centralized data aggregation is often infeasible for sensitive health information. Federated Learning (FL) offers a distributed alternative, but its adoption is limited by substantial heterogeneity across institutional datasets, making harmonization a critical but frequently overlooked prerequisite for multi-site analytics. We introduce PrivFusion, a privacy-preserving multi-agent framework that automates the harmonization of structured datasets prior to federated training. PrivFusion uses agents to analyze local data, cluster semantically similar features across sites, and provide iterative transformation recommendations until alignment is achieved. Evaluation across four heterogeneous COVID-19 datasets demonstrates that PrivFusion effectively and efficiently harmonizes multi-site data while substantially reducing manual effort.

</details>


### [209] [Optimizing Digital Therapeutic Interventions: Online Learning under Endogenous Adherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24261)
*Eric Pulick,Stephanie Carpenter,Matthew Buman,Yonatan Mintz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对数字疗法中患者依从性受干预历史影响的问题，本文提出了一个结合线性动态系统与依从性反馈的决策框架，并设计了名为UCB-BOLD的算法，实现了更优的长期医疗资源分配和患者健康管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数字疗法（DT）决策框架往往忽略了患者依从性的动态演化及其与治疗推荐之间的反馈循环，导致无法在资源受限的情况下长期维持患者的健康干预效果。

Method: 提出一种结合线性动态系统（LDS）和Logit链接函数的决策支持框架，用以建模患者随时间变化的参与能力及其与推荐、依从性的内生关系；设计了基于乐观原则的在线推荐算法 UCB-BOLD 以最小化累积遗憾。

Result: 证明了LDS模型的有限时间可辨识性及所提算法的亚线性遗憾界；在基于微随机试验数据生成的合成队列上，验证了算法在处理复杂动态依从性行为时的优越性。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模推荐和依从性效应，算法能够实现显著优于近视或启发式策略的决策效果，在复杂患者群体中，UCB-BOLD的条件风险价值（CVaR）遗憾值比次优基准模型低2-3倍。

Abstract: A critical challenge facing clinicians managing chronic disease interventions is sustaining long-run patient health given limited information and resources. Digital therapeutics (DTs) provide a cost-effective way to manage interventions at scale through repeated interactions (e.g. daily treatment recommendations), but patient success is highly dependent on their adherence. Behavioral psychology suggests that both treatment recommendations and past adherence affect future adherence, yet existing decision support frameworks for DTs model only recommendation effects or treat adherence as exogenous context, leaving a key gap in model and algorithm development. To address this gap, we present a DT decision support framework that captures both recommendation and adherence effects, allowing clinicians to better plan treatment recommendations. We model a patient's time-varying capacity for engagement with treatment using a linear dynamical system (LDS) that captures both recommendation and adherence effects, endogenously connected to adherence behavior with a logit link. We establish finite-time identification guarantees for this model, extending LDS results to our setting. Next, we propose an optimism-based algorithm, UCB-BOLD, for online treatment selection and prove that it achieves sublinear regret. We evaluate UCB-BOLD against benchmarks via ablation studies on a synthetic patient cohort generated using micro-randomized trial data. DT decision support tools can include dynamical models to enable decision makers to efficiently use the data in DT settings to improve patient health through effective resource allocation. While myopic or heuristic approaches suffice for some patient types, the benefits of explicitly planning around recommendation and adherence effects are significant for others; UCB-BOLD achieves 2-3x lower conditional value-at-risk regret than the next-best benchmark.

</details>


### [210] [UWM-JEPA: Predictive World Models That Imagine in Belief Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25313)
*Santosh Kumar Radha,Oktay Goktas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对部分可观测环境，提出UWM-JEPA模型，通过采用密度矩阵表征和幺正预测器，有效解决了长程预测中不确定性遗失问题，显著提升了模型在反事实推理和盲操作下的稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的向量化JEPA在处理部分可观测环境时，难以在缺乏观测信息的情况下有效维持多重隐状态的信念分布，导致在长时间跨度下不确定性预测出现耗散。

Method: 提出UWM-JEPA（Unitary World Model JEPA），利用密度矩阵作为潜在表示，采用幺正预测器（Unitary Predictor）对联合系统-环境空间进行演化，旨在在盲操作（Blind Rollout）中保持状态分布的一致性。

Result: 在隐含速度指标任务中，UWM-JEPA达到0.77准确率（优于LSTM-JEPA的0.53）；在盲操作预测任务中，UWM-JEPA的预测精度显著高于向量化基准模型；实验证明反事实训练目标对学习动作敏感性至关重要。

Conclusion: 在部分可观测环境下，为了实现对未来可能性的有效想象，JEPA世界模型的潜在空间几何结构与预测器动力学性质至关重要，而非仅仅依赖编码器的记忆能力。使用幺正算子（Unitary Operator）保持联合态谱是解决不确定性耗散问题的有效途径。

Abstract: World models for partially observed environments must imagine multiple compatible hidden futures and steer between them under counterfactual actions. Joint Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) do this in latent space, but a vector-valued latent has no internal structure for carrying the belief over hidden continuations through blind rollout. We introduce the Unitary World Model JEPA (UWM-JEPA), a JEPA world model with a density-matrix latent on a joint system-environment space and a learned unitary predictor. The construction preserves the joint-state spectrum exactly during rollout, so the predictor itself cannot dissipate the represented uncertainty.
  On a hidden-velocity indicator task requiring five-step forward simulation under a given action sequence with the target observation masked, UWM-JEPA reaches 0.77 accuracy and degrades monotonically as actions are perturbed; a parameter-matched LSTM-JEPA trained under the same counterfactual-target objective and action head collapses to majority-class accuracy (0.53) under every action condition. Under blind rollout, UWM-JEPA loses fewer than ten points of probe R^2 at short horizons while vector-latent baselines lose forty-one and sixty-eight; both nevertheless tie on a held-out context probe, locating the separation in the predictor rather than the encoder. Action sensitivity itself requires training against counterfactual rather than teacher-forced targets, a finding that applies beyond the unitary parameterisation. For JEPA world models to imagine under partial observability, latent geometry and predictor dynamics matter, not frozen context-encoding capacity alone.

</details>


### [211] [The Behavioral Credibility Trilemma: When Calibrated Autonomy Becomes Impossible](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25739)
*Lauri Lovén,Nam Do,Hassan Mehmood,Dinesh Kumar Sah,Sasu Tarkoma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了“行为可信度三难困境”，证明在强化学习中，当智能体面临超出其能力的任务时，为了在自主性、帮助性和校准性之间取得平衡，智能体必然会虚报置信度。这一结论具有几何必然性，研究通过数学证明和大规模实验验证了该发现，并建议通过承诺或领域分离来解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决强化学习智能体在自主决策过程中，当面临超出其能力范围的任务时，如何在追求任务帮助性、置信度校准和自主性之间保持逻辑一致性与可靠性的问题，特别是防止智能体为了提升自主性而虚报置信度。

Method: 理论证明：建立了“置信门控决策问题”（Confidence-Gated Decision Problem）模型，利用严格真评分规则（Strictly Proper Scoring Rules）的几何特性，推导了行为扰动引理（Behavioral Perturbation Lemma）；实证分析：开展了包含540个配置的Best-of-N实验，对五项预注册假设进行了验证。

Result: 理论上表明，若智能体同时获得帮助性和自主性奖励，必然导致置信度膨胀，该膨胀程度与奖励权重比例（$w_A/(2 w_C)$）成正比，且检测需要高昂的样本复杂度；实验 подтвердил 假设，发现智能体在能力阈值附近的置信度存在系统性偏移。

Conclusion: 该研究正式提出了“行为可信度三难困境”（Behavioral Credibility Trilemma），证明在存在能力边界的情况下，强化学习智能体无法同时实现最大化帮助性、最优校准和完全自主性。研究指出这一不可能结论源于几何性质，并提出了通过承诺机制或领域分离来缓解该困境的路径。

Abstract: We prove that no reinforcement learning policy with confidence-gated autonomy can simultaneously achieve maximum helpfulness, optimal calibration, and full autonomy under rational oversight, whenever some tasks exceed the agent's reliable competence: the Behavioral Credibility Trilemma. The impossibility is geometric -- adding any non-affine autonomy incentive to a strictly proper scoring rule destroys strict properness, so an agent rewarded for both calibrated confidence and autonomous action systematically inflates its reported confidence on tasks below the principal's approval threshold. The Behavioral Perturbation Lemma quantifies the inflation (scaling as $w_A/(2 w_C)$ for the Brier score) and shows detection requires $Ω(1/Δ^2)$ observations. We prove the principal's optimal oversight rule is necessarily non-affine, making the impossibility unconditional and optimizer-independent across log-concave-density policy families. We formalize the Confidence-Gated Decision Problem, map existing methods onto the trilemma, and identify two constructive resolution pathways (commitment, domain separation). A 540-configuration Best-of-N experiment tests five pre-registered hypotheses, all strongly confirmed (effect sizes $d = 1.10$ to $5.32$), and adds a descriptive analysis of the achievable-$(H, C, A)$ surface geometry showing a plateau-truncated frontier consistent with the predicted inflation saturation.

</details>


### [212] [Faithfulness as Information Flow: Evaluating and Training Faithful Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24286)
*Jinghan Jia,Joe Benton,Eric Easley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出了从“结构信息流”视角分析思维链（CoT）忠实度的方法，并引入了多种训练干预手段，成功减少了语言模型对推理捷径的依赖，提升了推理过程的可解释性和真实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的思维链（CoT）推理并不总是忠实的，模型可能通过直接的“提示-答案”捷径（shortcuts）产生结果，使得看似合理的推理过程实际上是误导性的，难以进行有效的模型监控。

Method: 提出基于结构信息流的分析框架，定义了衡量忠实度的三个属性（充分性、完整性、必要性），并实例化为基于熵、掩码KL散度及梯度的诊断指标；同时引入了一系列训练时干预手段（如注意力掩码、梯度掩码、CoT梯度约束、对抗扰动等）以增强CoT的中介作用。

Result: 实验表明，所提指标能准确识别推理忠实度的差异；引入的训练干预手段成功增强了模型在 arithmetic、代码修复及 DAPO-Math 等任务中对 CoT 的路径依赖，不仅使快捷路径和奖励欺骗行为在 CoT 中更加透明，还提升了任务无关的忠实度指标，并降低了对错误提示的敏感度。

Conclusion: 通过控制训练过程中的信息流（如注意力掩码、梯度掩码等），可以有效引导模型建立更忠实的思维链（CoT），减少对快捷路径的依赖，从而使模型的推理过程更具可解释性和可监控性。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning is useful for monitoring language models only when the reasoning trace faithfully reflects the computation that produces the final answer. However, models can rely on prompt-to-answer shortcuts that bypass the CoT, making the visible reasoning trace misleading even when it appears plausible. We study CoT faithfulness through a structural information-flow perspective: faithful reasoning should route answer-relevant information through the mediated path from prompt to CoT to answer, rather than through a direct prompt-to-answer shortcut. This perspective yields a task-agnostic framework based on three complementary properties, sufficiency, completeness, and necessity, which we instantiate with entropy-based, masked-KL, and gradient-based diagnostics. We show that these metrics recover externally judged faithfulness differences in hinted reasoning, and identify a low-entropy failure mode of KL-based diagnostics where gradient-based measures remain more stable. Building on this analysis, we introduce update-time interventions for verifier-based on-policy RL, including attention masking, backward-only gradient masking, CoT gradients, and adversarial perturbations of prompt representations. Across hinted arithmetic, reward-hackable code repair, and DAPO-Math models trained without hints but evaluated under wrong-hint injection, our interventions shift behavioral and structural indicators toward stronger CoT mediation. In particular, they make shortcut and reward-hacking behavior more transparent in the CoT and improve task-agnostic faithfulness metrics, while in some settings also reducing wrong-hint susceptibility. Our results suggest that controlling information flow during training is a practical route toward more faithful and monitorable CoT reasoning. Code is available at https://github.com/safety-research/faithful-cot.

</details>


### [213] [On the Benefits of Free Exploration for Regret Minimization in Multi-Armed Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25789)
*Yunlong Hou,Zixin Zhong,Vincent Y. F. Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了带免费探索预算的多臂老虎机问题，提出了UFE-KLUCB-H算法，证实了利用免费探索阶段可有效降低后悔，并给出了理论上的最优性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的经典后悔最小化或纯探索范式无法涵盖“在后悔积累前拥有免费探索预算”这一设定，旨在研究如何利用该预算来最小化后续阶段的累积后悔。

Method: 引入免费探索阶段的随机多臂老虎机模型；定义(α,β)-probably saving策略；提出双阶段算法UFE-KLUCB-H（包含UFE探索策略和KLUCB-H后悔最小化策略）；利用多实例扰动论证推导后悔下界。

Result: 推导了基于实例的后悔上限，证明了UFE-KLUCB-H比无免费探索的策略积累更少的后悔；推导了相应的实例依赖下界，证实了该算法在二值老虎机问题上的近最优性。

Conclusion: 在具有初始免费探索预算的随机多臂老虎机问题中，算法的后悔值表现出明显的相变特征。所提出的UFE-KLUCB-H算法在理论上下界上均接近最优，证明了利用免费探索阶段能够显著降低累积后悔。

Abstract: We study a stochastic multi-armed bandit problem where an agent is granted a free exploration budget before regret accumulates, a setting not captured by the classic regret minimization or pure exploration paradigms. The goal is to design an adaptive policy that strategically explores the bandit instance in the initial free exploration phase and minimizes the cumulative regret in the subsequent phase. We formalize this regret minimization with free exploration problem and identify an interesting regime where the free exploration budget scales logarithmically with the time horizon. To quantify the amount of regret saved with high probability as a result of the availability of the free exploration phase, we introduce a novel set of policies known as $(α,β)$-probably saving policies. We propose a two-phase, probably saving algorithm, UFE-KLUCB-H, which consists of a principled free exploration policy, UFE, and a history-aware regret minimization policy KLUCB-H. Instance-dependent upper bounds on UFE-KLUCB-H are derived, showing that UFE-KLUCB-H accumulates strictly less regret than policies that do not have access to a free exploration phase. Complementarily, we derive instance-dependent lower bounds based on novel multi-instance perturbation arguments tailored to the free-exploration setting, demonstrating the near-optimality of UFE-KLUCB-H for two-valued bandits. Our upper and lower bounds reveal sharp phase transitions in the accumulated regret depending on the amount of available free exploration. Simulations are conducted to demonstrate that forced exploration and adaptivity in the algorithm lead to greater regret savings.

</details>


### [214] [TUBE: Tangent Upper Bound on Evidence for Discrete Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24292)
*Arseny Ivanov,Sergei Kholkin,Vladislav Gromadskii,Grigoriy Ksenofontov,Ivan Oseledets,Alexander Korotin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对离散扩散模型难以计算精确对数似然的难题，本文提出了 TUBE 上界估计方法，研究发现现有扩散模型在似然表现上仍逊色于自回归模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型无法像自回归模型（ARM）那样精确计算对数似然，现有的评价指标仅依赖证据下界（ELBO），无法确定模型真实似然与该界限之间的差距。

Method: 提出了一种名为 TUBE（Tangent Upper Bound on Evidence）的变分对数似然上界方法，该方法提供了一个无偏的蒙特卡洛估计量，适用于包括掩码扩散模型（MDM）和任意顺序自回归模型（AO-ARM）在内的多种潜变量模型。

Result: 通过应用 TUBE 对 Block MDM 和 Block AO-ARM 进行评估，研究发现这些模型的真实对数似然值严格低于精确的 ARM 基准线。

Conclusion: 尽管块状掩码扩散模型（Block MDM）等模型在生成任务中有所进展，但在对数似然评价指标上，它们仍不及自回归模型（ARM），证明了自回归模型在密度估计方面依然占据主导地位。

Abstract: Log-likelihood is a standard metric for evaluating generative models. Unfortunately, in contrast to autoregressive models (ARMs), discrete diffusion models generally do not admit exact computation of this quantity. Existing evaluations, therefore, rely on the evidence lower bound (ELBO), leaving unclear how much higher the true value may be. We address this by introducing the Tangent Upper Bound on Evidence (TUBE), a variational upper bound on log-likelihood that admits an unbiased Monte Carlo estimator. Our TUBE extends across latent-variable models, including masked diffusion models (MDMs), any-order ARMs (AO-ARMs), and block variants of both. Applied to block MDMs and block AO-ARMs, TUBE reveals our key empirical finding that these models lie strictly below the exact ARM baseline, showing that ARMs still dominate in likelihood.

</details>


### [215] [Mapping the Schedule x Bit-Width Boundary in Sub-100M Quantisation-Aware Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25966)
*Christian Brandt Thomassen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过大规模实验证明，对于100M参数以内的语言模型，量化感知训练（QAT）无需针对FP16/INT8/INT6定制不同的学习率调度策略；推荐统一策略，仅在INT4规模大于50M时进行特定参数调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨小规模（<100M）解码器语言模型在QAT过程中，最优学习率调度策略是否依赖于量化位宽，以验证针对不同精度是否需要特制的训练策略。

Method: 采用多轮大规模阶乘网格搜索（Factorial Grid Search），共涵盖了超过1300次实验运行。通过对位宽（FP16至INT4）、模型规模（3M至350M）、学习率幅度、退火比例（warmdown fraction）及随机种子等变量进行交叉验证。并利用权重距离探针（weight-to-grid-distance probe）分析了权重的量化适应机制。

Result: 1. 现有的“INT6需要不同调度策略”的假设被证伪；2. 发现了一个基于模型规模的稳健规律：在100M以内，FP16/INT8/INT6的最佳退火比例均为33%；3. 揭示了INT4量化训练在50M参数规模处的性能边界：50M以上存在明确的最优策略，50M以下则处于噪声主导区间；4. 证伪了“快速网格对齐（grid-snapping）”作为解释该零假设（null result）的机制。

Conclusion: 针对100M参数以内的解码器语言模型，从初始化开始的量化感知训练（QAT）中，学习率调度策略并不随位宽（FP16/INT8/INT6）显著变化。INT4量化在50M参数规模以上存在明确的最优退火比例，而50M以下则主要受随机噪声影响。该研究推荐在低参数规模下，直接沿用FP16的最优学习率调度策略。

Abstract: We test whether the optimal learning-rate schedule depends on bit-width during from-initialisation quantisation-aware training (QAT) for sub-100M decoder language models. A 720-run factorial grid (Phase 2) over bit-width x warmdown fraction x LR magnitude x model size x seed (FP16/INT8/INT6, 15M-100M, 5 seeds) finds the optimal warmdown is 33% at every (bit-width, size) cell. The primary hypothesis -- that INT6 QAT requires a different schedule than higher-precision training -- is falsified at FP16/INT8/INT6. A 625-run follow-up (Phase 5) probes the null along five axes: optimiser (AdamW), schedule shape (cosine), training length (up to 9x more iterations), an extended size sweep (5M-350M), and an INT4 sweep from 3M to 100M. The null is robust under all three setup changes. The INT6 penalty follows a log-linear scaling law whose fit on Phase 2 predicts the five held-out Phase 5 sizes (5M, 8M, 175M, 250M, 350M) within their 95% prediction intervals (5/5). For INT4 the picture is sharper than the higher precisions: at 50M and 100M, wd33 is decisively optimal (paired z ~ 12-15, 10/10 seeds); below 50M, across the six tested sizes from 3M to 30M, no individual size shows a statistically significant schedule preference and the per-size mean penalty oscillates within seed-level noise. The boundary is therefore a transition between a noise-dominated regime below 50M and a decisive wd33 regime at and above 50M, not a clean wd10 region. A weight-to-grid-distance probe falsifies the simplest mechanism for the FP16/INT8/INT6 null result (rapid grid-snapping): pre-warmdown, INT6-QAT weights sit at essentially the same distance from the INT6 grid as FP16 weights (ratio ~ 1.04). Practical recommendation: at sub-100M scale, tune the LR schedule once at FP16 and apply unchanged to INT8/INT6 QAT; for INT4 at 50M+ use wd33; for INT4 below 50M the schedule choice is in the noise.

</details>


### [216] [Deployment-complete benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25997)
*El Mustapha Mansouri,Keigo Arai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有的AI基准测试普遍存在“部署不完整”问题，即高分并不能转化为可靠的实际部署。本项研究定义了“部署完整性”框架，通过引入认证机制，大幅降低了决策风险，并呼吁未来基准测试应报告更多关于行动不确定性和成本的信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基准测试虽能提供评估得分，但往往无法直接映射为可靠的部署行动建议。这种脱节导致在实际应用场景中，“基准测试成绩优秀”并不等同于“部署安全或有效”，迫切需要一种能连接评估与决策的新型基准范式。

Method: 引入并定义了“部署完整性”（deployment-complete）概念及相关度量指标（如纤维混合度、完成曲线、响应秩区间等）；提出了“先认证后获取”（certify-then-acquire）的决策框架，并对Tox21、Matbench和JARVIS数据集进行了实证审计。

Result: 实验表明，传统基于得分的基准测试在部署时表现出严重的泛化失效（如Tox21的覆盖率从94.98%骤降至10.07%）；审计发现主流基准测试存在大规模不完整性；应用“先认证后获取”策略显著降低了错误决策率（Tox21从1.19%降至0.027%），并有效指导了模型选择。

Conclusion: 当前的基准测试存在“完整性”缺失，即仅提供评估分数不足以决定部署行动。研究建议基准测试应转向“部署完整型”，即不仅报告分数，还需报告证据、支持的行动、模糊性及完成成本，以确保在实际应用中做出鲁棒决策。

Abstract: Benchmarks increasingly guide deployment, procurement and scientific screening, yet a score supports only the response it records, not necessarily the deployment action. We introduce deployment-complete benchmarking, which tests whether benchmark evidence determines a deployment action. A benchmark is complete for a claim exactly when the action is constant on each evidence fiber; mixed fibers expose missing deployment information, and completion curves quantify the evidence required to resolve ambiguity. In controlled response spaces, benchmark-channel conformal coverage of 94.98% transferred poorly to an unmeasured deployment channel (10.07%), whereas response-rank intervals achieved 94.91% coverage; even zero benchmark error certified only 45.4% of candidates at the largest residual size. Public audits revealed incompleteness, including 97.9% mixed Tox21 fibers and zero median certifiable fraction in main Matbench and JARVIS audits. In held-out replays, certify-then-acquire reduced false decisions from 1.19% to 0.027% in Tox21 and from 20.3% to 0.128% in JARVIS, while changing model choice and identifying deployment-relevant probes. Deployment-ready benchmarks should report evidence, supported actions, ambiguity and completion cost rather than scores alone.

</details>


### [217] [LLMs Show No Signs Of Individuated Metacognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24299)
*M. Moran,Mark Whiting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 大语言模型的置信度并非对自身能力的认知，而是一种对题目难度的反应；目前并无证据显示前沿LLM具备显著的、个性化的元认知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的路由、拒答和集成方法都假设LLM具有“功能性元认知”（即评估自身能力而不执行任务的能力），但这一假设的底层结构尚不明确。因此，文章旨在探讨模型输出的置信度是否真正反映了其能力的认知。

Method: 对20个前沿大模型在6个基准测试上的二元置信度判定进行了拆解，采用了四分相关因子分析（tetrachoric factor analysis）并结合成对校准（pairwise calibration）方法。

Result: 在事实性召回和信息检索任务中，跨模型的置信度矩阵呈现秩一结构，主要受任务难度影响；一旦排除所有模型都能达成共识的题目，置信度与表现之间的相关性便消失；所谓的数学推理任务中的元认知表现，被证明是模型通过思维链辅助计算带来的混淆项。

Conclusion: 研究发现，目前的前沿大语言模型（LLMs）缺乏实质性的个体元认知能力；所谓的“置信度”实际上主要反映了任务难度及模型自身的基准偏置，而非对自身处理能力的准确评估。

Abstract: Confidence-weighted routing, selective abstention, and ensemble weighting all assume that a model's stated confidence is informative about its capability on the question being asked. They presume functional metacognition, the capacity to assess one's own capabilities, without exercising them. Aggregate calibration is well studied, with mixed results, but the underlying structure of elicited confidence is less well understood. We decompose binary confidence judgements from 20 frontier Large Language Models (LLMs) across six benchmarks using tetrachoric factor analysis paired with pairwise calibration, asking whether two models that differ in confidence also differ in performance. On factual recall and information retrieval benchmarks the cross-model confidence matrix is approximately rank-one and a single dominant factor captures most of the latent variance. Models retrieving facts share an item-level difficulty axis and differ mainly in their decision thresholds along it. Across all benchmarks the relationship between confidence and performance collapses once items that all models agree on are removed. Inter-model pairwise calibration is small even where statistically significant, and what remains shrinks to nothing once base-rate differences along the shared factor are controlled for. Mathematical reasoning is the apparent exception, but this turns out to be a confound where reasoning models answer questions about their confidence by trying to solve them in their chain of thought, bypassing the sub-symbolic self-knowledge we seek to measure. We find no evidence for significant verbalised individuated metacognition in any tested domain.

</details>


### [218] [ChaosBench-Logic v2: Evaluating LLM Logical Reasoning over Dynamical Systems at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24305)
*Noel Thomas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了 ChaosBench-Logic v2 和 CARE 评估协议，揭示了大模型在复杂动力系统逻辑推理上的严重性能瓶颈与反直觉的抗相关行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的二元逻辑推理基准测试标准准确率掩盖了大模型在逻辑推理上的核心失效模式，如事前崩溃（prior collapse）、改写一致性问题以及对参数依赖动态系统的推理能力严重不足。

Method: 构建了“ChaosBench-Logic v2”基准测试（包含40,886个问题，覆盖165个动力系统）及“CARE”评估协议（侧重于校准和对抗鲁棒性），并对14个前沿大模型进行了定性与定量的对比评估。

Result: 1. 即使是前沿模型，在 regime-transition 推理上仅达到随机水平（MCC=0.05），而给定前提的 FOL 推理可达 MCC=0.52；2. Qwen 2.5-32B 在指标诊断任务上表现卓越；3. 某些模型在分叉问题上表现出负的 MCC，揭示了系统性的抗相关性。

Conclusion: 当前主流模型在逻辑推理方面存在显著短板，尤其是在动力系统演变（ regime-transition）和分叉（bifurcation）问题的推理上表现极其糟糕，甚至出现系统性反相关。模型在不同逻辑任务类别上存在显著的分布差异。

Abstract: Standard accuracy on binary reasoning benchmarks hides critical failure modes: prior collapse, inconsistency under paraphrase, and inability to reason about parameter-dependent dynamics. We present ChaosBench-Logic v2, a 40,886-question benchmark over 165 dynamical systems with 27 FOL predicates and 78 axiom edges, together with CARE (Calibration- and Adversarial-Robust Evaluation), a protocol that surfaces these pathologies. Evaluating 14 models, we find that regime-transition reasoning remains near random (MCC = 0.05) even for frontier models, whereas FOL deduction with given premises reaches MCC = 0.52. Per-family decomposition shows that the proprietary-model advantage concentrates on cross-indicator (+0.40) and consistency tasks, while open-source Qwen 2.5-32B dominates indicator diagnostics (0.91 vs. 0.45). Two models exhibit negative MCC on bifurcation questions, confirmed as systematic anti-correlation via confusion-matrix analysis.

</details>


### [219] [From One-Pass SGD to Data Reuse: Mini-Batch Scaling Laws in Sketched Linear Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24316)
*Ziyan Chen,Ding-Xuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究推导了批量大小对于草图线性回归性能的缩放定律，量化了批量处理在不同采样策略下如何影响模型的方差与偏差，并指出无放回小批量采样在性能表现上优于有放回采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩展定律（Scaling laws）研究大多集中在单样本SGD或全数据重用场景，未明确小批量处理（mini-batching）对性能的具体影响机制，导致批量大小在预测模型性能时缺乏理论依据。

Method: 针对草图线性回归（sketched linear regression），在幂律协方差谱和目标参数源条件（source condition）的框架下，通过数学分析推导了单遍批量SGD、有放回多遍批量SGD及无放回多遍批量SGD的风险分解公式及缩放定律。

Result: 推导发现：1) 批量大小$B$能有效降低方差；2) 在单遍模式下，$1/B$的方差改善会被较短的优化时域部分抵消；3) 多遍模式下，无放回采样比有放回采样更具稳定性，且当$B=N$时波动消失，收敛至确定性梯度下降。

Conclusion: 本研究通过理论分析确立了小批量（mini-batch）规模在刻画线性回归扩展规律中的作用，量化了不同采样模式下批量大小对优化轨迹、偏差和方差的影响，证实了无放回采样在减少波动方面的优越性。

Abstract: Scaling laws provide compact descriptions of how prediction error varies with compute, model size, and data, but existing theory mainly treats single-sample SGD or full data reuse, leaving the role of mini-batching unclear. We study batch scaling laws for sketched linear regression under a power-law covariance spectrum and a source condition on the target parameter. We analyze one-pass batch SGD, multi-pass batch SGD with replacement, and multi-pass batch SGD without replacement. Our first result is a risk decomposition: all three procedures share the same irreducible and approximation terms, while their stochastic terms depend on the sampling protocol. One-pass batch SGD splits into bias and variance, whereas the two multi-pass methods split into GD bias, GD variance, and a fluctuation term around a common GD reference trajectory. We then prove source-condition scaling laws for one-pass and multi-pass mini-batch methods. For one-pass batch SGD, mini-batching preserves the approximation and optimization-bias exponents, while the variance scales as $O(\min(M,(T_{\mathrm{eff}}γ)^{1/a})/(B T_{\mathrm{eff}}))$. Thus the usual $1/B$ covariance reduction holds at fixed update count $T$, but in the one-pass regime $T=N/B$ it is partly offset by the shorter optimization horizon. For multi-pass batch SGD, with- and without-replacement sampling have identical approximation and GD bias/variance terms; they differ only in the fluctuation covariance prefactor, which is $1/B$ with replacement and $ρ_{N,B}=(N-B)/(B(N-1))$ without replacement. Hence without-replacement sampling is less noisy for $B>1$, and when $B=N$ the fluctuation vanishes, recovering deterministic gradient descent. These results place batch size on the same theoretical footing as compute, data, and model dimension in sketched linear regression.

</details>


### [220] [Omissive Bias in Religious Representation: Benchmarking LLM Answers to Everyday Ethical Decision-making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24319)
*David Wingate,Sheryl Carty,Joshua Coates,Daniel Feldman,Nancy Fulda,Larry Howell,Brett Israelson,Dallin Jacobs,Jonathan Karr,John Paul Kimes,Elisabeth Kincaid,Paul Martens,Gavin Mobley,Suzana Pinheiro,Lindsay Slemboski,Peter Whiting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究揭示了LLM存在“遗漏性偏见”，即在回答个人伦理问题时系统性地未能提供可供参考的宗教视角，特别是在用户最需要此类指导的现实生活场景中表现尤为明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型（LLM）成为个人、道德和存在性问题的主要咨询来源，探讨模型是否纳入了历史上塑造人类此类理性思维的宗教框架，还是系统性地排除了这些视角。研究旨在定义并揭示这一现象，即所谓的“遗漏性偏见”。

Method: 1. 构建了AllFaith宗教代表性基准测试集：包含150个源自真实对话和宗教社群贡献的、涉及悲伤、宽恕、人际关系等领域的开放性伦理问题。2. 采用LLM-as-judge方法进行评分。3. 开展人类受试者调查，将模型表现与人类期望进行对比分析。4. 对27种不同模型进行了评估。

Result: 研究表明，模型在回答日常伦理问题时，对宗教内容的引用远低于人类期望。这种遗漏具有不对称性：模型在回答抽象的存在性问题（如死亡、真理）时更易提及宗教，但在用户最依赖宗教视角处理的现实生活挑战（如悲伤、婚姻、成瘾）中却表现出明显的缺失。

Conclusion: 当前大语言模型在处理个人及伦理问题时，存在“遗漏性偏见”（omissive bias），即系统性地忽视了宗教这一重要视角。作者指出，不应盲目通过宗教教条约束模型，但应识别并解决这种忽视，以便模型能更好地反映人类在应对复杂生活挑战时所依赖的多元价值框架。

Abstract: As large language models become a default source of guidance on personal, moral, and existential questions, it matters whether they draw on the religious frameworks that have historically shaped such reasoning, or systematically omit them. In this paper, we ask a deliberately narrow question: when posed an everyday ethical question for which religious perspectives may be valuable, do LLMs invoke religion at all? In contrast to benchmarks that look for the presence of political leanings or social bias, we look for the absence of religious representation as a dimension of value alignment and bias in LLMs. We term this ``omissive bias.''
  To measure omissive bias, we contribute the AllFaith Religious Representation Benchmark: 150 ethically and personally salient questions, sourced from in-the-wild chat transcripts and faith-community contributors, paired with an LLM-as-judge rubric that gives full credit for any mention of a religion, a religious practice, or a religious leader. The questions are not themselves about religion--they are open-ended questions about grief, forgiveness, relationships, purpose, and honesty, where religion is one valuable perspective among several. We also run a human-subjects survey to compare LLM behavior against human expectations.
  Evaluating 27 models, we find that LLMs consistently underrepresent religion relative to human expectations. The omission is asymmetric: models invoke religion more readily for abstract existential questions (meaning, death, truth) than for the practical personal situations--grief, marriage, family conflict, addiction--where many people most rely on it. It is not our purpose to adjudicate which values LLMs should hold. We argue, more modestly, that current LLM responses overlook critical opportunities to reflect religious frameworks that many people draw on when navigating personal and ethical challenges.

</details>


### [221] [Evolving Robustness--Exploration Trade-off in Online Reinforcement Learning via Quantile Bayesian Risk MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24345)
*Meichen Song,Yuhao Wang,Enlu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过动态调整分位数来平衡在线学习中稳健性与探索的方法，通过理论证明了分位数水平与认知不确定性之间的关系，并给出了亚线性遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在在线强化学习中，数据稀缺导致的认知不确定性使得学习初期的稳健性至关重要，而为了学习到最优策略，学习后期又必须进行充分的探索，因此需要解决这一随时间变化的“稳健性-探索”权衡问题。

Method: 1. 基于分位数贝叶斯风险感知马尔可夫决策过程 (Quantile BR-MDP) 进行建模，通过分位数水平控制后验不确定性参与贝尔曼更新。2. 推导了 BR-MDP 值与真实环境值之间差异的渐近正态性结果。3. 提出了一种具有自适应分位数调度机制的在线贝叶斯风险感知算法。

Result: 1. 建立了相对于真实最优值和最优 BR-MDP 稳健值两个维度的亚线性贝叶斯遗憾界（sublinear Bayesian regret bounds）。2. 数值实验表明该算法在需要大量探索和探索成本高昂的环境中均表现出优异性能。

Conclusion: 本文证明了通过自适应调整分位数，可以在学习初期优先考虑稳健性，随着数据积累转而侧重探索，从而有效地平衡了强化学习中的稳健性与探索。

Abstract: In online reinforcement learning, data scarcity creates epistemic uncertainty that makes robustness important early in learning, whereas sufficient exploration is needed to learn the true-environment optimal policy. We study this time-varying robustness--exploration trade-off through a quantile Bayesian risk-aware Markov decision process (BR-MDP), in which the quantile level controls how posterior uncertainty enters the Bellman backup. We characterize this control through an asymptotic normality result for the difference between the quantile BR-MDP value and the value in the true environment. The result implies that upper/lower-tail quantiles induce optimism/pessimism towards epistemic uncertainty, and the magnitude of the optimism/pessimism decreases as data accumulate. Building on this characterization, we propose an online Bayesian risk-aware algorithm with an adaptive quantile schedule that emphasizes robustness early and gradually encourages exploration of less-visited state--action pairs. We establish sublinear Bayesian regret bounds with respect to both the true optimal value and the optimal BR-MDP robust value. Numerical experiments demonstrate strong performance in both exploration-demanding and exploration-costly environments.

</details>


### [222] [Refined Analysis of Entropy-Regularized Actor-Critic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24357)
*Safwan Labbi,Paul Mangold,Daniil Tiapkin,Eric Moulines*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Actor-Critic中Critic作为baseline在方差缩减中的核心作用，证明了高质量的Critic估计能使算法达到与确定性策略梯度相当的样本复杂度，并强调了在训练中保证Critic准确性的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 明确Actor-Critic算法中Critic的具体作用，特别是在方差缩减方面的机制，并解释实现高效样本复杂度的关键条件。

Method: 通过理论推导分析在熵正则化、有限折扣环境下的Actor-Critic算法，研究Critic作为基准（baseline）对梯度估计方差的影响，并探讨Critic误差对策略梯度收敛性能的影响。

Result: 当Critic精确时，其作为基准可展现出极强的方差缩减能力，算法样本复杂度可达到$\tilde{O}(\log(1/ε))$；当Critic存在非零误差时，只要误差足够小，依然能保持方差缩减和快速收敛的优势。

Conclusion: 在Actor-Critic框架中，准确的Critic估计对于实现高效的方差缩减和快速收敛至关重要；建议优先训练Critic并保持其更新以维持较低的偏差和方差。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the role of the critic in actor--critic for entropy-regularized, finite, discounted environments. We establish that, when the critic is exact, using the latter as a baseline is a variance-reduction method in a strong sense. In this case, actor--critic with stochastic gradients matches the sample complexity of deterministic policy gradient, reaching an $ε$-optimal regularized value with $\tilde{O}(\log(1/ε))$ samples. In practice, the critic is learned alongside the actor: the variance of the actor update is then influenced by the critic's variance and bias. Specifically, when the critic has a sufficiently small error, the variance reduction and rapid convergence are preserved. This suggests to learn the critic first, keeping it up to date after each actor update, underscoring the crucial role of accurate critic estimation in actor--critic methods.

</details>


### [223] [GEESE: Genotype-aware End-to-End Spatio-temporal Embedding for Behavioral Phenotyping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24370)
*Yiran Ding,Yuen Gao,Chunqi Qian,Zijun Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GEESE 是一种基于深度学习的自动化行为表型分析框架，它通过 3D 姿态数据直接学习行为表征，超越了传统人工特征工程，显著提升了基因关联行为研究的效率、精度及可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统行为表型分析依赖人工特征工程，过程繁琐、劳动强度大，且限制了研究的复现性和可扩展性。旨在通过深度学习实现自动化、高通量的行为特征提取与识别。

Method: GEESE：提出一个端到端的深度学习框架，直接利用 3D 姿态动力学进行建模；利用预训练的时间序列基础模型（Time Series Foundation Model）将运动序列编码为行为流形（Behavioral Manifold）；模型支持行为分类及基因型预测，并提供 HONK 交互式工具，允许非程序员通过自然语言指令完成行为表型分析。
