Result: 在三种自闭症相关基因模型（CNTNAP2, CHD8, FMR1）的评估中，该方法性能优于人工特征基线；证明了学习到的表征能够精准捕捉基因型特异性的行为特征，并具有良好的跨背景泛化能力；HONK 工具成功降低了用户的使用难度。

Conclusion: GEESE 框架证明了自监督学习表征在行为表型分析中的有效性，提供了无需人工特征工程的自动化解决方案，显著提升了行为学研究的可扩展性与可重复性。通过 HONK 工具，降低了技术门槛，促进了该方法在神经科学实验中的应用普及。

Abstract: Behavioral phenotyping of genetic animal models currently requires labor-intensive manual feature engineering that limits reproducibility and scalability. We present GEESE, an end-to-end deep learning framework that learns behavioral representations directly from 3D pose dynamics without hand-crafted features. Using a pretrained time series foundation model, we encode movement sequences into a behavioral manifold that supports both behavior classification and genotype prediction. Evaluated across three autism-associated genetic models (CNTNAP2, CHD8, FMR1), our deep learning approach surpasses hand-crafted feature baselines in both tasks, revealing that learned representations capture genotype-specific behavioral signatures. The framework generalizes across genetic backgrounds, and an all-cohort model identifies both genetic background and genotype from movement patterns alone. We further provide HONK, an interactive intelligent tool enabling researchers without programming expertise to perform behavioral phenotyping from pose data through natural language interaction.

</details>


### [224] [AvAtar: Learning to Align via Active Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24395)
*Qi Yu,Ruizhong Qiu,Zhichen Zeng,My T. Thai,Huan Liu,Hanghang Tong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对最优传输（OT）对齐任务依赖大量人工标注的痛点，本文提出了 AvAtar 框架，通过伴随状态法量化样本梯度信息，实现了高效的主动学习，显著提升了对齐效率与模型质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最优传输在对齐任务中表现优异，但依赖于昂贵且稀缺的稀疏监督；现有研究缺乏如何主动获取高质量监督以优化 OT 对齐效果的方法。

Method: 提出主动对齐框架 AvAtar，利用基于梯度的影响衡量样本信息量；针对最优传输（OT）的不可微特性，采用伴随状态法（Adjoint-state method）将其转化为可高效求解的线性系统，并结合共轭梯度法实现线性复杂度。

Result: 在三个代表性对齐任务中表现出色，证明了该方法在有效性、可扩展性及泛化能力方面的优势。

Conclusion: AvAtar 提供了一种高效、通用的最优传输对齐主动学习框架，通过基于梯度的信息量评估显著降低了对稀疏监督的需求，提升了对齐性能。

Abstract: Alignment plays a fundamental role in many machine learning problems, such as multi-network analysis, multimodal learning, and point cloud registration. Recent works increasingly leverage optimal transport (OT) for distributional alignment, whose effectiveness largely depends on sparse supervision that is hard or costly to obtain in practice. Existing works, however, largely overlook how to actively acquire high-quality supervision to improve their alignment performance under OT frameworks. In this paper, we propose a principled active alignment framework for optimal transport alignment called AvAtar. We quantify the informativeness of a candidate by measuring its gradient-based impact on the global alignment result, computed as the gradient propagation from the global alignment result to all possible supervisions of the candidate through the entropy-regularized OT formulation. While differentiating through OT is challenging given its constrained nature, we leverage the adjoint-state method to reformulate the computation to a linear system solvable by the conjugate gradient method with linear complexity and guaranteed convergence. By encoding the global alignment result via effective utility functions, AvAtar is applicable to general alignment problems under the OT framework. Extensive experiments on three representative alignment tasks demonstrate the effectiveness, scalability, and generalizability of the proposed AvAtar.

</details>


### [225] [Generative OOD-regularized Model-based Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24405)
*Aysin Tumay,Jiahe Huang,Elise Jortberg,Rose Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对离线RL在稀疏空间中易产生OOD行为的问题，提出了一种基于生成模型密度估计的正则化算法GORMPO，有效提升了在稀疏及复杂环境下的策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的离线强化学习在稀疏状态-动作空间下容易产生超出分布（OOD）的动作，导致性能下降或安全性问题。

Method: 提出了GORMPO（Generative OOD-regularized Model-based Policy Optimization）算法，利用生成模型进行密度估计，将策略更新限制在数据集的高密度区域，从而避免OOD动作。

Result: 在真实医学数据集上比SOTA基线提升了17%，并在离线RL数据集上增强了基础模型。

Conclusion: 更好的OOD检测能力通常能带来更好的离线RL策略（在动力学稳定的环境中）；当动力学不确定时，谨慎的惩罚机制（配合较差的密度估计）更有效。研究同时也证明了生成模型在稀疏数据集上正则化策略更新的有效性。

Abstract: We study sequential decision-making with offline reinforcement learning (RL). Traditional offline RL policies may result in out-of-distribution (OOD) actions when training relies only on sparse offline representations. To ensure safe offline policies in a sparse state-action space, we explore how density estimation models can be integrated into model-based RL methods to avoid the OOD regions. Generative models are capable of explicitly modeling the density in sparse state-action spaces. Building on this, we introduce Generative OOD-regularized Model-based Policy Optimization (GORMPO), a density-regularized offline RL algorithm that uses generative density modeling to restrict policy updates to high-density areas of the dataset. Furthermore, we examine whether better OOD detection corresponds to better model-based offline policies. We compare (1) the OOD detection capabilities of various density estimators and (2) their performance within the GORMPO framework on a real-world medical dataset and sparse offline RL datasets. We theoretically guarantee GORMPO's performance under mild assumptions. Empirically, GORMPO outperforms state-of-the-art baselines by 17% on a real-world medical dataset and enhances the base model on the offline RL datasets. Our empirical findings show that better OOD detection generally results in improved policies in environments with stable dynamics, while conservative penalties with poor density estimation are favored when dynamics are uncertain.

</details>


### [226] [A Unified Python Framework for Direct PPO-based Control of AHUs with Economizer Logic and CO2-Constrained Ventilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24406)
*Erfan Haghighat Damavandi,Davide Papurello,Mahdi Alibeigi,Armin Keshavarz,Simone Canevarolo,Marco Condo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合PPO算法与层次化流逻辑的建筑HVAC智能控制框架，通过强化学习优化能源利用，同时通过硬约束确保室内空气质量，在能效与稳定性上均优于传统控制手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统HVAC控制系统难以应对建筑热环境的非线性特征及随时间变化的随机负载，导致能效与舒适度难以平衡。

Method: 提出了一种基于深度强化学习（DRL）的优化方法，核心采用近端策略优化（PPO）算法；构建了包含二阶RC热模型及CO2动态质量平衡的仿真环境；设计了确保室内空气质量（IAQ）的“层次化流逻辑”（Hierarchical Flow Logic）约束机制；并集成了焓基节能器实现免费供冷。

Result: 实验表明，相比基于遗传算法（GA）优化的PID控制器和传统开关控制器，PPO智能体在保持室内温度稳定性的同时，实现了更高的能源效率。

Conclusion: PPO算法在HVAC节能与环境质量控制方面优于传统的PID和开关控制，该端到端系统为智能建筑能源管理提供了稳健且具备泛化能力的解决方案。

Abstract: Optimizing HVAC (Heating, Ventilation and Air Conditioning) can enhance a building's energy efficiency while providing comfort levels for its occupants. Using conventional control systems to maintain HVAC functions is often difficult because of the nonlinear characteristics of a building envelope as it experiences stochastic load variations over time. This paper presents a new approach to optimizing HVAC systems through the use of Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) algorithms and the Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) algorithm implemented in a custom Python performance environment. The DRL system uses a second order resistor-capacitor thermal model and an integrated dynamic mass balance of CO2 to replicate the complex physics associated with buildings. One major innovation of this study is a "Hierarchical Flow Logic," which provides the means to ensure that indoor air quality (IAQ) is maintained by overriding the accepted actions of the agent that cause CO2 to exceed 1000 ppm. In addition, an enthalpy-based economiser is used to create free cooling from the outdoor environment. The experimental data shows that compared to PID controllers tuned by GA or traditional On-Off controls, a PPO agent has better temperature stability and energy efficiency overall. An end-to-end pipeline provides an avenue for robust and generalized solutions to help implement smart building energy management within the context of real hardware implementation.

</details>


### [227] [Synheart Capacity: A Theory-Driven Physiological Representation of Cognitive Capacity Dynamics from Wearable Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24416)
*Yisak Debele,Henok Ademtew,Israel Goytom*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Human cognitive performance is constrained by limited mental resources, yet continuous computational estimation of cognitive capacity dynamics remains an open challenge. We propose a theory-driven multimodal learning framework that models capacity-related cognitive state as a two-dimensional physiological representation defined by voluntary resource allocation (mental effort) and overload-related strain (stress). The proposed architecture combines dual-stream encoding of cardiac (IBI/HRV) and electrodermal (EDA) signals with late fusion and task-specific output heads that independently estimate probabilistic effort and stress states.
  Evaluation on the SWELL-KW dataset using strict leave-one-subject-out cross-validation demonstrates cross-individual generalization (stress: 70.0\% balanced accuracy; effort: 72.2\%), with significant gains from multimodal integration and theory-guided supervision. Rather than collapsing physiological dynamics into a single workload label, the proposed effort--stress state-space enables structured differentiation between distinct cognitive regimes, including productive engagement and overload-related strain. Predicted state trajectories exhibit significant demand-sensitive shifts under controlled workload manipulations, with effort and stress responding differentially across interruption and time-pressure conditions.
  These results suggest that physiologically grounded multidimensional state representations may provide a foundation for adaptive systems capable of continuous capacity-aware monitoring and human-centered interaction.

</details>


### [228] [LLMTabBench: Evaluating LLMs on Binary Tabular Classification From Zero to Few Shots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24417)
*Daria Grushina,Kseniia Kuvshinova,Alina Kostromina,Aziz Temirkhanov,Mile Mitrovic,Dmitry Simakov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 LLMTabBench 基准，系统评估了大语言模型在表格分类任务中的表现，发现其在少样本场景下优于传统模型，但也揭示了任务复杂度和上下文信息处理中的关键性能边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的针对表格数据的监督学习方法（如 TabPFN）依赖于标注数据，而大语言模型（LLM）的上下文学习能力在表格任务中的表现尚不明确且缺乏系统性评估。

Method: 引入了基准测试框架 LLMTabBench，利用真实与合成数据集，从零样本/少样本能力、先验知识与上下文交互、样本复杂性对性能的影响三个维度评估大语言模型（LLM）。

Result: 1. LLM 在零样本场景下表现出色；2. 上下文示例有时会与模型的先验知识产生冲突，导致性能下降；3. 存在数据复杂性上限，超过该上限后 LLM 性能下降且少样本提示的效果减弱。

Conclusion: LLM在表格数据低资源场景下具有竞争优势，但在处理复杂数据和融合上下文信息时存在局限性，需平衡先验知识与上下文示例的使用。

Abstract: Supervised classification for tabular data remains a core machine learning task, yet its reliance on large labeled datasets limits applicability in data-scarce domains. For such few-shot scenarios, specialized methods like TabPFN - a state-of-the-art Prior-Data Fitted Network - have set a high standard by leveraging large-scale synthetic pretraining, though they still require a context of labeled examples to function. In contrast, Large Language Models (LLMs) could offer a more flexible alternative via zero- and few-shot in-context learning directly from task descriptions, but their performance on tabular data remains inconsistent and poorly understood. We introduce LLMTabBench, a benchmark designed to systematically evaluate LLMs for tabular classification under data-scarce conditions. LLMTabBench explicitly probes (i) how LLM prior knowledge interacts with in-context information (task descriptions and few-shot examples), and (ii) how model performance scales with increasing data complexity, using both real-world and controlled synthetic datasets. Our findings include: (1) LLMs are highly competitive in zero-shot settings and can outperform alternative models, even when those models have access to few-shot examples; (2) incorporating additional few-shot examples can conflict with LLM prior knowledge, limiting or even degrading performance; and (3) there is a data complexity threshold beyond which LLMs' performance declines and few-shot examples become less effective. Together, these findings reveal fundamental constraints of in-context learning for tabular data and provide practical guidance for deploying LLMs in low-data regimes.

</details>


### [229] [ChainLearn: A Blockchain-Based Capacity-Aware Framework for Federated Ensemble Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24418)
*Karan Sharma,Aditya Tripathi,Rahul Mishra,Tapas Kumar Maiti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对医疗联邦学习中的异构计算环境，提出了一种容量感知的协调机制与区块链辅助架构，通过差异化部署模型和最小化通信量，显著提升了资源利用效率并满足了隐私保护需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联邦学习假设计算资源同质化，无法适应医疗机构间计算能力不均衡的实际情况；且现有方法在隐私保护和通信开销上仍存在局限。

Method: 提出一种容量感知协调框架：根据医院计算能力动态分配模型架构（MobileNetV3/EfficientNet/ResNet）；利用智能合约（Solidity）存储元数据（哈希值和指标），仅传输哈希值和标量而非模型参数；采用离线加权集成推断。

Result: 在PneumoniaMNIST和DermaMNIST数据集上，该方法实现了比等权集成更低的校准误差，精度与FedAvg等主流方法相当，通信开销降低了912,000倍以上。

Conclusion: 通过引入容量感知协调机制和区块链辅助的优化策略，该方法在异构计算环境下实现了高效、轻量级的联邦学习，显著降低了通信开销并保持了良好的模型性能。

Abstract: Federated learning is used in medical imaging where privacy prohibits centralizing data. Standard federated algorithms assume homogeneous hardware, identical architectures, and centralized aggregation, which fails when hospitals have unequal compute resources. We propose capacity-aware coordination: measure each hospital's throughput, assign capacity-appropriate architectures (MobileNetV3-Small, EfficientNet-B0, ResNet-50), and combine predictions via weighted ensemble. Weak and strong hospitals can participate without forcing uniform architectures.
  We separate on-chain policy from off-chain learning. A Solidity contract stores hospital registration, benchmark hashes, metrics, and weights. Hospitals train locally and submit only hashes and scalars (not parameters). Weighted ensemble inference is computed off-chain.
  Experiments on PneumoniaMNIST and DermaMNIST (5 seeds, 3 non-IID levels) show our method achieves lower or equal calibration error versus equal-weight ensemble and competitive accuracy versus FedAvg, FedProx, and FedMD. Communication overhead is 224 bytes per round, a reduction of over 912,000x compared to FedAvg.

</details>


### [230] [Batch Normalization Amplifies Memorization and Privacy Risks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24420)
*Ngoc Phu Doan,Chongyan Gu,Ihsen Alouani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了批量归一化（BN）不仅提升模型性能，更会无意中放大模型对异常数据的记忆，从而显著增加隐私泄露风险和成员推理攻击的脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然批量归一化（BN）在加速收敛和提升训练稳定性方面应用广泛，但其对数据隐私和模型记忆特性的具体影响此前缺乏深入研究。

Method: 采用三种互补的实证方法进行研究：(i) 非分布内（OOD）训练样本的非预期记忆能力分析；(ii) 通过梯度范数衡量单样本影响；(iii) 对成员推理攻击（MIA）的易感性评估。并辅以理论分析，探究BN在训练过程中放大异常样本影响的机制。

Result: 研究发现，相比于无BN的模型，带有BN的模型显著增强了对异常样本的记忆能力；这种增强直接转化为 privacy 风险，即BN模型对成员推理攻击的敏感度更高。理论分析也证实了BN在训练过程中放大了异常样本的逐步影响力。

Conclusion: 批量归一化（BN）层会放大异常值的影响力，从而导致深度学习模型产生更严重的隐私泄露和成员推理攻击（MIA）风险。

Abstract: Batch Normalization (BN) is widely adopted to enable faster convergence and more stable training of deep neural networks. However, its impact on privacy and memorization has remained largely unexplored. In this work, we investigate the effect of BN layers on the memorization of atypical or outlier samples and its implications for privacy leakage. We conduct an extensive empirical study using three complementary approaches: (i) unintended memorization of out-of-distribution training samples, (ii) per-sample influence measured via gradient norms, and (iii) susceptibility to membership inference attacks (MIA). Across multiple datasets and architectures, we consistently observe that BN substantially increases the memorization of outliers compared to models without BN. Critically, this amplified memorization translates directly into privacy vulnerabilities: models with BN exhibit significantly higher susceptibility to MIAs. We complement our empirical findings with a theoretical analysis showing that BN amplifies the per-step influence of outlier samples during training, providing mechanistic insight into this phenomenon. Our results highlight an underappreciated privacy risk associated with BN and provide both practical and theoretical insights into how normalization layers can amplify the influence of rare or sensitive training examples.

</details>


### [231] [Representation-Guided Discrete Molecular Graph Retrosynthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24428)
*Jiahai Huang,Anjie Qiao,Zhen Wang,Defu Lian,Yutong Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了Graph-oriented Representation Guidance (GRG)，通过将预训练的化学表征引导注入到分子图生成模型中，显著提升了逆合成任务的预测精度、多样性及训练收敛速度，并辅以无需额外训练的相似度重排序机制进一步优化预测结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于随机过程的分子图生成模型在大规模逆合成任务中通常采用隐式学习，缺乏对化学语义的直接表征引导，导致生成质量及收敛速度有待提升。研究旨在探索如何将计算机视觉领域中成功的“表征引导（Representation Guidance）”范式迁移并适配到分子逆合成任务中。

Method: 提出了一种Graph-oriented Representation Guidance (GRG) 框架，系统性地探索了教师模型表征、注入深度及引导策略的设计空间；此外还引入了一种基于表征相似度的重排序（reranking）机制。

Result: 在USPTO-50k数据集上显著提升了性能（Top-1/3/5/10准确率分别达到58.6/77.2/83.4/87.1，多样性提升至15.5）；在分布外数据集上表现稳健；训练收敛速度提升了30%-35%。

Conclusion: GRG通过引入面向图的表征引导机制，有效提升了单步逆合成预测的准确度、多样性及训练效率。该研究证明了表征引导作为一种强大的先验知识，能够帮助生成模型更有效地获取化学语义，并在分布外（OOD）场景下展现出良好的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Stochastic process-based molecular graph generators have become the state of the art for template-free single-step retrosynthesis. However, these models are typically trained only on product-reactant pairs, thereby acquiring chemistry-relevant representations in an indirect and implicit manner. Meanwhile, recent advances in computer vision demonstrate that offering representation guidance to a generator can effectively distill semantics from pretrained encoders into DiTs, substantially improving both convergence and generation quality. Whether similar gains extend to the retrosynthesis task, and what graph-specific design choices can make them work, remains an open question. To address these questions, we conduct a systematic empirical study over a unified design space spanning teacher molecular representations, endpoint and granularity choices, injection depths in the denoiser, correspondence strategies and guidance scheme. Guided by these considerations, we develop Graph-oriented Representation Guidance (GRG), which achieves 58.6 / 77.2 / 83.4 / 87.1 top-1 / 3 / 5 / 10 accuracy on USPTO-50k, while increasing diversity to 15.5, both substantially outperforming the adopted base generator. Notably, GRG consistently improves all top-k metrics in out-of-distribution settings, suggesting that representation guidance facilitates the acquisition of intrinsic chemical semantics. Meanwhile, the introduced representation guidance reduces the number of epochs by 35% and the wall-clock time by 30% to reach comparable performance. In addition, we introduce a simple yet effective representation-similarity-based reranking mechanism, which further improves the top of the ranked list without training an additional verifier.

</details>


### [232] [Balancing Fairness, Privacy, and Accuracy: A Multitask Adversarial Framework for Centralized Data-Driven Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24458)
*Imesh Ekanayake,Elham Naghizade,Jeffrey Chan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多任务对抗学习框架，通过动态平衡机制，有效解决了数据驱动应用中公平性、隐私保护与模型准确性之间难以共存的权衡难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前集中式数据驱动系统在处理公平性、隐私与准确性三者平衡时存在挑战，现有方法难以兼顾这三个目标，导致伦理标准与监管合规方面的缺失。

Method: 引入一种多任务对抗学习模型，通过优化成本函数动态平衡公平性、隐私与准确性；在训练过程中学习一种隐空间表征，在隐藏敏感属性的同时保留任务关键信息。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，该模型在严苛条件下实现了高水平的公平性与隐私保护，且不会造成显著的准确性损失，优于现有的基准方法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的多任务对抗模型在确保公平性与隐私保护的同时，实现了与现有最先进标准相当甚至更优的准确性，证明了其在多目标优化平衡中的有效性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: The integration of fairness and privacy in centralized data-driven applications is critical, especially as these systems increasingly influence sectors with significant societal impact. Current methods rarely address privacy, fairness, and accuracy together, which can potentially compromise ethical standards and privacy regulations. However, balancing these three objectives is quite challenging since each of objective often imposes conflicting requirements on the design and training of models, making it difficult to optimize one without compromising the others. This paper introduces a novel multitask adversarial model that treats fairness and privacy as integral objectives rather than afterthoughts, and learns a latent representation that hides sensitive attributes while preserving essential task-related information. Our approach dynamically balances fairness with accuracy and privacy through an optimized cost function with minimal performance loss even under strict conditions. Extensive testing on diverse datasets shows the ability of our model to achieve high standards of fairness and privacy without significant sacrifice to accuracy. Benchmarking against state-of-the-art privacy and fairness standards shows that our method enhances the robustness of privacy, fairness, and accuracy optimization, proving its adaptability across various datasets.

</details>


### [233] [The Normalized Maximum Likelihood for Regular Non-Smooth Models: Measure-Theoretic Foundations and Geometric Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24477)
*Trenton Lau,Gary P. T. Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对机器学习中广泛存在的“非光滑”模型（如 Lasso），该文提出了一个严格的 NML（归一化最大似然）计算框架，并通过创新的几何 MCMC 算法实现了复杂模型下随机复杂度的精确计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 NML（归一化最大似然）计算方法主要适用于平滑模型，而在现代机器学习中常用的非光滑模型（如 Lasso、稀疏 SVM 等）中失效。作者旨在为这些非光滑估计量构建通用的 NML 计算框架。

Method: 1. 基于几何测度论，利用保守雅可比矩阵（conservative Jacobians）将余面积公式（coarea formula）扩展至路径可微 Lipschitz (PDL) 估计量；2. 提出一种基于几何马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）的采样算法：Propose-and-Project Metropolis-Hastings (PDL-PPMH)，用于处理非光滑 MLE 水平集。

Result: 1. 在理论上证明了非光滑模型下随机复杂度的适定性；2. PDL-PPMH 算法在处理高维 Lasso 模型（P=2000）时展示了良好的鲁棒性；3. 实验表明基于确切 NML 准则的模型选择在预测性能上与交叉验证相当，且无需划分数据，具有更高的数据效率。

Conclusion: 该研究为非光滑模型下的 NML 码长提供了一个严格的计算框架，证明了其在自动微分环境下的理论一致性，并验证了其作为一种无需数据划分且具有高数据效率的模型选择准则，优于传统的交叉验证。

Abstract: The Normalized Maximum Likelihood (NML) codelength, or stochastic complexity, represents a principled criterion for universal coding. While recent coarea-based formulations provided a calculation method for smooth models, this framework collapses for the non-smooth estimators ubiquitous in modern machine learning (e.g., Lasso, Sparse SVMs). In this work, we provide a rigorous framework for computing the NML for regular path-differentiable Lipschitz (PDL) estimators. By applying classical geometric measure theory and bridging the coarea formula with conservative Jacobians, we prove that the stochastic complexity for non-smooth models is well-posed and theoretically consistent with the outputs of modern Automatic Differentiation. To compute this quantity exactly, we introduce the Propose-and-Project Metropolis-Hastings (PDL-PPMH) sampler, a geometric MCMC algorithm capable of traversing the non-differentiable level sets of the maximum likelihood estimator. We theoretically justify its components, including a stochastic tangent space proposal and a provably convergent non-smooth projection solver. We demonstrate the method's robustness by sampling from a high-dimensional Lasso posterior ($P=2000$), while simultaneously quantifying the computational scaling that governs the trade-off between exactness and mixing time. Crucially, we empirically demonstrate that our exact NML criterion provides a highly data-efficient alternative to cross-validation, achieving statistically indistinguishable predictive optima without requiring data splitting. Altogether, our work paves the way for the theoretical analysis of the NML codelength for regular non-smooth models.

</details>


### [234] [Zeroth-Order Nonconvex Nonsmooth Optimization with Heavy-Tailed Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24513)
*Zhuanghua Liu,Luo Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对重尾噪声环境下非凸非光滑问题的随机零阶算法，通过梯度估计器截断技术，实现了与一阶算法相当的理论收敛精度，并达到了该类问题的最优维度依赖复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现实机器学习应用中常见的非凸、非光滑问题，且由于无法访问梯度信息（仅能获取函数值），同时面临重尾噪声干扰，现有算法难以有效收敛至稳定的平稳点。

Method: 提出了一种基于在线转非凸（online-to-nonconvex）框架的随机零阶算法，核心改进在于对两点梯度估计器（two-point gradient estimator）采用了截断（clipping）策略，以应对重尾噪声。

Result: 该算法能够以 ${\mathcal O}(d^{\frac{p}{2(p-1)}}δ^{-1}ε^{-\frac{2p-1}{p-1}})$ 的零阶预言复杂度（oracle complexity）找到一个 $(\delta, \epsilon)$-Goldstein 平稳点。该维度依赖性匹配了随机凸非光滑问题的最优结果，且精度参数依赖性与针对非凸非光滑问题的一阶算法的一致。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的非凸非光滑随机零阶优化算法在重尾噪声环境下不仅具备理论上的最优性（维度依赖性与精度参数），还通过数值实验验证了其实际有效性，填补了在处理重尾噪声非凸非光滑问题时的算法缺口。

Abstract: This paper considers the nonconvex nonsmooth problem in which the objective function is Lipschitz continuous. We focus on the stochastic setting where the algorithm can access stochastic function value evaluations with heavy-tailed noise, which is prevalent in many popular machine learning applications. We propose a stochastic zeroth-order algorithm that refines the framework of online-to-nonconvex conversion by clipping the two-point gradient estimator. The theoretical analysis shows that our algorithm can find a $(δ, ε)$-Goldstein stationary point with zeroth-order oracle complexity of ${\mathcal O}(d^{\frac{p}{2(p-1)}}δ^{-1}ε^{-\frac{2p-1}{p-1}})$, where $d$ is the problem dimension and $p\in(1,2]$ is the order of bounded moments. Note that our dependence on dimension $d$ matches the best-known results of stochastic zeroth-order optimization for finding the sub-optimal solution of a stochastic convex nonsmooth problem. In addition, our dependence on accuracy parameters $δ$ and $ε$ is consistent with that of the best-known stochastic first-order algorithms for stochastic nonconvex nonsmooth problems. Finally, we conduct numerical experiments to demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [235] [Lake Detection and Water Quality Estimation in Sentinel-2 Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24515)
*Iulia Pleşu,Alexandra Băicoianu,Ioana Cristina Plajer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对比了多种机器学习模型在内陆水体监测中的应用，旨在提升水体识别的精度，并提出了一套优化后的色彩映射方案，以解决水质评估中可视化效果不佳、易被误读的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候变化和人类活动导致内陆水资源日益短缺且管理难度增加，亟需更可靠、自动化的水体监测方法；同时，现有的水质指数可视化方式在科学解读上往往存在局限性。

Method: 1. 对比三种机器学习架构进行水体识别与监测；2. 通过量化指标和实际案例评估模型性能；3. 将机器学习方法与经典的NDWI（归一化差异水体指数）进行对比分析；4. 开发并提出专门用于水质指数可视化的科学配色方案。

Result: 确定了在准确性、稳健性和实际应用方面表现最优的模型，并证明了数据驱动方法较传统索引阈值法的优越性；同时，提供的新配色方案提升了水质指数的可视化解读效果。

Conclusion: 该研究成功验证了机器学习在内陆水体监测中的有效性，提供了比传统NDWI更稳健的识别方法，并提出了一套更符合科学可视化需求的专业配色方案，以提升水质评估的解读与决策效率。

Abstract: With climate change and increasing human pressure on natural landscapes, inland water resources are becoming progressively scarcer, more vulnerable, and more difficult to manage sustainably. Reliable and automated methods for detecting, monitoring, and assessing surface water bodies are therefore of growing scientific and practical importance. In this paper, we investigate and compare three distinct machine learning architectures for water body identification and monitoring. Their performance is evaluated through quantitative metrics and real-world examples. Furthermore, a direct comparison with classical NDWI thresholding is conducted on a representative test image to highlight differences between data-driven and index-based approaches. This analysis allows us to identify the best-performing model in terms of accuracy, robustness, and practical applicability. Beyond detection, a major challenge for meaningful water quality assessment lies in the consistent and interpretable visualization of spectral water indices. Standard color mapping techniques are often inadequate or potentially misleading for environmental applications. To address this gap, we propose a suite of meaningful color schemes adapted for water quality indices, facilitating clearer interpretation, comparison, and decision-making for human users.

</details>


### [236] [ECHO: Terminal Agents Learn World Models for Free](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24517)
*Vaishnavi Shrivastava,Piero Kauffmann,Ahmed Awadallah,Dimitris Papailiopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ECHO 是一种新型的训练方法，通过要求智能体预测环境的即时响应（不仅仅是执行动作），将稀疏的终端奖励转化为密集的监督信号，大幅提升了 CLIAgent 的任务完成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能体强化学习（如 GRPO）主要依赖稀疏的最终奖励，忽略了环境在每一步返回的丰富反馈（std/错误/日志），导致失败的轨迹未能提供有效的策略梯度信号，训练效率低。

Method: 提出 ECHO (Environment Cross-entropy Hybrid Objective)，在标准策略梯度损失（如 GRPO）基础上增加了一个辅助损失，强制模型根据当前动作预测环境返回的观察 token（环境动力学建模）。该方法复用 GRPO 的前向传播，无需额外 rollout。

Result: 在 TerminalBench-2.0 上，ECHO 将 Qwen3-8B 和 Qwen3-14B 的 pass@1 表现翻倍；显著降低了环境 token 的交叉熵，即使在未见过的 OOD 任务上也能实现无验证器（verifier-free）的自我提升。

Conclusion: 环境返回的观察流（stdout/logs等）不仅是上下文，更是宝贵的密集监督信号。ECHO 通过引入环境预测辅助损失，无需额外采样即可实现高效的策略优化，证明了利用环境反馈进行自监督改进的可行性。

Abstract: CLI agents are the closest thing language models have to an embodied setting: the model emits commands, the terminal executes them, and the returned stream -- stdout, errors, files, logs, and traces -- records the consequences. We argue that this stream is a supervision signal, but standard agent RL discards it: GRPO-style training updates action tokens with sparse outcome-level rewards while ignoring environment responses already in the rollout. Failed rollouts provide little policy-gradient signal despite containing rich evidence about how the environment responds. We introduce ECHO (Environment Cross-entropy Hybrid Objective), a hybrid objective that combines the standard policy-gradient loss on action tokens with an auxiliary loss that trains the policy to predict environment observation tokens resulting from its own actions. ECHO reuses the same forward pass as GRPO, requires no additional rollouts, and turns terminal feedback into dense supervision for all rollouts. ECHO doubles GRPO pass@1 on TerminalBench-2.0: Qwen3-8B improves from 2.70% to 5.17%, and Qwen3-14B from 5.17% to 10.79%. ECHO also produces policies that better predict terminal dynamics, even on trajectories they did not generate: across held-out rollouts, it sharply reduces environment-token cross-entropy while GRPO alone barely changes it. From base Qwen3-8B, ECHO matches expert-SFT-then-GRPO performance on held-out terminal tasks without expert demonstrations, and recovers roughly half of the expert-SFT initialization benefit on TerminalBench-2.0. In some settings, the environment prediction loss alone enables verifier-free self-improvement, allowing policies to improve on unseen OOD tasks by learning only from environment interactions. Together, these results suggest that environment observations are not merely context for future actions, but a dense, on-policy supervision signal already present in every rollout.

</details>


### [237] [MindAlign: Bridging EEG, Vision, and Language for Zero-Shot Visual Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24523)
*Zexuan Chen,Sichao Liu,Runhao Lu,Huichao Qi,Alexandra Woolgar,Xi Vincent Wang,Lihui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种三模态对比学习框架，通过预训练EEG编码器并在统一潜在空间中对齐EEG、图像及LLM文本，显著提升了脑电信号视觉解码的准确度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决从大脑信号（特别是EEG）中解码视觉信息的挑战，克服脑电信号非平稳性及与视觉/语义空间对齐困难的问题。

Method: 提出两阶段训练范式：1) 基于遮蔽重构的EEG编码器自监督预训练；2) EEG、图像及LLM生成的文本三种模态在统一潜在空间内的对比学习（使用图注意力机制和时空卷积嵌入）。引入文本作为语义正则化器，并包含针对特定受试者的适应模块。

Result: 在Things-EEG2基准测试中，Top-1准确率达到54.1%，Top-5达83.4%，大幅超越现有基准；验证了在Things-MEG上的通用性，并证实解码结果与人类视觉神经生理学特征相吻合。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种基于EEG的视觉解码三模态对比学习框架，显著提升了零样本分类性能，证明了通过文本监督整合语义结构，并结合专门的编码器设计是实现鲁棒性视觉解码的关键。

Abstract: Visual decoding from brain signals is a key challenge at the intersection of computer vision and neuroscience, requiring methods that bridge neural representations and computational models of vision. We introduce a tri-modal contrastive framework for EEG-based visual decoding that aligns EEG, visual, and textual representations within a unified latent space. Our approach follows a two-stage design. First, we pre-train an EEG encoder via masked reconstruction on unlabeled trials, learning spatio-temporal regularities that transfer robustly to downstream tasks. Second, we jointly align EEG, image, and LLM-generated textual descriptions through contrastive learning, where text supervision acts as a semantic regularizer that injects linguistic structure into the shared space without overwhelming the primary EEG-image signal. The encoder integrates subject-specific adaptation, graph-attention over channels, and temporal-spatial convolutional embeddings. On the Things-EEG2 200-way zero-shot benchmark, our framework achieves 54.1% Top-1 and 83.4% Top-5 accuracy, substantially exceeding the strongest prior baseline (32.4% / 64.0%), with paired Wilcoxon tests confirming significance (p < 0.01) over all in-subject baselines. We validate generalization on Things-MEG. Analysis reveals that compact embedding geometries (CN-CLIP) outperform much larger backbones, and that decoding aligns with established neurophysiology of visual processing. This work is a critical step towards robust, semantically-grounded visual decoding from non-invasive temporal neural signals. The source code is publicly available in https://github.com/anon-eeg/eeg_image_decoding.

</details>


### [238] [Cross-Domain Energy-Guided Diffusion Generation for Off-Dynamics Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24810)
*Yu Yang,Yihong Guo,Anqi Liu,Pan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对离线强化学习中的动态失配问题，CEDGE 通过结合轨迹扩散模型与能量引导机制，实现了跨域轨迹的有效合成与长程规划优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有处理离线强化学习动态失配的方法局限于源数据筛选或基于转移（transition-level）的动力学建模，前者无法提升覆盖范围，后者在长序列下累积误差大，缺乏轨迹级别的生成能力。

Method: 提出 CEDGE（Cross-domain Energy-guided Diffusion GEneration）框架：基于源域数据训练轨迹扩散模型，通过包含“回报、域、行为”三个维度的能量引导函数对生成样本进行自适应调整，无需重训练模型即可适配目标域。

Result: 在 ODRL 基准测试中，CEDGE 在动态偏移环境下的轨迹规划表现优异，且生成的合成轨迹能显著提升下游策略的学习效果。

Conclusion: CEDGE 通过轨迹级别的能量引导生成，有效解决了离线强化学习中动态失配（Off-dynamics）引起的迁移难题，在动态偏移下的规划性能及合成数据质量方面均优于现有方法，且具有极高的适应效率。

Abstract: Off-dynamics offline reinforcement learning seeks to learn a target-domain policy from a large source dataset and a limited target dataset under mismatched transition dynamics. Existing approaches such as reward augmentation and data filtering are constrained to the source dataset and cannot synthesize new target behavior to improve coverage beyond the collected source trajectories. While recent model-based methods attempt to address this by learning target-aware dynamics, the generated experience is constructed only at the transition level, which leads to accumulated errors over long horizons. These limitations necessitate a shift toward trajectory-level generation for off-dynamics offline RL. We propose CEDGE, a Cross-domain Energy-guided Diffusion GEneration framework. CEDGE trains a trajectory diffusion model on source-domain trajectories and adapts the generated samples to the target domain through energy guidance. This guidance is derived by minimizing the distribution mismatch between the source and desired target-domain trajectories and is decomposed into return, domain, and behavior energy components. The resulting energy-guided trajectories are useful both for direct planning and as synthetic data for policy learning. Since target adaptation is achieved via energy guidance rather than retraining the diffusion model, CEDGE can be efficiently adapted to new target dynamics compared to previous methods. Experiments on the ODRL benchmark demonstrate that trajectory-level energy-guided generation improves diffusion planning under dynamics shifts and produces synthetic data that improves downstream target policy learning.

</details>


### [239] [What Are We Actually Decoding? Source Attribution for Non-Invasive Brain-to-Language Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24524)
*Xinyu Zhang,Sichao Liu,Runhao Lu,Alexandra Woolgar,Lihui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出神经语言解码性能常遭非神经信号干扰，提出了一套审计框架通过隔离结构性偏见和量化上下文证据，证明了神经解码性能应当进行严格的来源归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的神经语言解码研究中，解码器先验、嵌入度量和信号时长等“非神经证据”会导致性能虚高，缺乏对解码性能来源的明确归因，难以判断增益是否真正源于脑部刺激诱发的神经信号。

Method: 提出了一种审计框架，通过三个维度分离解码性能来源：(1) 结构性捷径（通过固定窗口和刺激身份分割排除）；(2) 窗口级刺激锁定证据；(3) 跨窗口上下文聚合（引入“组上下文偏差（GCB）”方法，通过加性逻辑偏差评估上下文证据的贡献）。

Result: 通过该框架发现：(1) 信号盲的随机噪声在变长解码中通过利用结构性泄露可达到高准确率，但在控制后失效；(2) 修正后，固定窗口检索仍能恢复可测量的MEG-音频区分度；(3) 引入GCB技术使上下文证据的贡献可被量化，在Gwilliams和MOUS数据集上性能显著提升且该增益经过了随机扰动验证。

Conclusion: 非侵入式神经语言解码的性能提升应当进行“来源归因分析（source-attributed）”，而非仅仅报告解码指标，以排除非神经信号的干扰（如结构性捷径）。

Abstract: In non-invasive neural language decoding, results can be inflated by sources that are not stimulus-evoked neural evidence: decoder priors, embedding-based metrics, and non-neural structural nuisances such as signal duration. The methodological challenge is therefore attribution: a reported gain is more informative when it can be traced to a specific source. We recast stimulus-locked MEG-to-audio retrieval as an auditing framework that separates apparent performance into three sources - structural shortcuts, window-level stimulus-locked evidence, and cross-window contextual aggregation - and provides a diagnostic for each. Signal-blind Gaussian noise reaches 66.3% Rank@1 (R@1) under variable-length decoding but collapses to near chance once fixed-duration windows and stimulus-identity splits are enforced, isolating structural leakage. Under these controls, fixed-window retrieval recovers measurable MEG-audio discriminability, while an oracle sentence-bucket diagnostic shows that 95.7% of Top-1 errors select the wrong sentence, localising the residual bottleneck to sentence-level competition. We audit this contextual source with Group Context Bias (GCB), an inference-time additive logit bias that pools sentence-consistent evidence across windows while leaving the base retrieval scores and candidate pool fixed. Used as a score-space intervention, GCB makes the contextual source measurable: R@1 shifts from 44% to 52% on Gwilliams and from 22% to 29% on MOUS under the same fixed setting. GCB is auditable under this design: its effect collapses under random-grouping perturbations and vanishes when local evidence is attenuated in MEG or is near chance in EEG, supporting its use as a controlled source-attribution intervention. These results suggest that brain-to-language performance should be source-attributed, not merely reported.

</details>


### [240] [Rethinking Federated Unlearning via the Lens of Memorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24545)
*Jiaheng Wei,Yanjun Zhang,He Zhang,Leo Yu Zhang,Chao Chen,Kok-Leong Ong,Jun Zhang,Yang Xiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对联邦学习中的遗忘需求，本文提出了基于记忆评估的 FedMemPrune 方法，通过区分并修剪特定于待删除数据的冗余参数，在平衡遗忘彻底性与模型效用方面取得了显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的联邦遗忘方法往往忽视了待删除数据与保留数据之间的重叠信息，导致遗忘效果不佳或引发客户端间的不公平性；作者试图从“记忆（Memorization）”的角度重新审视联邦遗忘问题。

Method: 1. 提出“群体记忆评估（Grouped Memorization Evaluation）”指标，用于量化和区分记忆信息与重叠信息；2. 开发“联邦记忆修剪（FedMemPrune）”算法，通过重置负责记忆的冗余参数来实现高效遗忘。

Result: 实验结果表明，FedMemPrune 在遗忘性能上接近重训练基准，且在消除记忆的效果上优于现有的联邦遗忘算法，同时不会牺牲保留知识的实用性。

Conclusion: FedMemPrune 通过“群体记忆评估”指标识别并修剪特定于待删除数据的冗余参数，能够有效实现联邦遗忘，且在保持模型效用的同时，比现有方法更彻底地消除记忆。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) increasingly needs machine unlearning to comply with privacy regulations. However, existing federated unlearning approaches may overlook the overlapping information between the unlearning and remaining data, leading to ineffective unlearning and unfairness between clients. In this work, we revisit federated unlearning through the lens of memorization. We argue that unlearning should mainly remove the unique memorized information attributable to the data to be forgotten, while preserving overlapping patterns that are also supported by the remaining data. Specifically, we propose Grouped Memorization Evaluation, an example-level metric that separates memorized knowledge from overlapping knowledge. Building on this metric, we introduce Federated Memorization Pruning (FedMemPrune), a pruning-based unlearning approach that resets redundant parameters responsible for memorization. Extensive experiments show that FedMemPrune closely matches retraining-based unlearning baselines while more effectively eliminating memorization than existing federated unlearning algorithms, yielding strong unlearning performance without sacrificing the utility of retained knowledge.

</details>


### [241] [RL with Learnable Textual Feedback: A Bilevel Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24547)
*Utsav Singh,Sidhaarth Sredharan,Souradip Chakraborty,Amrit Singh Bedi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对大语言模型推理任务学习效率低的问题，该论文提出Bi-NAC框架，通过双层优化实现奖励反馈与模型策略的协同训练，显著提升了模型的泛化能力与参数效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习方法在处理稀疏终端奖励时效率低下，当前的文本反馈方法多将反馈视为固定的或辅助性信号，未能充分挖掘“反馈质量应以提升策略效果为目标”这一核心属性。

Method: 提出Bilevel Natural Language Actor-Critic (Bi-NAC) 框架，将反馈生成与策略学习形式化为Stackelberg双层规划问题进行联合训练，使批评者模型能够针对性地生成能提升策略表现的文本反馈。

Result: 在MATH-500、MBPP和GPQA基准测试中，Bi-NAC在样本效率和参数效率上均优于传统RL和固定批评者基线；2B模型在MATH-500上达到46.6%（优于3B GRPO的41.4%），6B模型在GPQA上达到49.3%（优于7B GRPO的43.6%）。

Conclusion: 通过将反馈学习建模为双层优化问题，提出Bi-NAC框架，证明了让批评者（Critic）学习生成能有效提升策略（Actor）性能的反馈，比传统的辅助式反馈更高效，在参数规模较小的情况下表现优于基准模型。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards can improve LLM reasoning, but learning remains sample-inefficient when terminal rewards are sparse. This has motivated a growing line of work on RL with textual feedback, where a critic model generates natural language feedback to guide a reasoning model (the actor), augmenting scalar rewards with richer learning signals. However, existing methods typically treat feedback as fixed or auxiliary, which misses a key property: feedback should not merely be correct, but should improve the policy (actor model) when provided in context. This motivates a paradigm of learnable textual feedback for RL. Yet the learnability and usefulness of feedback depend on the policy's ability to learn from it, making RL with learnable feedback an inherently bilevel problem. We formalize this coupling as a Stackelberg bilevel program and derive Bilevel Natural Language Actor-Critic (Bi-NAC), which jointly trains a critic to generate reward-improving feedback and an actor to exploit it. Across MATH-500, MBPP, and GPQA, Bi-NAC improves sample and parameter efficiency over RL and fixed-critic baselines: our 2B model outperforms the 3B GRPO baseline, achieving 46.6% versus 41.4% on MATH-500, while our 6B model surpasses the 7B GRPO baseline, achieving 49.3% versus 43.6% on GPQA.

</details>


### [242] [Position: AI for Science Should Treat Measurement-to-Dataset Pipelines as Inference Components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24558)
*Ling Zhan,Xiaoyao Yu,Tao Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有AI4Science研究常忽视数据预处理流水线的不确定性（即“冻结透镜”问题）；本文揭示了该方法导致的三种核心失效模式，并通过实证证明当前处理流程缺乏稳定性，呼吁建立“可计算观测框架”以实现科学过程的透明与可溯源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI4Science领域常将处理后的数据集视为“既定事实”，忽略了多阶段（测量、重建、预处理）流水线作为一种推理组件所带来的不确定性，导致研究隐蔽假设、不可认证的迁移能力以及未受控的变异性。

Method: 提出针对AI4Science领域“冻结透镜（Frozen Lens）”问题的分析框架，通过大范围的神经科学实证审计（Empirical Audit），依据跨数据集稳定性标准检验数据处理流水线的有效性。

Result: 在神经科学的实证审计中发现，现有数据处理流水线在跨数据集稳定性标准下的存活率极低（仅约 0.0004%），证实了忽略流水线不确定性会严重威胁科学结论的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 科学研究应将数据处理流水线从“静态数据源”转变为“可计算的推理对象”，通过建立计算观测框架（Computable Observation Frameworks）来量化流水线的不确定性，提升科学结论的可审计性、可复现性和累积性。

Abstract: AI for Science (AI4Science) workflows often treat the released dataset as a fixed interface to the underlying system.
  However, in domains relying on \emph{indirect observation}, the learner observes a derivative representation produced by multi-stage measurement, reconstruction, and preprocessing pipelines.
  \textbf{We argue that these measurement-to-dataset pipelines are inference components: treating their outputs as ``given data'' freezes an observation model and obscures uncertainty over feasible pipeline choices.}
  We identify three failure modes arising from this ``frozen lens'': \textbf{(C1) hidden hypothesis space}, where the released dataset does not specify the pipeline configuration or its validity conditions; \textbf{(C2) uncertified transportability}, where a pipeline may be documented but its regime of validity is untested, so failures under distribution shift cannot be adjudicated; \textbf{(C3) ungoverned multiplicity}, where many defensible pipelines exist and dispersion is real but not propagated into uncertainty-aware evidence.
  We stress-test these claims with a large-scale neuroscience empirical audit, finding a survival rate of $\approx 0.0004\%$ under a cross-dataset stability criterion.
  We call on the AI4Science community to make pipelines \emph{computable} inference objects via domain-specific Computable Observation Frameworks.
  This shift enables quantifying pipeline adequacy and stability, converting implicit implementation choices into auditable, reproducible, and cumulative scientific evidence.

</details>


### [243] [Polymorphism Is Rotation: Operational Mechanistic Interpretability from a Two-Layer Transformer to Pythia-70m](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24577)
*Jordan F. McCann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现不同权重初始化的Transformer计算相同的函数，但内部表征存在随机旋转偏差（多态性）。只需一个由正交Procrustes计算的旋转矩阵，即可实现不同模型间特征字典和Steering vectors的无损迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索独立训练的Transformer模型虽然功能相似，但其内部表现形式（坐标系）是否存在某种系统性的差异，并试图通过对齐这些差异来实现跨模型特征与向量的有效迁移。

Method: 使用正交Procrustes拟合方法，基于单批次激活值计算模型间的对齐旋转矩阵 $R$；利用稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征字典的迁移、Steering vectors的对齐以及Kolmogorov-Smirnov检验验证 $R$ 的分布特性。实验对象涵盖Dyck-3小模型及Pythia-70m系列模型。

Result: 独立训练的模型存在符合 $\mathrm{SO}(d_{\mathrm{model}})$ Haar分布的随机旋转偏差。应用Procrustes旋转后，模型间的SAE重构性能几乎恢复到同模型内部水平（解释方差差异仅0.025），验证了“多态性”假设。同时也揭示了Steering vectors在迁移时受旋转矩阵及其不变子空间的影响。

Conclusion: 不同权重初始化的Transformer虽表现出“多态性”（即功能一致但内部表征存在随机旋转），但它们在残差流中计算的是相同的函数。通过正交Procrustes分析，可以证明这种差异仅是单一的全局随机旋转（符合Haar分布），且该旋转是可逆的。这为跨模型特征迁移和对齐提供了理论基础和操作方法。

Abstract: Independently trained transformers compute the same function in residual-stream bases that differ by a uniform random rotation on $\mathrm{SO}(d_{\mathrm{model}})$. We call this phenomenon polymorphism: same function, mutually unintelligible interior coordinates. One matrix multiplication per model pair removes it: an orthogonal Procrustes fit on a single batch of activations transfers sparse-autoencoder feature dictionaries and steering vectors between independently trained models, with no retraining.
  The phenomenon is invisible to the standard SAE universality metric. Decoder-column cosine similarity matches across seeds at 98%, the SAE-universality headline number, while an SAE trained on one seed reconstructs another seed's activations at negative explained variance, worse than predicting the constant mean. The decoder columns align; the encoder reads from a rotated frame. A single Procrustes rotation $R$ restores reconstruction to within 0.025 EV of the within-seed ceiling at every internal site.
  $R$ is Haar-distributed: $\|R - I\|_F$ matches the random-orthogonal prediction $\sqrt{2 d_{\mathrm{model}}}$ to 0.1% at $d_{\mathrm{model}} = 512$, and a Kolmogorov-Smirnov test of $R$'s eigenvalue spectrum against Haar $\mathrm{SO}(d_{\mathrm{model}})$ returns $p \approx 1.000$ pooled and per-pair. Diff-of-means steering vectors transfer in three regimes by alignment with $R$'s invariant subspace: clean when pinned by shared output weights, partial when overlapping the rotated subspace, inverted otherwise. With no shared I/O (Pythia), all three collapse to universally inverted. The same rotation account holds across training checkpoints within a single run.
  Validated on a 104k-parameter Dyck-3 transformer and nine independently-trained Pythia-70m seeds on The Pile, via a pre-registered four-bar operational framework. Frontier-scale (10B+) replication remains open.

</details>


### [244] [LAPLEX: The FFT of Learnable Laplace Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24584)
*Łukasz Struski,Hanna Blazhko,Piotr Kubaty,Jacek Tabor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 LAPLEX，通过可学习的坐标锚点实现高维数据的全秩线性算子，在无需存储稠密矩阵的情况下实现高效、适应性强的全局交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习线性代数计算中在“固定几何/精确计算”（如 FFT）与“自适应几何/高参数开销”（如密集矩阵、随机特征）之间存在矛盾，传统稠密层在超大维度下计算与存储受限。

Method: 提出 LAPLEX 层，这是一种基于可学习相位和坐标锚点的拉普拉斯核算子。它利用类似于 FFT 的缩放特性，将显式稠密矩阵的乘法转化为隐式计算，从而在不存储完整矩阵的前提下实现全秩线性变换。

Result: 支持高达 $10^9$ 维度的矩阵-向量乘法；在图像任务中，能在大规模数据（$3 \cdot 10^6$ 维度）上构建高效的协方差模型，同时保持空间结构信息且无需引入卷积归纳偏置。

Conclusion: LAPLEX 通过隐式学习锚点实现了全秩矩阵的近似，成功在存储成本与模型表现力之间实现解耦，使处理超大规模数据（$10^9$ 维度）的全局交互成为可能，同时摆脱了卷积偏置。

Abstract: Fast linear algebra in deep learning usually comes with a choice: fixed geometry and exact computation, as in the Fourier transform, or adaptive geometry paid for by dense parameters, random features, or low-rank surrogates. To move beyond this trade-off, we introduce LAPLEX, a class of exact, trainable (phased) Laplace-kernel operators.
  A LAPLEX layer is a typically full-rank dense matrix, implicitly defined by learnable coordinate anchors, with FFT-like scaling. Consequently, it supports trainable matrix--vector operations at vector dimensions up to $10^9$ on modern GPUs. As a neural layer, it yields compact projections and classification heads interpretable as soft, trainable routing models. The same primitive also serves as an efficient Gram operator, enabling high-dimensional covariance models on flattened images of dimension $3 \cdot 10^6$ that preserve visible spatial structure without imposing convolutional bias. These applications reflect a single principle: dense geometry can be learned without storing a dense matrix, which enables data-adaptive global interactions in regimes where ordinary dense layers are out of reach. In this sense, LAPLEX separates expressivity from storage cost: it behaves like a dense trainable matrix, but is represented and applied through a small structured set of parameters.

</details>


### [245] [Beyond Generative Priors: Minority Sampling with JEPA-Guided Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24631)
*Sol Park,Soobin Um*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了JEPA引导采样框架，通过结合联合嵌入预测架构作为世界模型来定义和引导现实世界中的“语义稀疏性”，克服了现有方法依赖生成器自身分布的局限性，实现了更准确的少数样本采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的少数类采样（Minority sampling）方法主要依赖于生成模型从训练数据中学习到的先验，这导致“稀疏性”仅局限于模型定义的分布，往往无法准确反映现实世界中的语义稀疏性。因此，需要一种基于现实世界先验的方法来定义和生成少数样本。

Method: 提出了一种名为JEPA引导（JEPA Guidance）的扩散采样框架。该方法使用联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）作为世界模型，通过JEPA所诱导的隐含密度来引导扩散过程，使生成轨迹偏向真实世界的低密度区域。此外，开发了原则性的近似策略和理论误差界限以降低计算开销。

Result: 在无条件、类别条件和文本到图像生成的任务中，JEPA引导显著提升了生成少数样本的保真度和语义有效性，在捕捉真实世界稀疏性的能力上优于传统的以生成器为中心的基准模型。

Conclusion: JEPA引导框架成功利用世界模型提供的先验知识，解决了生成式采样对于模型特定密度函数的依赖，通过引入近似计算策略，实现了在实际应用中高效且高质量地生成具有真实语义稀疏性的样本。

Abstract: Minority sampling aims to generate low-density instances on a data manifold and is of central importance in applications such as medical diagnosis, anomaly detection, and creative AI. Existing approaches, however, define minority samples relative to generative priors learned from training data, confining rarity to model-specific notions that may poorly reflect real-world semantics. In this work, we propose a world-centric perspective on minority sampling, which defines rarity with respect to real-world priors rather than generator-induced densities. To this end, we introduce JEPA guidance, a diffusion sampling framework guided by a Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) -- a class of world models that encode broad, semantically rich representations. JEPA guidance steers diffusion trajectories toward low-density regions under the implicit density induced by the JEPA, thereby aligning generated minorities with real-world semantic rarity. To make JEPA guidance computationally practical, we develop principled approximation strategies accompanied by theoretical error bounds, significantly reducing the overhead of guidance computation. Extensive experiments across unconditional, class-conditional, and text-to-image generation demonstrate that JEPA guidance consistently improves the fidelity and semantic validity of minority samples, outperforming generator-centric baselines in capturing real-world notions of rarity. Code is available at https://github.com/soobin-um/jepa-guidance.

</details>


### [246] [WLNO: Wavelet-Laplace Neural Operator for Solving Partial Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24658)
*Muhammad Abid,Arth Sojitra,Omer San*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WLNO 通过整合小波多尺度空间分解与拉普拉斯神经算子，有效解决了拉普拉斯模型在捕捉局部多尺度 PDE 特征方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的拉普拉斯神经算子 (LNO) 虽然擅长捕捉瞬态和稳态动力学，但缺乏明确的机制来提取复杂 PDE 解中固有的、空间局部的多尺度特征。

Method: 提出 Wavelet-Laplace Neural Operator (WLNO)，结合了拉普拉斯神经算子 (LNO) 和 Haar 小波多尺度空间分解。具体通过并行的小波分支（对特征图进行 DWT 分解并对四个子带进行 $1\times1$ 卷积，再通过 IDWT 重构）与 LNO 分支融合，并通过可学习的 Sigmoid 门控权重 $\alpha_\mathrm{wav}$ 自适应平衡空间与频率域特征。

Result: 在扩散方程、Burgers 方程、反应扩散系统、达西流和二维纳维-斯托克斯方程这五个基准问题上，WLNO 在各项指标上均优于 LNO，特别是在具有尖锐冲击前沿和复杂涡旋结构的问题中表现出显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 小波变换的多尺度空间分解是拉普拉斯域算子学习的有效补充。WLNO 在处理具有强多尺度结构（如冲击波、涡旋结构）的 PDE 系统时表现出色，且在平滑和椭圆型问题中表现稳定。

Abstract: This work introduces the Wavelet-Laplace Neural Operator (WLNO), a novel neural operator that fuses Haar wavelet multi-scale spatial decomposition with the Laplace-domain pole-residue formulation of the Laplace Neural Operator (LNO). While LNO captures transient and steady-state dynamics through learnable system poles and residues, it lacks an explicit mechanism for extracting spatially localized multi-scale features inherent in complex PDE solutions. WLNO addresses this by augmenting the LNO core with a parallel single-level Haar discrete wavelet transform (DWT) branch that decomposes the lifted feature map into four frequency subbands: approximation (LL), horizontal detail (LH), vertical detail (HL), and diagonal detail (HH) and applies independent learned $1\times1$ convolutions to each subband before reconstruction via the inverse DWT. The two branches are fused through a learnable sigmoid-gated weight $α_\mathrm{wav}$, initialized to give a small initial contribution to the wavelet branch, allowing the model to adaptively balance Laplace-domain dynamics against spatial multi-scale features throughout training. WLNO is evaluated against LNO on five benchmark PDE problems using identical hyperparameters, training data, and evaluation protocols: the diffusion equation, the Burgers equation, the reaction-diffusion system, Darcy flow, and the two-dimensional Navier-Stokes equation. WLNO consistently outperforms LNO on all five problems, with the most pronounced improvement on problems with strong spatial multi-scale structure, such as the Burgers equation with sharp shock fronts and the Navier-Stokes equation with coherent vortical structures, while remaining consistent across smoother and elliptic problems. These results demonstrate that wavelet-based multi-scale spatial decomposition is a principled and effective complement to Laplace-domain operator learning.

</details>


### [247] [IterInject: Indirect Prompt Injection Against LLM Agents via Feedback-Guided Iterative Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24659)
*Zixuan Chen,Jiaxiang Chen,Li Luo,Ke Xu,Xiaoxiang Huang,Tanfeng Sun,Xinghao Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种反馈引导的迭代攻击框架，显著增强了LLM Agent面临间接提示注入（IPI）时的脆弱性评估，并揭示了该攻击在模型内部层的机理机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于LLM的Agent广泛应用，它们极易受到间接提示注入（IPI）攻击。现有的攻击手段主要依赖静态载荷，缺乏有效且自适应的反馈机制来绕过Agent的复杂防御，且学界对IPI的内在机理认识尚不全面。

Method: 提出了名为 (该名称在原文以占位符 \oursys 表示) 的框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 基于规则的诊断器，用于生成结构化反馈；(2) 基于LLM的优化器，根据历史优化历史改进攻击载荷；(3) 合成步骤，利用失败模式生成新的伪装种子。此外，还采用了注意力层面的机理分析与因果干预研究。

Result: 在AgentDojo和InjectAgent基准测试中，该方法显著优于现有基线。在针对生产级编码Agent（Claude Code）的实验中，5/9的目标达到完全成功。机理分析发现中后层存在注意力机制介导的阈值现象，因果干预实验验证了该发现并指出了防御方向。

Conclusion: 本文提出的反馈引导迭代框架有效提升了间接提示注入（IPI）的攻击成功率，不仅超越了现有静态和自适应方法，还通过机理分析揭示了IPI在LLM中的注意力介导阈值机制，为防御研究提供了理论基础。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly deployed for complex tasks requiring planning, tool use, and interaction with external services. Their reliance on untrusted external content exposes them to indirect prompt injection (IPI), in which adversarial instructions embedded in retrieved data hijack agent behavior. Existing attacks rely on static payloads that cannot adapt to agent-specific defenses; even recent adaptive methods lack structured feedback to guide optimization. We introduce \oursys, a feedback-guided iterative framework that closes the loop between injection, diagnosis, and refinement: a rule-based diagnoser produces structured outcome labels with behavioral descriptions, and an LLM-based optimizer refines payloads conditioned on the full optimization history. A synthesis step generates new disguise seeds from failure patterns, enabling the strategy space to self-evolve. On AgentDojo and InjectAgent, \oursys substantially outperforms static baselines and existing adaptive methods across four victim models. Extension experiments on Claude Code, a production-grade coding agent with layered defenses, show that optimized payloads achieve full success on 5 of 9 targets; even those that resist full exploitation exhibit measurable improvement from iterative refinement. We further present a mechanistic analysis of IPI, identifying an attention-mediated threshold mechanism in mid-to-late layers; three causal interventions validate this finding and point to concrete defense directions.

</details>


### [248] [Trajectory-Based Difficulty Scoring for Reliable Learning on Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24680)
*Tomer Lavi,Bracha Shapira,Nadav Rappoport*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于梯度提升树预测轨迹的难度评估方法（TDS），通过量化实例预测稳定性来辅助识别困难样本，并证明其在主动学习、风险控制及模型误差分析等多个数据挖掘场景中的实用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度提升树在表格数据上表现强劲，但在处理长尾分布的困难样本时效果欠佳，亟需一种有效的评估方法来识别并利用这些难以预测的实例。

Method: 提出基于每棵树累积预测轨迹（cumulative prediction trajectories）的“轨迹难度分数”（TDS）。该方法计算样本的轨迹描述符（方差、震荡峰值、符号切换、尾部稳定性等），训练轻量回归模型预测样本误差，并通过经验累积分布函数（ECDF）标准化为 [0,1] 的难度分。

Result: TDS 在多种表格基准测试中与预测误差具有很高的秩相关性；在分类任务中优于现有难度与不确定性基准，在回归任务中表现竞争；在主动学习、选择性预测及保形预测的应用中效果显著。

Conclusion: TDS 是一种有效的样本难度衡量指标，不仅能精确识别梯度提升树中的困难样本，还能通过辅助主动学习、选择性预测和保形预测等任务，显著提升表格数据挖掘的工作流效率与模型可靠性。此外，结合 SHAP 归因分析可有效揭示模型的失败模式。

Abstract: Gradient-boosted trees achieve strong performance on tabular data, yet often leave a long tail of poorly predicted instances. We introduce a Trajectory-based Difficulty Score (TDS), an instance-level difficulty estimator for boosted ensembles derived from per-tree cumulative prediction trajectories. For each instance, we compute interpretable trajectory descriptors (e.g., variance, oscillation peaks, sign switches, and tail stability) and train a lightweight regression model to predict held-out loss. An empirical CDF calibrates the resulting signal into a score in $[0,1]$ that supports ranking hard cases. Across diverse tabular benchmarks and ensemble sizes, TDS exhibits strong rank correlation with error and outperforms established instance-hardness and uncertainty baselines on classification, while remaining competitive on regression. We then show how a single difficulty signal improves multiple data mining workflows: difficulty-driven active learning for label-efficient training, difficulty-thresholded selective prediction for improved risk-coverage trade-offs, and TDS-stratified (Mondrian) conformal prediction for more uniform conditional coverage. Finally, clustering high-TDS instances using SHAP attributions reveals coherent failure modes characterized by compact feature-value ranges, supporting error analysis and targeted data acquisition.

</details>


### [249] [Beyond the Aggregation Dilemma: Prior-Retaining Decoupled Learning for Multimodal Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24684)
*Hao Yan,Xuanru Wang,Jun Yin,Shirui Pan,Senzhang Wang,Chengqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对大模型背景下多模态图学习中过度聚合导致的性能反转问题，本文提出了SUPRA架构，通过解耦路径与辅助监督实现了性能提升与高效计算的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大模型（LFM）时代，现有的多模态图学习（MAGL）强制拓扑聚合机制会导致“聚合困境”，即产生表示病理（信噪比下降）和优化病理（梯度匮乏），导致复杂模型性能反不如简单的MLP。

Method: 提出SUPRA（共享-独特先验保持架构），采用解耦双路径范式：通过无拓扑MLP处理模态特定特征，利用轻量级共享GNN捕获结构协同，并引入辅助深度监督机制以缓解梯度匮乏问题。

Result: SUPRA达到了SOTA性能，且相比多模态图Transformer，峰值GPU内存需求降低了3.5倍，训练速度提升达4.4倍。

Conclusion: SUPRA通过解耦模式有效解决了高置信度LFM先验下的聚合困境，在提升性能的同时显著降低了计算资源需求，在高维多模态图学习任务中展现了优越性。

Abstract: Multimodal Attributed Graph Learning (MAGL) integrates intrinsic node attributes with structural topology via graph aggregation. However, as pretrained encoders evolve into Large Foundation Models (LFMs), the landscape of MAGL fundamentally shifts: under high-confidence LFM priors, mandatory aggregation introduces topological noise that overwhelms discriminative signals, triggering a counter-intuitive performance inversion where sophisticated MAGL architectures underperform simple topology-agnostic MLPs. Through systematic empirical and theoretical analysis, we identify that this inversion stems from a fundamental aggregation dilemma characterized by two concurrent pathologies: (1) Representational Pathology (SNR Degradation) - mandatory aggregation dilutes robust intrinsic features with topological noise, causing the noise penalty to outweigh its collaborative benefit; and (2) Optimization Pathology (Gradient Starvation) - topological aggregation attenuates gradient flow, while a shared task loss causes dominant modalities to prematurely suppress weaker ones. To resolve this dilemma, we propose SUPRA (Shared-Unique Prior-Retaining Architecture), a decoupled dual-pathway paradigm. SUPRA processes modality-specific features through topology-agnostic MLPs while capturing structural synergy via a lightweight shared GNN, with auxiliary deep supervision counteracting gradient starvation. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that SUPRA achieves state-of-the-art performance while requiring 3.5x lower peak GPU memory and up to 4.4x faster training time than Multimodal Graph Transformers.

</details>


### [250] [Streaming Reinforcement Learning under Partial Observability with Real-Time Recurrent Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24709)
*Noah Farr,Aryaman Reddi,Carlo D'Eramo,Jan Peters*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对部分可观测流式强化学习的痛点，论文提出了递归轨迹单元（RTU），在保持线性复杂度的前提下实现了精确的实时递归学习，显著提升了流式智能体处理长时记忆任务的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式强化学习（Streaming RL）在部分可观测环境（POMDP）中受到计算复杂度和梯度信息丢失（TBPTT在流式约束下退化为单步）的限制，现有的深度学习手段难以有效处理长时记忆依赖。

Method: 提出递归轨迹单元（RTU），这是一种对角递归架构，能够以与参数量呈线性的时间和内存复杂度实现精确的实时递归学习（RTRL），并将该架构集成到现有的流式强化学习算法中。

Result: 在MemoryChain基准测试中，RTU在长达128步的依赖链上保持性能，而基线算法均失败；在POPGym和部分可观测MuJoCo任务中，该方法在不使用回放缓冲区或批处理的情况下，表现与批处理PPO算法具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 递归轨迹单元（Recurrent Trace Units, RTU）为流式强化学习在部分可观测环境下的应用提供了一种高效且精确的解决方案，克服了传统方法（如TBPTT）在流式约束下的性能崩溃问题，实现了内存和计算效率与参数量呈线性关系，接近了有缓存批处理算法的性能。

Abstract: Streaming reinforcement learning has emerged as an online learning paradigm that conforms to the restrictions of natural learning agents that process data incrementally, i.e. with a batch size of 1 and no replay buffer. While streaming RL has recently been shown to scale with deep function approximation with full observability, partially observable settings have remained out of reach. Truncated backpropagation through time collapses to a one-step gradient horizon under the streaming setting, and exact real-time recurrent learning is prohibitively expensive. We close this gap using recurrent trace units, a diagonal recurrent architecture that enables exact RTRL with linear time and memory complexity in the parameter count, and show that they integrate cleanly into existing streaming algorithms across both discrete and continuous control. On a MemoryChain diagnostic with chain lengths from 2 to 128, our method sustains performance where streaming TBPTT(1) baselines using feedforward, GRU, and RTU networks collapse. On five POPGym tasks and on partially observable MuJoCo continuous control, the streaming approach is competitive with batched PPO on POPGym and recovers a substantial fraction of batched performance on masked MuJoCo, despite using no replay buffer or batched updates.

</details>


### [251] [Hardware-Aware Federated Learning for Speech Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24712)
*Beyazit Bestami Yuksel,Emrah Dikbiyik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种硬件感知联邦学习框架，通过结合硬件分析、Top-K客户端选择和自适应迭代策略，在异构边缘设备上显著降低了情感识别模型的训练耗时与通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习在真实边缘设备部署时，由于客户端在处理能力、内存和通信延迟方面的异构性，导致训练耗时长及系统成本高，需引入硬件感知机制以优化协作训练。

Method: 提出了一种硬件感知联邦学习框架，包含硬件分析（Profiling）、Top-K客户端选择机制以及自适应本地训练迭代次数（Adaptive Local Epochs）三种核心技术。在IEMOCAP数据集的非独立同分布（non-IID）场景下进行了验证。

Result: 与FedAvg、FedProx及随机Top-K选择方法相比，该方法在50轮联邦学习训练中达到0.352的验证准确率，同时缩短约36.5%的训练时间，并降低了40%的累计通信成本。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的硬件感知联邦学习框架通过优化客户端选择和自适应训练策略，在保证情感识别任务精度的同时，显著提升了异构边缘设备环境下的训练效率和资源使用率。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables privacy-preserving collaborative training across distributed edge devices, but real deployments involve heterogeneous clients with different processing power, memory capacity, and communication latency, which often increase round duration and system cost. This paper proposes a hardware-aware federated learning framework for emotion recognition on session-partitioned IEMOCAP that integrates hardware profiling, top-K client selection, and adaptive local epochs within a unified training loop. We compare the method against FedAvg, FedProx, and random top-K selection under a non-IID setup and show that, across 50 federated rounds and 5 independent trials, the proposed approach achieves competitive validation accuracy (0.352), reduces total training time by about 36.5% compared to FedAvg, and lowers cumulative communication cost by 40%.

</details>


### [252] [Reinforcement Learning for Reachability: Guaranteeing Asymptotic Optimality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24740)
*Amogh Palasamudram,Jakub Svoboda,Suguman Bansal,Krishnendu Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进的强化学习框架，利用PAC学习理论通过动态估计MDP参数，成功实现了从接近最优到精确最优的理论收敛，并提供了对收敛过程的深刻理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习处理可达性规范（reachability specifications）的方法虽然能实现渐近收敛，但缺乏对收敛动力学的深层理论洞察，且传统的PAC学习方法在实践中因依赖未知参数而受限。

Method: 提出了一种基于PAC（Probably Approximately Correct）学习的框架，通过在学习过程中迭代地优化和估计未知的MDP内部参数（如最小转移概率），从而克服了经典PAC学习需要预知参数的限制。

Result: 理论上证明了在迭代满足PAC条件下，算法能够收敛到精确的最优策略；实证评估结果验证了该方法在收敛动力学方面的理论洞察。

Conclusion: 通过在强化学习中结合PAC学习与参数估计，该论文论证了在迭代过程中平衡PAC条件，不仅能实现接近最优的策略，最终还能在极限情况下达到精确最优，并揭示了其收敛动力学。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for reachability specifications is fundamental in sequential decision-making, yet theoretical guarantees remain less explored. A recent work achieves asymptotic convergence to optimal policies. However, this approach provides limited insight into convergence dynamics. In this work, we present an alternative approach that provides deeper theoretical insights into convergence. Our approach builds on PAC learning with assumptions. PAC learning guarantees near-optimal policies with high confidence in finite time but requires knowing internal MDP parameters like minimum transition probability. We argue that while these parameters are unknown in RL, they can be iteratively refined and estimated with increasing accuracy. By iteratively satisfying PAC conditions, we show that exact optimality can be achieved in the limit. Empirical evaluations on standard benchmarks validate our theoretical insights into convergence dynamics.

</details>


### [253] [Aligning Molecular Graph Explanations with Chemical Identity via InChIfied Invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24742)
*Emanuele Guidotti,Sara Puglioli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出 InChIfied Invariants，通过利用 InChI 标识符消除不同分子表征带来的不一致性，极大地提高了分子机器学习模型在预测和归因分析方面的稳定性和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分子图特征化方法在处理化学等价但表示形式不同的分子图时，会导致预测结果和解释的不一致，这限制了模型在化学领域的可信度和可靠性。

Method: 提出了一种基于国际化学标识符 (InChI) 的分子特征构建方法——InChIfied Invariants。该方法通过将分子图映射为一系列对化学同一性转换具有不变性的节点、边和图特征，替代传统的 Daylight 标识符。

Result: 在 PubChem 的一百万个分子图实验中，InChIfied Invariants 的一致性达到 99.62%（传统方法仅为 0.35%）；在 MoleculeNet 任务中，该方法在保持预测性能的同时，实现了预测结果和归因解释的高度一致性。

Conclusion: InChIfied Invariants 有效解决了分子图表示在不同化学等价绘图下的不一致性问题，提升了机器学习模型预测和归因解释的稳健性，是现有分子特征化方法的优良替代方案。

Abstract: Obtaining consistent explanations for machine learning on molecular graphs requires predictions and attributions to be aligned with chemical identity. However, chemically equivalent drawings of the same molecule can induce different molecular representations, leading to inconsistent predictions and explanations. Here, we introduce InChIfied Invariants, a class of node, edge, and graph features based on the International Chemical Identifier (InChI) and designed to be invariant under transformations that preserve chemical identity. Using one million molecular graphs from PubChem Substances, we show that InChIfied Invariants produce identical representations for chemically equivalent graphs in 99.62% of cases, whereas standard Daylight invariants do so in only 0.35% of cases. Across MoleculeNet tasks, InChIfied Invariants preserve predictive performance while significantly improving prediction consistency across alternative graph depictions of the same molecules. We further perform a quantitative attribution analysis and show that explanations produced with standard molecular featurization methods vary substantially across chemically equivalent graphs, while InChIfied Invariants enforce consistent attributions by construction. We release open-source software implementing InChIfied Invariants, which can be used as a drop-in replacement for standard molecular graph features.

</details>


### [254] [Bilevel Optimization of Synthetic Trajectories for Multi-Turn LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24743)
*Shresth Verma,Mauricio Tec,Cheol Woo Kim,Kai Wang,Milind Tambe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BOOST 框架通过双层优化实现合成数据的自动重加权，解决了离线强化学习中合成数据质量不均的问题，有效提升了 LLM 在多轮任务中的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型在处理多轮长程交互时表现欠佳，尽管离线强化学习是解决该问题的有效路径，但受限于高质量多轮数据的稀缺性；而现有的数据增强方法未能有效处理合成数据的异构质量问题。

Method: 提出了一种双层优化框架（Bilevel Optimization Framework）：内层利用加权数据对 LLM 进行训练，外层在真实的验证任务上训练一个轻量级的重加权头（Reweighting Head），通过 PAC-Bayesian 界限指导合成数据的自动赋权。

Result: 实验表明，BOOST 能自动为符合真实数据分布且质量较高的合成轨迹分配高权重，显著提升了模型在多轮决策任务中的性能。

Conclusion: BOOST 通过任务导向的重加权机制，有效解决了多轮对话中合成数据质量参差不齐的问题，实现了在不依赖外部评估器的情况下对合成数据的智能筛选与利用，在多轮任务中表现优于现有基准。

Abstract: While LLMs excel at single-turn generation, they struggle with long-horizon, multi-turn interactions. Offline reinforcement learning (RL) offers a scalable approach, yet its performance hinges on the availability and quality of multi-turn trajectory data. A common remedy is to augment training with synthetic trajectories generated by LLMs or simulators, but synthetic data is highly heterogeneous in quality, and naively treating all trajectories as equally informative can degrade performance. We propose BOOST, a bilevel optimization framework where the inner level trains the LLM on reweighted data and the outer level trains a lightweight reweighting head on held-out real validation tasks, assigning continuous trajectory-level weights without requiring an external judge. To ground this approach, we derive a PAC-Bayesian bound revealing a three-way trade-off: synthetic data increases diversity but risks task-shift, while concentrating weight on high-quality trajectories improves empirical performance at the cost of effective sample size. Empirically, our method consistently outperforms multiple baselines. Analysis reveals it upweights synthetic trajectories that align with the real data distribution and exhibit higher qualitative merit.

</details>


### [255] [A Contractive Feedback Semantics for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24759)
*Zuyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于组合式、受限（guarded）反馈电路的折扣强化学习语义理论，通过将贝尔曼变换器视为可组合的开放组件，实现了对 RL 评估、抽象误差和复杂规范分析的统一框架处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的折扣强化学习主要基于封闭马尔可夫决策过程的贝尔曼方程，缺乏模块化和组合式的特性。本文旨在开发一套能够处理开放组件、具备组合性特征的补丁式（compositional）语义框架。

Method: 引入组合视角的强化学习（RL）建模：将单步决策过程视为开放的随机组件，利用 Banach 不动点定理将无限时域策略评估解释为可接受的受限（guarded）Banach 迹（trace）。

Result: 1. 给出了组件级误差在受限上下文中的控制边界（上下文同余）；2. 导出了基于 coalgebraic 图表的状态抽象及其误差边界；3. 建立了基于 Quantale 值合约的安全性及资源规范 Lifting 机制。

Conclusion: 该研究论证了折扣贝尔曼评估可以在受限（guarded）电路的类上实现收缩反馈语义，并展示了该框架在保障上下文同余、状态抽象和定量规范（安全、风险、资源）分析中的有效性。

Abstract: Discounted reinforcement learning is usually presented through Bellman equations on closed Markov decision processes. This paper develops a compositional view: a one-step decision process is treated as an open stochastic component, and infinite-horizon policy evaluation is obtained by closing a contractive feedback loop. The resulting semantics assigns typed Bellman transformers to open components, interprets series and parallel wiring as composition and tensoring of transformers, and interprets feedback as an admissible guarded Banach trace realized by a unique fixed point. This perspective yields three theoretical consequences. First, approximate component equivalence is a contextual congruence for admitted well-typed guarded one-hole contexts: local operator error remains controlled after plugging the component into a surrounding circuit that uses the hole once and whose feedback nodes have certified uniform guardedness. Second, exact and approximate state abstractions become commuting or near-commuting coalgebraic diagrams, giving value-preservation and explicit sup-norm distortion bounds. Third, under monotone $ω$-continuous contract-transformer semantics, safety, risk, and resource specifications can be represented as quantale-valued contracts, where local inductive bounds lift through wiring and feedback by least-fixed-point reasoning. Its central claim is not that all RL morphisms form a global traced monoidal category, but that discounted Bellman evaluation admits a contractive feedback semantics on the admissible class of guarded circuits.

</details>


### [256] [Muon in Vision Transformers: Optimizer-Recipe Interactions and Gradient Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24770)
*Ben S. Southworth,Shuai Jiang,Daniel McBride,Eric C. Cyr,Stephen Thomas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了 Muon 优化器在视觉 Transformer (ViT) 中的应用，发现 Muon 通过与强数据增强方案的紧密配合，能有效拓宽梯度矩阵的谱分布，从而在分类、分割及自监督学习任务中全面超越 AdamW。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon 作为一种新型矩阵感知优化器在 Transformer 中表现出色，但其在视觉 Transformer（ViT）中的行为机制尚不明确，且其对数据增强策略的依赖性缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 通过在 ImageNet-100 和 Pl@ntNet-300K 数据集上对比 Muon 与 AdamW，结合标准视觉训练方案（Mixup, Cutmix 等），并深入分析 ViT 训练过程中梯度矩阵的奇异值结构（SVD）来揭示性能差异的机理。

Result: 1. Muon 在 ViT 训练中持续优于 AdamW，特别是在长尾分布数据集上增益显著。2. Muon 的性能高度依赖强数据增强；缺乏增强会导致深层 MLP 块出现模式崩溃。3. 在强增强下，Muon 能使 QKV 层的梯度在更宽的频谱基底上分布。4. 在图像分割和 MAE 任务中表现出普遍的优越性。

Conclusion: Muon 是一种通过优化器与数据增强策略协同作用来提升 ViT 性能的算法。其核心优势在于能够扩展梯度矩阵的谱分布（特别是在 QKV 层），并依赖强数据增强来防止深层 MLP 块中的模式崩溃。Muon 在分类、分割及 MAE 等任务中均优于 AdamW。

Abstract: Muon is a recently developed matrix-aware optimizer that has shown strong results in transformer training, but its behavior in vision transformers (ViTs) is not yet well understood. We study Muon for ViT training, largely on ImageNet-100 and Pl@ntNet-300K, comparing against AdamW under standard vision recipes involving mixup, cutmix, smoothing, and random augmentation and erasing. Muon consistently outperforms AdamW, with especially large gains on long-tailed Pl@ntNet macro top-1. These gains are also recipe-dependent, where Muon benefits much more than AdamW from advanced and significant data augmentation techniques. To understand this interaction, we analyze the singular-value structure of matrix gradients throughout the ViT. Within Muon training runs, removing heavy data augmentation induces a late-training spectral concentration and mode collapse in gradient matrices, primarily in deep MLP-down blocks. Under a fixed "full" augmentation recipe, the clearest Muon-AdamW contrast appears instead in QKV gradients, where AdamW gradient energy remains concentrated in a much narrower basis while Muon spreads energy across substantially more singular modes. Muon in ViTs is therefore best understood as an optimizer-recipe interaction. Under a fixed recipe, Muon differs from AdamW most clearly in attention projections, where its gradients consist of a broader spectral basis. Within Muon, a full training recipe is important for preventing late spectral concentration and mode collapse in deep feedforward blocks. We further demonstrate efficacy in training ViTs on image segmentation and masked autoencoder models, where Muon outperforms AdamW in all settings considered.

</details>


### [257] [Hermite-NGP: Gradient-Augmented Hash Encoding for Learning PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24774)
*Jinjin He,Zhiqi Li,Sinan Wang,Bo Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Hermite-NGP 通过在 hash grid 中显式存储导数实现解析微分，大幅提升了神经 PDE 求解器的精度及运算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于神经渲染（NGP）的 PDE 求解器在计算空间梯度时，通常依赖于自动微分或有限差分，存在高计算开销、数值不稳定以及难以准确计算高阶导数的问题。

Method: 提出了 Hermite-NGP，这是一种梯度增强的多分辨率哈希编码方法。它在哈希网格顶点显式存储函数值及混合偏导数，利用 Hermite 插值实现梯度、雅可比矩阵和海森矩阵的解析计算，并引入了类多重网格 V-cycle 的多分辨率课程训练策略。

Result: 在 2D 和 3D PDE 基准测试中，误差降低至原有方法的 1/20，收敛速度提升 2 到 10 倍，在 1700 万参数规模下，单轮训练时间仅需 3.5 ms。

Conclusion: Hermite-NGP 通过将导数信息显式存储在空间网格中，并结合多分辨率课程学习，显著提升了求解偏微分方程（PDE）的精度和收敛速度，克服了传统自动微分方法的计算瓶颈和数值不稳定性。

Abstract: We propose Hermite-NGP, a gradient-augmented multi-resolution hash encoding designed to enable fast and accurate computation of spatial derivatives for neural PDE solvers. Unlike existing NGP-based approaches that rely on automatic differentiation or finite differences and suffer from instability or high cost, Hermite-NGP explicitly stores function values and mixed partial derivatives at hash grid vertices, allowing fully analytic evaluation of gradients, Jacobians, and Hessians via Hermite interpolation. This design preserves the efficiency and spatial adaptivity of NGP while supporting analytic differential operators up to second order. We further introduce a multi-resolution curriculum training strategy analogous to multigrid V-cycles to enable coarse-to-fine optimization. Across a range of 2D and 3D PDE benchmarks, Hermite-NGP achieves up to approximately 20 times lower error than prior neural PDE methods, and reduces wall-clock convergence time by 2 to 10 times compared to other solvers, with per-epoch training times as low as 3.5 ms for models with up to 17M parameters.

</details>


### [258] [Complement Submodular Information Measures for Balanced and Robust Data Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24779)
*Rishabh Iyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了“互补次模信息”（CSI）框架，通过在目标函数中引入子集与补集之间的结构关联，增强了子集选择在保持数据集整体结构（如同质性、长尾特征）方面的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统次模优化仅关注所选子集本身的覆盖率与多样性，忽略了子集与剩余数据（补集）之间的结构平衡，导致在需要保持完整数据集语义结构的场景下表现受限。

Method: 提出了“互补次模信息”（Complement Submodular Information, CSI）框架，将其应用于多种经典次模函数（如Facility Location, LogDet等），推导了其在有界曲率条件下的单调性与近 (1-1/e) 贪婪近似保证。

Result: 实验表明，CSI在鲁棒性子集选择任务中表现优于传统方法，能更好地保留稀有/长尾语义结构并抑制噪声，显著提高了下游模型的预测性能。

Conclusion: CSI通过在目标函数中显式建模子集与补集之间的结构信息，有效地解决了传统次模优化忽略补集结构的问题，并实现了更好的样本选择与性能提升。

Abstract: Submodular optimization has become a fundamental paradigm for data selection, retrieval, summarization, and representation learning due to its ability to model coverage, diversity, and representativeness. However, classical submodular objectives optimize only the selected subset and do not explicitly preserve structural information between the selected subset and the remaining data. In many modern machine learning applications, including train/validation/test splitting, benchmark construction, and robust subset selection, the quality of a selection depends critically on preserving balanced structure across both the selected subset and its complement.
  In this work, we introduce Complement Submodular Information (CSI), a new class of complement-aware submodular objectives that quantify shared structural information between a subset and its complement. Our framework induces complement-aware variants of several classical submodular functions including Facility Location, Graph Cut, LogDet, Saturated Coverage, Set Cover, Probabilistic Set Cover, and Feature Based Functions. We analyze the theoretical properties of CSI objectives and show that they exhibit approximate monotonicity under bounded curvature conditions, leading to near-$(1-1/e)$ greedy approximation guarantees.
