  Empirically, CSI objectives consistently outperform standard submodular objectives on robust hidden-slice-aware subset selection. In particular, CSI objectives significantly improve preservation of coherent rare/tail semantic structure while simultaneously suppressing noisy and isolated outliers, leading to substantially improved downstream predictive performance. Synthetic experiments further illustrate how different CSI instantiations capture complementary notions of representativeness, diversity, connectivity, and balanced neighborhood preservation.

</details>


### [259] [The Perception-Physics Paradox: Probing Scientific Alignment with TC-Bench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24782)
*Dingling Yao,Andrea Polesello,Adeel Pervez,Caroline Muller,Francesco Locatello*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了“感知-物理悖论”，指出当前视觉基础模型在卫星遥感中依赖视觉捷径而非底层物理规律。通过引入“科学对齐”框架和 TC-Bench 基准，研究证明仅靠数据扩展无法实现物理一致性，必须在学习过程中显式引入科学约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管视觉基础模型（VFMs）在卫星图像任务中表现良好，但往往依赖视觉相关性而非底层物理规律（“感知-物理悖论”）。这导致模型即使在分布外任务上准确率较高，在科学研究的真实场景中仍不可靠，缺乏鲁棒的推理能力。

Method: 1. 定义“科学对齐”（Scientific Alignment）概念，并以“结构同构性”（Structural Isomorphism）作为可测试的指标，确保潜在表示能唯一标识物理系统。2. 构建一套系统化的物理与因果可解释性探测协议。3. 发布 TC-Bench 数据集（基于热带气旋自动构建，涵盖强极端工况）来量化验证现有模型表现。

Result: 实验表明，现有的主流视觉基础模型在面对热带气旋的极端强度工况时，表现出明显的性能坍塌。这证实了仅通过扩大模型规模无法自动解决科学对齐问题（即模型并未真正学到物理常理）。

Conclusion: 当前视觉基础模型（VFMs）在卫星遥感领域并未自然地通过大规模扩展实现物理学层面的科学对齐。研究证明，仅依赖视觉相关性（快捷方式）会导致模型在极端条件下失效，必须将“科学对齐”作为表示学习的显式目标，通过结构同构性等物理约束来引导模型学习，才能实现真正的科研实用价值。

Abstract: While Vision Foundation Models (VFMs) excel at predictive tasks on satellite imagery, their performance can arise from visual correlations rather than underlying structural invariants, making even perception-based out-of-distribution accuracy a poor proxy for scientific utility. As a result, models may look correct without reasoning correctly, a discrepancy we term the Perception-Physics Paradox. To address this gap, we introduce scientific alignment as an implicit objective for representation learning in scientific domains. We study a principled, testable aspect of scientific alignment through structural isomorphism, which requires latent representations to uniquely identify physical systems up to a linear reparameterization. This perspective induces a hierarchy of necessary conditions and yields a systematic probing protocol for physical and causal interpretability. To operationalize this framework, we release TC-Bench, a global, reproducible benchmark dataset with an automated construction pipeline for tropical cyclone research, and show that current VFMs rely on visual shortcuts that collapse in intense regimes, indicating that scientific alignment does not arise as a natural byproduct of scaling alone.

</details>


### [260] [CONF-KV: Confidence-Aware KV Cache Eviction with Mixed-Precision Storage for Long-Horizon LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24786)
*Yubo Li,Yidi Miao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CONF-KV是一种利用LLM推理不确定性实现动态KV缓存压缩的管理器，能有效解决长序列推理下的显存压力，同时保持极高的信息获取精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着长序列LLM推理，KV缓存已成为显存瓶颈，且现有淘汰策略（如静态窗口或仅基于注意力权重）未能利用模型推理过程中的关键信号——“模型当前的不确定性”。

Method: 提出CONF-KV缓存管理器：1.动态预算分配：将模型下一Token的输出分布转换为置信度得分，根据不确定性评估调整缓存大小；2.淘汰策略：结合累积注意力分布与新近度进行Token排名；3.系统级优化：包含块级在线Softmax注意力、混合精度（FP16/INT8）存储以及金字塔型分层缓存分配方案。

Result: 在4K长度下，困惑度（Perplexity）增幅控制在1.5-2.1点；在32K Needle-in-a-Haystack任务中，检索准确率达到91.4%（显著优于滑动窗口的53.8%和H2O的80.6%）；在VisualWebArena任务中，实现了2.8倍的显存节省并保持了95.3%的性能。

Conclusion: CONF-KV通过引入基于模型不确定性的动态KV缓存管理机制，在保持极低显存占用（接近512窗口）的同时，实现了与全量KV缓存相近的性能，显著优于传统的固定窗口或基于注意力权重的淘汰策略。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM inference turns the key--value (KV) cache into the dominant GPU memory consumer and makes per-token attention increasingly expensive. Many common eviction policies use static recency windows or historical attention, leaving unused a signal computed on every decoding step: the model's current uncertainty. We introduce CONF-KV, a KV-cache manager that converts the next-token distribution into a scalar confidence score and uses it to choose the per-step cache budget, retaining more context when the model is uncertain and pruning aggressively when it is confident. Within each budget, tokens are ranked by a composite of accumulated attention mass and recency, while a protected recent window preserves local coherence. We combine the policy with blockwise online-softmax attention, mixed FP16/INT8 storage, and a pyramidal per-layer budget variant. Across four model families and generated lengths up to 4K, CONF-KV stays near the footprint of a fixed 512-token sliding window while remaining within 1.5--2.1 perplexity points of full KV. On Needle-in-a-Haystack up to 32K tokens, CONF-KV reaches 91.4% retrieval accuracy versus 53.8% for sliding windows and 80.6% for H2O; on 75 VisualWebArena tasks it retains 95.3% of full-KV success at 2.8 times lower peak memory.

</details>


### [261] [Active Learning for Stochastic Contextual Linear Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24803)
*Emma Brunskill,Ishani Karmarkar,Zhaoqi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过主动选择上下文而非被动接收来优化随机线性赌博机学习效率的方法，理论上实现了 $\sqrt{d}$ 倍的效率提升，并在多个实验场景中验证了其样本高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的随机上下文线性赌博机算法多采用被动的上下文采样策略，但在许多实际应用场景中，从业者有能力根据先验知识主动挑选或招募上下文，现有的研究忽略了这种主动采样对提升学习效率的潜力。

Method: 提出了一种针对上下文奖励对进行战略性采样（主动学习）的算法，通过优化上下文的选择过程来替代传统的被动采样方式，并证明了其随实例变化的理论性能保证。

Result: 理论分析表明，主动上下文采样策略在最优性能边界上比传统方法提升了 $\sqrt{d}$ 倍；实验证明在华法林剂量预测和笑话推荐任务中，该算法能显著减少样本需求。

Conclusion: 在随机上下文线性赌博机问题中，主动上下文采样策略不仅在理论上比传统被动采样具有更好的收敛边界（提升达 $\sqrt{d}$ 倍），且在实际应用（如医疗剂量预测、推荐系统）中能显著降低达到近最优策略所需的样本量。

Abstract: A key goal in stochastic contextual linear bandits is to efficiently learn a near-optimal policy. Prior algorithms for this problem learn a policy by strategically sampling actions but naively (passively) sampling contexts from the underlying context distribution. However, in many practical scenarios -- including online content recommendation, survey research, and clinical trials -- practitioners can actively sample or recruit contexts based on prior knowledge of the context distribution. Despite this potential for active learning, the role of strategic context sampling in stochastic contextual linear bandits is underexplored. We propose an algorithm that learns a near-optimal policy by strategically sampling rewards of context-action pairs. We prove instance-dependent theoretical guarantees demonstrating that our active context sampling strategy can improve over the minimax rate by up to a factor of $\sqrt{d}$, where $d$ is the linear dimension. We show empirically that our algorithm reduces the number of samples needed to learn a near-optimal policy, in tasks such as warfarin dose prediction and joke recommendation.

</details>


### [262] [Disentangled Double Machine Learning for Accurate Causal Effect Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24808)
*Guodu Xiang,Kui Yu,Yujie Wang,Richang Hong,Fuyuan Cao,Jiye Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DDML通过对协变量进行因果角色解耦及对残差进行正交化处理，解决了传统DML在因果推断中因干扰函数估计不准引发的偏差问题，提升了算法的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统双重机器学习（DML）在处理高维或有限样本时，由于无法区分潜在因子导致干扰函数估计不准确，进而引发处理残差与结果误差之间的依赖性，导致因果效应估计产生偏差和不稳定性。

Method: 提出了“解耦双重机器学习（DDML）”框架，包含两个核心模块：1. 因果角色解耦策略，将协变量分解为混杂因子、处理特定因子和结果特定因子以优化干扰函数估计；2. 残差依赖正交化策略，用于减少因干扰函数估计误差导致的残差相关性。

Result: 在合成数据、半合成数据和真实世界数据集上的实验结果表明，DDML在MAE和RMSE指标上显著优于13种主流基准算法。

Conclusion: DDML通过因果角色解耦和残差依赖正交化技术，有效解决了DML在处理高维或小样本数据时的偏差和不稳定性问题，显著提升了因果效应估计的精度。

Abstract: Confounding bias is a key challenge in causal effect estimation from observational data. Double Machine Learning (DML) addresses this issue by estimating treatment and outcome nuisance functions, constructing treatment and outcome residuals, and estimating causal effects from the residuals. However, DML often produces biased and unstable estimates in highdimensional or finite-sample scenarios. One reason is that DML estimates nuisance functions using all covariates without disentangling distinct latent factors, resulting in unreliable nuisance function estimation. Another is that imprecise nuisance estimation further introduces residual dependence between the treatment residual and the remaining outcome error, undermining the accuracy of causal effect estimates. To address these issues, in this paper, we propose Disentangled Double Machine Learning (DDML), a novel algorithm that integrates two key strategies. First, a causal role disentanglement strategy decomposes covariates into confounders, treatment-specific factors, and outcomespecific factors for enabling reliable nuisance function estimation. And second, a residual dependence orthogonalization strategy mitigates residual dependence caused by nuisance estimation errors for enhancing the precision of causal effect estimates. Experimental results on synthetic, semi-synthetic, and real-world datasets demonstrate that DDML significantly outperforms 13 state-of-the-art baseline algorithms in both MAE and RMSE.

</details>


### [263] [DriftingMol: Decoder-Coupled Drift for One-Pass Property-Conditional Molecular Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24841)
*Jiangjie Qiu,Yijun Li,Wentao Li,Xiaonan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DriftingMol通过将梯度反向传播至预训练解码器特征，实现了一种高效、精确的属性条件分子生成方法，在降低计算开销的同时提升了生成分子的属性相关性和独特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的属性条件分子生成方法在平衡生成质量（有效性、多样性）与属性控制精度时，往往面临计算成本高或性能不佳的问题。

Method: 提出DriftingMol框架，采用两阶段生成方案：首先使用固定的SELFIES beta-VAE构建潜在空间；随后利用解码器耦合漂移（decoder-coupled drift）技术，将漂移梯度通过解码器特征映射反向传播至DiT（Diffusion Transformer）生成器。

Result: 在ZINC250K数据集上，DriftingMol在QED和多属性条件生成任务中表现出色（QED Spearman相关性最高达0.598），且在保证高唯一性（94.7%）的同时，仅需单次生成器评估和单次解码器前向传递，显著降低了采样成本。

Conclusion: Decoder-coupled drift是一种高效的低成本机制，能够实现精确的属性条件分子生成，且无需昂贵的推断时优化，同时保持了高分子独特性。

Abstract: Property-conditional molecular generation should produce valid, diverse molecules while responding to continuous target values at low sampling cost. We introduce DriftingMol, a two-stage framework that adapts drifting models to a SELFIES latent molecular space. A frozen SELFIES beta-VAE provides the latent space, and the hidden representation of its decoder serves as the drift feature map. In decoder-coupled drift, decoder weights remain fixed, but drift gradients are backpropagated through the decoder feature map to a DiT generator, inducing a pullback metric aligned with molecular decoding. On ZINC250K, the default setting achieves QED Spearman correlation 0.493 with 94.7% uniqueness, while the strongest decoder-coupled condition reaches 0.510. Under protocol-matched four-property conditioning, decoder-coupled drift reaches mean Spearman correlation up to 0.598. Across 15 controlled variants, models that preserve the gradient path through decoder features achieve higher correlations than the tested latent-space, random-feature, and external-feature drift variants, while detached or stop-gradient decoder controls yield near-zero QED correlation and very low uniqueness. These results indicate that decoder-coupled drift is a useful low-cost mechanism for property-biased molecular generation, requiring one generator evaluation and one frozen decoder pass.

</details>


### [264] [Tiny Brains, Giant Impact: Uncovering the Keystone Neurons of LLM with Just a Few Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24846)
*Xiangtian Ji,Yuxin Chen,Zhengzhou Cai,Xiang Wang,An Zhang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现了LLM中的“基石神经元”（Keystone Neurons），证明了一小部分稀疏且稳定的神经元决定了模型能力，并据此提出一种仅更新这些关键神经元的微调策略，实现了高效能与性能的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）能力的内部工作机制尚不明确，缺乏对模型核心能力承载单元的系统性认识。

Method: 1. 跨任务激活分析：在多种开源Transformer模型中识别不同能力维度下的高激活神经元；2. 神经元剔除实验：通过移除识别出的极端稀疏子集（基石神经元）来验证其必要性；3. 提出Keystone Tuning：一种仅更新基石神经元参数的高效微调方法。

Result: 1. 成功识别出“基石神经元”：即使在极度稀疏的情况下，移除该子集会导致模型行为发生显著崩溃；2. 证实该机制在预训练阶段形成且权重高度校准；3. 基石微调在显著降低参数更新量（远小于全参数微调）的情况下，实现了同等甚至更优的下游任务性能，并有效缓解了灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: 基石神经元是模型内源性且稳定的关键组件，其预训练后的精确参数对模型表现至关重要。仅针对该子集进行微调（Keystone Tuning）可在保持通用能力的同时实现高效的任务适应。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) display strong comprehensive abilities, yet the internal mechanisms that support these behaviors remain insufficiently understood. In this work, we show that across a wide range of open-weight Transformers, a subset of neurons remains consistently highly activated during inference across tasks of multiple capability dimensions. By probing along the cross-task activation strength, an extremely sparse subset is isolated, whose removal causes a collapse in model behavior, which we term keystone neurons. Our analysis reveals that keystone neurons are a stable and intrinsic neuron subset of the model that is largely established during pretraining. The parameters associated with these neurons are tightly calibrated during the training process, and their precise values are critical for the capabilities of the model. Building on these insights, we propose a supervised fine-tuning approach that updates only keystone neurons, achieving task gains comparable to or even better than full-parameter fine-tuning while better preserving performance in other capability dimensions, despite modifying a much smaller number of parameters.

</details>


### [265] [The Concept Allocation Zone: Tracking How Concepts Form Across Transformer Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24856)
*James Henry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了Transformer中概念形成是跨越多层的渐进过程，并提出了“概念分配区”（CAZ）框架来精准量化概念在深度方向上的演化与因果作用，超越了传统单一“最佳层”的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机械可解释性研究往往过于关注“最佳层”（Peak Layer），这种单一视角的快照不足以解释Transformer模型中概念形成的完整动态过程，需要更细致的框架来刻画概念在深层结构中的演化。

Method: 引入“概念分配区”（CAZ）概念，并开发了三个层级指标（分离度、概念一致性、概念速度）来识别和界定CAZ，无需手动逐层扫描；通过对34个模型、7个概念的实证评估，结合消融实验进行因果验证。

Result: 研究发现分离曲线（Separation Curve）常呈多模态，且存在传统峰值检测无法发现的“温和CAZ”，这些区域在93-100%的消融实验中表现出因果活性。验证了跨架构对齐方式是基于深度匹配的，而非单层的。

Conclusion: 概念形成是一个跨越Transformer多层的渐进式过程，而非单一层的事件。该研究提出的“概念分配区”（CAZ）能够比传统的“最佳层”方法更准确地捕捉模型在残差流中组织几何结构以实现概念可分性的过程。

Abstract: Concept formation in transformer language models is depth-extended, not a single-layer event: concepts emerge gradually across a contiguous region of the residual stream. Mechanistic interpretability methods identify the single layer of peak class separation -- the "best layer" -- capturing a snapshot rather than the process itself. We introduce the Concept Allocation Zone (CAZ): the depth interval within which a concept becomes measurably separable, the region allocated to its geometric expression. We formalize the CAZ through three layer-wise metrics (Separation, Concept Coherence, Concept Velocity) and derive principled boundary detection without manual layer sweeps. A CAZ is not a concept: it is the depth region within which the model organizes its geometry to make a concept separable. A single concept typically participates in multiple CAZes; multiple concepts may share one. Empirical validation across 34 models from 8 architectural families and 7 concepts reveals that the separation curve S(l) is frequently multimodal. A scored detector uncovers "gentle CAZes" -- subtle allocation regions invisible to standard peak detection but causally active in 93-100% of cases under ablation (16 of 34 models; 26 in the companion validation paper). The framework generates seven testable predictions; four yield clear verdicts (two not supported, one partially supported, one supported), one had its precondition invalidated by the data, and two are underpowered -- with cross-architecture alignment confirmed as depth-matched rather than monolithic under leave-one-concept-out cross-validation. Reference implementation: rosetta_tools v1.3.1 (doi:10.5281/zenodo.20361433).

</details>


### [266] [Unifying Value Alignment and Assignment in Cross-Domain Offline Reinforcement Learning with Heterogeneous Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24862)
*Zhongjian Qiao,Jiafei Lyu,Chenjia Bai,Peisong Wang,Siyang Gao,Shuang Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对异构跨域离线RL中的“值误分配”问题，本文提出了V2A框架，通过模态感知学习与价值校正策略，有效提升了跨域策略迁移的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在异构跨域离线强化学习中，现有的数据过滤方法未考虑多源域和多样化行为策略带来的“值误分配”问题，导致价值对齐受损及数据过滤偏差，进而降低策略性能。

Method: 提出V2A框架，包括：1. 采用时间一致的模态表征学习提取动态模态；2. 使用模态感知的优势学习（modality-aware advantage learning）以校正价值对齐；3. 采用数据过滤范式选择性地共享源域数据进行策略学习。

Result: V2A方法在多个异构跨域离线强化学习设置中显著优于现有的强基线方法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的V2A框架有效地解决了异构跨域离线强化学习中的值误分配（value misassignment）问题，通过结合动态对齐、价值对齐和价值分配，实现了高效的跨域策略迁移，在复杂场景下优于现有方法。

Abstract: Cross-domain offline reinforcement learning (RL) aims to learn a policy in the target domain with a limited target domain dataset and a source domain dataset that exhibits a dynamics shift. Training directly on the original source dataset typically leads to performance collapse. Recent studies perform data filtering from the perspective of dynamics alignment or value alignment to enable efficient policy transfer. However, these studies are typically validated on single-domain or single-behavior-policy source datasets. In this work, we explore a more general heterogeneous cross-domain offline RL setting, where the source datasets may be collected from multiple source domains by diverse behavior policies. We first uncover a critical yet overlooked issue in this setting: value misassignment. Empirically and theoretically, we demonstrate that value misassignment can undermine value alignment, mislead data filtering toward selecting suboptimal samples, and loosen the suboptimality gap, thereby degrading the agent's performance. To address this issue, we propose V2A, which integrates dynamics alignment, value alignment, and value assignment. V2A first employs temporally-consistent modality representation learning to extract dynamics modalities from the source dataset, followed by modality-aware advantage learning to rectify value alignment. Finally, it adopts a data filtering paradigm to selectively share source data for policy learning. Empirical results show that V2A significantly outperforms strong baseline methods under general heterogeneous cross-domain offline RL settings.

</details>


### [267] [Cluster Frequency Conformal Prediction for Local Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24872)
*Tomer Lavi,Bracha Shapira,Nadav Rappoport*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多类分类中类别覆盖不均匀的问题，提出CFCP框架，通过利用特征空间的局部聚类频率分布来优化局部有效性，在提升各类覆盖均衡的同时保持了良好的预测效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的共形预测方法虽然提供分布无关的覆盖保证，但在多类分类任务中可能导致特定类别或子群体覆盖不足，难以满足高风险应用的安全需求。

Method: 提出了一种名为“簇频率共形预测（CFCP）”的插件框架。该方法首先对学习到的嵌入进行聚类，利用校准数据估计聚类的标签频率分布；对于每个测试点，通过对邻近聚类的分布进行软混合（辅以全局先验及可靠性收缩）来构建样本特有的概率向量，最后利用标准集构造器进行共形化处理。

Result: 在多个图像和文本数据集的对比中，CFCP在15/16项基准测试中实现了最优的类别覆盖率，并保持了极具竞争力的预测集大小效率，部分场景下表现远超基准方法。

Conclusion: CFCP通过聚合表示空间的簇频率信息，提供了一种有效且可靠的局部信号，能够显著改善多类共形预测中的类别覆盖率和整体预测效率。

Abstract: Conformal prediction provides distribution-free coverage guarantees, but in many-class classification it may still under-cover specific classes or subpopulations, preventing safe deployment in high-stakes applications. We propose Cluster Frequency Conformal Prediction (CFCP), a plug-in framework that adapts conformal prediction to local structure in a learned representation space. CFCP clusters learned embeddings, estimates cluster-level label-frequency distributions from calibration data, and for each test point constructs a sample-specific probability vector by softly mixing nearby cluster distributions regularized with global-prior and reliability-aware shrinkage. This vector is then conformalized using standard set constructors. In the disjoint-split regime, CFCP inherits standard finite-sample marginal validity. Under additional assumptions, CFCP further admits a local-validity interpretation. Since representation clusters aggregate locally similar samples, their empirical class frequencies provide a stable estimate of local label ambiguity. Across image and text benchmarks, CFCP achieves the best class coverage in 15/16 dataset/score-family comparisons and a competitive prediction set size efficiency, with several settings substantially more efficient. Overall, our results show that cluster-frequency information provides an effective localized signal for improving classwise reliability in many-class conformal prediction.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [268] [MemForest: An Efficient Agent Memory System with Hierarchical Temporal Indexing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23986)
*Han Chen,Zining Zhang,Wenqi Pei,Bingsheng He,Ming Wu,Jason Zeng,Michael Heinrich,Wei Wu,Hongbao Zhang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: MemForest 是一个针对长上下文 LLM Agent 的记忆框架，通过引入 MemTree 分层索引和并行更新机制，克服了传统系统全量重写带来的高延迟问题，显著提升了记忆构建的吞吐量与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM Agent 的记忆系统存在粗粒度的状态管理和串行更新管道，导致大规模记忆积累时维护成本高、延迟长，且频繁的全量更新限制了可扩展性。

Method: 1. 将记忆架构重构为“写入高效的临时数据管理”问题；2. 设计并行块提取（Parallel Chunk Extraction）机制，解耦记忆构建过程；3. 引入 MemTree，一种层级时间索引结构，将全局状态重写转变为局部路径更新。

Result: 在 LongMemEval-S 基准测试中达到 79.8% 的 pass@1 准确率，同时记忆构建吞吐量比当前最先进系统（如 EverMemOS）高出约 6 倍。

Conclusion: MemForest 通过采用分层时间索引和并行处理机制，高效地解决了长上下文 LLM Agent 在记忆维护方面的性能瓶颈，实现了高吞吐量与保持高准确率的平衡。

Abstract: Memory is a fundamental component for enabling long-context LLM agents, supporting persistent state across interactions through a continuous serve-and-update lifecycle. Despite substantial prior work, existing systems suffer from significant maintenance overhead due to two key limitations: coarse-grained state management and inherently sequential update pipelines. In particular, updates are often tightly coupled with LLM inference and require full-state rewrites, leading to poor scalability and growing latency as memory accumulates. To address these challenges, we present MemForest, a memory framework that reformulates agent memory as a write-efficient temporal data management problem. MemForest breaks the sequential bottleneck via parallel chunk extraction, decoupling memory construction into concurrent, independent operations. To further eliminate coarse-grained maintenance, we introduce MemTree, a hierarchical temporal index that organizes memory as time-ordered trees rather than flat global summaries. This design replaces full-state rewrites with localized per-node updates, reducing maintenance cost to the affected tree paths while naturally preserving temporally evolving states. We evaluate MemForest on two long-context memory benchmarks, LongMemEval-S and LoCoMo. On LongMemEval-S, MemForest achieves the best overall performance among stateful baselines, reaching 79.8% pass@1 accuracy while sustaining a memory construction throughput approximately 6x higher than state-of-the-art approaches including EverMemOS.

</details>


### [269] [Extending the (Elementary) Mathematical Data Model and MatBase with two new constraint types: inexistence and anti-existence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24021)
*Christian Mancas*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文扩展了（初等）数学数据模型，引入了两种新的约束类型（inexistence 和 anti-existence），通过形式化定义和高效算法设计，实现了对总计 7 种存在性约束的管理与自动化代码生成，并在 MatBase 系统中得到了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了弥补现有（初等）数学数据模型在存在与非存在约束方面的不足，通过引入对偶约束类型来增强数据模型表达能力和数据完整性校验能力。

Method: 提出了两种新的约束类型（inexistence 和 anti-existence）及其四个子类，并对所有 7 种约束子类的良构性、可满足性、一致性和最小性进行了形式化分析；设计并证明了恒定复杂度的管理算法和线性的约束强制执行算法；在 MatBase 原型系统中进行了实现。

Result: 开发了处理 7 种存在性相关约束的算法，证明了这些算法在理论上达到最优（恒定或线性复杂度），并成功在 MatBase 智能数据知识库管理系统原型中实现了自动代码生成。

Conclusion: 该研究成功扩展了（初等）数学数据模型中的约束类型，证明了所提出的 7 种存在性约束及其算法在理论上的完备性、最优性及系统实现的有效性。

Abstract: This research paper introduces two new constraint types and four subtypes of database constraints added to our (Elementary) Mathematical Data Model, which are the duals of the existence and non-existence ones. They are formally defined, characterized, and exemplified with real-life instances. The well-formedness, satisfiability, coherence, and minimality of sets of all 7 subtypes of existence constraints are studied. Corresponding SQL-embedded pseudocode algorithms for managing such sets are provided and proved to be of constant complexity, sound, complete, and optimal. Also provided are algorithms for enforcing these new types of constraints, called inexistence and anti-existence. Their characterization proves that they have linear complexity in the sum of the arities of the function (Cartesian product)s involved, and are sound, complete, and optimal as well. All these algorithms were implemented in both versions of our intelligent data and knowledge base management system prototype MatBase, which automatically generates code for enforcing all the 7 subtypes of existence constraints.

</details>


### [270] [AvalancheBench: Evaluating Enterprise Data Agents Through Latent World Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24183)
*Darek Kleczek,Fuheng Zhao,Alexander W. Lee,Julien Tissier,Pawel Liskowski,Ugur Cetintemel,Anupam Datta*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: AvalancheBench 是一个通过“潜在世界恢复”评估企业数据智能体分析深度和逻辑准确性的新型基准，旨在诊断智能体在解析数据背后复杂逻辑关系时的真实能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多关注工作流完成度或报告的表面合理性，而非分析的深度与逻辑严密性。缺乏一种能够量化“早期分析错误如何导致后续结论偏差”的受控环境。

Method: 通过从已知的“潜在世界（latent world）”生成观测数据来构建基准，评估智能体对数据背后结构（如关键片段、驱动因素、时间事件及逻辑关系）的恢复能力，而非仅仅评估工作流执行结果。

Result: 在电子商务用例中，领先的编程智能体仅能恢复 26% 的分析准则，且分析失误表现出明显的聚集性，如错误细分客户和合并时间事件。

Conclusion: AvalancheBench 揭示了现有企业数据智能体在分析深度和准确性上的严重不足（仅能恢复 26% 的分析准则），其在处理客户细分和时间序列事件合并方面表现较弱，证明了该基准在诊断智能体分析能力方面的有效性。

Abstract: We introduce AvalancheBench, a benchmark for evaluating enterprise data agents through \emph{latent world recovery}. AvalancheBench improves on existing benchmarks in three ways. First, it evaluates analytical understanding rather than pipeline completion: systems are scored on whether they recover the segments, drivers, temporal events, and relationships that explain the data, not merely on whether they execute a workflow or produce a plausible report. Second, it provides ground truth for goal-driven analytics by generating observations from a known latent world, enabling partial credit for incomplete but valid recoveries. Third, it exposes how early analytical mistakes propagate into later conclusions: missed segments, merged events, or wrong attributions can lead to systematically wrong recommendations. In this sense, AvalancheBench complements real-data benchmarks by providing a controlled setting for diagnosing whether agents recover the analytical structure behind enterprise data. On a first e-commerce use case, the strongest configuration of a leading coding agent recovers only 26\% of the rubric, with failures concentrated in generic customer segmentations and merged temporal events.

</details>


### [271] [LEARNT: A Practical Estimator for Cardinality of LIKE Queries with Formal Accuracy Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24308)
*Hai Lan,Zhifeng Bao,Divesh Srivastava,Shixun Huang,Yuwei Peng,Yang Yu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: LEARNT 是一种专为 LIKE 查询设计的基数估计器，通过创新的桶分类架构、高效的过滤器存储和马尔可夫模型，在保持低内存和快速构建的同时，显著提升了前缀、后缀及子串查询的估计准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LIKE 查询基数估计方法在准确性、查询处理开销和对不同查询模式（前缀、后缀、子串）的适应性上存在局限，急需一种具备鲁棒性和理论保证的高效估计方案。

Method: 1. 构建桶分类（bucket-classification）模型进行基数估计；2. 使用内存高效的桶分层过滤器架构（含布隆过滤器和辅助表）；3. 针对空查询，采用专门的过滤器和前缀遍历策略；4. 通过马尔可夫建模处理任意长查询字符串；5. 建立理论框架以优化参数选择以平衡存储与精度。

Result: 在四个真实数据集上的实验表明，LEARNT 的平均 Q-error 比 CLIQUE 和 LPLM 低 1.3-1.7 倍，尾部误差更小，构建速度快达 70 倍，且内存占用具有竞争力。

Conclusion: LEARNT 通过在实际工作负载中结合桶分类、布隆过滤器和马尔可夫模型，实现了对 LIKE 查询基数估计的高准确性、低开销以及理论保证，性能优于当前主流方法（CLIQUE 和 LPLM）。

Abstract: We study the problem of cardinality estimation for LIKE queries on string data, focusing on the most common patterns in real workloads: prefix, suffix, and substring queries. We propose LEARNT, a LIKE query Estimator with Accuracy, Robustness, Negligible overhead, Tunability, and Theoretical guarantees. LEARNT formulates estimation as a bucket-classification problem, and upon correct classification, it yields formal bounds on Q-error for the queries with non-empty answer. It employs a memory-efficient bucketed layered-filter architecture with Bloom filters and compact auxiliary tables, together with optimizations that exploit query skew to reduce storage. For the queries that have empty answer, LEARNT incorporates dedicated filter-based and prefix-walk strategies, providing probabilistic guarantees on correct identification. Furthermore, to support arbitrarily long query strings, we extend LEARNT with Markov modeling scheme that composes short-query statistics into estimates for longer queries. A theoretical framework guides parameter selection to minimize storage under accuracy and robustness constraints. Extensive experiments on four real-world datasets show that LEARNT consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods such as CLIQUE and LPLM, achieving 1.3-1.7x lower mean Q-error, significantly lower tail errors, and up to 70x faster construction with comparable memory usage.

</details>


### [272] [Top-k Approximate Functional Dependency Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24925)
*Xiaolong Wan,Xixian Han*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 针对传统阈值驱动的近似函数依赖（AFD）发现存在的缺陷，本文提出了全局Top-k挖掘方案，通过论证并放弃极小性要求，设计了高效的TALE-Opt算法，显著提升了挖掘效率与结果可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于阈值的AFD发现方法存在输出规模不可控、阈值选取困难以及度量指标受LHS维度影响等问题，需要一种更直接、可控的全局Top-k挖掘方法。

Method: 1. 确立了基于$\mu^+$的全局Top-k AFD发现范式；2. 设计了TALE-Base算法（基于广度优先搜索的基准算法）；3. 设计了TALE-Opt算法（采用Apriori风格生成、计算复用及基于单调性和乐观上界的剪枝策略）。

Result: 实验表明，在41个真实数据集上，TALE-Opt算法实现了高达99.81%的剪枝率，相较TALE-Base算法最高可获得78.81倍的加速。

Conclusion: 提出了TALE框架用于全局Top-k近似函数依赖（AFD）发现，通过放弃极小性约束，实现了高效且可控的结果输出，并在实际数据集中验证了其显著的性能优势。

Abstract: Approximate functional dependencies (AFDs) relax exact functional dependencies by tolerating a bounded degree of violation, making them suited for data quality auditing. Threshold-based discovery returns all dependencies above a user-specified cutoff, but output size is uncontrollable, the right threshold varies across datasets, and widely used measures are sensitive to LHS dimensionality. We study global top-$k$ AFD discovery, where neither the LHS nor the RHS is fixed and the $k$ strongest dependencies under $μ^+$ are returned directly. The cross-attribute comparability of $μ^+$ makes such a global ranking well-defined. We prove a Triangle Incompatibility Theorem showing that minimality, global top-$k$ ranking, and exact-$k$ output cannot simultaneously hold under any non-monotonic scoring function, justifying the removal of the minimality requirement. We present two algorithms: TALE-Base, which returns the exact global top-$k$ result by exhaustive level-wise evaluation, and TALE-Opt, which reduces computation through Apriori-style candidate generation, LHS computation reuse, and two complementary pruning rules exploiting exact FD monotonicity and an optimistic upper bound on $μ^+$. Experiments on 41 real-world datasets show that TALE-Opt achieves pruning ratios up to 99.81\% and speedups over TALE-Base up to 78.81$\times$.

</details>


### [273] [CS-PQ: Cache-Friendly SIMD Product Quantization for Large-Scale ANNS Index Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25521)
*Y. T. Ma,K. C. Huang,X. K. Jiang,M. L. Wang,X. Yao,R. H. Chen,G. Zhang,Z. L. Shao*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 针对现有 PQ（乘积量化）构建在 CPU 上效率低下的问题，本文提出了 CS-PQ 框架，通过创新的面向质心的 SIMD 向量化及缓存优化技术，显著加速了向量索引构建过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向量维度和规模的激增使得现有 PQ 构建方法面临挑战：现有的 GPU 方案受限于“一对一”执行模式导致数据传输开销巨大，而通用的 CPU 方案未能针对 PQ 构建的内在特性进行优化。

Method: 1. 提出面向向量的 SIMD（Vector-oriented SIMD）范式：通过跨 PQ 质心（Centroids）而非子向量维度（Subvector dimensions）进行向量化，解耦了量化粒度与 SIMD 宽度；2. 重构执行流水线以提升缓存局部性（Cache locality）；3. 重构 PQ 计算逻辑，在保证正确性的前提下消除冗余操作。

Result: 在在大规模数据集上的实验表明，CS-PQ 相较于目前最先进的 CPU 实现方案，在不牺牲 ANNS 准确性的前提下，性能提升最高可达 10.7 倍。

Conclusion: CS-PQ 通过优化缓存利用率、SIMD 向量化范式和计算逻辑，有效解决了 CPU 环境下 PQ 构建的性能瓶颈，在保持极高准确性的同时实现了显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Product Quantization (PQ) construction is deeply integrated into vector index construction for Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search (ANNS). The rapid growth in vector dimensionality and volume has significantly increased the computational cost of PQ. Existing GPU-based PQ accelerations are ill-suited for PQ construction due to its "one-to-one" execution pattern (one compute, one data load, i.e., data transfer overhead dominates). Although CPU-based solutions are prevalent, they are essentially general-purpose designs that fail to capture the intrinsic characteristics of PQ construction.In this paper, we propose CS-PQ, a Cache-friendly, SIMD-optimized PQ framework based on modern CPUs. CS-PQ introduces a vector-oriented SIMD paradigm that decouples quantization granularity from SIMD width by vectorizing across PQ centroids rather than subvector dimensions. It further restructures the execution pipeline to improve cache locality and reformulates PQ computation to eliminate redundant operations while preserving correctness. Experiments on large-scale datasets show that CS-PQ achieves up to 10.7 times speedup over state-of-the-art CPU-based PQ implementations without sacrificing ANNS accuracy.

</details>


### [274] [Same Data, Different Schemas: Robustness of LLM-based Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25838)
*Nitin Kanchinadam,Aditya Menachery,Amol Deshpande*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出一种评估Text-to-SQL模型对不同模式结构鲁棒性的框架，发现模型在逻辑等价的模式间表现极不稳定，即使补充额外的模式规范信息也难以完全解决该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在Text-to-SQL任务上表现出色，但尚不清楚其在面对同一底层数据但不同模式结构表示时的鲁棒性；目前缺乏系统性的方法来评估这种模式敏感性。

Method: 提出一个基于通用E/R（实体-关系）模型生成逻辑等价关系模式的评测框架。通过改变将概念设计转化为关系时的“分片”（shredding）方式，在保持语义、问题和数据不变的前提下，创建多种结构差异化的模式变体，并利用对偶比较热图分析四大主流LLM的性能一致性。

Result: 实验证明模式结构对LLM行为有显著影响；即使模式在概念上等价，模型输出的查询结果往往大相径庭。提供E/R规范上下文虽有提升效果，但无法完全消除这种不一致性。

Conclusion: 当前Text-to-SQL评测忽视了对模式鲁棒性的考察。该框架不仅揭示了模型的一致性缺陷，还通过模式变体为数据增强训练和生成候选查询计划提供了新思路，有助于提升模型性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) consistently achieve strong results on text-to-SQL benchmarks, but their robustness to schema variations remains poorly understood. Recent work suggests that the schema structure matters, but does not provide a clear and systematic way to evaluate model behavior when different schemas represent the same underlying data. We address this problem by presenting a framework to evaluate and benchmark text-to-SQL techniques over equivalent relational schemas generated from a common E/R model. By varying the ``shredding'' choices used to translate the conceptual design into relations, we create multiple schema variants that differ structurally while preserving the same underlying semantics. This gives us a controlled setting in which the natural language questions and data remain fixed, and only the schema changes. We use this framework to evaluate four leading LLMs on the same questions across multiple schema variants (for two separate domains), and summarize consistency patterns using pairwise comparison heatmaps. Our results show that schema structure significantly affects LLM behavior: across conceptually equivalent schemas, models often produce SQL queries with very different answers. We also find that providing additional context (specifically, the original E/R specification) improves the performance, but does not fully ameliorate the inconsistencies. In addition to demonstrating that the current text-to-SQL evaluations miss an important notion of robustness, our framework provides a way to generate a large number of synthetic datasets that can be used to train new models, and suggests a mechanism to make text-to-SQL more robust by generating additional candidate plans for a given natural language query through systematic schema variations.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [275] [Efficient Uniform Sampling of Surjections via their Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24068)
*Arnaud Carayol,Pablo Rotondo*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对随机满射及其“剖面”结构的高效、最优采样算法，突破了现有对 $n/k$ 比值的限制，并解决了随机映射采样领域的特定公开问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对从 $[n]$ 到 $[k]$ 的随机满射采样问题，现有通常假设（如 $\frac{n}{k}$ 为常数）限制了算法的适用性。本文旨在开发一套无需特定比例约束、且对于所有 $n \geq k$ 均具有统一性能保证的高效采样算法。

Method: 1. 利用“剖面”结构简化采样问题，将随机满射的采样转化为对其剖面分布的采样。
2. 将参数空间划分为三个区域，根据不同区域的特性优化重采样率和采样开销。
3. 基于精确尺寸的玻尔兹曼采样器（Boltzmann samplers）实现，并根据参数区域采取针对性的分配策略。

Result: 1. 提供了一套覆盖整个参数空间 ($n \geq k$) 的最优随机满射剖面采样算法。
2. 算法的时间复杂度在输出的期望长度上达到了对数因子内的最优。
3. 解决了 Devroye 和 Los (2025) 关于 $k > n/\log n$ 时随机映射剖面采样的公开问题。

Conclusion: 本文提出的算法能够在大范围的参数空间 ($n \geq k$) 内高效且最优地对随机满射及其“剖面”(profile) 进行采样。这不仅在理论上填补了该领域的部分空白，而且具有实际可实现的价值，同时还解决了随机映射剖面采样方面的公开问题。

Abstract: In this article, we develop efficient sampling algorithms for random surjections from $[n]$ to $[k]$ for all $n \geq k$. We make no assumption about $n$ and $k$. In particular, we do not make the common assumption that the ratio $\frac{n}{k}$ is constant. All our guarantees are uniform in $n$ and $k$.
  Our first insight is that all the complexity in sampling random surjections is captured by sampling a smaller structure which we call the \emph{profile} of the surjection. More precisely, the profile associates to each occurring preimage size $s$ the number of preimages of size $s$. Using standard techniques, we show that the problem of sampling surjections reduces to sampling the profile with the induced distribution. This is partly explained by the fact that profiles are always sublinear, with at most $\sqrt{2n}$ entries in the worst case.
  We provide a complete set of algorithms to directly sample the \emph{profile} of a random surjection with the induced distribution, covering the full parameter space. These algorithms are shown to be optimal up to logarithmic factors in the expected size of the output. Our algorithms are based on exact-size Boltzmann samplers, which are standard rejection-based samplers. We partition the parameter space into three main regions. In each region, we optimize both the rejection rate and the cost of each sampling round.
  Profiles capture a number of relevant statistics of random surjections and might be of independent interest. In a related context, profiles have been recently studied by Devroye et al. for random mappings. As a spin-off result, we answer an open question from Devroye and Los '25 by providing an optimal algorithm also for the profiles of a random mapping when $k > n/\log n$.
  The results of this article are not only of theoretical interest but lead to samplers implementable in practice.

</details>


### [276] [A Tight Bound on Localization of Electrical Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24130)
*Ori Gurel-Gurevich,Asaf Nachmias,Sushant Sachdeva*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文改进了图论中关于单位电流分布的数学界限，将上界从O(log² n)降低至O(log n)，并指出该结果在常数范围内是最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为图上的电学性质（特别是电流分布）提供更紧凑的数学界限，以优化谱图理论中的相关算法性能。

Method: 通过数学证明方法，优化了谱图理论中有关电流分布的分析，改进了先前Schild-Rao-Srivastava (2018)提出的O(log² n)界限。此外，通过人机协作（ChatGPT协助证明及作者人工重写）验证了证明逻辑。

Result: 证明了在任意n顶点无权图中，随机边两端单位电流的L1范数至多为2 log n；在加权图中，传递电流矩阵绝对值项的谱范数同样至多为2 log n。

Conclusion: 该研究改进了无权图中单位电流L1范数的上界，并证明了加权图中传递电流矩阵的谱范数界限为O(log n)，在常数因子内是紧致的。

Abstract: We prove that for any unweighted graph on n vertices the L1 norm of a unit electric current between the endpoints of a random edge is at most 2 log n. Furthermore, we show that on any weighted graph the spectral norm of the entry-wise absolute value of the symmetric transfer-current matrix is at most 2 log n. This bound is tight up to constants and improves the O(log^2 n) bound from [Schild-Rao-Srivastava, SODA '18]. The initial proofs were generated by OpenAI's ChatGPT 5.5 Pro; the authors have verified and rewritten them to enhance readability and provide additional context.

</details>


### [277] [A Comprehensive Evaluation of Vertex Elimination Algorithms for Algorithmic Differentiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24280)
*Alex Crane,Pål Grønås Drange,Eli Friedman,Paul D. Hovland,Jan Hückelheim,Andrew Lyons,Yosuke Mizutani,Macéo Ottavy,Blair D. Sullivan*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文针对算法微分中的NP完全问题“最优顶点消去”，设计了高效的整数规划求解器，实现了更大规模的精确求解，验证了现有启发式算法的有效性，并提供了理论上的近似界限支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算法微分（AD）的核心计算问题——最优顶点消去是NP完全问题。尽管启发式算法广泛使用，但由于缺乏可扩展的精确求解器，难以量化评估其在实际任务中的表现及性能。

Method: 设计并应用了针对“最优顶点消去（Optimal Vertex Elimination）”和“最小边数（Minimum Edge Count）”问题的整数规划（IP）新公式，开发了比现有技术规模大1-2个数量级的求解器，并结合理论分析给出了算法的近似下界及参数化近似算法。

Result: 实现了大幅提升求解规模的精确算法，验证了流行启发式算法的高质量性能；给出了AD前向和反向模式的紧凑分析，并获得了相关问题的首个近似下界。

Conclusion: 本文通过开发可扩展的精确求解方法，成功构建了中等规模图的最优解语料库。研究验证了现有启发式算法在实际数据中的高效性，并从理论上揭示了最优顶点消去的复杂度瓶颈。

Abstract: The algorithmic differentiation (AD) of mathematical functions can be interpreted as a sequence of vertex eliminations in an underlying directed acyclic graph. The problem of determining a minimum-cost elimination ordering, which we call Optimal Vertex Elimination, is NP-complete. Consequently, much effort has been devoted to the design of heuristics. Many of these heuristics are widely believed to perform well in practice, but this hypothesis has so far been difficult to test due to the lack of scalable exact methods.
  We design and engineer new integer programming formulations for Optimal Vertex Eliminatioin and for a related objective we call Minimum Edge Count. Our implementations scale to graphs one-to-two orders of magnitude larger than existing techniques, enabling the assembly of a corpus of medium-sized graphs for which optimal solutions are known. This corpus facilitates a study of existing heuristics, confirming that on real data popular methods achieve high quality solutions.
  We also make several theoretical contributions. We give a tight analysis of the forward and reverse modes of AD, and extend our techniques to provide a simple algorithm for Optimal Vertex Elimination with approximation ratio parameterized by the size of a minimum source-sink separator. On the complexity side, we give the first approximation lower bounds for both problems.

</details>


### [278] [Covering vertices by sequential stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24711)
*Mengyuan Hu,An Zhang,Yong Chen,Zhikai Chen,Wei Ding,Guohui Lin,Jiaxuan Ma,Yue Sun*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文针对图论中的 $k \ge 2$ 的序列 $[k, \ell]$-Star Packing 问题，通过局部搜索与摊还分析改进了四个关键变体的近似比率，并证明了剩余未解决情况 $k=2$ 的 APX-hardness。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列 $[k, \ell]$-Star Packing 问题在 $k \ge 2$ 时是强 NP-hard 的。为了解决在该复杂约束下的优化问题，研究者旨在寻找更优的近似算法，并完善该问题在不同参数设置下的计算复杂度分析。

Method: 提出并采用了局部搜索（local search）与摊还分析（amortized analysis）相结合的算法框架，并引入了“增广配置”（augmenting configurations）技术来跨越分散的邻域进行局部改进操作。

Result: 针对四种不同参数设置（$k, \ell$ 取值组合）给出了精度更高的近似算法。具体包括：当 $k \ge 3, \ell = \infty$ 时达到 $\frac{k+1}{2}$-近似；当 $k=2, \ell=\infty$ 时达到 $\frac{4}{3}$-近似；当 $2=k < \ell$ 时达到 $(1 + \frac{\ell}{\ell+1})$-近似；当 $3 \le k < \ell$ 时达到 $(1 + \max\{\frac{k-1}{2}, \frac{(k+1)\ell}{3(\ell+1)}\})$-近似。此外，证明了 $k=2$ 情况下的 APX-hardness。

Conclusion: 本文不仅改进了多个特殊配置下的近似比，同时也证明了 $k=2$ 时 $[k, \ell]$-Star Packing 问题的 APX-hard 性质，补全了该问题的复杂性理论图谱。作者提出的“增广配置”技术为处理此类图论打包问题提供了有效思路。

Abstract: We study the problem of covering the maximum number of vertices in a graph by a collection of vertex-disjoint stars, each with a number of satellites in a given interval $[k, \ell]$, where $1 \le k < \ell$ and $\ell$ can be infinity. This is referred to as sequential {\sc $[k, \ell]$-Star Packing} problem. It is solvable in polynomial time when $k = 1$, but becomes strongly NP-hard when $k \ge 2$. In this paper, we propose either the first or an improved approximation algorithm for the following four sequential settings: 1) a $\frac {k+1}2$-approximation algorithm when $k \ge 3$ and $\ell = \infty$, improving the previous best ratio of $\frac {(k+1)^2}{2k+1}$; 2) a $\frac 43$-approximation algorithm when $k = 2$ and $\ell = \infty$, improving the previous best ratio of $\frac 32$; 3) the first $(1 + \frac \ell{\ell+1})$-approximation algorithm when $2 = k < \ell$; and 4) the first $(1 + \max\left\{\frac {k-1}2, \frac {(k+1) \ell}{3 (\ell+1)}\right\})$-approximation algorithm when $3 \le k < \ell$. Besides the main algorithmic techniques being local search coupled with amortized analysis, we observe augmenting configurations to bridge two distant neighborhoods for a local improvement operation. Additionally, the problem has been shown APX-hard when $k \ge 3$; we prove its APX-hardness for the last remaining case where $k = 2$.

</details>


### [279] [Approximation algorithms for the prize-collecting rural postman problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24944)
*Hong Li,Jianping Li,Wei Li,Runtao Xie,Xiaoxiao Yang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了奖励收集邮递员问题（PCRPP），证明了传统将其规约为PCTSP的方法存在2倍逼近比的性能瓶颈，并提出了一种新的多项式时间算法，实现了严格小于1.6的逼近比，在基准测试中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对奖励收集邮递员问题（PCRPP）的现有逼近算法（通常基于规约为PCTSP）存在固有2倍逼近比的理论性能瓶颈，旨在寻找更高效的近似求解方案。

Method: 分析了将PCRPP转化为PCTSP的通用方法的局限性，并设计了一种新的多项式时间逼近算法来直接解决PCRPP问题。

Result: 在理论上证明了该算法的逼近比严格小于1.6；在包含118个实例的公开基准测试集上，平均最优性间隙为3.39%，最大最优性间隙为12.12%。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种针对PCRPP问题的多项式时间逼近算法，其逼近比优于2（严格小于1.6），突破了传统将PCRPP规约为PCTSP方法所固有的2倍逼近比限制。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the prize-collecting rural postman problem (PCRPP), a variant of the rural postman problem. Given a PCRPP instance consisting of an undirected graph whose edges have nonnegative lengths and nonnegative profits, together with a root vertex, the goal is to find a closed walk that starts and ends at the root vertex and minimizes the sum of its length and the profits of all edges that the walk does not traverse. A natural way to design an approximation algorithm for the PCRPP is to construct a prize-collecting traveling salesman problem (PCTSP) instance from the given PCRPP instance, apply an approximation algorithm to the PCTSP instance, and then convert the resulting PCTSP solution into a PCRPP solution. We show that this approach has an inherent factor-two barrier: even if the constructed PCTSP instance is solved exactly, the resulting PCRPP solution can have objective value arbitrarily close to twice the optimum value of the original PCRPP instance. Our main result is a polynomial-time approximation algorithm with approximation ratio strictly smaller than 1.6 for the PCRPP. On a public 118-instance benchmark set, the proposed algorithm has average and maximum optimality gaps of 3.39\% and 12.12\%, respectively.

</details>


### [280] [CAFS: A Cache-Aware Frequency Sort for Low-Cardinality Integer Data on x86-64](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25040)
*Vasiliy S. Shlyk*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: CAFS 是一种利用低基数特性的新型 AVX2 优化整数排序算法，在 OLAP 数据处理场景下的性能表现远超主流通用排序算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现实世界的 OLAP 负载中，整数列的基数 K 往往远小于数组长度 N。现有的通用排序算法（如基于比较的排序或常规基数排序）未能充分利用这一特征，导致在低基数场景下存在不必要的资源浪费（性能受限于 O(N log N) 或与 K 无关的字节遍历）。

Method: 设计了一种结合 Chao1 基数估计器（基于 1024 个采样）、具备溢出保护策略的自适应调度器以及基于 AVX2 指令集（使用 cmpeq, movemask 和 tzcnt）的 SIMD 桶排序方案。算法核心循环为无分支设计。

Result: 在涵盖 58 种不同数组规模的 59 万次基准测试中，CAFS 在 K << N 的范围内，相比 pdqsort 速度提升 1.7-3.1 倍，相比 IPS4o 提升 1.7-3.5 倍，相比 vqsort 提升 1.2-2.3 倍。在 K 接近 6.7x10^5 之前保持显著性能优势。

Conclusion: CAFS 是一种专为整数排序设计的算法，在低基数（K << N）场景下性能显著优于现有主流排序算法（如 pdqsort, IPS4o, vqsort 等），是现代 OLAP 引擎处理字典编码或分组聚合数据的理想选择。

Abstract: Integer sorts in OLAP engines often run on columns whose cardinality $K$ is much smaller than the array length $N$. After a group-by stage the intermediate key column has $K$ bounded by the number of distinct group keys, and even a column-store scan typically operates on dictionary-encoded categorical fields where $K$ never exceeds a few thousand. A comparison sort on such a column still pays $Θ(N \log N)$ comparisons, and a radix sort still pays $Θ(N \cdot B/b)$ byte passes, irrespective of $K$. This paper describes CAFS, an integer sort that does exploit it on x86-64 with AVX2. The algorithm combines a SIMD bucket sized to one cache line, a Chao1 cardinality estimator over 1024 strided samples (kept in a heap-allocated 40 KB open-addressing table), and an adaptive dispatcher backed by a spill safety guard. The hot loop is branchless and uses AVX2 cmpeq together with movemask and tzcnt to locate the matching lane. We benchmarked CAFS on a full-factorial grid of 58 array sizes $N$ from $10^3$ to $3 \cdot 10^7$ with dense $K$ schedules per $N$, producing 592770 timed runs against pdqsort, IPS4o, vqsort, ska_sort, and std::sort. In the $K \ll N$ band the throughput is 1.7 to 3.1x that of pdqsort, 1.7 to 3.5x IPS4o, and 1.2 to 2.3x vqsort. The operational crossover against pdqsort is at $K \approx 1.3 \cdot 10^5$; against ska_sort, $K \approx 8.14 \cdot 10^5$; against vqsort, $K \approx 6.7 \cdot 10^5$; and against IPS4o the curves only converge near $K = N$. Of the five baselines, only vqsort actually overtakes CAFS once the crossover is passed, which makes the vqsort threshold at $K \approx 6.7 \cdot 10^5$ the binding constraint on the operational range of CAFS.

</details>


### [281] [The Dirichlet Mechanism for rounding with strong negative correlation, with applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25078)
*David G. Harris,George Z. Li,Nitya Raju,Renata Valieva*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于狄利克雷分布的依赖舍入机制，通过引入更强的负相关属性，简化了调度算法设计并提升了多个组合优化问题的近似比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在二分图分配问题中，现有基于分数松弛的独立舍入方法会导致左节点负载不均（负载方差大）。虽已有多种依赖舍入算法解决此问题，但需要更强的负相关性及更简化的算法实现。

Method: 狄利克雷机制（Dirichlet mechanism）：为二分图中的每个左节点（如机器）为其边抽取狄利克雷随机变量，然后由每个右节点（如任务）根据这些值独立选择边。

Result: 1. 在在线依赖舍入（oblivious online dependent rounding）问题上，将近似比提升至 0.68；2. 在无关机调度加权完工时间最小化问题上，将近似比提升至 1.387。

Conclusion: 提出了一种称为“狄利克雷机制”（Dirichlet mechanism）的全新依赖舍入方法，该方法在负相关性方面强于现有算法，且结构更简单，无需平局决胜机制。在在线依赖舍入和最小化加权完工时间调度问题上均取得了更好的近似比。

Abstract: Many optimization and scheduling problems can be abstracted in terms of a bipartite ``assignment graph" $G = (L \cup R, E)$, where the goal is to select exactly one edge for each right-node. For example, a right-node may correspond to a job, and a left-node to a possible machine assignment. A common strategy to solve such problems is to obtain a fractional relaxation $x_e$ for each edge $e$, and then have each right-node independently select an edge with probability $x_e$. However, this may cause the left-nodes to become unevenly loaded, leading to suboptimal solutions for some problems.
  To address this, a number of algorithms for dependent rounding with strong negative correlation have been developed, e.g. Bansal, Srinivasan & Svensson (2021), Im & Shadloo (2020), Im & Li (2023), Harris (2024), Naor, Srinivasan & Wajc (2025). We introduce a new method for this, which we call the \emph{Dirichlet mechanism}. It is based on having each left-node draw Dirichlet random variables for its edges, and then having each right-node select an edge based on these values. This achieves quantitatively stronger negative correlation than previous algorithms, and is also simpler since it avoids the need for a tie-breaking mechanism.
  We illustrate the mechanism with improved approximation ratios for two problems. For oblivious online dependent rounding, we achieve a $0.68$-approximation which improves upon the previous $0.652$-approximation of Naor, Srinivasan & Wajc (2025). For the problem of scheduling jobs on unrelated machines to minimize weighted completion time, we achieve a $1.387$-approximation which improves upon the $1.398$-approximation of Harris (2024). (A recent algorithm of Li (2025) based on iterated rounding also provides a $1.36$-approximation if the weights of each job are independent of machine.)

</details>


### [282] [Approximate Algorithms for Chamfer Distance Under Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25280)
*Gil Halevi,Daniel Zhang,Jason Zhang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出并研究了“平移下的倒角距离（CDuT）”，并针对该指标提出了一系列精确计算及高效近似算法，旨在解决点集匹配中由平移引起的冗余差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 倒角距离（CD）是衡量点集相似性的流行指标，但在某些场景下，点集的绝对位移并不影响其本质相似性。为了消除平移带来的不必要干扰，作者提出了“平移下的倒角距离（CDuT）”，旨在优化点集间的匹配效果。

Method: 1. 在一维情况下提出精确的二次时间算法；2. 在高维情况下提出(2+ε)-近似的近二次时间算法；3. 提出时间复杂度为 O(mn^2ε^{-(d+1)}) 的 (1+ε)-近似算法；4. 在特定假设下，针对决策版本提出近二次时间的 (1+ε)-近似算法；5. 进行了细粒度复杂度分析。

Result: 作者成功为 CDuT 问题提供了一套完整的算法框架，涵盖了从精确解到高效近似解的不同维度，有效解决了 CDuT 计算在不同约束条件下的性能瓶颈。

Conclusion: 本文通过定义并研究“平移下的倒角距离（CDuT）”，填补了度量学习中的这一空白，并为该指标提供了多种精确及近似计算方案，同时探讨了其算法复杂性理论。

Abstract: Given two sets of points A and B, $|A| = m$, $|B| = n$, the Chamfer distance from $A$ to $B$ is defined as $\operatorname{CD}(A,B) = \sum_{a\in A} \min_{b\in B} d(a,b)$, where $d$ is a distance metric. Chamfer distance is a popular measure of dissimilarity between two sets of points that has seen increasing usage in computer vision and information retrieval as a substitute for the more computationally demanding Earth Mover's distance. We propose a new problem, Chamfer distance under translation, defined as $\operatorname{CDuT}(A,B) :=\min_{t\in \mathbb{R}^d} \operatorname{CD}(A+t,B)$, where $A+t$ denotes the translation of every point in $A$ by $t$. Chamfer distance under translation is valuable in cases where translations capture aspects of the data unlikely to be relevant for dissimilarity, such as temporal, spatial, or other semantic information. For Chamfer distance under translation, we provide four algorithms: (1) an exact quadratic time algorithm in one dimension, (2) a near quadratic time ($2+\varepsilon$)-approximation algorithm in higher dimensions, (3) a $(1+\varepsilon)$-approximation algorithm with running time $\mathcal{O}(mn^2\varepsilon^{-(d+1)})$, and (4) a near-quadratic time $(1+\varepsilon)$-approximation algorithm for answering the decision version of $\operatorname{CDuT}$ given a separation assumption on $B$. We additionally explore the fine-grained complexity of $\operatorname{CDuT}$.

</details>


### [283] [Algorithms with Polynomially-Improved Approximation Factors for the $2 \rightarrow q$ Norm, and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25303)
*Samuel B. Hopkins,Stefan Tiegel*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文针对 $2 \rightarrow q$ 范数问题给出了首个在多项式时间内超越传统谱分析基准的近似算法，并利用平方和（SOS）方法提升了鲁棒统计学习任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $2 \rightarrow q$ 范数在组合优化、量子信息和算法统计中具有核心地位。此前缺乏在多项式时间内超越简单谱方法（$d^{1/4}$ 近似）的普适算法，且已有最优化近似限受指数时间假设（ETH）限制。

Method: 利用平方和（Sum-of-Squares）层次与半正定规划（SDP）技术，针对 $q > 2$ 的超收缩（hypercontractive）设定设计了多项式时间近似算法。针对 $q=4$ 的特殊情况，实现了 $d^{1/8}$ 的近似比。

Result: 该算法在多项式时间内优于现有的 $d^{1/4}$ 谱基准；对于 $q=4$ 实现了 $d^{1/8}$ 近似；并证明了该技术可直接优化鲁棒均值估计、协方差估计、回归及聚类等问题的性能。

Conclusion: 本文首次给出了在多项式时间内超越谱方法（Spectral Algorithm）基准的 $2 \rightarrow q$ 范数近似算法，并构建了平方和（Sum-of-Squares）证明，为鲁棒统计任务提供了更优的理论保证。

Abstract: The $2 \rightarrow q$ norm of a matrix $X \in \mathbb{R}^{n \times d}$ is defined as $\lVert X \rVert_{2 \rightarrow q} = \sup_{\lVert v \rVert_2 = 1} \lVert Xv \rVert_q$. We give polynomial-time multiplicative approximation algorithms for this norm when $q > 2$ (i.e. in the hypercontractive setting). This problem either directly captures or is closely related to long-standing open problems in combinatorial optimization and hardness of approximation (e.g. Small Set Expansion), quantum information (e.g. Best Separable State), and algorithmic statistics.
  Very little is known about what approximation factors we can achieve for this problem in polynomial time, even though such approximations have significant downstream consequences. Barak, Brandão, Harrow, Kelner, Steurer, and Zhou showed that no polynomial-time algorithm can achieve an approximation factor better than $2^{\sqrt{\log n}}$, assuming the Exponential Time Hypothesis (FOCS'12). On the other hand, a simple spectral algorithm gives a $d^{1/4}$-approximation as a baseline. We give, to the best of our knowledge, the first polynomial-time approximation algorithm beating this baseline by polynomial factors. For the important special case of $q = 4$ it achieves a $d^{1/8}$-approximation. All previous algorithms required additional assumptions on $X$, or only surpassed the baseline for small values of $n$.
  Moreover, we construct sum-of-squares certificates for the $2 \rightarrow q$ norm. This directly implies improved algorithms for robust mean and covariance estimation, robust regression, and clustering, when the data only satisfies a bound on its $q$-th moment.

</details>


### [284] [A Note on Approximability of Densest At-Least-k-Subgraph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25464)
*Bundit Laekhanukit,Pasin Manurangsi,Ohad Trabelsi*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了“密集至少-$k$-子图”(DAL$k$S) 问题，证明了基于 D$k$S 的硬度规约，将其不可近似性界限推至 (2 - ε)，并证明了该问题在参数化复杂度下是 W[1]-困难的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管学术界已知 DAL$k$S 存在 2-近似算法，但该问题的确切不可近似性界限尚不明确。此外，既有的 (2 - ε) 不可近似性结果依赖于小集合扩展假设 (SSEH)，且无法扩展至参数化版本。

Method: 通过将密集 $k$-子图问题 (D$k$S) 规约至密集至少-$k$-子图问题 (DAL$k$S)，证明了其硬度。并利用针对 D$k$S 的更强假设推广了该结果。

Result: 1. 若 D$k$S 难以在常数因子内近似，则 DAL$k$S 难以在 (3/2 - ε) 因子内近似；2. 在更强假设下，DAL$k$S 的不可近似性界限被提升至 (2 - ε)；3. DAL$k$S 的精确求解在参数 $k$ 下是 W[1]-困难的。

Conclusion: 本文证明了DAL$k$S问题的不可近似性界限，并在特定假设下将其界限提升至紧致的 (2 - ε)，同时指出了该问题在参数化复杂度下的 W[1]-困难性。

Abstract: We study the Densest At-Least-$k$-Subgraph (DAL$k$S) problem, in which we are given an undirected graph $G$ and an integer $k$, and the goal is to find a subgraph of $G$ with at least $k$ vertices with maximum density. The best-known algorithm, independently discovered by Khuller and Saha (2009) and by Andersen (2007), yields a 2-approximation for DAL$k$S in polynomial time.
  In this note, we provide a (simple) reduction from Densest $k$-Subgraph (D$k$S) to Densest At-Least-$k$-Subgraph, which shows that, if D$k$S is hard to approximate to within any constant factor, then DAL$k$S is hard to approximate to within $(3/2 - \varepsilon)$ factor for every $\varepsilon > 0$. This holds in both the normal (non-parameterized) and the parameterized (by $k$) settings.
  We then generalize the reduction to provide a tight $(2 - \varepsilon)$ factor hardness of approximating Densest At-Least-$k$-Subgraph, albeit under a stronger hypothesis which roughly states that Densest $k$-Subgraph is hard to approximate to within $k^{1 - δ}$ factor for any constant $δ> 0$. Once again, this extends naturally to the parameterized setting. Previously, $(2 - \varepsilon)$ factor inapproximability for DAL$k$S was only known under the Small Set Expansion Hypothesis (Bergner, 2013; Manurangsi, 2017), which does not apply to the parameterized version of the problem.
  Furthermore, we show that the exact version of DAL$k$S is W[1]-hard (parameterized by $k$).

</details>


### [285] [Engineering Practical Succinct Bit Vectors: A Space-Time Pareto Analysis on Apple Silicon ARM64 Cores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25528)
*Ishant Garg*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文对位向量的 Rank/Select 查询进行了底层架构优化，通过非对称块定义和超块索引设计，在 Apple Silicon 上显著提升了查询速度，并验证了压缩表示在保证空间效率的同时具备极高的访问性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现代计算机体系结构下重新评估和优化简洁数据结构（Succinct Data Structures）的实用性能，特别是解决位向量在 Rank 和 Select 操作中的空间与运行效率平衡问题。

Method: 1. 对比评估：评估了 BlockBitVec（两级块基准）、FastBitVec（非对称超块实现）和 RRRBitVec（熵压缩）三种方案；2. 非对称块优化：采用 4096/256 位块边界以加速 Rank 计算；3. 索引优化：使用超块级采样索引限制 RRRBitVec 中的扫描范围至 L1 缓存；4. 正确性验证：使用模糊测试（fuzzer）执行超过 7800 万次断言。

Result: 1. 在 ARM 架构上，非对称 4096/256 位块设计使 Rank 查询速度提升 1.4 倍，空间开销仅为 7.8%；2. RRRBitVec 的 Rank 延迟呈现密度依赖的钟形曲线，极值密度下查询速度提升 39%；3. 引入超块采样后，RRRBitVec 的 Select 查询相比传统二分查找实现了 4.9 倍的速度提升（33.7 ns）。

Conclusion: 针对现代 ARM 架构（Apple Silicon），通过对位向量（bit vector）Rank 和 Select 查询的工程优化，证明了优化的块索引空间开销与查询延迟之间存在显著的性能增益，并验证了 RRR 压缩格式在特定密度下的查询性能优势。

Abstract: Succinct data structures use space close to the information-theoretic minimum while answering queries directly on the compressed representation. In this paper, we present a practical engineering study of rank and select queries on bit vectors. We evaluate a classic two-level block baseline (BlockBitVec), an asymmetric superblock implementation (FastBitVec), and an entropy-compressed representation (RRRBitVec) based on the Raman, Raman, and Rao (RRR) coding scheme. On Apple Silicon (M-series ARM architecture), we demonstrate a 1.4x speedup in rank queries through asymmetric 4096/256-bit block boundaries, with a rank index overhead of 7.8%. We investigate the empirical behavior of RRRBitVec and observe a symmetric density-dependent bell-curve for rank latency -- where queries at extreme densities (1% and 99%) run up to 39% faster due to offset elimination at boundary classes. We further show that RRRBitVec achieves a 4.9x speedup over classic binary-search select baselines, running in 33.7 ns at uniform density by using a superblock-level sampling index that restricts sequential scans to L1-cache lookups. All implementations are validated against a correctness fuzzer executing over 78 million assertions with no failures. Source code and test harnesses are publicly available.

</details>


### [286] [Weighted Clique and Independent Set in Edge-Distant Hereditary Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25724)
*Eshwar Srinivasan,Ramesh Hariharasubramanian*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本工作研究了遗传图类的边编辑演化（边删除/非边添加），定义了“$\mathcal{G}$-边距离”概念，并证明了对于具有 k 个边编辑距离的图，加权最大团和加权最大独立集问题可在 $O^*(2^k)$ 时间内解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对 Singh 和 Sivaraman 最近提出的“边顶点（edge-apex）”和“边添加（edge-add）”类，旨在研究其在加权最大团问题（WMCP）和加权最大独立集问题（WMISP）上的算法复杂性，并将其推广至任意 k 次边编辑距离的图类。

Method: 1. 定义“$\mathcal{G}$-边距离” $\xi_{\mathcal{G}}(G)$，量化图 $G$ 与遗传类 $\mathcal{G}$ 之间的距离；2. 基于动态规划或分治思想，针对该参数化模型开发算法；3. 利用已知转递图的性质，将通用结论具体化至转递边距离问题。

Result: 1. 为边顶点和边添加类提供了 WMCP 和 WMISP 的算法；2. 证明了对于距离为 k 的图，若原类 $\mathcal{G}$ 中问题可多项式时间求解，则新问题可在 $O^*(2^k)$ 时间内求解；3. 证明了转递边距离为 k 的图上述问题亦可在 $O^*(2^k)$ 时间内求解。

Conclusion: 该研究通过引入“$\mathcal{G}$-边距离”概念，成功将单一修改（边删除/非边添加）的算法特性推广至更通用的情况，证明了在父类 $\mathcal{G}$ 可解的前提下，k-边距离图上的加权最大团和加权最大独立集问题均可在 $O^*(2^k)$ 时间内解决。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the algorithmic aspects of two natural extensions of hereditary classes: the edge-apex class and the edge-add class, recently introduced by Singh and Sivaraman. These are defined as the graph classes obtained by at most one edge deletion or one non-edge addition, respectively, from a hereditary class $\mathcal{G}$. Building on earlier results showing that both classes remain hereditary and admit finite forbidden induced subgraph characterizations whenever $\mathcal{G}$ does, we focus on the Weighted Maximum Clique Problem (WMCP) and the Weighted Maximum Independent Set Problem (WMISP).
  We first present algorithms for WMCP and WMISP on both the edge-apex and edge-add classes of hereditary graph classes. Extending this framework, we introduce the notion of the $\mathcal{G}$-edge distance of a graph $G$, denoted by $ξ_{\mathcal{G}}(G)$, which quantifies how far $G$ is from the class $\mathcal{G}$ in terms of the minimum number of edge deletions or non-edge additions needed to transform it into a member of $\mathcal{G}$.
  By parameterizing with respect to this distance, we show that both WMCP and WMISP can be solved in $O^*(2^k)$ time on graphs whose $\mathcal{G}$-edge distance is $k$, provided these problems admit polynomial-time algorithms within the class $\mathcal{G}$. This result extends earlier algorithmic characterizations of the single edge-apex and edge-add classes to the more general setting of $k$-edge-distant graphs. By combining our general results with known properties of transitive graphs, we show that WMCP and WMISP can be solved in $O^*(2^k)$ time for graphs with transitive-edge distance $k$.

</details>


### [287] [On the Complexity of Bilevel Independent Set Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25927)
*Komal Muluk*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文系统性地分析了双层独立集及区间选择问题的计算复杂性，分类了不同决策机制下的难度等级，并为特定情况下的区间选择问题提供了多项式时间动态规划算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在一类参与者被划分为领导者和跟随者、且优化问题交织的双层优化结构下，独立集问题及其特殊情况——区间选择问题的计算复杂性。

Method: 通过对领导者和跟随者目标函数类型（求和型与瓶颈型）及跟随者反应机制（乐观与悲观）的排列组合，系统梳理了问题的各类变体，并利用计算复杂性理论分析难度，对区间选择问题设计了动态规划算法。

Result: 双层独立集问题的各类变体被成功归类至多项式层次结构中，复杂度涵盖了P、NP-完全以及$\Sigma_2^p$-完全；对于双层区间选择问题的求和型目标函数变体，提供了高效的多项式时间动态规划解法。

Conclusion: 针对双层独立集（Bilevel Independent Set）问题，文章确定了不同变体的计算复杂性边界（P到$\Sigma_2^p$-完全）；对于双层区间选择（Bilevel Interval Selection）问题，提出了时间复杂度为 $\mathcal{O}(n^4\log n)$ 的动态规划算法。

Abstract: We consider a bilevel optimization problem in which the ground set is partitioned between two decision makers, a leader and a follower, whose optimization problems are interleaved. We study the Bilevel Independent Set problem, and its special case, the Bilevel Interval Selection problem, on different variants emerging from a combination of the type of leader's objective function, the type of follower's objective function, and the setting in which the follower reacts, i.e., either optimistically or pessimistically. Here we consider sum and bottleneck type objective functions. We investigate the computational complexity of all these variants for the Bilevel Independent Set problem, and sort them into their respective level of the polynomial hierarchy. Our results range from $\mathsf{P}$, $\mathsf{NP}$-completeness to $Σ_2^\mathsf{p}$-completeness. For the Bilevel Interval Selection problem, we give a dynamic programming algorithm running in time $\mathcal{O}(n^4\log n)$ for the variants in which the leader and the follower have objective functions of the sum type.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [288] [Cross-Platform Fused MoE Dispatch in Triton: Portable Expert Routing Without CUDA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23911)
*Subhadip Mitra*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 论文提出了TritonMoE，一种基于OpenAI Triton开发的、可跨硬件平台（NVIDIA/AMD）部署的高性能MoE调度内核，通过融合计算显著减少了内存访问开销，在多种主流模型上实现了与甚至优于CUDA原生内核的性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的高性能MoE内核（如Megablocks等）大多依赖CUDA，导致硬件绑定（锁定在NVIDIA），且MoE推理面临内存访问不规律和路由开销严重的瓶颈。

Method: 开发了基于OpenAI Triton的融合MoE调度内核，核心创新在于“融合门控+上投影（fused gate+up GEMM）”算子，通过共享L2缓存输入和寄存器内SiLU激活减少了全局内存访问。

Result: 在NVIDIA A100上，对于推理批处理量（≤ 512 tokens），其吞吐量达到Megablocks的89-131%；实现了跨NVIDIA A100和AMD MI300X的完全代码迁移性和一致性。

Conclusion: TritonMoE实现了高性能且跨平台的MoE推理，证明了使用OpenAI Triton编写高性能算子的可行性。在处理特定负载时表现优于现有的CUDA实现，但也指出了在极端路由不平衡情况下的优化空间。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures power the majority of frontier large language models, but their inference is bottlenecked by irregular memory access patterns and expert routing overhead. Existing optimized MoE kernels (Megablocks, Tutel, FasterMoE) are implemented in CUDA and locked to NVIDIA hardware. We present TritonMoE, a fused MoE dispatch kernel written entirely in OpenAI Triton that performs the complete forward pass -- router scoring, token permutation, expert GEMMs, and weighted output combination -- using only portable Triton primitives. Our key optimization is a fused gate+up GEMM kernel that computes both SwiGLU projections from shared L2-cached input tiles with in-register SiLU activation, eliminating 35% of global memory traffic. On an NVIDIA A100, TritonMoE achieves 89-131% of the throughput of the CUDA-optimized Megablocks at inference batch sizes (<= 512 tokens) across Mixtral-8x7B, DeepSeek-V3, and Qwen2-MoE configurations. All 162 correctness tests pass on both NVIDIA A100 and AMD MI300X with zero code changes, validating cross-platform portability. We additionally characterize sensitivity to routing imbalance under Zipfian-skewed expert assignments and identify the regime -- 64+ experts under extreme skew -- where our fixed-tile scheduling underperforms Megablocks' block-sparse layout, motivating dynamic block-to-expert assignment as future work. Code is available at https://github.com/bassrehab/triton-kernels.

</details>


### [289] [Can LoRA Fusion Support Cross-Domain Tasks in Cloud-Edge Collaboration?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23913)
*Yatong Wang,Fali Wang,Naibin Gu,Zheng Lin,Zhengxiao Liu,Dingyu Yao,Zhiwei Zhang,Jianxin Shi,Weiping Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对云边协同中LoRA适配器跨域融合性能差的问题，本文提出了支持资源受限设备训练的“剪枝-训练-恢复”框架及跨域评估基准MMLU-CD，并开发了LoRA-CR模块以解决参数冲突，显著提升了融合效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对云端大模型在跨域问题求解中，由于隐私限制无法获取分散在边缘设备端的多领域数据，且现有边缘设备计算能力不足以支持全模型微调及现有LoRA融合方法在跨域场景下性能失效的问题。

Method: 1. 提出“剪枝-训练-恢复”（prune-train-recover）框架，支持在边缘设备端进行低资源环境下的局部LoRA训练；
2. 构建跨域基准测试集MMLU-CD，用于显式评估多领域协作能力；
3. 开发LoRA-CR（LoRA Conflict-Resolution）模块，通过解决多适配器间的参数冲突来优化融合性能。

Result: 实验表明，现有的LoRA融合方法在跨域基准MMLU-CD上表现极差（甚至不如基础LLM）；引入LoRA-CR模块后，LoRA融合性能提升了最高3.8%。

Conclusion: 现有LoRA融合方法在跨域任务中表现不佳，其根本原因是参数冲突。研究提出的LoRA-CR模块有效缓解了冲突，证实了冲突缓解是云边协同LoRA融合中的关键因素。

Abstract: Cloud-hosted large language models (LLMs) commonly rely on LoRA for domain adaptation, yet domain data are distributed across multiple edge devices and cannot be uploaded due to privacy constraints. This raises a fundamental question: how can knowledge from multiple private edges be integrated into a cloud LLM for cross-domain problem solving? A natural solution is to train LoRA adapters locally and fuse them in the cloud; however, existing pipelines rely on unrealistic assumptions that edge devices can host cloud-scale LLMs and are evaluated mainly on single-domain tasks. To address these limitations, we propose a prune-train-recover framework that enables local LoRA training on pruned models and privacy-preserving cloud integration. We further introduce MMLU-CD, a cross-domain benchmark that composes multiple domain samples into a single instance, enabling explicit evaluation of cross-domain problem solving. This allows us to ask a concrete question: Can existing LoRA fusion methods support cross-domain tasks in cloud-edge collaboration? Our empirical answer is negative. Existing LoRA fusion methods perform poorly on MMLU-CD, often underperforming the base LLM, revealing their inability to support cross-domain problem solving. We attribute this failure to parameter conflicts among LoRA adapters and propose a simple conflict-resolution module, LoRA-CR, which mitigates conflicting updates and improves LoRA fusion performance by up to 3.8%. These results identify conflict mitigation as a critical yet largely overlooked factor in cloud-edge LoRA fusion, warranting further investigation in future research.

</details>


### [290] [VineLM: Trie-Based Fine-Grained Control for Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23914)
*Nikos Pagonas,Matthew Lou,Tianyi Peng,Dan Rubenstein,Kostis Kaffes*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: VineLM 是一种为 Agentic 工作流设计的动态调度系统，它通过运行时细粒度的自适应模型选择取代了静态分配，有效提升了多模型协作下的性能与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作流管理系统在配置阶段采用静态分配，无法根据运行时的实际开销、延迟或特定请求的性能需求灵活调整各阶段的模型选择，导致资源利用效率低下。

Method: 提出了一种利用带注释的模型选择前缀树（Trie）表示工作流执行路径的方法；结合检查点（checkpointing）和级联分析（cascade profiling）技术进行路径估计；并在运行时基于当前执行状态和剩余预算动态重新规划（re-rooting）剩余步骤的模型选择。

Result: 在 NL2SQL 和数学推理任务中，VineLM 优化了成本-延迟-准确率的配置边界，在相同请求预算下将准确率提升了 18%，同时通过稀疏分析将离线分析开销降低了 98%-99.8%。

Conclusion: VineLM 通过实现运行时细粒度的模型选择，突破了静态工作流的执行限制，在保持预算的前提下显著提升了模型性能，同时大幅降低了离线评估的计算开销。

Abstract: Agentic workflows interleave configurable LLM stages with tool stages and often include retries or refinement loops. Existing workflow managers profile full workflow configurations offline and assign each request a static workflow-level plan that binds each configurable LLM stage to a single model, reuses that model across repeated loop iterations, and does not revisit those choices at runtime. We present VineLM, a workflow manager that enables fine-grained control by choosing the model for each stage invocation as execution unfolds under request-level objectives such as maximizing accuracy under cost or latency budgets. VineLM represents feasible executions as an annotated trie of model-choice prefixes and uses checkpointing and cascade profiling to estimate path accuracy, cost, and latency without exhaustively profiling every request on every path. At runtime, VineLM re-roots the trie after each stage invocation and replans over the remaining subtrie using the realized execution prefix and remaining latency budget. On NL2SQL and math reasoning workflows, VineLM improves the cost-latency-accuracy frontier over coarse workflow-level baselines, achieving up to 18% higher accuracy at the same per-request budget with its sparse profiling reducing offline profiling cost by 98-99.8% when compared to exhaustive profiling.

</details>


### [291] [The Model Parking Tax: Quantifying the Hidden Energy Cost of Always-On GPU Model Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23918)
*Sai Sathvik Vadari*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了GPU常驻推理的功耗真相：闲置功耗主要由CUDA上下文加载引起的DVFS状态切换决定，而非显存占用。因此，在节能策略中应将重心从显存优化转向对请求间隔的调度管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI推理行业为了避免冷启动延迟，通常将模型常驻GPU显存中，造成了巨大的闲置功耗开销。现有研究缺乏对闲置GPU功耗与显存占用之间关系的经验分析。

Method: 结合了18天的生产遥测数据（335,267个样本）与受控的剂量-反应实验。实验覆盖了三种GPU架构（H100、A100、L40S）和三种显存技术（HBM3、HBM2e、GDDR6），并通过HuggingFace模型（Qwen2.5-7B）进行了验证。

Result: 1. CUDA上下文加载会强制GPU进入特定的DVFS状态，导致额外功耗大幅上升（+26-66W）。2. 显存占用对闲置功耗的影响极小（每个GB小于0.02W），基本可忽略不计。3. 若请求间隔超过1-5分钟，卸载模型比保持常驻更节能。

Conclusion: 待机模式下的“停车税”（parking tax）主要由CUDA上下文加载触发的状态切换（DVFS）决定，而非显存占用量。通过冷启动盈亏平衡分析，研究建议对于等待间隔超过1-5分钟的情况，应采用模型卸载策略。

Abstract: The AI inference industry keeps models loaded in GPU memory around the clock to avoid cold-start latency, implicitly treating idle power as a fixed cost of readiness. Yet the structure of this cost has never been empirically decomposed - and never across GPU architectures. We present the first cross-architecture measurement of idle GPU power as a function of VRAM allocation, combining 18 days of production telemetry (335,267 samples, 14 H100 GPUs) with controlled dose-response experiments on three GPU architectures spanning three memory technologies: NVIDIA H100 (HBM3, 80 GB), A100 (HBM2e, 80 GB), and L40S (GDDR6, 48 GB). We observe that idle power is piecewise constant on all three architectures: the CUDA context forces a discrete DVFS transition consuming +26-66 W over bare idle (26-50 W on HBM architectures, 66 W on GDDR6), while the marginal VRAM effect is bounded below measurement relevance ($|β| < 0.02$ W/GB) on every device tested. The CUDA context accounts for >98% of the parking tax regardless of memory technology. We validate this finding with a real HuggingFace model (Qwen2.5-7B) on all three architectures, confirming <0.5 W difference from empty tensors on every device, and capture cold-start power profiles during model loading. We derive a cold-start breakeven model showing energy-optimal behavior depends on request arrival rate and loading latency - not model size - with breakeven intervals of 1-5 minutes. Our results identify a constraint consistent across all tested architectures: idle-with-context power is determined by DVFS state, not memory occupancy.

</details>


### [292] [RASC: Region-Aware Self-Calibration for Dense 2D Sensor Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23963)
*Yinglei Ma,Fei Xiao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出RASC算法，通过局部聚类与线性共识机制，解决了已部署传感器阵列因应力导致的参数漂移问题，在无需额外校准的情况下高效恢复了系统精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于BJT的2D温度传感器阵列在工厂校准后，受热应力和机械应力影响，传感器增益与偏移参数会发生严重漂移，且部署后的实验室重校准方案不可行。

Method: 提出五阶段RASC (Region-Aware Self-Calibration) 算法：将全局校准问题分解为局部簇层问题；在簇内采用截尾平均场重构与Huber IRLS（迭代重加权最小二乘法）进行稳健交替估计；在簇重叠区域通过线性共识算法进行全局协调，并证明其具有指数收敛性。

Result: 在7632帧实际部署数据中，RASC将固定模式噪声降低了约71%，精度恢复至+/-0.1摄氏度；在高维阵列仿真中，其性能接近集中式EKF（扩展卡尔曼滤波），且带宽消耗降低约4倍。

Conclusion: RASC算法通过局部聚类、稳健迭代重构及线性共识机制，在无需外部实验室参考的情况下，有效校准了部署后的BJT传感器阵列，实现了高精度温控需求。

Abstract: BJT-based 2D temperature-sensor arrays are factory-calibrated to +/-0.1 degC, but post-deployment thermal and mechanical stresses drift their per-sensor gain-offset parameters by an order of magnitude, and in-lab recalibration is impractical. We present RASC (Region-Aware Self-Calibration), a five-stage algorithm that decomposes the global ill-posed problem into local cluster-level problems, runs robust alternating estimation (trimmed-mean field reconstruction + Huber IRLS) inside each cluster, and reconciles overlapping estimates by linear consensus on the cluster-overlap graph with provable exponential convergence. On 7,632 frames from a deployed 16x16 array exhibiting ~5x factory-spec non-uniformity, RASC cuts the locally-non-smooth fixed-pattern residual by 71+/-5% (10-fold CV), restoring +/-0.1 degC accuracy while perturbing the calibrated field by only 0.041 degC RMSE; reduction concentrates at the edges (78% vs 55% interior). In simulations on 8x8 to 32x32 arrays, RASC matches an oracle centralized EKF within 0.10 degC with ~4x lower bandwidth.

</details>


### [293] [Energy-Aware Computing in the Year 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24569)
*Roblex Nana Tchakoute,Claude Tadonki*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文综述了高性能计算与AI时代的能耗挑战，提出了一套综合性节能计算分类体系，旨在通过从微观硬件到宏观调度的跨层面优化，实现可持续的绿色计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着高性能计算（HPC）进入Exascale时代及生成式AI的兴起，ICT领域能耗呈爆发式增长，这一能耗瓶颈已成为气候变化与环境可持续性面临的严峻挑战，迫切需要系统性的节能计算解决方案。

Method: 对现有的高性能计算（HPC）及节能计算相关文献进行了综述，构建了一套涵盖硬件、软件、测量工具、任务调度、电压调节、负载整合、联邦学习及冷却技术等维度的综合分类体系。

Result: 提出了一个多维度的节能计算分类框架，重点分析了大规模AI应用的资源需求，并探讨了ICT领域迈向绿色、可持续计算的未来核心研究方向。

Conclusion: 本文通过构建全方位的节能计算分类体系与路线图分析，指出实现可持续计算需要硬件、软件及系统运维层面的协同优化，并强调了应对AI巨大能耗挑战的紧迫性。

Abstract: High-Performance Computing (HPC) has recently entered the Exascale era, and considerable efforts are being made to fully harness this potential power for large-scale applications, such as cutting-edge generative AI (training and exploitation). The corresponding energy consumption is very high, and forecasts are alarming, making this metric a critical systemic bottleneck. Addressing this issue presents a genuine challenge for the entire cloud-edge-HPC continuum at all scales, from low-power IoT microcontrollers to multi-megawatt data centers. Beyond financial costs, green computing is driven by considerations related to climate change and environmental concerns such as carbon footprint ($CO_2e$), as well as constraints on energy production and supply, leading to a real need to regulate {\em information and communication technology} (ICT) activities. This article presents a comprehensive overview of energy-efficient computing, taking into account the most recent and significant contributions. Based on this exploration of the state of the art, we design and describe a holistic taxonomy of the aforementioned publications, structured around various perspectives, including {\em hardware and software aspects, measurement instrumentation, software optimizations, dynamic task scheduling, voltage scaling, workload consolidation, federated learning}, and {\em cooling}. Particular emphasis is placed on large-scale AI, which receives significant attention due to its considerable resource requirements. We conclude with an analysis of a forward-looking roadmap that considers the main perspectives of sustainable computing.

</details>


### [294] [TSFLora: Token-Compressed Split Fine-Tuning for Wireless Edge Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23988)
*Xianke Qiang,Zheng Chang,Li Wang,Ying-Chang Liang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对边缘设备资源有限的问题，提出 TSFLora 框架，通过在分割联邦学习过程中应用 Token 压缩技术，大幅降低了模型微调时的通信代价与设备内存需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LAM 边缘微调方案面临严重约束：联邦学习（Federated Fine-tuning）对设备内存要求过高，而分割学习（Split Learning）则导致过高的通信链路带宽消耗。作者旨在解决在资源受限边缘设备上进行高效、私有的 LAM 微调问题。

Method: TSFLora 框架：结合了基于注意力机制的 Token 选择、Token 合并（Token Merging）、低位激活量化（Low-bit Activation Quantization）以及 LoRA（低秩自适应）。其核心是将分割后的传输数据（中间 Token 序列）进行压缩后再上传至服务器。

Result: 在 ViT 模型上针对 CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100 和 TinyImageNet 数据集的实验表明：通信开销减少达 6.8 倍，端侧内存占用降低 41%，同时模型精度保持在竞争水平。

Conclusion: TSFLora 提出了一种高效的边缘端 LAM 微调方案，通过在分割联邦学习中引入创新的中间表征压缩技术，在显着降低通信开销和端侧内存占用的同时，保证了模型精度，为资源受限的边缘设备在大模型微调方面提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Adapting large AI models (LAMs) to personalized edge data is challenging because wireless devices have limited memory, computation, and uplink capacity. Federated fine-tuning preserves data privacy but still requires each device to host the full model, while split learning reduces device memory at the cost of heavy activation transmission. This paper proposes TSFLora, a token-compressed split fine-tuning framework for communication-efficient LAM adaptation at the edge. TSFLora combines attention-guided token selection, token merging, low-bit activation quantization, and LoRA-based adaptation within a split federated training pipeline. The key idea is to compress the intermediate token sequence before transmission so that the system reduces both uplink traffic and server-side processing without changing the frozen backbone.
