Experiments on ViT models over CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and TinyImageNet show that TSFLora achieves up to \textbf{6.8$\times$} communication reduction and \textbf{41\%} memory saving while maintaining competitive accuracy.

</details>


### [295] [Resident KV Claims: A Conformance Contract for Future Reuse under Active KV Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24259)
*Lukas Stepanek*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“常驻KV声明”的运行时契约，旨在解决LLM推理中KV缓存资源争用的可观测性和管理问题，通过显式声明和调度可见性机制，取代了现有的隐式缓存驱逐策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着KV缓存复用机制的复杂化，现有的缓存策略缺乏定义“未来复用意图（future-reuse intent）”的便携式契约。当缓存空间不足时，常驻KV和活跃KV的资源竞争会导致不可控的驱逐，缺乏调度可见性。

Method: 引入“常驻KV声明”契约，包含物化谓词、生命周期状态、可行性评估及遥测数据。通过在vLLM中开发最小原型及配套的MicroRuntime和litmus测试套件，实现对缓存驱逐、拒绝和抢占等行为的精确追踪与管理。

Result: 在vLLM原型中，该机制成功将原本不可控的资源争用导致的结果转变为调度器可见的“主动拒绝（active refusal）”，并能精确归因阻塞记录，提供了对KV缓存复杂生命周期行为的全面观测能力。

Conclusion: 本文提出了“常驻KV声明（Resident KV Claims）”这一契约机制，通过将KV缓存的生命周期与调度决策显式绑定，解决了当活跃KV与常驻KV竞争资源时，系统无法有效管理可重用状态的问题，实现了从“沉默的缓存失效”到“可追溯的策略决策”的转变。

Abstract: KV-cache reuse mechanisms increasingly expose priority, duration, offload, routing hints, scheduler modes, and event streams. These mechanisms help preserve reusable prefixes, but they do not by themselves define a portable contract for accepted future-reuse state when resident KV and active live KV cannot both fit. We introduce resident KV claims, a conformance contract that binds future-reuse intent to a materialization predicate, lifecycle state, active/resident feasibility outcome, and claim-level telemetry. In controlled vLLM allocator probes, a 60-block resident claim and a 70-block active prefill exceed an 80-block usable KV pool. Write no-admit prevents the active request from becoming future reusable state, but it still allows active allocation to evict residents from the shared pool. A minimal vLLM prototype shows that hard protected resident claims convert this failure mode into scheduler-visible active refusal with direct blocking-claim attribution. The result is not a production speedup or a new cache-replacement algorithm. It is a runtime contract that turns unreported resident loss into reconstructable active/resident arbitration. A companion MicroRuntime and vLLM litmus suite distinguish ordinary eviction, soft priority, write no-admit, accepted hard claims, materialization failure, demotion, expiry, active refusal, and trace-level outcome reconstruction.

</details>


### [296] [Optimus: Elastic Decoding for Efficient Diffusion LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24832)
*Chiyue Wei,Cong Guo,Bowen Duan,Junyao Zhang,Haoxuan Shan,Yifei Wang,Yangjie Zhou,Hai "Helen" Li,Danyang Zhuo,Yiran Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Optimus 是一款针对扩散大模型（DLLM）的弹性服务系统，通过动态调整解码粒度来匹配实时负载，有效解决了固定分块策略在不同吞吐需求下的性能低效问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的自回归（AR）解码受限于序列化执行导致 GPU 利用率低；虽扩散式大模型（DLLM）支持并行解码，但固定块大小的策略在低负载时资源闲置，高负载时又因过度计算导致冗余，难以应对生产环境中的动态负载需求。

Method: 提出了 Optimus 服务系统，包含两个核心机制：1) 基于分块解码（Chunked Decoding）的细粒度执行框架，支持无损自适应调整；2) “饱和感知调度”（Saturation-aware Scheduling），通过闭环反馈机制根据运行时负载动态选择最优块大小，并配合深度优化的注意力算子实现硬件加速。

Result: Optimus 在多种负载条件下均表现出极佳的稳定性，相较于传统的 AR 解码吞吐量提升最高达 6.1 倍，较固定块大小的扩散式模型提升达 4.3 倍，同时在满足延迟约束的前提下大幅提高了系统吞吐容量。

Conclusion: Optimus 提出的动态弹性解码策略有效解决了扩散大模型在不同负载下的性能瓶颈，通过自适应解码粒度实现了 GPU 利用率与计算效率的最优均衡，显著提升了端到端的服务吞吐量。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) serving is fundamentally limited by inefficient hardware utilization. Autoregressive (AR) decoding underutilizes GPUs due to its strictly sequential execution, while diffusion LLMs (DLLMs) improve throughput by decoding multiple tokens per iteration. However, fixed block-size diffusion decoding exhibits strong load sensitivity: large blocks exploit idle GPU resources under low load, but saturate early and incur substantial redundant computation under high load. As a result, throughput gains vanish beyond saturation, and no single decoding granularity performs well across dynamic serving workloads.
  We present Optimus, a serving system that enables elastic decoding for diffusion LLMs by dynamically adapting decoding granularity to runtime load. The key idea is to treat decoding granularity as a runtime control variable, balancing GPU utilization and token efficiency. Optimus combines chunked decoding, which enables fine-grained execution without retraining, with saturation-aware scheduling, a closed-loop mechanism that selects chunk sizes based on runtime conditions. Together with system-level optimizations and customized attention kernels, Optimus achieves significant performance improvements while preserving model accuracy. Experiments show that Optimus delivers up to 6.1x throughput improvement over AR decoding and 4.3x improvement over fixed-block diffusion LLM, while maintaining stable performance across diverse load regimes and improving end-to-end serving capacity under latency constraints. The source code is available at https://github.com/dubcyfor3/Optimus.

</details>


### [297] [Kavier: Exploring Performance, Sustainability, and Efficiency of LLM Ecosystems under Inference through Cache-Aware Discrete-Event Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25247)
*Radu Nicolae,Alexandru Iosup,Animesh Trivedi,Jesse Donkervliet*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对LLM推理生态缺乏参考架构和评估工具的问题，本文提出了LLM推理参考架构，并研发了高效的离散事件模拟器Kavier，用于预测和优化LLM系统的可持续性、性能及成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理工作负载的日益增长带来了严峻的性能、财务和可持续性挑战，但目前缺乏统一的参考架构和专业的模拟工具来指导LLM生态系统的设计与决策，导致研发和维护过程存在盲目性和高昂成本。

Method: 1. 提出并验证了一个LLM推理生态系统的参考架构（RA）；2. 开发了模拟器Kavier，基于离散事件模拟（Discrete-Event Simulation）和缓存感知技术（Cache-aware），重点针对KV缓存和前缀缓存（Prompt Prefix Caching）策略进行建模和性能评估。

Result: 通过实验验证：1. Kavier在处理大规模模拟时具备高精度和高性能；2. 能够定量分析不同KV缓存和前缀缓存策略对系统综合表现（性能、资源消耗、效率）的具体影响。

Conclusion: Kavier填补了LLM推理生态模拟工具的空白。通过验证后的参考架构与模拟器，能够有效帮助研究人员和工程师在无需高昂实际开销的情况下，准确预测和优化LLM生态系统的性能、可持续性和成本效率。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are widely used by our increasingly digitalized society, but raise sustainability, performance, and financial concerns, especially as inference workloads grow. To improve the design and operation of LLM ecosystems, we envision simulators and simulation-based digital twins becoming primary decision-making tools. LLM ecosystems leverage many heterogeneous components, making simulation a non-trivial, yet critical operation. The simulation challenge is exacerbated by the absence of a comprehensive reference architecture of LLM ecosystems; the lack of such a conceptual model can be costly and could misguide the designers and engineers. Without a reference architecture, even the most experienced stakeholders could tinker in researching, engineering, or maintaining LLM ecosystems. In this work, we bring a three-fold contribution to the scientific community. Firstly, we synthesize, propose, and validate a reference architecture (RA) of LLM ecosystems under inference. Then, adhering to the reference architecture, we design Kavier, the first simulation instrument able to predict the performance, sustainability, and efficiency of LLM ecosystems under inference, through discrete-event and cache-aware simulation, focusing on Key-Value-(KV-)Caching and prompt prefix caching policies. Through experiments with a Kavier prototype and real-world traces, (i) we measure the accuracy of Kavier and its performance in massive-scale simulations, (ii) we compare the performance of different KV-Caching policies, and (iii) we analyze the performance, sustainability, and efficiency of LLM ecosystems under various prefix caching policies. Overall, we show that Kavier enables operators, researchers, and engineers to predict LLM ecosystems in a time, performance, and cost-efficient way.

</details>


### [298] [DECICE: AI-Driven Scheduling and Digital Twin Integration for the Cloud-HPC-Edge Compute Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25292)
*Aasish Kumar Sharma,Felix Stein,Mirac Aydin,Michael Bidollahkhani,Sachin P. Nanavati,Mohsen Seyedkazemi Ardebili,Giorgi Mamulashvili,Mojtaba Akbari,Jonathan Decker,Zoya Masih,Julian M. Kunkel*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: DECICE是一个面向云-HPC-边缘连续体的开源智能调度框架，核心在于通过基于RNN的AI调度算法和数字孪生系统，跨异构环境优化工作流部署，实现能效监控与动态调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决高性能计算、云与边缘计算连续体中，复杂计算任务在异构资源间高效、低碳调度及管理的需求，以应对日益增长的分布式计算挑战。

Method: 提出了集成AI调度器（IAIS）和数字孪生基础设施：IAIS利用RNN进行预测并结合形式化工作流建模进行约束感知映射；数字孪生技术汇总实时指标、碳强度数据及异常预测，实现能效感知型调度；通过Kubernetes集成及Slurm插件桥接云原生与HPC编排。

Result: 实现了跨平台的统一工作流摄取与调度，成功整合了开源的AI调度引擎与数字孪生监控系统，支持云原生与HPC环境的协同工作，并通过多领域的用例验证了框架的有效性与系统性能。

Conclusion: DECICE项目成功构建了一个跨云、高性能计算（HPC）和边缘计算的智能工作负载调度开源框架，通过AI驱动的调度策略与数字孪生技术，有效提升了复杂环境下计算任务的能效与资源利用率。

Abstract: This paper presents the DECICE project (Device Edge Cloud Intelligent Collaboration framEwork), a Horizon Europe Research and Innovation Action (Grant No. 101092582, December 2022 to November 2025) that developed an open-source framework for intelligent workload scheduling across the cloud-HPC-edge compute continuum. A consortium of 12 partners across 6 European countries organized the work into six work packages covering AI-driven scheduling, digital twin infrastructure, system architecture and integration, monitoring, use case validation, and dissemination. The two core technical contributions are an Integrated AI Scheduler (IAIS) employing RNN-based prediction and formal workflow modeling for constraint-aware workload mapping, and a Digital Twin aggregating real-time metrics with carbon intensity and anomaly prediction for energy-aware scheduling. The framework operates within Kubernetes environments, supports unified workflow ingestion from multiple formats, and bridges cloud-native and HPC orchestration through a Slurm integration layer. We present the project vision, the overall architecture, contributions from each work package, quantitative evaluation results, and the open-source release.

</details>


### [299] [Multithreaded Fine-Grained Asynchronous BSP for Integer Sorting with LCI and OpenMP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25340)
*Minyu Cheng,Jiakun Yan,Marc Snir*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对传统BSP模型在处理不规则负载时的局限性，提出了一种结合LCI与OpenMP的多线程FA-BSP方法，通过细粒度异步通信与多线程并行，显著提升了不规则科学计算的性能与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的块同步并行（BSP）模型因其僵化的全局通信机制，难以应对不规则工作负载。尽管现有的细粒度异步BSP（FA-BSP）有所改进，但通常受限于“单进程单核心”模型的制约，无法充分挖掘多核架构的潜力。

Method: 提出了一种基于轻量级通信接口（LCI）和OpenMP的多线程细粒度异步BSP（FA-BSP）方法。通过利用LCI的细粒度、零拷贝主动消息替代传统的同步MPI集合通信，实现计算与通信的高效重叠，并结合OpenMP进行多线程并行化。

Result: 使用NAS并行基准测试中的整数排序（IS）进行评估，结果显示多线程FA-BSP在处理不规则数据分布时，显著超越了传统的块同步MPI实现，在计算-通信重叠和负载均衡方面表现出色。

Conclusion: 提出了多线程FA-BSP框架，通过LCI和OpenMP的结合，为处理不规则负载的科学计算应用提供了一种高效且可扩展的解决方案，优于传统的BSP模型。

Abstract: The bulk synchronous parallel (BSP) model struggles with irregular workloads due to rigid global communication. While fine-grained asynchronous BSP (FA-BSP) improves overlap, existing implementations typically rely on a limiting one-process-per-core model. This paper proposes a multithreaded FA-BSP approach combining Lightweight Communication Interface (LCI) and OpenMP to fully exploit multicore architectures. We evaluate this design using the NAS Parallel Benchmark Integer Sort (IS), retaining the original irregular Gaussian distribution to rigorously test load balancing. By replacing synchronous MPI collectives with OpenMP multithreading and LCI's fine-grained, zero-copy active messages, we enable efficient computation-communication overlap. Our evaluation demonstrates that multithreaded FA-BSP significantly outperforms traditional bulk-synchronous MPI implementations, offering a scalable solution for irregular scientific applications.

</details>


### [300] [An Empirical Evaluation of Quantum-Inspired QUBO Methods for Heterogeneous HPC Workflow Mapping and Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25350)
*Aasish Kumar Sharma,Christian Boehme,Julian Kunkel*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文通过实证对比指出，尽管QUBO在小规模调度中有效，但目前在处理更复杂的HPC工作流规模和约束时，其扩展性和可行性均不及经典算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构HPC工作流调度是复杂的组合优化问题，传统精确求解器面临扩展性瓶颈，研究旨在评估基于量子启发式QUBO方法作为优化范式在HPC任务调度中的潜力与局限。

Method: 系统性实验评估：比较了三种QUBO变体（单次SA、多次SA、层级化QAOA启发式）与经典基准（MILP、CP-SAT、GA、HEFT）。通过统一流程追踪可行性、Makespan及资源利用率，并在不同规模（3-20个任务）和惩罚参数下进行压力测试。

Result: 所有QUBO方法在小规模验证集上均能找到最优解；但随着规模扩展及约束复杂化（特别是引入通信成本时），QUBO-SA在15个任务以上，QAOA启发式在10个任务以上时可行性急剧下降，而经典求解器表现稳健。

Conclusion: 尽管QUBO在小规模问题上有效，但对于HPC工作流调度，现有的QUBO方法在处理复杂约束和规模扩展时表现出明显的局限性，经典求解器（MILP、CP-SAT等）在稳健性和可行性方面仍占优。

Abstract: Heterogeneous HPC workflow scheduling under multiple hard constraints poses a challenging combinatorial optimization problem. Classical exact solvers guarantee optimality but face scalability limits, motivating interest in quantum-inspired Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization (QUBO) as an alternative optimization paradigm. This work presents a systematic empirical evaluation of QUBO-based scheduling methods against classical baselines including MILP, CP-SAT, GA, and HEFT. We evaluate three QUBO variants, single-run simulated annealing, multi-attempt annealing, and a layered QAOA-inspired schedule, with hybrid enhancement strategies on validation workflows (3-4 tasks) and synthetic scaling instances (5-20 tasks). All solvers are assessed through a unified pipeline tracking feasibility, makespan, and resource utilization under progressive constraint activation and controlled penalty sweeps. All approaches recover the expected optimal makespan on validation instances, confirming formulation correctness. However, feasibility degradation emerges for specific QUBO variants as constraint interactions intensify, particularly when communication costs are introduced. Penalty analysis reveals a sharp feasibility threshold for QUBO-SA, where insufficient penalties consistently fail and moderate-to-strong penalties restore feasibility. Scaling experiments show that classical solvers remain robust across all tested sizes, while QUBO-SA loses feasibility beyond 15 tasks and the QAOA-inspired variant beyond 10 tasks. The study provides a clear empirical characterization of the reliability boundaries of quantum-inspired QUBO formulations for HPC scheduling and identifies regimes where classical approaches remain preferable under current solver capabilities.

</details>


### [301] [Fine-Tuning and Serving Gemma 4 31B on Google Cloud TPU: A Technical Comparison with GPU Baselines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25645)
*Jatin Kishnani,Mayank Goel,Amit Singh,Pulkit Agrawal,Sairanjan Mishra*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本研究展示了在Google TPU (v5p/v6e) 上微调与部署Gemma 4模型的完整技术路径，实测表现出优于H100 GPU的训练速度、推理延迟及显著的成本经济性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 填补当前开源生态中在Google TPU硬件上进行大模型（如Gemma 4）生产级微调和部署的工具链空白，并对比TPU与GPU平台的性能与成本差异。

Method: 将基于PyTorch/HuggingFace/FSDP的GPU原生微调方案迁移至JAX + Tunix/Qwix技术栈，涵盖网格配置、LoRA命名、分片注解、梯度检查点等；推理阶段采用vLLM-TPU Docker架构，并在Google TPU v5p-8（训练）和TPU v6e-8（推理）上进行性能评估。

Result: TPU训练速度比2xH100 GPU基准快1.61倍，成本低2.12倍；推理吞吐量相当，但TPU的首字延迟降低了2倍（235ms vs 475ms）；总体训练加服务的综合成本降低了1.82倍。

Conclusion: 本研究成功验证了在TPU架构上微调和部署Gemma 4 31B模型的可行性，证明了TPU在大型语言模型训练和推理任务中相比GPU具有显著的成本效率和性能优势（特别是更快的首字延迟和更低的总体拥有成本）。

Abstract: We present the first end-to-end demonstration of fine-tuning and serving Google's Gemma 4 31B model on TPU hardware, providing an empirical comparison of TPU and GPU platforms for large language model adaptation. Using LoRA on a Google TPU v5p-8 for training and TPU v6e-8 (Trillium) for inference, we document the full set of code-level adaptations required to port a GPU-native training recipe, built on PyTorch, HuggingFace TRL, and FSDP, to the JAX + Tunix/Qwix stack. These adaptations span mesh configuration, LoRA module naming conventions, sharding annotation corrections, gradient checkpointing, data pipeline restructuring, and a custom Orbax-to-safetensors checkpoint merging procedure.
  For inference, we detail the vLLM-TPU Docker setup necessary to serve Gemma 4 on v6e-8 and characterize the resulting latency and throughput profile. Compared with a 2xH100 GPU baseline under identical hyperparameters, TPU training completes 1.61x faster at 2.12x lower cost. Inference throughput is within 3% across platforms, while TPU achieves 2x lower time-to-first-token (235 ms vs. 475 ms). Together, the TPU configuration is 1.82x cheaper for a representative train-plus-service workload.
  Our work removes a critical gap in the open tooling ecosystem and provides practitioners with a reproducible, production-ready recipe for Gemma 4 deployment on TPU infrastructure.

</details>


### [302] [When Agents Control Robots: A Zero Trust Policy Model for Agentic Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25653)
*Tharindu Ranathunga,Kavishka Fernando,Susan Rea*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 研究分析了由大模型驱动的协作机器人系统安全漏洞，并提出了一种名为ZTPM的零信任策略模型，通过将物理影响分级并实施运行时策略强制执行，以抵御智能体信息物理系统的潜在威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型驱动的多智能体系统在工业机器人控制领域的广泛应用带来了潜在的安全风险，尤其是在涉及物理执行阶段的安全性问题尚缺乏有效的防护机制。

Method: 1. 设计了“Cobot-Claw”四智能体巡检系统；2. 定义了5类针对智能体信息物理系统（CPS）的攻击模型；3. 提出了ZTPM，包含25个类型化原语及5个执行域，并引入“物理影响分级（Physical Impact Tiers）”机制。

Result: 通过对60个执行轨迹的实证评估，发现大模型的动作参数选择具有模型依赖性和非确定性，验证了在物理执行边界实施策略管控的必要性。

Conclusion: 基于大模型驱动的工业机器人系统存在独特的安全威胁，亟需在物理执行边界引入零信任策略模型（ZTPM）进行强制管控。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems powered by large foundation models (LFMs) are increasingly deployed to control industrial robots through natural language, creating deployments in which security failures produce physical consequences. We analyse this threat landscape through Cobot-Claw, a deployed four-agent system for UR3e robotic arm control, and identify five attack classes specific to agentic cyber-physical systems. We propose ZTPM, a Zero Trust Policy Model comprising 25 typed primitives across five enforcement domains with Physical Impact Tiers as a runtime policy dimension. An empirical evaluation across 60 execution traces on two LFM backends provides initial evidence that actuation parameter selection is model-dependent and non-deterministic, motivating the need for policy-level enforcement at the physical actuation boundary.

</details>


### [303] [Bandwidth-Aware LLM Inference on Heterogeneous Many-Core Supercomputers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25655)
*Yao Lu,Zhongzhi Luan,Gen Li,Jiaxing Qi,Shiqing Ma,Bin Han,Shizhe Shang,Hailong Yang,Depei Qian*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: THInfer 是一款专为异构多核处理器设计的高性能 LLM 推理框架，通过算子优化、计算图融合和混合并行流水线技术，在极度受限的带宽条件下大幅提升了推理吞吐量与扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 推理在大规模异构多核处理器（如天河超算 MT-3000）上部署时，面临严重的存储带宽限制和复杂的内存架构挑战，现有 GPU 推理框架无法直接迁移且性能不佳。

Method: 1. 为 VLIW SIMD 架构（MT-3000）开发高性能算子库（优化 FP16 内核）；2. 采用密度驱动的计算图融合、统一内核调度及分阶段流水线注意力融合技术；3. 实现 P-B-D（Prefill-Buffer-Decode）流水线与基于 MPI 和 hthreads 的两级通信机制，支持混合并行与多集群协作。

Result: 在 Llama 系列模型上，7B 模型吞吐量较 DeepSpeed 在 2xV100S 上提升 62%-73%，较 A800 上提升 67%-84%；13B/30B 模型表现出色；70B 模型在 GPU 框架无法运行的情况下仍能稳定推理。

Conclusion: THInfer 通过硬件-软件协同设计与并行策略优化，解决了异构多核处理器（如 MT-3000）在 LLM 推理中的带宽瓶颈问题，实现了高效、可扩展的推理能力，为异构架构上的大模型部署提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) inference is limited by high computational cost and memory bandwidth demands, making deployment on heterogeneous many-core processors challenging. Taking the MT-3000 processor used in the Tianhe supercomputer as an example, its limited main-memory bandwidth and distributed memory hierarchy exemplify these bottlenecks, making it difficult to directly migrate existing GPU-based inference frameworks. To address this problem, we propose THInfer, a hardware-aware inference framework that maximizes data locality under bandwidth-constrained conditions through hardware-software co-design and parallel strategy optimization. THInfer incorporates three key techniques: (1) a high-performance operator library for the VLIW SIMD architecture, providing hand-optimized FP16 kernels that achieve up to 70 percent of the peak performance per cluster; (2) a density-driven computation graph fusion and unified kernel scheduling mechanism, combined with a staged pipelined attention fusion method; and (3) a Prefill-Buffer-Decode (P-B-D) pipeline and bounded buffer management strategy, which supports hybrid parallelism and enables efficient multi-cluster collaboration through two-level communication based on MPI and hthreads. Experiments on the Llama model series show that THInfer improves throughput on the 7B model by 62 percent to 73 percent over DeepSpeed on two V100S GPUs and by 67 percent to 84 percent over the A800 GPU. The 13B and 30B models also demonstrate comparable or better performance. Moreover, THInfer maintains stable performance on the 70B model, whereas typical GPU-based frameworks fail to run under the same setting. Overall, THInfer significantly enhances throughput, reduces latency, and improves scalability, providing a feasible system solution for efficient and scalable LLM inference on heterogeneous many-core architectures.

</details>


### [304] [Profiling-Driven Adaptive Distributed Transformer Inference on Embedded Edge Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25682)
*Muhammad Azlan Qazi,Alexandros Iosifidis,Qi Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对嵌入式边缘硬件分布式Transformer推理的通信瓶颈，提出Prism框架，通过动态决策和压缩技术，显著降低了延迟与能耗，证明了自适应执行策略在资源有限场景中的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多依赖于不准确的模拟，未能揭示在真实嵌入式边缘硬件上进行Transformer分布式推理时，由于硬件通信开销带来的实际性能损耗。

Method: 1. 硬件原型研究：搭建基于WiFi连接的NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano集群。
2. 瓶颈分析：识别出CPU-GPU阶段通信（由于缺乏NVLink/PCIe路径，必须通过GLOO和CPU内存进行转储）为主要瓶颈。
3. 优化策略（Prism）：结合Segment Means压缩技术与轻量级离线分析，根据模型和硬件状态在运行时动态决定是否采用分布式推理。

Result: 在ViT等中等规模模型上，相比传统的全张量交换分布式执行，该策略将延迟降低了65%-77%，能耗降低了34%-52%。

Conclusion: 在嵌入式边缘设备上进行分布式Transformer推理时，盲目进行全张量交换往往不如单设备推理高效。通过基于离线性能分析的自适应策略（如Prism），在本地执行与分布式执行之间进行动态选择，是实现实际性能提升的关键。

Abstract: Distributing Transformer inference across embedded edge devices can alleviate individual memory and compute constraints, yet practical benefits on real hardware remain unclear: prior work relies largely on simulations that overlook hardware-specific communication overheads. We present a hardware prototype study on NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano devices connected over WiFi. Our key finding is that the dominant bottleneck is not just network bandwidth but also the CPU-GPU staging during communication. Because Jetson's integrated GPU architecture lacks the PCIe/NVLink pathway that NCCL requires, all inter-device data communication should be routed through GLOO and staged in CPU memory; an overhead that scales with communication data volume and makes full-tensor exchange slower than single-device inference across the batch sizes for medium sized models such as ViT. We therefore evaluate Prism by combining Segment Means compression with lightweight offline profiling to adaptively select between local and distributed execution at runtime. Experiments show that this strategy reduces latency by 65%-77% and energy consumption by 34%-52% relative to full-tensor exchange in static distributed execution setup, demonstrating that profiling-driven adaptation is essential for practical distributed Transformer inference on embedded hardware.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [305] [Accelerating Long-Tail Generation in Synchronous RLHF Training via Adaptive Tensor Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23945)
*Long Zhao,Qinghe Wang,Jiaan Zhu,Youhui Bai,Zewen Jin,Chaoyi Ruan,Shengnan Wang,Cheng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PAT是一种针对RLHF生成阶段的自适应张量并行（TP）方法，通过动态调整TP配置及轻量化状态重构，有效解决了响应长度不均导致的GPU低利用率问题，大幅提升了训练速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在RLHF流程中，生成阶段因不同序列长度导致GPU负载不均衡，且静态张量并行（TP）配置无法适应动态变化的batch特征，导致计算资源严重浪费。

Method: 提出了PAT（Adaptive Tensor Parallelism）方法，包含：1. 预测引导的在线重配置策略，依据离线分析决定重配置时机与目标配置；2. 轻量级在线重配置机制，通过KV缓存迁移与重计算平衡、原地权重重分片及通信组复用，减少状态更新开销。

Result: 在LLaMA3.1-8B和Qwen3-14B模型上，PAT将生成延迟降低了34.6%，端到端RLHF训练迭代延迟降低了27.2%。

Conclusion: PAT通过自适应调整TP配置，成功解决了RLHF生成阶段因响应长度不一导致的GPU利用率低下问题，显著提升了训练效率。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) has become a key post-training paradigm for improving model quality. However, the synchronous three-stage RLHF pipeline is often bottlenecked by the generation stage, where response-length skew causes the effective batch size to shrink rapidly during decoding, leaving GPUs underutilized while a few long responses remain unfinished. Mainstream frameworks employ a static tensor parallelism (TP) configuration that cannot adapt to changing batch characteristics, leaving substantial performance headroom unexplored. We propose PAT, an adaptive TP method that dynamically reconfigures TP during the generation stage of each RLHF iteration. PAT introduces two key techniques. First, a predictor-guided online reconfiguration method decides both the reconfiguration point and the target TP configuration based on offline profiling, triggering reconfiguration only when the predicted latency benefit outweighs the reconfiguration overhead. Second, a lightweight online reconfiguration mechanism updates only the states and layouts affected by TP changes: it adapts unfinished decoding states through a cost-model-based choice between KV-cache migration and recomputation, performs in-place weight resharding, and reuses cached communication groups. We implement PAT on top of SGLang and integrate it with the VeRL framework. Evaluations on LLaMA3.1-8B and Qwen3-14B using DeepScaleR show that PAT reduces generation latency by up to 34.6% and end-to-end RLHF training iteration latency by up to 27.2% compared to the original VeRL setup.

</details>


### [306] [Identifying and Mitigating Systemic Measurement Bias in Production LLM Inference Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24217)
*Ashok Chandrasekar,Jason Kramberger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 现有的LLM基准测试工具因受限于单进程架构及Python GIL，在高并发下会导致性能指标失真。本文提出了多进程评估框架和NTPOT指标，解决了测量偏差，实现了生产级高并发场景下的精准性能评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大模型基准测试工具在生产环境高并发下存在严重的测量偏差，导致由于客户端性能瓶颈而非模型本身造成的TTFT和TPOT指标虚高。

Method: 1. 利用M/G/1排队模型分析证明了现有单进程异步基准测试工具中Python GIL导致的测量偏差；2. 设计了一种多进程、低开销的评估框架；3. 提出了新的复合性能指标——归一化输出Token时间（NTPOT）。

Result: 通过新框架消除了客户端排队开销，实现了在每秒数千次查询规模下对大模型推理引擎性能的准确、可复现评估。

Conclusion: 实验证明，提出的多进程评估框架与NTPOT指标有效消除了客户端测量偏差，能够准确、稳健地评估高并发场景下大模型的服务性能。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) transition from research environments to production deployments, evaluating their performance against strict Service Level Objectives (SLOs) has become critical. However, current evaluation methodologies suffer from severe measurement bias at scale. We demonstrate that widely used benchmarking utilities rely on single-process, asyncio-driven architectures that introduce fundamental client-side queuing bottlenecks under high concurrency. By modeling the benchmarking client as an $M/G/1$ queue, we mathematically demonstrate how the Python Global Interpreter Lock (GIL) artificially inflates Time to First Token (TTFT) and Time Per Output Token (TPOT) metrics as request rates scale. To resolve this systematic inaccuracy, we propose an unbiased, multi-process evaluation framework that effectively distributes client-side load, ensuring negligible queuing overhead. Furthermore, we formalize a composite metric, Normalized Time Per Output Token (NTPOT), to robustly amortize end-to-end latency, including prefill and scheduling delays across sequence lengths. Our empirical evaluation demonstrates that this methodology successfully isolates pure serving engine performance, enabling accurate, reproducible profiling of LLMs at production scales exceeding thousands of queries per second.

</details>


### [307] [When Does Synthetic Patent Data Help? Volume-Fidelity Trade-offs in Low-Resource Multi-Label Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24296)
*Amirhossein Yousefiramandi,Ciaran Cooney*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过严格控制实验，揭示了专利分类任务中合成数据的性能提升主要归因于数据规模增大而非合成质量，并指出合成数据在不同任务（分类vs检索）间存在显著的性能权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究在大规模语言模型生成合成数据用于专利分类时，性能的提升是源于“合成数据的真实价值”还是单纯的“数据规模扩增效应”，并揭示数据合成在不同场景下的表现差异。

Method: 实验涵盖6种开源LLM、4种真实数据规模、64个WIPO类别及3种分类器架构。采用“重复采样对照组”以剔除数据扩增的体积效应，并引入多种泄露控制手段（标签掩码、指令脱敏等）及数值稳定性验证。

Result: BERT-for-Patents分类任务中，合成数据带来的F1分数提升主要由数据规模驱动（+0.024受控增益 vs +0.58总增益）；合成数据在分类任务中表现优异，但会导致检索任务性能显著下降（nDCG@10下降26%）；此外，揭示了ModernBERT在特定算子下的数值坍缩现象。

Conclusion: 合成专利文本的效果具有高度的“任务与指标特异性”，不能简单通过提示词范式进行泛化。此外，性能提升主要源于数据规模而非合成质量，且现有评估指标（如MMD）在不同数据尺度下存在相关性反转。

Abstract: We study when LLM-generated synthetic data helps low-resource multi-label patent classification, separating true synthetic value from the confound that larger augmented sets can win by volume alone. Across six open-source LLMs (3.8-12B), four real-data regimes, 64 WIPO assistive-technology labels, two generation strategies, and three classifier families, the headline BERT-for-Patents micro-F1 jump from 0.120 to 0.702 is largely volume-driven. A duplicate-to-match real-only control that resamples 165 patents to the augmented size reaches 0.678; the controlled synthetic gain is only +0.024 over this control, but +0.219 over focal-loss reweighting, the strongest non-augmentation baseline. The main finding is that fidelity metrics change meaning with scale: at extreme scarcity, MMD correlates positively with classification gain (r=+0.95), but at 1:10 the relation flips (r=-0.73; Fisher z=+6.47, p<0.001). Fixed-budget mixing finds a 20-30% real / 70-80% synthetic optimum; paraphrase scaling collapses from a 165-document seed; and shuffled mixing beats curriculum ordering, ensembling, and classifier-based filtering. Leakage controls -- label-name masking, instruction-level label removal, fine-grained evaluation, and keyword-overlap audits -- argue against label-string dependence as the main driver for BERT-for-Patents. The apparent ModernBERT collapse under label removal is traced to a Flash-Attention-2 + bf16 numerical artifact, recovering 65% of lost performance with fp32 eager attention. Finally, the same corpus that improves classification by up to +0.58 raw micro-F1 hurts a Jaccard-label-overlap retrieval proxy; even a standard-patent-only filter leaves a 26% nDCG@10 drop. Thus, synthetic patent text is task- and metric-specific, not reducible to prompt genre alone.

</details>


### [308] [Beyond Control-Flow: Integrating the Resource Perspective into Multi-Collaborative Process Modeling from Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24546)
*Anton Antonov,Humam Kourani,Alessandro Berti,Gyunam Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过引入资源感知生成流水线，利用中间语言和正交布局算法，实现了从自然语言描述到包含完整资源协作细节（泳道、角色、消息交互）的BPMN 2.0模型的自动化生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于LLM的自动化流程建模方法主要关注控制流（活动顺序），但忽略了业务流程中关键的资源协作维度（即组织与角色间的交互）。

Method: 提出了一种资源感知生成流水线，包含三个核心组件：1. 使用紧凑的可执行中间语言（定义组织泳道池及角色泳道）；2. 通过中间语言强制LLM包含资源细节，并利用消息事件处理跨组织交互；3. 采用正交布局算法自动处理BPMN元素的空间排列。

Result: 在十个业务流程和九个模型上的实验表明，该方法在保持控制流质量的同时，具备极强的资源发现能力，且引入的运行时开销仅为边缘影响。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的资源感知生成流程有效弥补了现有文本到模型（Text-to-Model）方法在流程建模中忽略协作视角的不足，通过引入中间语言和自动化布局算法，实现了对复杂BPMN 2.0协作图的高质量生成。

Abstract: Process modeling is a sub-domain of Business Process Management (BPM) focused on the translation of process artifacts into formal models. This task traditionally requires extensive human input and domain expertise in both BPM notations and the specific business context. While Large Language Models (LLMs) can now automate much of this manual work, current text-to-model approaches focus predominantly on the control-flow perspective-ordering activities without considering the collaborative aspect of the processes. In this paper, we introduce a resource-aware generation pipeline that produces formal BPMN 2.0 collaboration diagrams from natural-language descriptions. Rather than solely prompting an LLM for raw XML, we describe a compact, executable intermediate language with mandatory resource details defining both the organization (pool) and the role (lane). Cross-organization dependencies are materialized using the standard formal notation for such interactions-message events-while an orthogonal layout routine automatically handles the spatial arrangement of elements within pools and lanes. Experiments on ten business processes with nine LLMs show strong resource discovery while preserving control-flow quality and adding only marginal runtime overhead. This approach moves generative modeling toward a more comprehensive, multi-collaborative representation of business operations.

</details>


### [309] [MEMOR-E: In-Context and Fine-Tuned LLM Personalization for Alzheimer's Assistive Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23941)
*Maissa Abir Smaili,Eren Sadikoglu,Ransalu Senanayake*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了MEMOR-E，一款辅助阿尔茨海默病患者的四足机器人。该研究通过微调大语言模型实现认知行为模拟，并利用上下文学习与可解释技术，为患者提供个性化支持，同时确保护理人员可监督且交互可信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病患者面临记忆力和语言能力下降，影响日常生活。为了提供社交辅助支持，研究人员旨在开发一款能够提供用药提醒、常规指导、记忆导向互动和陪伴的机器人，并探索大语言模型在该场景下的可行性。

Method: 1. 开发了MEMOR-E移动四足机器人；2.利用235名阿尔茨海默病患者和合成健康对照组的音频转录本，微调大语言模型（LLM）以模拟阶段一致的认知行为；3. 采用上下文学习（ICL）方法，引入第二个LLM生成领域和严重程度相关的认知错误总结；4. 引入可解释人工智能（XAI）机制，将模型输出转换为人类可读的证据。

Result: 研究结果表明，MEMOR-E能够生成阶段感知、非诊断性的认知总结，有效支持个性化辅助交互，且通过XAI机制提高了模型输出的可解释性，便于护理人员进行监督。

Conclusion: MEMOR-E机器人通过微调大语言模型，能实现分阶段的认知行为模拟和非诊断性认知总结，具备了提供个性化辅助交互和建立可信赖人机交互的潜力。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease is a neurodegenerative disorder marked by progressive declines in memory and language that reduce independence in daily life, motivating socially assistive robotic support. This paper presents MEMOR-E, a mobile quadruped robot with an interactive tablet interface that assists patients and caregivers through medication reminders, routine guidance, memory oriented interactions, and companionship. We evaluated the feasibility of fine tuning large language models (LLMs) to emulate stage consistent cognitive behavior and interpret responses across standard neuropsychological language tasks, using audio transcriptions from 235 Alzheimer's patients and synthetically generated healthy controls. We also report findings on using in context learning (ICL) in LLMs, where a second LLM produced domain and severity level cognitive error summaries. Our results show that MEMOR-E can generate stage aware, non diagnostic cognitive summaries that support personalized assistive interactions, while explainable AI mechanisms translate model outputs into transparent, human readable evidence to enable caregiver oversight and trustworthy human robot interaction.

</details>


### [310] [Why We Need World Models for AGI: Where LLMs Fail and How World Models May Outperform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23972)
*Feisal Alaswad,Batoul Aljaddouh,Maher Alrahhal,Poovammal E,Talal Bonny*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文探讨了LLM在长程逻辑推理中的局限性，提出了LDI框架，并通过Flux基准实验证明：显式建模潜在状态动态的智能体在复杂推理任务中的成功率远高于仅依赖序列预测的语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型（LLM）在语言生成和知识任务表现优异，但在因果推理、持久状态跟踪和长程规划方面存在显著瓶颈。作者认为这是因为序列预测目标与潜在环境动态推理之间存在客观目标的不匹配。

Method: 1. 提出“潜在动态推理（LDI）”概念框架，将语言视为潜在环境动态的观测证据；2. 创建基于自然语言规则的“Flux”序列推理基准环境；3. 将文本规则编译为明确的状态转换模拟器，对比基于纯文本观测的LLM与直接在潜在状态空间中训练的强化学习（RL）智能体。

Result: 在Flux环境中，拥有显式潜在状态访问能力的智能体表现远优于LLM，获胜率约为79%（LLM仅为11%）。分析显示LLM主要在状态跟踪误差、非法动作和短视推理等问题上遭遇失败。

Conclusion: 仅依靠序列预测不足以支持鲁棒的长程动态推理，必须引入持久状态跟踪和转换建模机制。

Abstract: Large language models achieve strong performance in language generation and knowledge-intensive tasks, yet remain limited in settings requiring causal reasoning, persistent state tracking, and long-horizon planning. We argue that these limitations may arise from an objective-level mismatch between sequence prediction and reasoning over latent environment dynamics. To formalize this distinction, we introduce Latent Dynamics Inference (LDI), a conceptual perspective that interprets language and multimodal observations as partial evidence of underlying transition dynamics. To empirically investigate this perspective, we introduce Flux, a sequential reasoning environment specified entirely through natural-language rules. As a proof-of-concept case study, the rules are first compiled into an explicit state-transition simulator, illustrating that structured latent transition dynamics can, in some cases, be operationally extracted from textual rule descriptions. This enables a controlled comparison between the LLMs operating purely over textual observations and reinforcement-learning agents trained directly within the extracted latent state space. Within this case study, agents operating with explicit access to the latent state space exhibit substantially more stable behavior in long-horizon gameplay, achieving an aggregate win rate of approximately 79% versus 11% for LLMs. Qualitative analysis further reveals failure modes consistent with unstable persistent state tracking, including invalid actions, state-tracking errors, and short-horizon reasoning failures. The complete implementation of the Flux environment available at https://github.com/FeisalAlaswad/FLUX-RL-Agent Within the evaluated setting, these results suggest that strong sequence prediction alone may struggle to support robust long-horizon dynamic reasoning without mechanisms for persistent state tracking and transition modeling

</details>


### [311] [Beyond Predefined Learning Objects: A Thinking-Learning Interaction Model for Up-to-Date Autonomous Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23987)
*Hong Su*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对传统机器人学习受限于预设框架的问题，本文提出了一种“思维-学习”双向交互模型，使机器人能够动态地适应环境变化、自主发现特征并优化动作策略，显著增强了应对未知任务的适应性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人学习方法往往依赖预定义的输入、输出或任务结构，导致机器人在面对开放、动态环境时难以适应新特征、新类别或优化任务逻辑，缺乏长期运行下的自主适应性。

Method: 提出了“思维-学习（Thinking-Learning）”交互模型：以思维引导学习（识别变化、筛选准则、组织数据、规划验证），并以学习反馈增强思维（更新知识库、优化特征选择、动作策略及推理过程），实现二者间的双向循环与协同演化。

Result: 实验表明，在特征适应方面识别准确率从0.419提升至0.845；在动作重构方面平均动作长度从13.0降低至4.0；在学习增强思维方面，有效证据筛选率从0.272提高至0.965。

Conclusion: 本文提出的“思维-学习”交互模型通过双向反馈机制，成功使机器人突破了预定义学习设置的限制，实现了在开放动态环境中自主适应输入特征、类别扩展、模型更新及动作策略优化，显著提升了机器人的长期自主适应能力。

Abstract: Autonomous robots operating in open and changing environments cannot always rely on predefined inputs, outputs, and action routines. Although existing learning methods enable robots to improve their performance through environmental interaction, the objects of learning are often fixed in advance, such as input features, recognition outputs, network structures, task goals, or action sequences. This limits their ability to adapt when new features, new categories, or more efficient task routines appear during long-term operation. To address this problem, this paper proposes a thinking-learning interaction model for autonomous robots. The core idea is that thinking guides learning by identifying potential changes, selecting useful evidence, organizing training materials, and planning verification actions, while learning promotes thinking by updating task knowledge, feature-selection experience, action strategies, and future reasoning processes. Based on this bidirectional mechanism, the robot can gradually move beyond predefined learning settings and adapt its recognition relations and action relations through continuous interaction with the environment. Specifically, the proposed model supports adaptive input feature discovery, output category expansion, learning model update, and action routine reconstruction. Experimental results show that the proposed model improves the final recognition accuracy from 0.419 to 0.845 in feature adaptation, achieves higher new-category formation accuracy and model-update success rate, and reduces the average action length from 13.0 to 4.0 in action routine reconstruction. In learning-enhanced thinking, the useful evidence selection rate increases from 0.272 to 0.965, indicating that learning results can effectively improve future evidence selection and reasoning.

</details>


### [312] [Reason--Imagine--Act: Closed-Loop LLM Decision Making with World Models for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24004)
*Zhengqi Sun,Yiwen Sun,Boxuan Liu,Tailai Chen,Tianxu Guo,Jiabin Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RIA 是一个将 LLM 推理与在线世界模型验证相结合的自动驾驶决策框架，通过在执行前对动作进行物理仿真验证，显著提升了自动驾驶的安全性与任务完成度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现存大语言模型应用于自动驾驶时，仅关注语义决策会导致物理层面的不安全性；现有方法缺乏在线的显式动力学验证，导致语义意图与物理落地之间存在脱节。

Method: 提出 RIA（Reason-Imagine-Act）闭环框架：1. LLM 推理器负责生成动作模板和子动作；2. 动作条件世界模型进行短时程滚动预测（rollouts）；3. 安全评分模块对比并筛选出最安全的动作，并将结果反馈至下一轮推理。

Result: 在 CARLA 模拟环境（1000 轮实验）中，达到 80.05% 的路线完成率、51.10% 的抵达率，碰撞率低至 0.20%，且显著优于 CARLA TM 和 MADA 等训练无关（training-free）基准模型。

Conclusion: RIA 框架证明了将大语言模型（LLM）的语义推理与基于动作条件的世界模型（World Model）相结合，能够有效弥合自动驾驶决策中语义意图与物理可行性之间的差距，从而在线实现更安全的自动驾驶决策。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are promising for autonomous driving, but semantics-only decision policies can yield physically unsafe behavior in dynamic traffic. Existing methods either perform online language reasoning without explicit dynamics verification or use world models mainly in offline pipelines, leaving a gap between semantic intent and physical feasibility at decision time. We propose Reason--Imagine--Act (RIA), a closed-loop framework that couples an LLM reasoner with an action-conditioned world model for online safety verification. At each step, the LLM proposes an action template and candidate sub-actions, the world model performs short-horizon rollouts, and a safety scorer selects the safest executable action with feedback to the next reasoning step. Under a unified CARLA point-goal protocol (1000 episodes), RIA achieves 80.05% route completion, 51.10% arrival rate, and 0.20% collision rate. Under the same closed-loop interface, RIA consistently outperforms training-free baselines, including CARLA TM and MADA, on core closed-loop metrics. For reproducibility, code is available at https://github.com/pku-smart-city/source_code/tree/main/RIA.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [313] [Causality as the Statistical Conscience of Artificial Intelligence: From Pearl's Ladder to Trustworthy Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24076)
*Ernest Fokoué*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文论证了因果推理对于实现鲁棒且可信的AI至关重要。通过证明因果结构是分布外泛化的必要条件，并统一了多种因果估计框架，文章指出当前AI的局限性源于因果盲目，并提出了相应的统计学纠偏方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI主要依赖统计相关性而非因果机制，导致其在分布漂移下表现脆弱、存在偏见且缺乏真正的智能。论文旨在论证因果推理是实现真正智能和可信AI的必要基础。

Method: 1. 证明了关于因果泛化的“统计必要性定理”；2. 提出了一个将Pearl的do-calculus、潜在结果框架、双重机器学习和不变风险最小化统一为“因果统计估计量”的框架；3. 分析了AI三大失效模式（LLM幻觉、RLHF奖励欺骗、分布漂移）并提供了基于因果的解决方案。

Result: 研究表明，任何实现分布外（OOD）泛化的算法都必须包含因果结构，从而在数学上确立了从P(Y|X)向P(Y|do(X))转换的必要性；同时证明了因果盲目性是当前AI系统失败的根本原因。

Conclusion: 真正可信的AI依赖于因果统计。因果推理是实现鲁棒性、泛化能力及克服当前AI缺陷的关键，统计学界需在构建因果可靠的AI系统中发挥核心作用。

Abstract: Modern Artificial Intelligence achieves remarkable predictive power by optimizing statistical risk functionals over vast corpora. Yet a gap separates this from genuine intelligence: the inability to distinguish correlation from causation. This paper argues that causal inference (identifying mechanisms invariant under intervention) is AI's indispensable statistical conscience. Without causal grounding, AI systems are correlation machines: powerful in familiar domains, brittle under distribution shift, and biased in high-stakes settings. Three contributions develop this argument. First, a Statistical Necessity Theorem for Causal Generalization: any algorithm achieving out-of-distribution generalization must encode causal structure, formalizing the distinction between prediction P(Y|X) and intelligence P(Y|do(X)). Second, a unified framework connects Pearl's do-calculus, the Potential Outcomes framework, Double Machine Learning, and Invariant Risk Minimization as a family of Causal Statistical Estimators, each identifying interventional distributions under different assumptions. Third, three AI failure modes (hallucination in large language models, reward hacking in reinforcement learning from human feedback, and degradation under distribution shift) are manifestations of causal blindness, each admitting a principled statistical remedy. Trustworthy AI is, at its core, a problem of causal statistics. The statistical community is not merely equipped to solve it -- it is the only community with the foundational tools to do so rigorously.

</details>


### [314] [MEDAL: Manifold Embedding Distillation via Autoencoder Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24244)
*Irene Chang,Tarek M. Zikry,Genevera I. Allen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对现有降维方法缺乏量化校验手段的问题，MEDAL 通过自编码器蒸馏技术将静态嵌入转化为可重用的映射模型，实现了对降维结果的留出法评估及超参数优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的非线性降维方法（如 t-SNE、UMAP）多依赖视觉主观判断，缺乏定量验证标准，核心原因在于这些方法无法提供外推映射（out-of-sample mapping）或逆映射，导致无法像监督学习那样进行留出法（held-out validation）验证。

Method: 开发了 MEDAL（流形嵌入蒸馏自编码器学习）框架：训练一个约束自编码器，其瓶颈层（bottleneck）强制匹配现有的教师嵌入，同时解码器负责重建原始输入，从而实现对静态嵌入的蒸馏，构建出具有外推能力和近似逆映射功能的模型。

Result: 实验证明 MEDAL 能实现对降维结果的留出法验证，有效进行超参数调优，发现生物学上有意义的区域，并能识别新样本的分布偏移。

Conclusion: MEDAL 提供了一个通用的验证框架，使得非线性降维方法（如 t-SNE、UMAP）能够进行定量验证和超参数调优，克服了传统方法缺乏外推映射和逆映射的局限性，提高了降维研究的科学严谨性。

Abstract: Low-dimensional embeddings are widely used as visual summaries of high-dimensional data and to enable downstream scientific discoveries. Yet, popular nonlinear dimension reduction methods, such as t-SNE and UMAP, are often selected based on visual appeal alone and without rigorous quantitative validation. A major reason is that manifold embeddings typically do not provide an out-of-sample map nor an inverse back to the original feature space; this makes held-out validation, the gold standard in supervised learning, all but impossible. To address these challenges, we develop a novel framework, MEDAL (Manifold Embedding Distillation via Autoencoder Learning), which distills a fitted manifold embedding into a reusable encoder--decoder model. MEDAL trains a constrained autoencoder whose bottleneck exactly matches any teacher embedding while the decoder reconstructs the original input; this yields an explicit map for new samples, an approximate inverse, and a pointwise reconstruction-based measure of distortion in the manifold space. This converts static manifold embeddings into models that can be evaluated on held-out data, enabling quantitative validation including comparing different dimension reduction methods as well as hyperparameter tuning. Across multiple benchmark and scientific case studies, we show that MEDAL enables held-out validation to determine optimal manifold embeddings and hyperparameters, reveals biologically coherent regions that are difficult to preserve in two dimensional embeddings, and detects distribution shift when new samples are mapped into a fixed reference manifold. MEDAL provides a general validation wrapper to any existing dimension reduction technique that will improve the rigor and

</details>


### [315] [Multicalibration Boosting: Theory, Convergence, and Transferability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24364)
*Hanxuan Ye,Hongzhe Li*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了 MCBoost 的统一理论框架，阐明了多重校准的内部机制（Bregman 投影）、收敛性与风险权衡，并通过早停法与有限样本分析，为提升现代预测模型的公平性与可靠性提供了系统的指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现有关于多重校准 Boosting (MCBoost) 的理论研究碎片化且假设过于严格的问题，旨在建立一个更通用、更符合现实条件的统一理论框架，并深入探讨其与预测风险之间的权衡（trade-off）。

Method: 提出了一种统一的 MCBoost 框架，覆盖了包括多重准确性（multiaccuracy）、BatchGCP 和 BatchMVP 在内的现有变体；采用 Bregman 投影视角分析 boosting 迭代，并引入了改进的收敛速度分析及基于早停法的原则性停止规则。

Result: 论文发现即便模型具有高准确性和灵活性，仍可能存在显著的错误校准；揭示了多重校准与预测风险之间的权衡机制；证实了 boosting 迭代收敛于在审计类函数空间的 Bregman 投影；并提供了在协变量偏移下更广泛的迁移性保证。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个统一的 MCBoost 理论框架，证明了其收敛性、有限样本保证及在协变量偏移（Covariate Shift）下的迁移能力，为多重校准提供了更稳健的理论基础与实践准则。

Abstract: Multicalibration extends classical calibration by requiring predictions to be unbiased over a rich collection of functions, encompassing both prediction slices and subpopulations. It has emerged as a powerful framework for fairness, robustness, and reliable prediction, yet the theoretical understanding of multicalibration boosting (MCBoost) remains fragmented and often relies on restrictive assumptions. In this work, we develop a unified and refined perspective on MCBoost that subsumes existing variants, including multiaccuracy, BatchGCP, and BatchMVP. We uncover several phenomena that provide new insights into its practical behavior: even highly accurate and flexible predictors can remain substantially miscalibrated; enforcing multicalibration introduces a calibration-risk trade-off; and early stopping plays a central role in controlling this trade-off. On the theoretical side, we establish a general framework for MCBoost under weaker and more realistic conditions. We show that the boosting iterates converge to a Bregman projection of the population-optimal predictor onto the cumulative span generated by the audit class, thereby explicitly characterizing the function space on which multicalibration is achieved. We further derive convergence rates under different smoothness assumptions, finite-sample guarantees, and principled stopping rules that ensure multicalibration at termination. Finally, we extend the theory of universal adaptability under covariate shift, providing more general transfer guarantees and clarifying when multicalibrated predictors generalize across domains. These results provide a more complete theoretical foundation and practical guidance for multicalibration boosting, positioning it as both a unifying framework and a reliable post-processing approach for modern predictive models.

</details>


### [316] [Clustering based on Stochastic Dominance with application for risk averters and risk seekers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24422)
*Hua Li,Xue Jia,Yilin Kang,Wing-Keung Wong*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用随机占优（SD）统计量构建聚类分析的新框架，通过改进传统聚类算法，更精准地筛选符合投资者风险偏好的资产组合，并在实证中证明了其在资产配置中的优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统聚类方法依赖几何距离（如欧几里得距离），无法有效反映资产间本质的随机占优风险关系，难以满足不同偏好投资者的资产选择需求。

Method: 构建了基于一阶、二阶和三阶随机占优（SD）测试统计量的“随机占优系数矩阵”，并据此改进了K-means和层次聚类算法（共生成12种变体）；同时设计了SD-SC系数和SD-DBI指标作为聚类有效性评价工具。

Result: 通过对美国纳斯达克指数和中国沪深300指数成分股的实证分析，验证了该方法的有效性和稳健性；将结果应用于单指数模型修正及全球最小方差组合构建，表现优于传统方法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于随机占优（SD）测试统计量的聚类框架，能够有效捕捉资产间的风险偏好关系，优于传统的几何距离聚类，为不同风险偏好投资者的资产配置提供了更精确的工具。

Abstract: Stochastic Dominance (SD) theory provides a rigorous framework for selecting superior assets tailored to the asset allocation needs of investors with varying risk preferences (i.e., risk-averse, risk-seeking, and risk-neutral). However, traditional stock clustering methods typically rely on geometric metrics such as Euclidean distance, which often fail to effectively capture the intrinsic risk dominance relationships among assets. To address this limitation, this paper proposes an innovative clustering analysis framework based on SD test statistics. Methodologically, this study deeply integrates SD theory with machine learning algorithms. Transcending the limitations of traditional reliance on geometric distance, we innovatively utilize test statistics from first-, second-, and third-order SD to construct a "Stochastic Dominance Coefficient Matrix." Building upon this matrix, we modify the classic K-means and Hierarchical Clustering algorithms. Specifically, we derive 12 distinct algorithm variants tailored to different orders of SD relationships. Simultaneously, we construct the SD-SC coefficient and the SD-DBI index as specialized validity indices to evaluate the clustering performance. Empirically, we analyze constituent stock data from a representative developed market (the US NASDAQ Index) and an emerging market (China's CSI 100 Index). The results verify the effectiveness and robustness of the proposed method. Furthermore, we apply the clustering results to the modification of the Single Index Model and the construction of Global Minimum Variance Portfolios (GMVP). The findings demonstrate that the proposed method effectively facilitates customized asset allocation for investors, holding significant theoretical value and practical implications.

</details>


### [317] [Affinity Graph Connectivity in Convex Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24673)
*Sam Rosen,Jason Xu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文将凸聚类的有限样本理论推广至一般连通图，利用随机游走方法量化了图结构对聚类性能的影响，并指出输入亲和权重的调节是提升凸聚类效果的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的凸聚类理论主要局限于特定设置，缺乏对一般连通图作为亲和权重结构的研究，无法充分揭示不同底层数据连接结构对聚类性能的影响。

Method: 利用随机游走（random walks）理论框架，结合随机图模型的集中不等式，推广了凸聚类的有限样本界限。

Result: 推导出了针对一般连通图的凸聚类有限样本误差界限，提供了新的质心恢复收敛速率，并揭示了聚类性能与图连接性之间的定量关系。

Conclusion: 凸聚类中超参数的调节（特别是亲和权重）对于其性能至关重要，且该研究为理解不同图结构下聚类行为提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: We generalize finite-sample bounds for convex clustering to the setting where affinity weights appearing in the objective correspond to a general connected graph. These bounds and their analysis lead to a better understanding of clustering behavior under various implied connectivity structures behind the data and to new rates of convergence for centroid recovery. The new theoretical framework is based on random walks, which allow application of concentration inequalities related to random graph models, and formalizes the relationship between the clustering performance and the connectivity of the graph structures. Through the form of the bound and empirical results, we argue proper tuning of hyperparameters to convex clustering problems should also include tuning of input affinity weights.

</details>


### [318] [Counterfactually Safe Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25114)
*Jingyi Li,Peng Wu,Chengchun Shi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对强化学习在优化全局平均收益时可能忽视个体安全的问题，本文从反事实角度定义了个体伤害，并提出了一种双阶段学习框架，在实现高性能的同时有效控制了个体伤害事件的发生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习算法主要关注总体平均回报最大化，忽视了个体水平的性能差异，可能导致特定个体面临安全风险。

Method: 提出了一种两阶段学习流程：第一阶段从反事实角度定义并量化“个体伤害”（定义为行动结果显著劣于基准线）；第二阶段在保证伤害率受控的前提下优化策略。

Result: 通过模拟和真实世界数据集实验，验证了所提方法在兼顾性能与个体安全性方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 研究证明该方法在最大化期望收益的同时，能有效控制个体伤害率，且在有限样本下具有理论上的次优间隙上限。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning algorithms are generally designed to maximize the expected return across a population. However, a policy that is optimal on average may be suboptimal for certain individuals, leading to potential safety concerns. To address this, we first formalize the notion of individual harm from a counterfactual perspective and define harm as the event in which a chosen action results in a strictly worse outcome than a baseline alternative. We then propose a general two-stage procedure for learning policies that maximize the expected return while accounting for individual harm. We further establish the finite-sample properties of the learned policy, derive an upper bound on its sub-optimality gap, and show that the harm rate remains well-controlled. Numerical experiments on both simulated and real-world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [319] [Nyström Kernel Stein Discrepancy Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25173)
*Florian Kalinke,Zoltán Szabó,Bharath K. Sriperumbudur*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文证明了利用Nyström方法加速KSD拟合优度检验是统计上有效的，解决了传统O(n²)算法在自助法框架下计算开销大的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的KSD拟合优度检验存在两个主要痛点：计算复杂度为样本量的二次方（O(n²)），且其渐进零分布通常难以处理，需要依赖计算开销巨大的自助法。尽管Nyström方法可以加速KSD估计，但其对自助法检验统计性质的影响尚不明确。

Method: 从理论上证明了基于Nyström加速的KSD估计器在自助法（bootstrap）检验中能够保留与经典二次时间估计器相同的渐进性质；并通过球形数据和函数型数据的数值实验验证了其效率。

Result: 证明了Nyström加速的KSD检验在渐进水平和局部一致性上与全量计算方法保持一致，且数值实验表明该方法在大幅节省运行时间的同时，统计表现相当。

Conclusion: Nyström加速方法能够在保持与二次时间算法相当的统计功效（渐进水平和局部一致性）的同时，显著降低核Stein差异（KSD）拟合优度检验的计算复杂度，使其在大规模数据集上更加实用。

Abstract: Kernel Stein discrepancy (KSD) is among the most popular goodness-of-fit (GoF) measures on general domains with a large number of successful deployments. One of the main applications of KSD is in constructing powerful GoF tests. However, tests relying on the classical U-/V-statistic-based KSD estimators have two major drawbacks. (i) Their runtime scales quadratically in the number of samples. (ii) Their asymptotic null distribution is computationally intractable in most cases, typically handled by bootstrapping. While it is known that the Nyström method permits accelerating KSD estimation with no loss of statistical accuracy under mild conditions, to the best of our knowledge, the fundamental question of its impact on bootstrap-based GoF testing is open; resolving this question is the focus of the current paper. In particular, we prove that the key properties of the quadratic-time bootstrapped KSD-based GoF test (asymptotic level and local consistency) are preserved by its Nyström acceleration. We numerically demonstrate the efficiency of the accelerated KSD estimator and bootstrap in the context of GoF testing of spherical and functional data. Our numerical results show that the Nyström-accelerated method performs statistically on-par with the quadratic-time approach, while requiring substantially smaller runtime.

</details>


### [320] [Learning manifold diffusion semigroups from graph transition matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25383)
*Xiuyuan Cheng,Nan Wu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将图扩散过程与流形热半群关联的数学框架，证明了在低正则条件下，即使面对非均匀采样，图转移矩阵迭代也能以 O(N^{-2/(d+6)}) 的速率收敛于热半群，并有效解决了样本外推广问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究在将图拉普拉斯算子推广到流形热半群逼近时，通常需要较高的函数正则性要求；此外，如何处理离散样本中的非均匀采样密度，并保证在较长扩散时间下的收敛性仍是关键问题。

Method: 利用有限 i.i.d. 样本，通过环境高斯核构建图扩散过程；引入图转移矩阵 P 的右归一化技术以校正非均匀采样；通过算子分析法，在 L∞ 范数下推导了 P^n f 与流形热半群 Qf 的误差界，并应用核卷积方法实现了样本外推广。

Result: 在 L∞ 范数下，证明了误差界 $\| P^n f - Q_t f \|$ 达到经典的 O(N^{-2/(d+6)}) 收敛速率（忽略对数因子）；该结果适用于扩散时间 t 高达 O(1) 或更长的情况，且在非均匀采样密度假设（p ∈ C³）下依然有效。

Conclusion: 研究证明了在仅需低正则性（如 L∞）假设下，图扩散过程可以有效地收敛于流形热半群。通过右归一化处理，该方法对非均匀采样密度具有鲁棒性，且在样本内和样本外推广中均保持了一致的收敛速率。

Abstract: We consider graph diffusion processes constructed from finite i.i.d. samples drawn from an unknown manifold embedded in ambient Euclidean space, where the graph affinity is defined by an ambient Gaussian kernel matrix. We show that the manifold heat semigroup $Q_t = e^{tΔ}$ can be approximated directly by iterating the graph transition matrix $P$, under only low regularity assumptions on the test function $f$, including the case $f \in L^\infty$. We bound $\| P^n f - Q_t f \|$ in $\infty$-norm, with the operator application to $f$ properly defined, and we recover the classical graph-Laplacian pointwise rate $O(N^{-2/(d+6)})$ up to logarithmic factors, for diffusion times $t $ up to $O(1)$ and longer. The rate holds for in-sample error as well as out-of-sample generalization, where the estimator of $Q_t f$ at a new point is defined via kernel convolution. To handle non-uniform sampling densities on the manifold, we introduce a right-normalization of the graph transition matrix; under the assumption that the sampling density $p$ is $C^3$ and bounded away from zero, the same convergence rates hold. We numerically demonstrate the performance of the proposed estimator on simulated data.

</details>


### [321] [Mean-Shift PCA by Knockoff Mean](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25460)
*Mengda Li,Zeng Li,Jianfeng Yao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对PCA对均值偏移敏感的问题，论文提出通过主动引入特定的噪声（knockoff mean-shift）来消除有害的均值漂移，利用随机矩阵理论实现了简单高效的鲁棒主成分提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准PCA对样本均值偏移高度敏感，极少量偏差即可导致主成分发生巨大漂移。现有的鲁棒PCA方法难以有效处理高维混合模型中的均值偏移污染结构。

Method: 提出了一种简单的两阶段PCA算法，核心思想是主动引入“替身均值（knockoff mean）”扰动，利用随机矩阵理论（RMT）分析均值偏移对谱的影响，从而识别并移除受污染的成分。

Result: 理论上证明了均值偏移产生的“尖峰（spikes）”与原始协方差矩阵的稳定特征值在谱上是可分的；实验表明该方法能有效提取真实主成分，且性能独立于混合比例。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了均值偏移导致的扰动在频谱上是可分离的，且原始特征空间对这种污染具有渐进不变性。通过引入“替身（knockoff）”均值扰动，该方法能够有效地在无需复杂鲁棒优化的情况下实现稳健的主成分分析。

Abstract: Removing noise is difficult, but adding noise is easy. In this work, we show how to eliminate mean-shift noisy components from PCA by deliberately introducing knockoff mean-shift perturbation. Standard PCA is highly sensitive to shifts in the sample mean: a small fraction of samples from a shifted distribution can cause large deviations in the leading principal components. In high-dimensional regimes, existing Robust PCA approaches cannot handle the mean-shift contamination structure inherent in the mixture model. Using tools from Random Matrix Theory, we prove that the mean-shift spikes are spectrally separable from the stable eigenvalues of the original covariance. Furthermore, the original eigenspace remains asymptotically invariant to the contamination, independent of the mixture weight. Exploiting this spectral stability, we propose a simple, two-stage PCA algorithm by adding knockoff mean that identifies and removes the mean-shift component using only standard PCA operations.

</details>


### [322] [Guided Flow Matching for Forward and Inverse PDE Problems with Sparse Observations: Algorithm and Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25509)
*Xifeng Zhang,Jin Zhao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Reconstructing PDE solutions from sparse observations is a core challenge in scientific computing. We present FM4PDE, a flow-matching generative framework that learns the joint distribution of PDE coefficients (or initial states) and solutions (or final states), enabling both forward simulation and inverse recovery with limited paired data. At inference, sampling is guided by a composite loss that enforces agreement with sparse measurements and reduces the PDE residual; we support deterministic, stochastic, and hybrid samplers. We provide error guarantees for these guided procedures. For the deterministic optimizer, a coercivity condition ensures trajectory boundedness and a phase-wise contraction yields logarithmic complexity in the target accuracy. For the stochastic sampler, we introduce adaptive guidance and assume dissipativity of the velocity field to obtain uniform moment bounds independent of the noise-floor parameter. This leads to polynomial-time error bounds, and a matching lower bound shows constant guidance induces an unavoidable positive bias, motivating adaptivity. A hybrid deterministic-stochastic analysis is also provided. Experiments on static and time-dependent benchmark PDEs demonstrate competitive accuracy and faster inference than diffusion-based generative models.

</details>


### [323] [PAC Learning with Bandit Feedback: Sharp Sample Complexity in the Realizable Setting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25678)
*Steve Hanneke,Qinglin Meng,Shay Moran,Amirreza Shaeiri*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了只提供“正确/错误”反馈的多类PAC学习问题，通过引入“强盗DS维度”和“伪箱”结构，精确刻画了其样本复杂度，并提出了一种通用的ListCascade算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在可实现设置下，研究多类PAC学习在“强盗反馈”（仅知预测是否正确）这一受限条件下的样本复杂度，并寻求其最优刻画。

Method: 定义了新的组合维度“强盗DS维度（bandit DS dimension）”；引入了“伪箱（pseudo-boxes）”组合结构；提出了ListCascade算法以实现复杂度上界。

Result: 推导出样本复杂度取决于强盗DS维度，该维度通过聚合伪箱各坐标的邻居数来计算，并证明了该复杂度在对数因子范围内对各类假设空间都是最优的。

Conclusion: 本文为多类PAC学习中带强盗反馈的设置提供了最优样本复杂度的完整刻画，并提出了一种通用的ListCascade算法，为理解强盗学习与列表学习之间的关系提供了新视角。

Abstract: We study the problem of multiclass PAC learning with bandit feedback in the realizable setting. In this framework, there is an unknown data distribution over an instance space $\mathcal{X}$ and a label space $\mathcal{Y}$, as in classical multiclass PAC learning, but the learner does not observe the labels of the i.i.d. training examples. Instead, in each round, it receives an unlabeled instance, predicts its label, and receives bandit feedback indicating only whether the prediction is correct. Despite this restriction, the goal remains the same as in classical PAC learning. We provide a general characterization of the optimal sample complexity of this problem, sharp for every concept class up to logarithmic factors. Our characterization is based on a new combinatorial dimension, termed the bandit $\mathrm{DS}$ dimension, defined via generalized combinatorial structures we call pseudo-boxes. These extend the pseudo-cubes underlying the $\mathrm{DS}$ dimension by allowing a different number of neighbors in each coordinate. In contrast to the $\mathrm{DS}$ dimension, which governs the full-information setting by counting the number of coordinates in the pseudo-cube, the bandit $\mathrm{DS}$ dimension aggregates the number of neighbors across coordinates, leading to a characterization in which the sample complexity scales with the total number of neighbors. We also propose a general learning algorithm achieving the upper bound, based on an algorithmic principle called ListCascade, which connects bandit learning to list learning and may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [324] [From DPPs to $k$-DPPs: identifiability analysis via spectral decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25526)
*Hideitsu Hino,Keisuke Yano*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文分析了DPP中谱分解参数的可识别性，揭示了将DPP条件化为固定基数k-DPP后，由于伸缩、旋转等几何不变量的存在，导致参数识别变得更加复杂且存在明确的非可识别性空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究行列式点过程（DPP）的几何结构及其谱分解参数（$Λ, U$）的可识别性问题，特别是分析条件化（ conditioning）对DPP统计模型参数推断带来的本质影响。

Method: 利用谱分解 $L=UΛU^{\top}$，通过分析$k$-DPP下的条件分布结构，结合群论不变量（scale, sign similarity, rotation）及参数维数统计（维度计数定理）来界定可识别性差距。

Result: 量化了全DPP与$k$-DPP之间的可识别性差距：全DPP仅存在离散的符号不变量，而$k$-DPP不仅存在伸缩、符号和旋转三种明确的不变量，且当$\binom{N}{k}<N(N+1)/2$时，存在额外的连续非可识别性。

Conclusion: 对于行列式点过程（DPP），谱参数（特征值与特征向量）的可识别性受限于特定的几何不变量。对于固定基数的k-DPP，由于存在伸缩、符号相似性、特征空间旋转以及维度约束带来的冗余，其参数识别难度远高于全DPP。

Abstract: We study the geometry of determinantal point processes (DPPs) through the spectral decomposition $L=UΛU^{\top}$. The spectrum $Λ$ governs the cardinality distribution via elementary symmetric polynomials, while the eigenspace orientation $U$ governs the conditional law within each fixed-cardinality stratum. Conditioning on cardinality $k$ yields the $k$-DPP, for which the identifiability structure changes fundamentally: the spectral parameter becomes identifiable only up to a common scale, and the eigenspace rotation parameter is identifiable only through squared minors of the eigenvector matrix. We characterize the identifiability gap precisely, via three explicit invariances (scale, sign similarity, and eigenspace rotation) and a dimension-counting theorem showing the existence of additional continuous non-identifiability whenever $\binom{N}{k}<N(N+1)/2$. In contrast, for the full DPP the non-identifiability comes only from the discrete sign similarity.

</details>


### [325] [Nonstationary Generalized Linear Bandits with Discounted Online Mirror Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25590)
*Joongkyu Lee,Min-hwan Oh*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对非平稳广义线性赌博机（GLBs）引入了 DOMD-GLB 算法，通过折现在线镜像下降，成功将每轮计算和内存开销降为常数级 $O(1)$，同时保证了数学上的高效遗憾界，克服了现有相关文献中算法复杂度随时间线性增长的瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有处理非平稳广义线性赌博机（GLBs）的方法（如滑动窗口、重启或折扣MLE）计算及内存成本随时间增加，且通常涉及非凸投影步骤，限制了在大规模或长期任务中的应用。

Method: 提出了一种基于折现在线镜像下降（Discounted Online Mirror Descent, DOMD）的估计框架，利用该方法进行参数更新，从而避免了传统基于最大似然估计（MLE）方法在处理非平稳性时需要重新处理所有历史观测数据的开销。

Result: 证明了算法在漂移环境下具有 $\tilde{O}(c_μ^{-1/2} d^{3/4} P_T^{1/4} T^{3/4})$ 的动态遗憾上界，在分段平稳环境下具有 $\tilde{O}(c_μ^{-1/3} d^{2/3} Γ_T^{1/3} T^{2/3})$ 的动态遗憾上界，且每轮计算和内存消耗均为 $O(1)$。

Conclusion: 作者提出了 DOMD-GLB 算法，这是首个在非平稳广义线性赌博机（GLBs）中实现单轮计算和内存成本与时间无关（O(1)）的算法，同时保持了强有力的动态遗憾上界，在计算效率上显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: We study nonstationary generalized linear bandits (GLBs), where the expected reward is modeled through a nonlinear link function with an unknown time-varying parameter. This framework encompasses a broad class of reward models, including linear, Bernoulli, and binomial rewards. Existing approaches are predominantly based on maximum-likelihood estimation (MLE), using sliding-window, restart, or discounting mechanisms to handle nonstationarity. Although these methods achieve statistically efficient regret guarantees, they generally require revisiting past observations at every round, which leads to computation and memory costs that grow with time; moreover, several of them rely on a non-convex projection step. In this paper, we propose DOMD-GLB, a new algorithm for nonstationary GLBs that utilizes discounted online mirror descent (DOMD) for parameter estimation, thereby incurring only $O(1)$ computation and memory costs per round. We prove dynamic regret bounds of order $\tilde{O} \big(c_μ^{-1/2} d^{3/4} P_T^{1/4} T^{3/4}\big)$ in drifting environments and $\tilde{O}\big(c_μ^{-1/3} d^{2/3} Γ_T^{1/3} T^{2/3}\big) $in piecewise-stationary environments, where $d$ denotes the feature dimension, $T$ the time horizon, $P_T$ the path length, $Γ_T$ the number of change points, and $c_μ$ a curvature parameter associated with the link function, while substantially improving computational efficiency over prior work. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first algorithm for nonstationary GLBs with per-round computation and memory costs independent of time.

</details>


### [326] [Optimal Design for Multinomial Logit Model with Applications to Best Assortment Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25592)
*Joongkyu Lee,Min-hwan Oh*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对组合动作空间的MNL臂问题，本文提出了高效的实验设计框架（MILP与Lifted Design），在保证统计最优性的同时解决了计算复杂性问题，并给出了最佳组合识别算法的最优样本复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MNL 臂问题具有组合动作空间，导致传统的实验设计方法在计算上极其难以处理（不可行），现有的在线学习算法缺乏高效的实验设计框架。

Method: 1. 提出了两种方案：基于0-1混合整数线性规划（MILP）的精确/近似求解器；2. 提出了基于Lifted Design的完全多项式时间近似 surrogate 优化方法；3. 利用 Kiefer-Wolfowitz 等价定理建立了G-最优性保证及统计-计算权衡分析。

Result: 1. 实现了可扩展且具备统计效率的实验设计；2. 对于具有线性效用和非均匀收入的MNL臂问题，实现了 $\tilde{O}\big(\frac{d \log N}{Δ^2}\big)$ 的实例依赖性样本复杂度。

Conclusion: 该论文为多项式Logit（MNL）臂问题提供了一种计算高效且具备统计最优性的实验设计框架，并成功将其应用于最佳组合识别任务中，实现了理论上的最优采样复杂度。

Abstract: We study optimal experimental design for multinomial logit (MNL) bandits, where an agent repeatedly selects a subset of $K$ items from a ground set of size $N$ and observes single-choice feedback. Unlike linear or generalized linear bandits, MNL bandits have a combinatorial action space, which makes classical optimal design approaches and naive optimization over all subsets computationally intractable. We propose a computationally efficient optimal design framework for MNL models that achieves both statistical efficiency and scalability through two complementary approaches: (i) an exact or certified-approximate reformulation of the design oracle as a $0$-$1$ mixed-integer linear program (MILP) with solver-certified early stopping, and (ii) a fully polynomial-time lifted design that replaces the nonlinear objective with a tractable surrogate. Using the Kiefer-Wolfowitz equivalence theorem, we establish near G-optimality guarantees and characterize the induced statistical-computational trade-offs. As an application, we develop a best assortment identification algorithm for MNL bandits with linear utilities and non-uniform revenues, and prove an instance-dependent sample complexity of $\tilde{O}\big(\frac{d \log N}{Δ^2}\big)$, where $d$ is the feature dimension, $N$ is the number of arms, and $Δ$ is the minimum revenue gap.

</details>


### [327] [StrTransformer: Source-Wise Structured Transformers for Unsupervised Blind Source Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25648)
*Yuan-Hao Wei*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为 StrTransformer 的新型盲源恢复框架，通过直接优化潜在源矩阵与结构化 Transformer 分支，配合有序多尺度控制器，实现了对复杂时域数据的有效分解与结构化建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决传统盲源分离和潜在建模过程中依赖编码器推断潜在变量的局限性，并希望通过引入可微分的结构约束来更有效地恢复源信号并提取具有时空特征的潜在轨迹。

Method: 提出了 StrTransformer 框架：1. 直接优化潜在源矩阵及观察空间混合器；2. 利用分支结构化 Transformer 处理各源轨迹，包含多尺度补丁标记化、掩码机制及局部偏置的 Transformer 架构；3. 引入掩码补丁重构能量作为结构先验；4. 开发了有序多尺度控制器，以实现分支间的尺度专门化。

Result: 研究表明，StrTransformer 能够成功将 learned 分支收敛到不同的时间尺度结构中，并能够在事后评估中准确恢复与源对齐的潜在轨迹；同时证明了模型目标函数的解耦结构和置换对称性的有效降低。

Conclusion: StrTransformer 提供了一种直接优化潜在源矩阵而不依赖传统编码器的方法，通过结构化先验和有序多尺度控制器实现了盲源恢复，并能有效地将不同的潜在分支专门化为不同的时域特征。

Abstract: This paper proposes StrTransformer, a source-wise structured Transformer framework for blind source recovery and branch-wise latent modeling. Instead of using an encoder to infer latent variables, StrTransformer directly optimizes the latent source matrix together with an observation-space mixer and source-wise structural Transformer branches. The mixer enforces reconstruction consistency, while each Transformer branch imposes a differentiable structural constraint on one latent source trajectory. Specifically, each source is converted into multi-scale patch tokens, randomly masked, processed by a locality-biased Transformer, and evaluated through a masked patch reconstruction energy. This energy acts as an implicit source-wise structural prior. To encourage different latent branches to specialize into different temporal regimes, StrTransformer further introduces an ordered multi-scale controller that learns branch-specific patch-scale weights, ordered scale centers, and locality attention slopes. The resulting objective combines observation reconstruction, source-wise structural regularization, and modular auxiliary penalties for separation and scale specialization. We analyze the decoupling and coupling structure of the objective, the regularized exact-reconstruction fiber, and the reduction of permutation symmetry induced by ordered branch descriptors. A controlled case study shows that the learned branches converge to distinct temporal-scale structures and recover source-aligned latent trajectories under post-hoc evaluation.

</details>


### [328] [Efficient Benchmarking Is Just Feature Selection and Multiple Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25773)
*Sam Bowyer,Acyr Locatelli,Kris Cao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文通过引入核岭回归和 mRMR 特征选择算法，实现了 LLM 高效基准预测技术的显著优化，在保持低计算成本的同时提升了预测精度与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高效评估基准测试的方法计算复杂度较高，亟需一种更高效、更准确的方法通过少量问题子集预测整体性能以降低 LLM 评估成本。

Method: 将基准测试预测建模为多重回归问题，引入“核岭回归（Kernel Ridge Regression）”用于预测，并应用“最大相关最小冗余（mRMR）”算法进行问题子集选择。

Result: 相比现有方法，该方法在 MAE、RMSE 指标上误差更小，在 Spearman $\rho$ 和 Kendall $\tau$ 相关性上表现更强，且运算速度更快，在不同数据划分下的子集选择稳定性更高。

Conclusion: 通过核岭回归与 mRMR 特征选择相结合，能够以更低的计算成本更准确地预测 LLM 基准测试得分，且该方法在性能指标和稳定性上优于现有算法。

Abstract: Efficient benchmarking techniques aim to lower the computational cost of evaluating LLMs by predicting full benchmark scores using only a subset of a benchmark's questions. By reframing this problem as an instance of multiple regression with feature selection, we find that existing efficient benchmarking methods can be greatly improved by simply using kernel ridge regression at the prediction stage. Additionally, using an information-theoretic feature-selection algorithm called minimum redundancy maximum relevance (mRMR), we can further improve upon these methods by selecting question subsets that will be maximally useful for prediction. Except in very data-poor settings, these approaches consistently achieve smaller prediction errors (in both MAE and RMSE), and greater ranking correlation between predicted and true scores (in both Spearman $ρ$ and Kendall $τ$) across a range of benchmarks using both binary and continuous metrics. Furthermore, mRMR subsampling is much faster than competitor methods (which often involve fitting probabilistic models or running clustering algorithms), and is more likely to select the same questions under different random seeds or training data splits. Tutorial code can be found at https://github.com/sambowyer/mrmr_eval .

</details>


### [329] [Statistical Inference for Stochastic Gradient Descent Beyond Finite Variance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26000)
*Jose Blanchet,Peter Glynn,Wenhao Yang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在随机梯度具有无限方差时仍能为SGD轨迹构建有效置信区间的稳健方法，通过一种简单、无需复杂参数估计的自归一化统计量处理了尾部不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模随机优化中，当随机梯度具有无限方差（即不满足经典渐近正态性假设）时，传统的基于SGD的统计推断面临挑战，现有的方法依赖于未知的扰动参数且难以实现。

Method: 利用Polyak-Ruppert平均估计器与由随机梯度构造的经验二阶矩归一化因子联合弱收敛的结果；通过自归一化统计量抵消尾部依赖项；采用子抽样（subsampling）校准方案来估计临界值，无需显式估计尾部指数或稳定律参数。

Result: 该方法具备模型无关性；在有限方差和无限方差两种情形下均能保持渐近有效性；仿真实验验证了其在不同设置下均具有可靠的覆盖率。

Conclusion: 提出了一种在随机梯度缺乏二阶矩（即无限方差）情况下依然有效的SGD置信区间构建方法，为在大规模随机优化中进行不确定性量化提供了实用的工具。

Abstract: Stochastic gradient descent (SGD) is a foundational algorithm for large-scale statistical learning and stochastic optimization. However, statistical inference based on SGD iterates remains challenging when stochastic gradients have infinite variance, as the relevant limiting distributions depend on unknown nuisance parameters. In this paper, we develop an efficient, model-agnostic methodology for constructing confidence regions from SGD trajectories that applies in both finite- and infinite-variance regimes. The procedure is based on a joint weak convergence result for the Polyak-Ruppert averaged estimator and an empirical second-moment normalizer constructed from stochastic gradients along the SGD trajectory. This joint limit yields a self-normalized statistic in which the leading tail-dependent scaling terms cancel. We then use a subsampling calibration scheme to estimate the relevant critical values, avoiding explicit estimation of tail indices, slowly varying functions, or stable-law parameters. The resulting confidence regions are straightforward to implement and are asymptotically valid under both the finite- and infinite-second-moment regimes. Simulation studies show reliable coverage in various settings, supporting the proposed method as a practical tool for uncertainty quantification in stochastic optimization.

</details>
